Moreover, identical supervision across RGB and non-RGB modalities may lead to modality imbalance. We propose a unified framework for quantized semantic distillation tailored to edge deployment. By jointly optimizing distillation and quantization within a unified teacher-anchored framework, our method ensures consistent training under quantization, suppressing hard negatives and enlarging decision margins. Additionally, we design a lightweight cross-attention adapter that enhances non-RGB representations through RGB-guided semantic transfer, narrowing the modality gap. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements on non-RGB modalities while maintaining deployment efficiency.

</details>


### [34] [Efficient 3D Whole-Body PET Image Denoising via Conditional Rectified Flow With Optimized Sampling Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16690)
*Jiale Shen,Guolin Wang,Chenhao Wang,Xinhui Su,Wei Luo,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单次前向的条件3D整流流（3D Flow）框架，用于全身PET图像去噪，结合优化的非均匀采样策略，在约30秒内完成全3D体积重建（对比3D DDPM基线需数小时），并通过零样本迁移在独立临床数据集及低至标准剂量1/100的未见过剂量水平上展现出良好的全局图像质量和病灶显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低PET辐射暴露对患者安全至关重要，但超低剂量成像因严重噪声影响诊断。现有3D扩散模型虽重建保真度高但推理时间长；而2D替代方案难以保持体积一致性，影响全身PET分析。为弥合二者差距，需要一种兼顾重建保真度与计算效率的方法。

Method: 提出单次前向的条件3D整流流（3D Flow）框架，采用优化的非均匀采样策略，通过一次性线性插值速度匹配目标进行训练。该模型以单通道处理方式重建全3D体积。

Result: 该方法约30秒即可完成全3D体积重建，远快于3D DDPM基线的数小时推理。在独立临床数据集上的零样本迁移评估显示，模型在全局图像质量和病灶显著性上优于3D DDPM与DDIM基线，尤其对具有挑战性的短采集数据表现良好；同时在未见剂量水平（低至1/100标准剂量）上展现出有前景的零样本迁移性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在重建保真度与计算效率之间取得平衡，可作为超低剂量全身PET图像去噪的候选方案。虽经伪影可视化比较支持，仍建议进一步开展病灶级验证。

Abstract: Reducing radiation exposure in Positron Emission Tomography (PET) is important for patient safety; however, ultra-low-dose imaging suffers from severe noise, which may affect diagnostic interpretation without appropriate image enhancement. While current 3D deep generative models, particularly diffusion models, have shown strong reconstruction fidelity, their practical use can be limited by long inference times. In contrast, faster 2D-based alternatives may have difficulty maintaining volumetric consistency, an important consideration for whole-body PET imaging analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose a one-pass conditional 3D rectified flow (3D Flow) framework for whole-body PET image denoising that incorporates a novel optimized non-uniform sampling strategy. The model is trained with a one-pass linear-interpolant velocity-matching objective. This approach reconstructs a full 3D volume in approximately 30 seconds in our implementation, compared with multi-hour inference for the evaluated 3D DDPM baseline. Evaluations including zero-shot transfer to an independent clinical dataset show that our model achieves favorable global image quality and lesion conspicuity compared with the evaluated 3D DDPM and DDIM baselines, including on challenging short-acquisition data. Furthermore, the proposed method shows promising zero-shot transfer performance across the evaluated datasets and unseen dose levels (down to 1/100 of the standard dose), with artifact-focused visual comparisons supporting the need for further lesion-level validation. By balancing reconstruction fidelity and computational efficiency, this work presents a candidate approach for ultra-low-dose whole-body PET image denoising.

</details>


### [35] [MAETrack: Unleashing the Potential of Pretrained Geometric Priors for 3D Single Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16695)
*Sifan Zhou,Qiwei Wang,Linyue Tan,Ziyu Liu,Ziyu Zhao,Xiaobo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAETrack，一个轻量级自适应框架，通过层选择性初始化和几何残差门控来解决3D自监督掩码自编码器预训练特征在3D单目标跟踪中微调次优的问题。研究发现深层网络偏向重建预训练任务而浅层保留可迁移几何信息，据此仅初始化骨干网络浅层并强化搜索BEV特征中结构显著区域，在标准基准上以有限计算开销持续优于普通微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练在2D视觉中已彻底改变表征学习，但其向3D单目标跟踪的迁移能力尚未被充分理解。直接微调自监督3D编码器（如MAE）常导致次优适应，因为重建目标与跟踪所需的时空匹配要求不完全对齐。作者将这一困难解释为逐层迁移错配：浅层保留可迁移几何线索，深层则过度专化于重建自监督任务。

Method: 提出MAETrack轻量级自适应框架，包含两个核心组件：1）层选择性初始化（LSI），仅从预训练权重初始化跟踪骨干的浅层阶段而重新初始化深层阶段；2）几何残差门控（GRG），在模板-搜索融合前通过残差空间调制强化搜索BEV特征中的结构显著区域。

Result: 在标准3D SOT基准上的大量实验表明，MAETrack在有限计算开销下持续优于普通微调基线。更广泛地，结果揭示了有效迁移的关键在于跟踪导向的迁移原则：保留浅层几何信息同时使深层表征适应下游目标。

Conclusion: 从3D重建预训练到3D跟踪的有效迁移不仅仅取决于部分微调，而是依赖于一个面向跟踪的迁移原则——保留浅层几何特征并将深层表征重新适配到下游跟踪目标。MAETrack通过层选择性初始化和几何残差门控实现了这一原则，为利用重建式自监督预训练进行3D跟踪提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-training has transformed representation learning in 2D vision, yet its transferability to 3D single object tracking (SOT) remains insufficiently understood. Directly fine-tuning self-supervised 3D encoders, such as masked autoencoders (MAE), often leads to sub-optimal adaptation because the reconstruction objective is not fully aligned with the spatial-temporal matching requirements of tracking. In this paper, we observe that this difficulty can be interpreted as a layer-wise transfer mismatch: shallow layers tend to preserve transferable geometric cues, while deeper layers become increasingly specialized to the reconstruction pretext task and are less suitable for downstream tracking. Based on this observation, we propose MAETrack, a lightweight adaptation framework for transferring pre-training MAE representations to 3D SOT. MAETrack includes Layer-Selective Initialization (LSI), which initializes only the shallow stages of the tracking backbone from pre-trained weights while re-initializing deeper stages, and Geometric Residual Gating (GRG), which reinforces structurally salient regions in the search BEV features before template-search fusion through residual spatial modulation. Extensive experiments on standard 3D SOT benchmarks show that MAETrack consistently improves upon vanilla fine-tuning baselines with limited computational overhead. More broadly, our results suggest that effective transfer from 3D reconstruction pre-training to 3D tracking is not merely a matter of partial fine-tuning, but depends on a tracking-oriented transfer principle that preserves shallow geometry while adapting deeper representations to the downstream objective.

</details>


### [36] [PriorPose: Reference-Guided Joint Deformation and Alignment for Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16727)
*Yihan Chen,Huan Ren,Wenfei Yang,Hang Du,Tianzhu Zhang,Feng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PriorPose，一个参考引导的显式类别先验对应框架，在共享特征空间中联合解决类别级物体姿态估计中的规范化与对齐问题，在多数基准指标上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别级姿态估计方法分为两类：无先验方法直接回归NOCS坐标，将规范坐标系隐式记忆在网络权重中，导致参数绑定类别典型朝向、分布偏移下泛化差；有先验方法通常采用串行的"先变形后对齐"流程，未充分约束的规范补全会污染对应关系并造成误差级联。因此需要一种保持显式类别先验、避免误差级联的联合求解方案。

Method: PriorPose采用参考引导的对应框架：1) 参考引导的seed transformer将部分观测与类别先验作为token集输入，通过几何感知种子融合；2) 联合预测可见点的逐点NOCS场与先验的规范变形以重建完整规范实例；3) 深度姿态头从诱导对应关系回归(R,t,s)；4) 两部分的形状一致性目标（规范空间与相机空间一致性损失）耦合对应、变形与姿态。

Result: 在标准与更大类别基准上，PriorPose在大多数评估指标上达到新SOTA，尤其在严格姿态阈值下表现突出；在宽松姿态与IoU指标上保持竞争力，并在形状变化与域偏移下展现出更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PriorPose通过显式先验与联合优化，减少对记忆化的规范朝向的依赖，避免了先变形后对齐的误差级联，在类别级姿态估计任务上实现了更优的准确率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Category-level object pose estimation seeks to recover a similarity transform $(R,t,s)$ for unseen instances without instance-specific CAD models. Most competitive methods are correspondence-based: prior-free variants regress canonical (NOCS) coordinates directly from local observations and implicitly memorize the canonical frame in the weights, which ties the parameters to category-typical orientations and hurts generalization under distribution shift; prior-based variants introduce a category prior but typically follow a serial deform-then-align pipeline, where underconstrained canonical completion can corrupt correspondences and induce error cascades in pose. We propose PriorPose, a reference-guided correspondence framework that keeps the category prior explicit and solves canonicalization and alignment jointly in a shared feature space. A reference-guided seeded transformer embeds the partial observation and the category prior as token sets and fuses them via geometry-aware seeds, from which the network jointly predicts a per-point NOCS field for visible points and a canonical deformation of the prior that reconstructs a full canonical instance, while a deep pose head regresses $(R,t,s)$ from the induced correspondences. A two-part shape consistency objective, with canonical-space and camera-space consistency losses, couples correspondence, deformation, and pose, reducing reliance on memorized canonical orientations and avoiding deform-then-align error cascades. Experiments on standard and larger-category benchmarks demonstrate that PriorPose sets new state-of-the-art results on most evaluated metrics, especially under strict pose thresholds, while remaining competitive on relaxed pose and IoU metrics and showing improved robustness under shape variation and domain shift.

</details>


### [37] [De-GAN - Dynamic Parameter Tuned GAN for 3D Medical Image Segmentation: A Step Towards Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16755)
*Zoha Usama,Azadeh Alavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DE-GAN，一种对比度增强的条件生成对抗网络，通过输入自适应动态卷积、风格感知特征混合和坐标编码合成切片自适应的FLAIR图像，以解决脑肿瘤分割中增强肿瘤（ET）低对比度、组织重叠以及跨扫描仪/站点域偏移的问题。生成的FLAIR与原始MR模态拼接后用于训练3D U-Net，在BraTS 2015/2018/2019数据集上多数TC/ET指标优于基线和静态EnhGAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤分割仍具挑战性，因为增强肿瘤（ET）对比度低且与周围组织重叠，同时扫描仪和站点差异导致域偏移，影响分割性能。

Method: 提出DE-GAN，一种对比度增强的条件GAN，采用输入自适应动态卷积、风格感知特征混合与坐标编码来合成切片自适应的FLAIR图像；通过标签引导的类条件目标分离肿瘤核心（TC）和ET的强度，同时保持解剖结构。生成的FLAIR与原始MR模态拼接后用于训练3D U-Net分割模型。

Result: 在BraTS 2015、2018和2019数据集上，DE-GAN在多数报告的TC/ET指标上优于基线和静态EnhGAN替代方案，且保留原始与增强FLAIR两者的组合带来最大增益。

Conclusion: DE-GAN通过保留原始和增强FLAIR相结合的方式有效提升了脑肿瘤分割尤其在TC/ET指标上的表现，并已开源代码与预训练模型。

Abstract: Brain tumor segmentation remains difficult because enhancing tumor (ET) has low contrast and overlaps surrounding tissue, while scanner and site variation causes domain shift. We propose DE-GAN, a contrast-enhancing conditional GAN that combines input-adaptive dynamic convolutions, style-aware feature mixing, and coordinate encoding to synthesize slice-adaptive FLAIR images. A label-guided, class-conditional target separates tumor-core (TC) and ET intensities while preserving anatomy. The generated FLAIR is concatenated with the original MR modalities and used to train a 3D U-Net. Across BraTS 2015, 2018, and 2019, DE-GAN improves segmentation over the baseline and static EnhGAN replacement on most reported TC/ET metrics, with the largest gains from retaining both original and enhanced FLAIR. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/zkhansuri-ui/DE-GAN.

</details>


### [38] [HLC-GS: Risk-Map-Guided Height-Layer Consistency Gaussian Splatting for DSM Reconstruction from Optical Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16772)
*Jie Yang,Yingdong Pi,Qiyan Luo,Xiaoyu Wang,Lekang Wen,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HLC-GS是一种基于风险图引导的高度层一致性高斯泼溅方法，用于从光学卫星影像重建数字表面模型（DSM），通过显式建模逐像素高度层一致性来缓解高度层混合问题，提升重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在DSM重建中采用alpha加权聚合高斯高度，会将不同高度层的泼溅混合在同一像素或采样位置，产生非物理的中间高程和高度层混合误差。

Method: 提出HLC-GS，包含风险图模块、主导层可靠性校正模块和次要层抑制模块。风险图定位具有异常高度离散和不可靠主导层响应的风险像素，后两个模块分别正则化不可靠主导层响应并抑制弱支持的远次要层响应。

Result: 在DFC2019和IARPA2016数据集上与六种SOTA方法比较，HLC-GS整体精度更优。相比最新的EOGS，平均MAE从1.46m降至1.18m，平均RMSE从2.78m降至2.58m，PAG2.5从86.09%提升至88.61%。

Conclusion: 显式建模逐像素高度层一致性可有效减轻高度层混合问题，提高基于3DGS的光学卫星影像DSM重建几何质量。

Abstract: A Digital Surface Model (DSM) is a fundamental geospatial data product for representing the elevation of the Earth's surface. Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown considerable potential for DSM reconstruction from multi-view optical satellite imagery due to its explicit scene representation and efficient optimization. However, in 3DGS-based DSM generation, alpha-weighted aggregation of Gaussian altitudes may blend splats from different height layers at the same rendered pixel or DSM sampling location, producing non-physical intermediate elevations and height-layer mixing errors. To address this problem, we propose HLC-GS, a risk-map-guided height-layer consistency Gaussian Splatting method for DSM reconstruction from optical satellite imagery. HLC-GS consists of a risk map module, a dominant-layer reliability correction module, and a secondary-layer suppression module. The risk map localizes high-risk pixels with abnormal height dispersion and unreliable dominant-layer responses, while the latter two modules regularize unreliable dominant-layer responses and suppress weakly supported far secondary-layer responses. Extensive experiments are conducted on the DFC2019 and IARPA2016 datasets. Compared with six state-of-the-art DSM reconstruction methods, HLC-GS achieves better overall accuracy. Compared with the latest and precision-enhanced EOGS, HLC-GS reduces the average MAE from 1.46 m to 1.18 m and the average RMSE from 2.78 m to 2.58 m over the evaluated scenes, while improving PAG$_{2.5}$ from 86.09\% to 88.61\%. Overall, these results demonstrate that explicitly modeling per-pixel height-layer consistency alleviates height-layer mixing and improves the geometric quality of 3DGS-based DSM reconstruction from optical satellite imagery.

</details>


### [39] [FSANet: Frequency-Spatial Aware Network for Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16773)
*Ruibo Wang,Ziyi Shen,Huaming Wu,Dong Liang,Kun Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FSANet，一种融合先验知识与双域求解器的图像分割网络，通过结构先验模块、双域感知模块和边缘估计模块分别恢复细节、提取显著特征并去噪、增强边缘感知，解决遮挡、光照不足和不规则结构带来的分割难题。同时构建了包含10种非理想场景的开源数据集SceneX作为新基准，实验验证了其高效性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的分割方法虽精度高，但严重依赖长距离空间特征，计算开销大，且忽视先验知识和噪声模式，导致细节丢失和边界不清晰。此外，缺乏覆盖多种真实场景的综合分割数据集也限制了现有方法的性能提升。

Method: 提出双域求解器架构FSANet，包含三大核心模块：(1)结构先验模块，用于恢复被忽视的细节；(2)双域感知模块，在捕获显著特征的同时分离噪声；(3)边缘估计模块，增强边缘感知以实现更精确分割。同时引入SceneX开源数据集，涵盖10种具有挑战性的非理想场景作为新基准。

Result: 大量实验表明FSANet在效率和有效性上均优于现有方法，并在新基准SceneX上验证了其鲁棒性和真实场景适用性。

Conclusion: FSANet通过融合先验知识与双域求解策略，在保持高效的同时提升了分割的细节恢复与边界精度；SceneX数据集为评估和提升分割模型在非理想场景下的鲁棒性提供了新基准。

Abstract: Image segmentation remains challenging due to occlusions, poor lighting, and irregular structures. Although transformer-based methods achieve high accuracy, they rely heavily on long-range spatial features, leading to high computational costs and neglecting prior knowledge or noise patterns, resulting in missing details and unclear boundaries. To address these issues, we propose Frequency Spatial Aware Network (FSANet), which integrates prior knowledge with a dual-domain solver to sequentially adapt to diverse segmentation tasks. Specifically, we design three key modules: (1) Structure Prior Module, which recovers overlooked details; (2) Dual-Domain Awareness Module, which captures salient features while disentangling noise; and (3) Edge Estimation Module, which enhances edge awareness for more precise segmentation. In addition, the limited availability of comprehensive segmentation datasets covering various real-world scenarios hinders the performance of existing methods. To address this, we introduce SceneX, a novel open-source dataset featuring 10 challenging non-ideal scenarios, establishing a new benchmark for evaluating and improving the robustness and real-world applicability of the segmentation models. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of FSANet.

</details>


### [40] [IMVS: Interactive Medical Volume Segmentation with Test-Time Adaptation - A New Method for Annotating Radiology Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16775)
*Abhilaksh Singh Reen,Kushal Borkar,Ritvik Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMVS 是一个面向放射学影像标注的人工在环框架，将三个组件组合成闭环：轻量级 2D 切片掩膜适配器（SMA，在线从用户涂鸦微调）、冻结的体积掩膜追踪器（VMT，跨相邻切片传播校正后的掩膜）以及软教师-学生对齐（限制遗忘）。在 8 个公开 CT/MRI 数据集上，IMVS 在质量上与强交互式基线持平，同时大幅降低标注成本：比熟练的基于复制的全手动流程快 14.4 倍（比朴素手动快 22.3 倍），比逐切片方法快 4.6 倍，比 3D 交互方法快 1.9 倍。在轮廓清晰器官上 MedSAM2 和 ScribblePrompt 具有竞争力或更强，而 IMVS 的优势在困难目标和交互效率上最为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模放射学数据集标注受限于在 3D 体积中逐切片手工勾画结构的巨大工作量。现有交互方法效率不高：逐切片方法（含许多基础模型）忽略切片间连续性；而 3D 和基于视频的方法用固定传播器传播提示，从不适应目标体积，在低对比度或病理性结构上会漂移并需要重新提示。因此需要一种既能利用切片间连续性、又能自适应目标体积的交互式标注框架。

Method: IMVS 不是提出新的分割原语，而是将三个组件组合成闭环：(1) 轻量级 2D 切片掩膜适配器（SMA），从用户涂鸦在线微调，骨干无关（支持 UNet++、DeepLabV3、TransUNet）；(2) 冻结的体积掩膜追踪器（VMT），在相邻切片间传播校正后的掩膜；(3) 软教师-学生对齐，限制遗忘（防止在线微调导致遗忘）。三者构成人工在环闭环流程。

Result: 在 8 个公开 CT/MRI 数据集上，IMVS 的标注质量与强交互基线相当，但标注效率显著提升：比熟练的基于复制的全手动工作流快 14.4×（比朴素手动快 22.3×），比逐切片方法快 4.6×，比 3D 交互方法快 1.9×。在轮廓清晰的器官上 MedSAM2 和 ScribblePrompt 仍有竞争力或更强，但 IMVS 在困难目标（低对比度/病理性结构）以及交互效率上优势最大。

Conclusion: IMVS 通过在线自适应（SMA 微调）与跨切片传播（VMT）相结合的闭环设计，在保持分割质量的同时大幅削减了放射学影像的标注人工成本。其优势尤其体现在困难目标和交互效率上，表明自适应传播机制是提升交互式 3D 标注效率的有效方向。代码与演示视频已开源。

Abstract: Annotating large radiology datasets is bottlenecked by the manual effort of delineating structures slice-by-slice in 3D volumes. Interactive methods reduce this effort but stay interaction-inefficient: slice-wise methods (including many foundation models) ignore inter-slice continuity, while 3D and video-based methods propagate a prompt with a \emph{fixed} propagator that never adapts to the target volume, so it drifts on low-contrast or pathological structures and must be re-prompted. We present IMVS, a human-in-the-loop annotation framework that composes three components into a closed loop rather than a new segmentation primitive: a lightweight 2D Slice Mask Adapter (SMA) fine-tuned online from user scribbles, a frozen Volume Mask Tracker (VMT) that propagates corrected masks across adjacent slices, and a soft teacher--student alignment that limits forgetting. The SMA is backbone-agnostic (UNet++, DeepLabV3, TransUNet). Across 8 public CT/MRI datasets, IMVS matches strong interactive baselines in quality while sharply cutting annotation effort: $14.4\times$ faster than a proficient copy-based manual workflow ($22.3\times$ over naive manual), $4.6\times$ over slice-wise and $1.9\times$ over 3D interactive methods. MedSAM2 and ScribblePrompt stay competitive or stronger on well-delineated organs; IMVS's advantage is largest on challenging targets and on interaction efficiency. Source code and Demo Video: https://github.com/AbhilakshSinghReen/imvs.

</details>


### [41] [Unifying Semantic Priors and High-Frequency Traces: Enhancing V-JEPA with Mixture-of-Experts for Robust Synthetic Image Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16778)
*Simone Teglia,Irene Amerini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）应用于深度伪造检测，提出双流架构MoE-JEPA，通过残差混合专家机制增强V-JEPA 2骨干网络，并结合噪声流分支和门控注意力多实例学习模块，在包含30万张图像的SID-Set基准上以95.54%的准确率刷新了SOTA，且超越了规模更大的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造图像的泛滥加剧了虚假信息的传播，威胁公共信任与信息完整性。现有深度伪造检测器多依赖Vision Transformer（ViT）捕捉低级不一致性，但其全局理解能力不足以在压缩或社交媒体传输等挑战性场景中单独区分真实与伪造内容。作者认为JEPA这类世界模型对视觉现实的泛化表征可作为强大的先验，故探索将其引入深度伪造检测。

Method: 提出MoE-JEPA双流架构：(1) 以V-JEPA 2为骨干，增强残差混合专家（MoE）机制以动态内化取证知识；(2) 增加噪声流分支；(3) 引入门控注意力多实例学习（MIL）模块确保精确的空间语义理解。该方法充分利用JEPA模型的固有世界理解作为深度伪造检测器的强先验。

Result: 在SID-Set基准（含30万张AI生成、篡改及真实图像）上，MoE-JEPA达到95.54%的准确率，刷新了最先进水平，并成功超越了规模更大的模型。

Conclusion: 实证表明JEPA模型的固有世界理解可作为深度伪造检测的强先验；通过MoE机制、噪声流分支和门控注意力MIL的融合，MoE-JEPA在极具挑战的场景中实现了优越的性能，为深度伪造检测提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: The unchecked proliferation of manipulated images on social media platforms has increased the spread of misinformation, posing a severe threat to public trust and information integrity. Modern deepfake detectors typically rely on Vision Transformers (ViTs) to capture the low-level inconsistencies that characterize fully synthetic or locally tampered images. However, the global understanding of such foundation models is not enough to discriminate alone between real and fake multimedia content, especially in challenging scenarios where images are compressed or transmitted through social media. In this paper we pioneer the application of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) models to deepfake detection, taking advantage of the generalized representation of visual reality that such World Models have exhibited. We hypothesize, and empirically demonstrate, that the intrinsic world understanding of JEPA models can be used as a strong prior for a deepfake detector. To fully exploit JEPA capabilities, we propose MoE-JEPA, a dual-stream architecture for deepfake detection. By enhancing a V-JEPA 2 backbone with a Residual Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) mechanism, along with a noise stream branch, our model dynamically internalizes forensic knowledge. Furthermore, a Gated Attention Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) module is employed to ensure precise spatial semantic understanding. Evaluated on the SID-Set benchmark, comprising 300K AI-generated, tampered and authentic images, MoE-JEPA establishes a new state-of-the-art with an accuracy of 95.54%, successfully outperforming vastly larger models.

</details>


### [42] [PSMP-CLIP: Patch-Prompt SAM and Multi-Semantic Prompting for CLIP-Based Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16785)
*Xuezhi Xiang,Guanghao Wu,Heqi Xiang,Jiayao Liu,Xiaoheng Li,Yiming Chen,Shanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PSMP-CLIP方法，通过补丁提示驱动的SAM2分割（PPSS）和多语义引导的提示正则化（MSGPR）解决零样本异常检测中CLIP方法存在的异常图粗糙与语义提示受限问题，在14个数据集上取得极具竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的零样本异常检测方法存在两大缺陷：一是产生的异常图较为粗糙（coarse anomaly maps），定位精度不足；二是使用的语义提示（semantic prompts）种类有限，难以充分刻画各类异常。这些局限制约了零样本场景下的异常检测与定位性能。

Method: 方法由两个核心组件构成：(1) 补丁提示SAM2分割（PPSS）：直接从中间补丁特征中采样提示（prompt），避免了阈值漂移（threshold drift）问题，从而引导SAM2生成精确的分割掩码；(2) 多语义引导提示正则化（MSGPR）：使用多个可学习提示（learnable prompts），并以语义锚点（semantic anchors）加以约束，以保持模型的泛化能力。

Result: 在14个数据集上进行了广泛实验，取得了极具竞争力的性能，并在MVTec AD、BTAD、DTD-Synthetic、CVC-ClinicDB、TN3K、Endo和Kvasir等多个数据集上达到了最优的像素级AUROC。

Conclusion: PSMP-CLIP通过结合SAM2的精确分割能力与多语义提示正则化策略，显著提升了零样本异常检测的定位精度与泛化性，验证了其在多种工业与医学场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection aims to localize anomalies without target-domain samples. Existing CLIP-based methods suffer from coarse anomaly maps and limited semantic prompts. We propose PSMP-CLIP, integrating patch-prompt SAM2 segmentation (PPSS) and multi-semantic guided prompt regularization (MSGPR). PPSS samples prompts directly from intermediate patch features, avoiding threshold drift and guiding SAM2 to produce precise masks. MSGPR uses multiple learnable prompts constrained by semantic anchors to preserve generalization. Experiments on 14 datasets show highly competitive performance, achieving the best pixel-level AUROC on MVTec AD, BTAD, DTD-Synthetic, CVC-ClinicDB, TN3K, Endo, and Kvasir.

</details>


### [43] [TEDi: Temporal Memory-Enhanced and Denoising Transformer for Surgical Instrument Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16797)
*Jiahong Yuan,Weiming Mi,Tao Zhang,Haoyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TEDi，一种用于手术器械分割的时序记忆增强与去噪Transformer。该方法通过Memory Search Enhancement（查询级记忆库+记忆搜索增强编码器检索历史帧的判别性表征）和Temporal Consistency Denoising（构建时序一致参考作为跨帧语义锚点，抑制时序不稳定的预测）两个核心模块，解决了现有查询式分割方法忽略跨帧时序先验与一致性约束导致的查询表征不稳定、类别识别次优问题。在EndoVis 2017和EndoVis 2018两个基准数据集上持续超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询式手术器械分割方法大多依赖逐帧预测，忽视跨帧时序先验以及时序一致性约束。这种局限性导致查询表示不稳定、类别识别效果次优，从而限制了手术场景理解与计算机辅助手术下游任务的效果。

Method: 提出TEDi框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Memory Search Enhancement：引入查询级记忆库和记忆搜索增强编码器，从历史帧检索判别性表征来丰富当前帧特征；(2) Temporal Consistency Denoising：构建时序一致参考作为跨帧语义锚点，抑制时序上不稳定的预测并促进跨帧语义一致性。

Result: 在EndoVis 2017和EndoVis 2018两个基准数据集上进行大量实验，TEDi在各项指标上持续优于现有最先进方法，验证了该方法在推进计算机辅助手术方面的潜力。

Conclusion: TEDi通过查询级记忆增强与时序一致性去噪有效解决了查询式分割中时序信息利用不足的问题，显著提升了手术器械分割的稳定性与类别识别精度，为计算机辅助手术提供了更强的视觉理解基础，并已开源代码供社区使用。

Abstract: Query-based segmentation methods have shown promising potential for surgical instrument segmentation and recognition, which is essential for scene understanding and downstream tasks in computer assisted surgery. However, most existing approaches predominantly rely on per-frame predictions and overlook cross-frame temporal priors as well as temporal-consistency constraints. This limitation often leads to unstable query representations and suboptimal category recognition. In this paper, we propose TEDi, a Temporal memory-Enhanced and Denoising transformer for surgical instrument segmentation that addresses these is sues through Memory Search Enhancement and Temporal Consistency Denoising. The former introduces a query-level memory bank and a memory search enhancement encoder to retrieve discriminative representations from historical frames, enriching current-frame features. The latter constructs a temporally consistent reference as a cross-frame semantic anchor to suppress temporally unstable predictions and promote semantic coherence across frames. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets, EndoVis 2017 and EndoVis 2018, demonstrate that TEDi consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, highlighting its potential to further advance computer-assisted surgery. Our code is available at github.com/argon-xixi/TEDi.

</details>


### [44] [Hyper-RED: Scalable Event Pre-training via Semantic Hypergraph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16811)
*Meisen Wang,Zhiqiang Tian,Wei Bao,Chengjie Wang,Shaoyi Du,Siqi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hyper-RED，一种基于超图的图像到事件预训练框架，通过构建图像、事件及跨模态语义超图并引入超图关系蒸馏损失，将图像的高阶语义结构迁移至事件表示，在五个事件数据集、三个任务上实现从ViT-S到ViT-L的一致性扩展并达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在鲁棒视觉感知中潜力巨大，但大规模标注事件数据稀缺限制了事件表示学习的扩展。虽然预训练图像模型可提供可扩展的语义监督，但现有图像到事件方法依赖刚性的像素级或token级对齐，忽视了模态间在纹理、密度和外观上的差异，可能导致语义塌缩并限制迁移性。

Method: Hyper-RED利用超图对多个图像和事件token间的高阶语义关联进行建模与对齐。给定配对事件-图像样本，使用DINOv3提取空间token表示，构建图像、事件和跨模态语义超图（每个超边连接多个语义相关的token）。引入超图关系蒸馏损失，施加互补的模态内与跨模态约束，使事件编码器继承图像派生的语义组织，同时保留局部关系一致性和事件特有特征。

Result: 在三个任务（涵盖五个事件数据集）上的实验表明，模型从ViT-S到ViT-L实现了一致性扩展，并取得了最先进的性能（图1所示）；代码已开源。

Conclusion: Hyper-RED通过超图建模高阶语义关联并采用软性对齐替代刚性一对一对应，能够更好地兼容模态差异、避免语义塌缩，为事件表示学习提供了一种简单、无痛且可扩展的图像到事件预训练范式。

Abstract: Event cameras have shown great potential for robust visual perception, yet scaling event representation learning remains challenging due to the scarcity of large-scale annotated event data. Pretrained image models provide scalable semantic supervision, but existing image-to-event methods rely on rigid pixel-wise or token-wise alignment that overlooks modality discrepancies in texture, density, and appearance, potentially causing semantic collapse and limiting transferability. To address this issue, we propose Hyper-RED, a simple, painless, and scalable image-to-event pretraining framework that transfers high-order semantic structures from images to events. Hyper-RED uses hypergraphs to model and align high-order semantic associations among multiple image and event tokens, enabling cross-modal knowledge transfer while accommodating modality-specific differences rather than enforcing rigid one-to-one correspondence. Specifically, given a paired event--image sample, Hyper-RED leverages DINOv3 to extract spatial token representations and constructs image, event, and cross-modal semantic hypergraphs, where each hyperedge connects multiple semantically correlated tokens. We further introduce a hypergraph relational distillation loss that imposes complementary intra- and cross-modal constraints, enabling the event encoder to inherit image-derived semantic organization while preserving local relational consistency and event-specific characteristics. Experiments on three tasks across five event datasets demonstrate consistent scaling from ViT-S to ViT-L and state-of-the-art performance (Fig.1). The code is available at: https://github.com/meisenwang/Hyper--RED.

</details>


### [45] [What Breaks Local Watermarks? A Robustness Benchmark for Local Invisible Image Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16832)
*Kai Yao,Bence Szilágyi,Sebestyén Kamp,Máté Poór,Máté Szilveszter,Matyas K. Zsoldos,Marc Juarez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个针对局部图像水印的系统性鲁棒性基准，在55种图像变换下评估五种方法，发现所有方法均在某些变换下脆弱，MaskWM在载荷恢复和定位上最强但图像质量最低，且鲁棒性强烈依赖变换类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部水印研究各自评估自己提出的方法，变换、数据集和指标选择不一致，导致难以跨方法比较，整体鲁棒性图景模糊。为填补这一空白，建立统一基准。

Method: 构建包含55种图像变换的基准，分为四类：信号失真、坐标对齐变化、间接局部编辑、直接水印编辑。评估五种方法（MaskWM、WAM、OmniGuard、TrustMark、PixelSeal），这些方法原生支持局部化或需极少适配即可支持。

Result: 所有方法均在某些变换下失效。MaskWM在载荷恢复和定位上最强，但干净设置下图像质量最低。同步化可改善MaskWM在若干几何变换下的载荷恢复，但进一步牺牲图像质量。信号失真常被最强方法容忍，而几何错位和生成式局部编辑（如修补、外扩）可完全破坏载荷恢复。载荷恢复与定位相关但不完全等同，二者都强烈依赖变换对水印区域的影响。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了局部水印鲁棒性的关键因素，为后续方法设计和评估提供了统一参照，并指出几何和生成式局部编辑是最具挑战性的变换类型。

Abstract: Local image watermarking embeds an invisible signal into selected image regions rather than spreading it across the entire image, enabling payload recovery from specific objects or regions without perceptibly altering the image. Existing studies evaluate the robustness of payload recovery and localization under image transformations, but they often focus on their own proposed method, resulting in narrow evaluations with inconsistent choices of transformations, datasets, and metrics. These inconsistencies across studies limit direct comparisons across methods and muddle the overall picture of local watermark robustness. To address this gap, we present the first systematic robustness benchmark for local watermarks across 55 image transformations, including (i) signal distortions, (ii) changes in image coordinate alignment, (iii) indirect local edits, and (iv) direct watermark edits. The benchmark evaluates MaskWM, WAM, OmniGuard, TrustMark, and PixelSeal, all methods that either provide native localization or require minimal adaptation to support it. Our results show that all evaluated methods are vulnerable to some transformation, with MaskWM standing out as offering the strongest payload recovery and localization, although it has the lowest image quality in the clean setting. Synchronization further improves MaskWM's payload recovery under several geometric transformations, albeit at an additional cost to image quality. A key finding is that local watermark robustness depends strongly on the nature of the transformation: signal distortions are often tolerated by the strongest methods, while geometric misalignment and generative local edits, such as inpainting and outpainting, can completely impair payload recovery. We observe that payload recovery and localization are related but not interchangeable, and both strongly depend on the transformation's impact on the watermark region.

</details>


### [46] [StackTok: Accelerating VLMs Inference with Budget-Adaptive Visual Token Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16841)
*Zhenbin Wang,Lei Zhang,Lituan Wang,Wei Huang,Yan Wang,Zhenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StackTok，一种免训练的视觉令牌选择器，将查询相关性视为目标、视觉覆盖视为按预算校准的支持，通过大小索引的覆盖参考和查询-视觉亲和熵调整支持目标，并采用参考门控的交错选择策略，在高分辨率输入下跨裁剪共享令牌预算。在五种VLM和十个基准上于所有测试的模型-预算设置中位列免训练选择器第一，高分辨率LLaVA-NeXT-7B上仅用5.6%令牌保留95.26%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分辨率提高使VLM视觉令牌序列增长，显著增加推理成本。现有免训练选择方法选取紧凑令牌子集时优先考虑查询相关性、视觉覆盖或两者固定折衷，但合适的平衡随查询和令牌预算变化：局部问题偏向相关性，整体问题需要更广覆盖。因此需要一种能自适应校准相关性与覆盖平衡的免训练选择器。

Method: StackTok包含三部分：1) 从仅覆盖的贪心序列构建大小索引的覆盖参考；2) 用查询-视觉亲和熵调整支持目标；3) 采用参考门控的交错选择策略，依据当前子集的覆盖赤字在相关性导向与覆盖导向的添加之间切换。对高分辨率输入，按局部提名令牌的组合边际增益在各裁剪间分配一个共享令牌预算。

Result: 在五种VLM、十个不同的图像理解基准上评估，StackTok在每种测试的模型-预算设置中均位列免训练选择器第一；在LLaVA-NeXT-7B高分辨率下，仅用160/2880（5.6%）视觉令牌即保留95.26%的全令牌性能。

Conclusion: StackTok证明可通过免训练的方式自适应平衡查询相关性与视觉覆盖，无需重训模型即可大幅降低VLM推理开销。其预算校准的覆盖支持机制有效应对不同查询类型与令牌预算，为高分辨率VLM高效推理提供了通用且领先的令牌选择方案。

Abstract: Increasing image resolution produces ever-longer visual-token sequences in vision-language models (VLMs), substantially raising their inference cost. To reduce this overhead without retraining, existing methods select compact token subsets that prioritize query relevance, visual coverage, or a fixed trade-off between them. The appropriate balance, however, varies across queries and token budgets: localized questions favor relevance, whereas holistic questions demand broader visual coverage. We introduce StackTok, a training-free selector that treats query relevance as the objective and visual coverage as budget-calibrated support. StackTok builds a size-indexed coverage reference from a coverage-only greedy sequence and adjusts its support target using query--vision affinity entropy. A reference-gated interleaved selection policy then switches between relevance- and coverage-oriented additions according to the current subset's support deficit. For high-resolution inputs, StackTok allocates one shared token budget across crops according to the combined marginal gain of locally nominated tokens. Evaluated with five VLMs over ten distinct image-understanding benchmarks, StackTok ranks first among training-free selectors in every tested model--budget setting. On high-resolution LLaVA-NeXT-7B, it retains 95.26% of full-token performance with only 160 of 2{,}880 (5.6%) visual tokens.

</details>


### [47] [FAHCD-Net: Frequency-Adaptive Heatmap-Conditional Diffusion Networks for Robust Facial Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16842)
*Jun Wan,Jiwei Hu,Shengkai Hu,Qilu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FAHCD-Net，一种级联框架，将频率自适应热图条件扩散模型（FAHCD）与平滑度正则化（SR）损失相结合，用于在面部结构变化、信息丢失与噪声干扰等挑战性条件下的人脸关键点检测（FLD）。通过层级频率自适应（HFA）模块抑制冗余高频噪声，配合SR损失增强生成热图的平滑性，在多个基准上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FLD方法在面部结构变化、信息丢失和噪声干扰等挑战性条件下表现不佳，这些因素严重损害了学习到的面部特征的完整性和准确性，因此需要一种能够同时应对噪声干扰并保持关键面部结构的方法。

Method: 提出级联框架FAHCD-Net，包含两个核心组件：(1) FAHCD模型，内含层级频率自适应（HFA）模块，通过多层频率分解与自适应重建抑制冗余高频噪声、保留关键面部结构；(2) 平滑度正则化（SR）损失，进一步缓解高频噪声干扰并增强生成的热图平滑性。两者级联，逐步从含噪输入中生成更准确的关键点热图，充分利用数据的统计与频域分布特征。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集上的大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性与鲁棒性，在挑战性场景下的FLD任务中取得最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将频率自适应的扩散模型与平滑度正则化损失级联，FAHCD-Net有效解决了挑战性条件下FLD的性能退化问题，验证了频域信息利用与噪声抑制在关键点检测任务中的价值。

Abstract: Facial Landmark Detection(FLD) is a crucial task in various applications and has achieved significant advancements in recent years. However, current FLD methods still struggle under challenging conditions, where facial structural variations, information loss, and noise interference severely compromise the integrity and accuracy of learned facial features. To address these issues, we propose Frequency-Adaptive Heatmap-Conditional Diffusion Network (FAHCD-Net), which integrates a Frequency-Adaptive Heatmap-Conditional Diffusion (FAHCD) model with a Smoothness Regularization (SR) loss in a cascaded framework. Specifically, the FAHCD model incorporates a Hierarchical Frequency Adaptation (HFA) module designed to suppress redundant high-frequency noise through multi-layer frequency decomposition and adaptive reconstruction, thereby preserving essential facial structures. Additionally, the SR loss is proposed to further mitigate the interference of high-frequency noise and enhance the smoothness of the generated landmark heatmaps. By cascading the FAHCD model with the SR loss, FAHCD-Net effectively leverages both statistical and frequency-based distribution characteristics of the data to progressively generate more accurate landmark heatmaps from noisy inputs. Extensive experiments on popular benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method, achieving state-of-the-art performance in FLD tasks under challenging scenarios. The source code is available at https://github.com/HJWKryptonite/FAHCD-Net.

</details>


### [48] [RegRet: Enhancing Region-Level Retrieval in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16847)
*Xun Liang,Honghui Yang,Weihang Pan,Ruisi Zhao,Boyuan Pan,Yao Hu,Wenxiao Wang,Binbin Lin,Deng Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RegRet，一个基于大型多模态模型（LMM）的区域级检索框架，通过集成区域感知编码器和多阶段训练流程（局部细节描述与区域对比学习）来增强区域表征，同时保持全局检索性能。作者还构建了包含 22.5 万对比对的 REGMB 基准，覆盖四种多模态检索任务。实验表明 RegRet 在零样本设置下超越强基线，经对比学习训练后平均提升超过 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域级检索在电商产品搜索和 RAG 等实际应用中至关重要，但现有的大型多模态模型（LMM）主要聚焦于全局级任务，难以捕获有效的区域级表征，存在明显的能力缺口。

Method: 提出 RegRet 框架，核心包括：(1) 区域感知编码器（Region-Aware Encoder），捕获细粒度区域特征并与全局背景上下文相平衡；(2) 多阶段训练流程，包含局部细节描述和区域对比学习任务，以增强细粒度理解与表征判别力；(3) 构建 REGMB 基准，包含 22.5 万对比对，覆盖四种多模态检索任务，弥补区域级对比训练数据缺失和评估任务多样性不足的问题。

Result: 大量实验验证了方法的有效性：RegRet 在零样本设置下超越强基线；加入对比学习训练后，在 REGMB 和公开基准上平均提升超过 20%，同时在全球级检索任务上取得相当或更优的结果。

Conclusion: RegRet 成功弥合了 LMM 在区域级检索上的能力差距，实现了区域表征增强与全局检索性能的兼顾，并配套提供了规模可观、任务多样的基准数据集，为该方向后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Region-level retrieval aims to align user-specified image regions with relevant regions or textual descriptions, playing a crucial role in realworld applications such as e-commerce product search and RAG. Although recent Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have made significant strides in multimodal retrieval, they primarily focus on global-level tasks and struggle to capture effective region-level representations. To bridge this gap, we present RegRet, an LMM-based Region-level Retrieval framework that enhances the regional representations without compromising overall global retrieval performance. At its core, RegRet integrates a Region-Aware Encoder to capture detailed regional features while balancing them with the global background context. To further enhance the fine-grained understanding and discriminability of representations, we design a multi-stage training pipeline that includes detailed localized captioning and regional contrastive learning tasks. In addition, considering the absence of region-level contrastive training data and the limited diversity of evaluation tasks in current benchmarks, we introduce the REGMB benchmark. It comprises 225k contrastive pairs, covering four multimodal retrieval tasks. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach. RegRet outperforms strong baselines in the zero-shot setting. Further training with contrastive learning leads to an average improvement of more than 20\% on both REGMB and public benchmarks, while achieving comparable or better results on global-level retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [49] [TecoPrompt: Temporal-Conservative Prompt Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16858)
*Zeyi Shao,Haowen Hua,Jiaxin Zhang,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TecoPrompt，一个针对少样本提示学习在标签噪声下鲁棒性的闭环框架。它利用最优传输（OT）伪标签和基于EMA的轨迹稳定性窗口来识别并重写噪声标签，再通过三组损失函数（对应干净、中等、噪声子集）将修正标签反馈到提示训练中。在七个含合成噪声数据集及Food101N上均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示学习通过调整少量上下文标记来适配CLIP等视觉-语言模型，但在少样本监督下，即使是中等程度的标签噪声也会严重干扰提示优化。现有的鲁棒学习方法缺乏基于时序视角的伪标签可靠性验证，容易产生确认偏差（confirmation bias），因此需要一种能有效识别并修正噪声标签的鲁棒提示学习机制。

Method: TecoPrompt构建闭环鲁棒提示学习框架：1）在CLIP语义空间中采用熵正则化的最优传输（entropic OT）计划，获得全局一致的标签候选；2）通过轨迹稳定性验证候选可靠性——仅当OT候选在K-epoch时间稳定窗口内保持不变且通过基于指数移动平均（EMA）的置信门限时，才重写噪声标签，以降低确认偏差；3）将重写后的标签通过包含干净、中等、噪声三个子集的三组损失函数（tri-group objective）重新整合到提示训练中。

Result: 在七个数据集上（覆盖合成对称噪声、非对称噪声及真实噪声Food101N）均取得显著性能提升。例如OxfordPets数据集在50%非对称噪声下，准确率从0.775提升至0.843。

Conclusion: TecoPrompt通过引入时序稳定性验证与最优传输伪标签相结合，有效缓解了少样本提示学习中的标签噪声干扰和确认偏差问题，验证了闭环伪标签反馈机制对鲁棒提示优化的有效性，在合成与真实噪声场景下均优于基线。

Abstract: Prompt learning adapts vision-language models, such as CLIP, by adjusting a small set of context tokens. However, under few-shot supervision, even moderate label noise can disrupt prompt optimization. To address this issue, we propose TecoPrompt, a closed-loop robust prompt-learning framework that revisits optimal transport (OT) pseudo-labeling from a temporal perspective. TecoPrompt employs an entropic OT plan in the CLIP semantic space to obtain globally consistent label candidates. It verifies the reliability of these candidates by examining trajectory stability: a noisy label is only rewritten if the OT candidate remains unchanged within a K-epoch temporal stability window and passes a confidence gate based on Exponential Moving Average (EMA). This approach helps reduce confirmation bias. The rewritten labels are then integrated back into prompt training using a tri-group objective that includes three loss functions aligned with clean, mid, and noisy subsets. Experiments on seven datasets with synthetic symmetric and asymmetric noise, as well as Food101N, demonstrate significant performance improvements. For example, on the OxfordPets dataset, with 50% asymmetric noise, TecoPrompt achieves an accuracy of 0.843, up from 0.775.

</details>


### [50] [Measuring Annotation Efficiency for Handwritten Devanagari Recognition: Sample-Complexity Curves for Four Pretraining Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16859)
*Manglesh Kumar Pandey,Sumit Kumar Banshal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究直接量化了天城文手写文本识别系统中真实标注转录数量与识别性能之间的关系。通过固定识别器、优化器和评估协议，仅改变微调用的真实转录词数（9档预算，从10到4000）和四种初始化方案（每点6个种子），将所得曲线转换为标注等效项。结果显示，监督合成预训练达到CER 0.50仅需81个转录词，而随机初始化需要355个，标注乘数为4.40。零样本参考下，该预训练约等于136个真实转录词的价值。优势随目标精度提高而缩小，在最苛刻目标下无法与零节省区分。仅迁移编码器的第四臂分离了预训练方法与迁移范围的效果，观察到掩码图像建模在有限预算范围内产生负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写文本识别系统的训练需要词图像及其对应的人工转录标注。对于仅有少数专家能够识读的文字（如天城文），人工转录成为瓶颈——因为训练出的模型本应节省这些专家的时间。因此核心问题在于：需要多少转录才使识别器变得有用，以及预训练能在多大程度上削减这一成本。该研究直接测量了这一问题的答案。

Method: 保持识别器、优化器与评估协议不变，仅改变用于微调的真实转录词数量（9档预算：10至4000），并比较四种初始化方案（随机初始化、监督合成预训练、仅编码器迁移、掩码图像建模），每点使用6个种子以确保统计可靠性。将性能-预算曲线转换为标注等效项进行量化，并计算标注乘数（label multiplier）及置信区间。

Result: 达到CER 0.50时，监督合成预训练仅需81个真实转录词，而随机初始化需355个，标注乘数为4.40 [3.56, 4.99]。零样本情况下，该预训练的价值相当于约136个真实转录词。但此优势随目标精度提高而缩小，在最苛刻目标下无法与零节省区分。掩码图像建模在有限预算范围内表现出负迁移。

Conclusion: 预训练（尤其是监督合成预训练）在低标注预算下能显著降低人工转录成本，但其收益在更高精度目标下递减甚至消失。研究强调了不同预训练策略的迁移效果差异，并指出本研究的稀缺性是通过对大规模语料库进行子采样构造的。这一发现对手写识别尤其是稀缺文字系统的实用化部署具有指导意义。

Abstract: To train handwritten text recognition systems we need word images and their corresponding transcriptions, and these transcriptions are produced manually. For a script that can be read by only a small number of specialists, this manual transcription is a limitation, because the trained models are supposed to save the time of those same specialists. A relevant question therefore arises: how many transcriptions are needed before a recogniser becomes useful, and how much of that cost can pretraining remove? In this study the answer is measured directly for handwritten Devanagari. We keep the recogniser, optimiser and evaluation protocol the same and change only the number of real transcribed words used for fine-tuning across nine budgets from 10 to 4,000 and four initialisation regimes, with six seeds at every point. The resulting curves are then converted into annotation-equivalent terms. A CER of 0.50 is reached by supervised synthetic pretraining using only 81 transcribed words, whereas random initialisation requires 355, which gives a label multiplier of 4.40 [3.56, 4.99]. There is a zero-shot reference point as well: with no real transcribed words at all, this pretraining is worth about 136 of them. This advantage gets smaller as the target accuracy improves, and at the most demanding target we measure, it cannot be distinguished from no saving at all. A fourth arm in which only the encoder is transferred separates the effect of the pretraining method from that of transfer scope, and masked image modelling is observed to transfer negatively over a bounded range of budgets. We emphasise that the scarcity in this study is constructed by subsampling a large corpus.

</details>


### [51] [tcnerv:dual-domain temporal context modeling for implicit neural video compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16870)
*Xuezhi Xiang,Yixin Zhao,Heqi Xiang,Jiayao Liu,Shanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TCNeRV，一种利用重建上下文的视频隐式神经表示（INR）压缩方法。通过多尺度时间上下文融合（MTCF）模块在解码器各尺度注入门控历史特征，以及时间嵌入残差编码（TERC）模块预测内容嵌入并仅编码残差，在约300万参数下，于UVG数据集取得36.08 dB的平均PSNR，较HNeRV-Boost提升2.20 dB，相对于HM、DCVC和HiNeRV分别降低BD-rate 22.06%、66.73%和29.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频INR通常独立解码各帧，中间特征未基于先前重建结果进行条件化，内容嵌入也缺乏显式的时间预测，导致时间冗余未被充分利用。作者旨在通过同时利用特征域和嵌入域的重建上下文来提升视频压缩效率。

Method: 提出TCNeRV框架，包含两大核心模块：(1) 多尺度时间上下文融合（MTCF），在解码器多个尺度注入门控的历史特征，使中间特征充分条件化于先前重建； (2) 时间嵌入残差编码（TERC），预测每个内容嵌入并仅编码其残差，而非完整嵌入，从而减少待编码信息量。模型参数约3M。

Result: 在UVG数据集上平均PSNR达36.08 dB，比HNeRV-Boost高出2.20 dB；相比HM、DCVC和HiNeRV，BD-rate分别降低22.06%、66.73%和29.85%，在有限模型容量下表现出有竞争力的率失真性能。

Conclusion: TCNeRV验证了在特征域与嵌入域同时显式建模时间预测可显著提升视频INR的率失真性能，以较小的模型容量实现了优于强基线方法的压缩效果，为视频INR的时间上下文利用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Video compression aims to minimize reconstruction distor tion under a constrained bit rate. Existing video implicit neural representations (INRs) often decode frames independently, leaving intermediate features unconditioned on previous reconstructions and content embeddings without explicit temporal prediction. We propose TCNeRV, which exploits reconstructed context in both feature and embedding domains. Its multi-scale temporal-context fusion (MTCF) module injects gated historical features at multiple decoder scales, while temporal embedding-residual coding (TERC) predicts each content embedding and codes only its residual. With approximately 3M parameters, TCNeRV achieves an average PSNR of 36.08 dB on the UVG dataset, outperforming HNeRV-Boost by 2.20 dB. It reduces BD-rate by 22.06%, 66.73%, and 29.85% relative to HM, DCVC, and HiNeRV, respectively, demonstrating competitive rate-distortion performance with limited model capacity.

</details>


### [52] [SPEAR NeXT Causal Latent Forecasting Across Multiple Horizons for Spectral Temporal Earth Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16871)
*Rajiv Ranjan,Udaiveer Singh,Shashank Tamaskar,Dharmendra Saraswat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍SPEAR NeXT，一个紧凑的像素级多模态光谱-时间基础模型，其时间自监督被形式化为仅基于过去信息的多时域潜在地球状态预测，利用预训练SPEAR编码器和因果掩码Transformer实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测本质上是动态的，但许多基础模型中的时间信息是通过重建、不变性或回顾性序列摘要来学习的，未能充分利用前向预测的动态特性，因此需要一种更自然的时间自监督形式。

Method: 该方法将时间自监督设计为仅基于过去数据的多时域潜在地球状态预测。具体流程：1) 利用预训练的SPEAR模型将光学、雷达和环境观测瞬时状态编码为32维紧凑嵌入；2) 由因果掩码Transformer对这些嵌入的时间演化进行建模，从先前观测预测多个未来潜在状态；3) 使用旋转位置编码（RoPE）表示相对时间顺序，月与年嵌入分别编码季节相位和年际背景。

Result: 论文提出了一种紧凑的像素级多模态光谱-时间基础模型SPEAR NeXT，实现了仅过去的、多时域的潜在地球状态预测，并利用RoPE及月/年嵌入来表征时间结构。

Conclusion: SPEAR NeXT通过前向潜在状态预测的形式进行时间自监督，结合紧凑嵌入与因果Transformer，为地球观测中的动态时序建模提供了一种新的基础模型范式。

Abstract: Earth observation is inherently dynamic, yet temporal information in many foundation models is learned through reconstruction, invariance, or retrospective sequence summarization. SPEAR NeXT is introduced as a compact pixel-wise multimodal spectral temporal foundation model in which temporal self supervision is formulated as past only, multi horizon latent Earth state prediction. Instantaneous states are first encoded by the pretrained SPEAR model from optical, radar, and environmental observations into compact 32 dimensional embeddings. Their temporal evolution is then modeled by a causally masked Trans former that predicts multiple future latent states from pre ceding observations. Relative temporal order is represented using Rotary Position Embeddings, while month and year embeddings encode seasonal phase and interannual con text.

</details>


### [53] [GRACE: Geometry- and Ray-Aware Camera-Efficient Multi-View Pedestrian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16872)
*Taigo Sakai,Kazuhiro Hotta,Hiroki Kouno,Naoki Kato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE是一种相机高效的多视角鸟瞰(BEV)行人跟踪器，通过三类组件（体引导融合、射线条件化、BEV轨道恢复）缓解减少相机带来的BEV响应拉伸和轨道分裂问题，在仅用两个WildTrack相机时将MOTA从83.54提升至91.07。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少相机数量可降低部署成本，但会移除某些视角，这些视角在投影过程中可校正BEV响应远离真实行人位置的拉伸现象，并弥补会导致轨道分裂的短暂分数下降。作者希望通过相机高效的方案在减少相机的同时维持甚至提升跟踪精度。

Method: GRACE包含三个组件：(1) 体引导融合(Volumetric-Guided Fusion)——将基于单应性的BEV特征与通过3D空间提升(lifting)的特征相结合；(2) 射线条件化(Ray Conditioning)——将每个相机的观察方向暴露给融合网络；(3) BEV轨道恢复(BEV Track Recovery, BTR)——仅利用低置信度检测来延续既有轨道，且这些检测不能开启新轨道，从而避免错误起始导致的分裂。

Result: 在仅用两个WildTrack相机的情况下，GRACE将MOTA从基线TrackTacular的83.54提升至91.07。

Conclusion: GRACE通过融合3D提升特征、引入射线方向条件化并设计低置信度检测仅延续轨道而非开启新轨道的机制，有效应对了相机减少时的BEV投影失真与轨道分裂问题，显著提高跟踪精度。

Abstract: Reducing the number of cameras reduces the deployment cost but removes views that correct BEV responses stretched away from true pedestrian positions by projection and short score drops that can split tracks} in Bird's-Eye View (BEV) tracking. We introduce GRACE, a camera-efficient multi-view tracker with three components. Volumetric-Guided Fusion combines homography-based BEV features with features lifted through 3D space. Ray Conditioning exposes each camera's viewing direction to the fusion network. Its tracking component, BEV Track Recovery (BTR), uses low-confidence detections only to continue existing tracks. The same detections cannot start new tracks. With two WildTrack cameras, GRACE improves MOTA from 83.54 for TrackTacular, our baseline, to 91.07.

</details>


### [54] [NeuroTS-Net: Multi-Class Semantic Segmentation of Pediatric Brain Tumors in Multi-Modal MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16873)
*Darius Peteleaza,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Bogdan Neamtu,Arpad Gellert,Mariana Sandu,Claudiu Matei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroTS-Net，一种用于儿童脑肿瘤多类语义分割的三维编码器-解码器卷积神经网络。该网络通过双尺度原始-细节流、自适应低分辨率上下文选择和保持细节的多路径下采样，在保留精细强度与边界信息的同时高效建模肿瘤整体上下文。在BraTS 2026儿科数据集上无需外部数据或预训练权重训练，性能优于nnU-Net和MedNeXt，内部验证集上全肿瘤与肿瘤核心Dice分别达0.938和0.937，官方挑战验证集分别为0.927和0.926。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童脑肿瘤是儿童癌症相关死亡的主要原因，其子区域体积小、罕见且对比度低，使得手动精确勾画极具挑战性。可靠的自动分割对于支持诊断、治疗规划和疗效评估至关重要，因此亟需开发针对这一场景的高性能分割方法。

Method: 采用三维编码器-解码器CNN架构，包含三个关键组件：双尺度原始-细节流（dual-scale raw-detail stream）用于保留精细强度信息；自适应低分辨率上下文选择（adaptive low-resolution context selection）用于高效建模肿瘤整体上下文；保持细节的多路径下采样（detail-preserving multipath downsampling）用于保留边界信息。在BraTS 2026儿科数据集上训练，不使用外部数据或预训练权重，并与nnU-Net和MedNeXt在同一实验协议下对比。

Result: NeuroTS-Net在所有对比方法中表现最优：内部验证集上全肿瘤（WT）和肿瘤核心（TC）Dice分数分别为0.938和0.937；官方挑战验证集上分别为0.927和0.926，均优于nnU-Net和MedNeXt基线。

Conclusion: NeuroTS-Net通过精细细节保留与高效上下文建模的有机结合，在儿童脑肿瘤分割任务上取得了优于主流基线方法的性能，证明了其有效性与实用性，且无需外部数据或预训练权重，代码已开源。

Abstract: Pediatric brain tumors are a leading cause of cancer-related mortality in children, and their small, rare, and often low-contrast subregions make accurate manual delineation challenging. Reliable automated segmentation is therefore needed to support diagnosis, treatment planning, and response assessment. Accordingly, we introduce NeuroTS-Net, a three-dimensional encoder-decoder convolutional neural network architecture for multi-class semantic segmentation that incorporates a dual-scale raw-detail stream, adaptive low-resolution context selection, and detail-preserving multipath downsampling. These components preserve fine intensity and boundary information while efficiently modeling broader tumor context. NeuroTS-Net was trained on the BraTS 2026 pediatric dataset without external data or pretrained weights and evaluated against nnU-Net and MedNeXt under the same experimental protocol. NeuroTS-Net outperformed the baseline methods, achieving whole-tumor and tumor-core Dice scores of 0.938 and 0.937 on the internal validation set and 0.927 and 0.926 on the official challenge validation set. The code is open-sourced at: https://github.com/maenstru56/NeuroTS.

</details>


### [55] [Accelerated Decoding of Centroid Positional Encoding for Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16874)
*Carmelo Scribano,Filippo Muzzini,Nedyalko Prisadnikov,Mohammad Mahdi,Yuqian Fu,Giorgia Franchini,Danda Pani Paudel,Marko Bertogna,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于实例分割中预测解码阶段的效率优化。针对一种基于正弦质心编码的实例分割方法（其中每个像素回归其实例质心的位置嵌入），作者提出了一种优化的CUDA解码算法实现，专门解决现代GPU上的并行化、同步和内存访问问题，相比CPU方案和朴素GPU实现显著降低了解码开销并提升了端到端推理延迟，凸显了编码方案与解码算法联合设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码阶段将网络原始输出转化为任务级表示，占执行成本的很大比重，但长期被忽视，常采用通用CPU例程或低效GPU内核实现，阻碍了模型效率提升所能带来的收益，因此亟需针对现代输出表示设计高效的解码算法。

Method: 针对正弦质心编码的具体特征，设计并实现了定制的CUDA解码程序，从并行化策略、线程同步机制和内存访问模式三方面入手，专门优化从稠密嵌入中提取实例掩码的流程。

Result: 该实现显著降低了解码开销，改善端到端推理延迟，在性能上优于基于CPU的实现和朴素的GPU实现。

Conclusion: 高效的解码是实现先进输出表示全部优势的关键，强调在面向实时计算机视觉系统时应将编码方案与其解码算法进行联合设计，而非孤立优化模型推理。

Abstract: Beyond model inference, the decoding stage, which converts raw network outputs into task-level representations, constitutes a significant portion of the execution cost. Despite its practical impact, prediction decoding has received comparatively little attention and is often implemented using generic CPU routines or inefficient GPU kernels, limiting the benefits of advances in model efficiency. In this work, we investigate the decoding overhead associated with a recent sinusoidal centroid encoding for Instance Segmentation, in which each pixel regresses a positional embedding of its instance centroid. This approach allows flexible segmentation without predefined proposals, but extracting instance masks from dense embeddings incurs a high computational cost. We present an optimized CUDA-based implementation of the decoding algorithm tailored to this encoding, explicitly addressing challenges related to parallelization, synchronization, and memory access on modern GPUs. Our solution significantly reduces decoding overhead and improves End-to-End inference latency, outperforming both CPU-based approaches and naive GPU implementations. The results demonstrate that efficient decoding is essential to fully exploit the advantages of advanced output representations and highlight the importance of jointly designing encoding schemes and their decoding algorithms for real-time computer vision systems.

</details>


### [56] [Multi-modal Knowledge Preserving Adapter for Embedding Backward Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16875)
*Jaeseok Byun,Gukyeong Kwon,Han-Kai Hsu,Meher Gitika Karumuri,Zhikang Zhang,Hao Yang,Davide Modolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MKP-Adapter，首个无需更新主干网络的Adapter-only向后兼容训练（BCT）方法，用于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的嵌入模型升级。通过多层级保留损失维护嵌入空间的几何结构，并集成焦点重加权策略，在图像、文本、视觉文档和视频检索等多样基准上实现强向后兼容性，且仅需可忽略的额外延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型升级通常需要昂贵的数据库重索引，因为新查询嵌入与旧数据库嵌入不兼容。虽然BCT通过训练时强制兼容性缓解此问题，但现有方法往往需要更新主干网络，这在实际中不可行，原因包括显著训练成本、性能退化风险以及专有模型权重的访问受限。因此亟需一种仅更新Adapter、不更新主干网络的BCT方案。

Method: MKP-Adapter采用仅Adapter的BCT方法。作者识别出该方法的核心挑战在于在保持向后兼容性的同时保留新嵌入的知识。为此提出多层级保留损失（multi-level preservation loss）来在BCT过程中维持嵌入空间的几何结构，并集成焦点重加权策略（focal re-weighting）以优先学习困难样本。该模型仅基于预提取的嵌入进行训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多种多模态基准（图像、文本、视觉文档和视频检索任务）及多种模型类型上实现了较强的向后兼容性。MKP-Adapter仅使用预提取嵌入训练，且相对原始主干前向传播仅带来可忽略的额外延迟，彰显其高效性。

Conclusion: MKP-Adapter证明了无需更新主干网络即可实现多模态嵌入模型的向后兼容升级，大幅降低训练成本并避免性能退化风险，同时保持高效和良好的兼容性表现。

Abstract: Upgrading embedding models typically requires expensive database re-indexing, as new query embeddings are incompatible with existing database embeddings. While Backward Compatible Training (BCT) mitigates this by enforcing compatibility during training, existing approaches often require updating the backbone model. This is impractical because of significant training cost, the risk of performance regression, and limited access to proprietary model weights. We introduce Multi-modal Knowledge Preserving Adapter (MKP-Adapter), the first adapter-only BCT approach for Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) that requires no backbone updates. We identified that the primary challenge in adapter-only BCT is preserving the knowledge of the new embeddings while enforcing backward compatibility. Hence, we propose a multi-level preservation loss that maintains the geometric structure of the embedding spaces throughout BCT. Furthermore, a focal re-weighting strategy is integrated to prioritize learning from challenging samples. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves strong backward compatibility across diverse multi-modal benchmarks (image, text, visual document, and video retrieval tasks) and model types. Notably, MKP-Adapter is trained solely on pre-extracted embeddings and requires only negligible additional latency relative to the original backbone forward pass, highlighting its efficiency.

</details>


### [57] [VOR-Bench: A Human Perception-Driven Benchmark for Video Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16878)
*Haonan Huang,Tianrui Qiu,Xianghao Zang,Yinan Du,Zhixiang He,Chi Zhang,Hao Sun,Zhongjiang He,Tianwei Cao,Xuchong Zhang,Hongbin Sun,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VOR-Bench，一个针对视频物体移除（VOR）评估的全新基准，旨在解决现有评估范式存在的两大局限：参考视频不可靠以及传统指标与人类偏好不一致。VOR-Bench包含三个集成组件：首个同时提供成对编辑视频和涂鸦掩码的数据集VORD（涵盖模型生成、工具渲染、相机采集的多样数据）、自动生成真实且运动连贯配对视频的rMPAF框架，以及首个基于感知驱动的VLM打分模型VOR-MDSM（覆盖视觉属性并与人类主观判断匹配）。实验表明VOR-Bench与人类感知高度一致（相关系数>0.9）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频物体移除（VOR）评估存在两个关键局限：一是参考视频不可靠（questionable references），二是传统算术指标与人类偏好之间存在错位。作者旨在构建一个更真实、更贴合人类感知的评估框架。

Method: 提出VOR-Bench，由三个组件构成：(1) VORD数据集——首个同时提供配对编辑视频和涂鸦掩码的基准数据集，其数据谱系涵盖模型生成、工具渲染和相机采集三类，确保真实场景下的鲁棒评估；(2) rMPAF框架——结合基于图像的物体移除与微调的视频生成模型的优势，自动生成具有真实感且运动连贯的配对视频；(3) 提出三个评估维度并引入VOR-MDSM——首个专门面向掩码引导VOR的基于感知驱动的VLM打分模型，弥合算术指标与人类感知之间的差距。

Result: 大量实验表明，VOR-Bench得到的评估结果与人类感知高度一致，与主观评估的相关系数（ρ）超过0.9。作者将发布VOR-Bench及其文档以保证完全可复现性。

Conclusion: VOR-Bench通过可靠数据集、自动配对视频生成框架和感知驱动的VLM打分模型，有效解决了VOR评估中参考不可靠与指标-人类偏好错位两大问题，实现了与人类主观判断高度吻合的评估，并将开源以促进可复现性。

Abstract: Despite its crucial role in video object removal (VOR), existing evaluation paradigms face two critical limitations: questionable references and a misalignment between tradi- tional metrics and human preference. To address these challenges, we introduce VOR- Bench, which advances VOR evaluation through three integrated components. First, we present the VOR Dataset (VORD), the first benchmark dataset providing both paired edited videos and graffiti masks. Its unique strength lies in a diverse data spectrum, which encompasses model-generated, tool-rendered, and camera-captured data, ensuring robust assessment across real-world scenarios. Second, we develop rMPAF, a realistic Motion- capable Paired-video Acquisition Framework. By combining the strengths of image- based object removal and fine-tuned video generation models, rMPAF automatically generates realistic, motion-coherent paired videos. Finally, we propose three evaluation dimensions and introduce VOR-MDSM, the first perception-driven VLM-based scoring model specifically designed for mask-guided VOR. It bridges the gap between arithmetic metrics and human perception by covering the essential visual attributes and matching nuanced human judgment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VOR-Bench yields evaluation results that align closely with human perception, achieving a remarkable cor- relation (\r{ho} > 0.9) with subjective assessments. We will release VOR-Bench along with its documentation to ensure full reproducibility.

</details>


### [58] [Temporally Consistent Graph Extraction and Matching for Longitudinal Angiographic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16889)
*Linus Kreitner,Laurin Lux,Carmen Baumann,Daniel Rueckert,Martin J. Menten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种先匹配后联合精修的血管图处理策略，通过在图提取后进行早期匹配，再利用联合信息去除假性膨出和合并节点，相比单独精修或不清洗，能够获得更高的匹配区域且避免图碎片化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管图提取、精修与匹配流程对分割图的细微差异高度敏感，即使是分割图中微小的变化也会导致生成截然不同的血管图。这些伪影严重阻碍了同一受试者随时间变化的连续血管图之间的准确匹配。

Method: 提出"先匹配、后联合精修"的策略：在基础图提取后立即进行早期匹配，然后利用两个图的联合信息去除假性膨出（spurious bulges）并合并节点（merging junctions），从而实现两图协同精修。

Result: 在复杂视网膜血管图实验中，该策略相比"单独精修"或"不精修"方案，实现了更高的匹配区域面积，且不会导致血管图的碎片化。

Conclusion: 联合精修策略优于分离精修或不精修，通过共享匹配信息的协同精修方式能够有效提升时序血管图匹配的准确性与完整性。

Abstract: Recent advances in angiographic imaging have enabled longitudinal visualization of the microvasculature. Image processing pipelines based on vessel graphs are able to resolve subtle temporal changes at the level of individual blood vessels. However, current strategies for graph extraction, refinement, and matching are highly sensitive, with even minuscule differences in the underlying segmentation map resulting in substantially different vessel graphs. These artifacts severely inhibit the ability to accurately match sequential vessel graphs of the same subject over time. To address this problem, we propose a strategy that matches graphs before jointly refining them. Specifically, we perform an early matching after basic graph extraction before removing spurious bulges and merging junctions in both graphs using joint information. In experiments with complex retinal vessel graphs, we demonstrate that this strategy results in a higher matched area without graph fragmentation compared to separate or no refinement, respectively.

</details>


### [59] [PiPS: Post-Hoc Prototypical Explanations for Interpretable Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16909)
*Miłosz Adamczyk,Tymoteusz Zapala,Piotr Borycki,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PiPS，首个完全事后（post-hoc）的可解释原型分割方法，无需修改或微调任何预训练网络即可生成直观、空间局部化的原型解释，同时完整保留模型100%的原始预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在医疗诊断、自动驾驶等关键系统中的部署日益普遍，确保其可解释性至关重要。基于原型的推理通过"看起来像这个"的范式模拟人类认知过程，但在全局图像分类中已被充分研究，其在语义分割等稠密预测任务中的可解释性却鲜有探索。现有基于原型的可解释分割模型依赖事前（ante-hoc）架构，需要昂贵的从头训练和网络结构修改，导致预测性能明显下降。

Method: 提出PiPS（Post-hoc interpretable Prototypical Segmentation），一种完全事后处理的可解释原型分割方法。该方法能从任何预训练网络中提取直观、空间局部化的原型解释，无需修改网络结构或进行微调，从而保留模型100%的原始预测性能。

Result: PiPS在语义分割模型上成功生成原型解释，完全不需要修改或微调预训练网络，保持了模型原有的完整预测性能（100%），相比现有事前架构方法性能下降的限制具有显著优势。

Conclusion: PiPS为高级计算机视觉任务中透明系统的安全、经济高效部署开辟了新途径，解决了原型可解释分割中事前架构的成本与性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: With the increasing deployment of deep neural networks in critical systems, such as medical diagnostics and autonomous vehicles, ensuring their interpretability is crucial to building trust in decision-making systems. In the field of explainable artificial intelligence, prototype-based reasoning has gained particular popularity, as it mimics human cognitive processes by explaining model decisions based on visual similarity under the looks like this paradigm. While this paradigm has been thoroughly investigated in the context of global image classification, the interpretability of dense predictions, particularly semantic segmentation, remains largely unexplored despite its immense importance in tasks requiring precise object localization. Existing prototype-based interpretable segmentation models rely on ante-hoc architectures, which entails significant limitations because they require costly training from scratch and modifications to the network structure, ultimately leading to a noticeable drop in predictive performance compared to standard black-box models. To address this issue, we propose PiPS (Post-hoc interpretable Prototypical Segmentation), the first fully post-hoc solution for generating prototypical explanations for semantic segmentation models. Our method enables the extraction of intuitive, spatially localized explanations from any pre-trained network without modification or fine-tuning, thereby preserving 100% of the model's original predictive performance. This approach opens a new avenue for the safe and cost-effective deployment of transparent systems in advanced computer vision tasks. Codebase available at https://github.com/gmum/PIPS.

</details>


### [60] [NeuroSymbEAD: A Large Scale Neuro-Symbolic Caption Dataset for Omni-Directional Embodied Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16919)
*Muhammad Ahmed Ullah Khan,Mohammed Elamine,Sheikh Talha Uddin,Didier Stricker,Sk Aziz Ali,Muhammad Zeshan Afzal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 NeuroSymbEAD，一个大规模神经符号标注描述数据集，基于 KITTI-360 构建自车中心知识图谱，对静态与动态物体标注类别、朝向、距离等信息，并生成多层级文本描述（作为自车场景地图的轻量表示），用于自动驾驶场景中的常识理解、交通场景理解与 3D 推理等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内场景任务中，基于自然语言的目标接地描述（grounded captioning）及其复杂关系表示已被广泛采用，而神经符号表示在处理结构化信息方面效果显著。作者希望将这一范式迁移到自动驾驶室外场景，通过结构化符号化描述弥补现有视觉-语言模型在交通场景理解、三维推理与可解释自动驾驶感知方面的不足。

Method: 构建一个涵盖静态与动态物体的自车中心知识图谱（ego-centric KG），标注类别、heading 方向、朝向及距离；在该知识图谱上生成多层级文本描述。数据标注流水线支持生成多样化的地图片段，可将模拟或真实物体填充到任意 3D 目标检测网络预测的包围框内，并构建分层文本描述。基线任务包括室外场景地图重建、视觉识别与目标接地，并使用预训练的接地网络与自回归描述生成网络进行评测。

Result: 基于 KITTI-360 建立了神经符号与本体论描述生成的基准，验证了将 3D 驾驶场景转换为结构化自车中心语言的可行性与表现，为视觉-语言模型和基础模型提供了交通场景解释、3D 推理及可解释自动驾驶感知方面的评测基准。

Conclusion: NeuroSymbEAD 通过将 3D 驾驶场景转化为结构化自车中心语言，建立起一个面向视觉-语言与基础模型的基准，能够推动交通场景解释、三维推理与可解释自动驾驶感知的发展。

Abstract: This paper introduces NeuroSymbEAD, a large-scale neuro-symbolic caption dataset featuring an ego-centric knowledge graph (KG) of static and dynamic objects annotated with classes, categories, heading directions, orientations, and distances from the ego-vehicle. These annotations are used on the KITTI-360 dataset to generate multilevel textual captions representing a lightweight version of an ego-centric scene map. Outdoor scene-map reconstruction, visual recognition, and object grounding establish baselines for driving common sense and traffic/scene understanding. For these purposes, natural language-based grounded captioning of objects and their complex relationships is a widely adopted contextual representation for indoor scene tasks. Neuro-symbolic representations have proven effective in handling structured information for various computer vision and language applications. Our data annotation pipeline allows the generation of varied map segments, populating simulated or real objects within the bounding boxes predicted by any 3D object detection network, and building hierarchical text captions. We benchmark our neuro-symbolic and ontological caption generation using pre-trained grounding and learned auto-regressive captioning networks. By converting 3D driving scenes into structured ego-centric language, NeuroSymbEAD provides a benchmark for vision-language and foundation models for traffic-scene explanation, 3D reasoning, and interpretable autonomous-driving perception.

</details>


### [61] [Evaluating Mesh Reconstruction Methods for Crop Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16926)
*Karanvir Singh,Theo Morales,Binh-Son Hua,Mukesh Saini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了7种用于作物表型分析的3D网格重建管线，通过定性定量指标测量输出保真度与一致性。结果显示GGGS、PGSR和2DGS优于其他管线，其中GGGS在包含用户评分、Chamfer距离、LPIPS、PSNR和SSIM的5维雷达图中比第二名2DGS高出约27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农作物表型分析对研究其全生命周期、提高产量和粮食生产至关重要，但远程种植地点的作物难以让专家现场考察。3D重建技术可通过作物数字化解决这一难题，使专家可随时随地访问3D作物模型。因此，需要系统评估当前3D重建管线在作物表型分析中的适用性。

Method: 对7种网格重建管线进行系统评估，通过定性与定量相结合的方式测量其输出网格的保真度（fidelity）和一致性（consistency）。定量指标包括用户评分（User ratings）、Chamfer距离、LPIPS、PSNR和SSIM，并采用雷达图进行综合对比。

Result: GGGS、PGSR和2DGS三种管线产生的网格在定量指标和视觉输出上均优于其他管线。其中GGGS在包含5个维度的雷达图上比第二名2DGS约高出27%。

Conclusion: GGGS被验证为作物表型3D重建中最优的管线选择，其次是2DGS，为远程作物表型分析提供了可靠的技术方案参考。

Abstract: Phenotyping an agricultural crop is crucial for studying its entire life cycle, as it provides vital insights to improve yield and, ultimately, food production. Doing the same for crops grown on remote sites is a challenge for the specialists who cannot be available on-site. 3D reconstruction techniques offer a promising solution to this problem by enabling crop digitization, allowing specialists to access the resulting 3D crop models from anywhere at any time. In this work, we evaluate recent 3D reconstruction pipelines for crop phenotyping. We focus on 7 mesh reconstruction pipelines and measure the fidelity and consistency of their outputs qualitatively and quantitatively. Our results suggest that the meshes produced by the GGGS, PGSR, and 2DGS are preferable to the other pipelines, owing to their quantitative metrics and visually pleasing outputs. The GGGS pipeline is better than the second-best pipeline (2DGS) by about 27\% on the radar chart with 5 dimensions, namely, User ratings, Chamfer distance, LPIPS, PSNR, and SSIM.

</details>


### [62] [MedPCFM-TED: One-Step Point Cloud Flow Matching for Implant Generation via Teacher-Guided Endpoint Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16934)
*Kamil Kwarciak,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出教师指导端点蒸馏（TED），一种用于点云条件颅骨植入物生成的一步蒸馏框架，通过教师指导端点评测和几何匹配损失训练一步学生模型，无需显式路径拉直。在SkullBreak上取得最佳整体性能，在SkullFix上具有竞争力，并在一步方法中达到最强Chamfer距离性能，每样本生成约0.04秒，显著加速生成且不牺牲重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅骨植入物生成是医学成像中的重要任务，但现有基于点云的生成方法（特别是流匹配）虽具出色的重建质量和高效采样，推理时仍需多次神经函数评估，这限制了快速生成多个可行的植入物候选方案。

Method: TED采用教师-学生蒸馏范式：训练一个一步生成的学生模型，利用教师引导的端点监督（teacher-guided endpoint supervision）和几何匹配损失（geometric matching losses），同时避免显式的路径拉直（path straightening），实现条件点云颅骨植入物的一步生成。

Result: 在SkullFix和SkullBreak两个基准上评估：TED在SkullBreak数据集上取得最佳整体性能，在SkullFix上保持竞争力，并且在所比较的一步方法中拥有最强的Chamfer距离性能；每样本生成时间约为0.04秒。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，一步蒸馏能够在不牺牲重建质量的前提下，大幅加速条件点云颅骨植入物的生成，为该任务提供高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Cranial implant generation is an important task in medical imaging. Recent point cloud based generative methods, particularly flow matching, offer strong reconstruction quality and efficient sampling, but still require multiple neural function evaluations during inference. This limits rapid generation of multiple plausible implant candidates. We propose Teacher-guided Endpoint Distillation (TED), a simple one-step distillation framework for conditional cranial implant generation on point clouds. TED trains a one-step student using teacher-guided endpoint supervision and geometric matching losses, while avoiding explicit path straightening. We evaluate TED on the SkullFix and SkullBreak benchmarks. TED achieves the best overall performance on the SkullBreak dataset, remains competitive on SkullFix, and provides the strongest Chamfer distance performance among the compared one-step methods. In addition, TED generates implants in approximately 0.04s per sample. These results show that one-step distillation can substantially accelerate conditional point cloud implant generation without sacrificing reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [63] [High-Fidelity Video Quality Assessment with VQA-Specific Saliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16946)
*Hakan Emre Gedik,Shashank Gupta,Alan Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HFVQA框架，基于固定尺寸时空（ST）块并兼容预训练视频基础模型（ViFM），通过多尺度采样（含原始分辨率）保留高质量线索，同时引入轻量辅助网络从质量监督中蒸馏出VQA特定显著图，仅处理约12%候选ST块即实现SOTA性能，使高保真ViFM视频质量评估在计算上可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的无参考视频质量评估（NR VQA）虽然取得了进展，但视频数据庞大，深度模型处理成本高昂。VQA中保持原始分辨率线索和密集时间信息对准确性至关重要，而现有省算力的预处理策略（如分片）会改变输入数据分布，阻碍对预训练视频基础模型（ViFM）的有效复用。因此需要一种既能保持高保真时空信息又具备计算可行性的方案。

Method: HFVQA基于固定尺寸时空（ST）块构建，完全兼容预训练ViFM。它在多个尺度（包括原始分辨率）采样ST块，并进行最小化时间子采样以保留低层质量线索和语义上下文。为限制计算量，引入轻量辅助网络与ViFM编码器端到端联合训练，从质量监督中直接蒸馏学习"VQA特定显著图"，该显著图捕捉任务相关重要性模式，反映视频质量感知主要由一小部分时空区域主导。最后结合高保真时空线索与学习到的任务特定显著图。

Result: 在标准NR VQA基准上取得SOTA性能，同时仅处理约12%的候选ST块，大幅降低计算负担，使高保真ViFM的VQA在计算上可行。

Conclusion: 通过学习任务特定的显著图来聚焦少数关键时空区域，可在大幅削减计算量的同时保留高保真原始分辨率线索，实现高效且高质量的无参考视频质量评估，验证了"质量感知主要由小部分时空区域主导"这一假设的有效性。

Abstract: No-reference video quality assessment (NR VQA) has recently seen promising progress with deep learning. However, video data is inherently large, and processing them with deep models incurs high computational cost. This challenge is particularly acute in VQA, where preserving original-resolution cues and dense temporal information is critical for accuracy. Existing efficiency-driven preprocessing strategies, such as fragmenting, reduce computation but alter the input data distribution, limiting effective reuse of pretrained video foundation models (ViFMs). To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{H}igh-\textbf{F}idelity \textbf{V}ideo \textbf{Q}uality \textbf{A}ssessment (\textbf{HFVQA}), a framework built on fixed-size spatio-temporal (ST) patches that is fully compatible with pretrained ViFMs. HFVQA samples ST patches across multiple scales, including the original resolution, with minimal temporal subsampling to preserve low-level quality cues and semantic context. To limit computation, HFVQA introduces a lightweight auxiliary network trained end-to-end with the ViFM encoder to learn \textit{VQA-specific saliency}. Distilled directly from quality supervision, this saliency captures task-specific importance patterns, reflecting that video quality perception is dominated by a small subset of spatio-temporal regions. By combining high-fidelity spatio-temporal cues with learned, task-specific saliency, HFVQA achieves SOTA performance on standard NR VQA benchmarks while processing as little as 12\% of candidate ST patches, making high-fidelity ViFM-based VQA computationally tractable.

</details>


### [64] [Symmetry-Aware Likelihood-Orbit Aggregation for Selective Left-Right Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17004)
*Zhouzhi Xiong,Chuxi Zhang,Weizhen He,Yi Chen,Qi Li,Donglian Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对冻结视觉语言模型(VLM)在细粒度左右关系判断上不可靠的问题，提出Relation-Orbit方法：在水平反射干预后，将八个归一化似然值分配到查询支持和反事实角色中，并以封闭形式对比（无需学习融合参数）构建选择性验证信号，仅当符号对比超过基于Clopper-Pearson置信上界选定阈值时才断言声明。在VSR和GQA四个冻结VLM上，Relation-Orbit在全部八个数据集-骨干组合中均比全八轨Orbit-Max基线获得更高的10%选择性风险校准目标平均测试覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结视觉语言模型在细粒度左右空间方向声明上仍然不可靠，且原始声明似然度无法可靠地对验证错误进行排序。因此核心问题是在固定水平反射干预后，如何将诱导的似然度测量恰当地组合成具有选择性的验证信号，以区分可靠与不可靠的声明。

Method: 提出Relation-Orbit，一种无学习融合参数的封闭形式对比方法。它将八个归一化似然度分配到由反射、逆关系和实体交换决定的查询支持角色与反事实角色中；仅当符号对比超过阈值时才断言声明，该阈值基于留出数据上的逐点Clopper-Pearson置信上界选择。实验中与全八轨Orbit-Max基线对比，并辅以LLaVA-1.5/COCO评估、约化轨道控制实验及两侧分区诊断来刻画结构优势。

Result: 在VSR和GQA的四个冻结VLM上，Relation-Orbit在全部八个数据集-骨干设置中均比全八轨Orbit-Max基线在10%选择性风险校准目标下获得更高的平均测试覆盖率；其对接近全部弃权的一侧干预分数的增益另有单独报告。

Conclusion: 通过将反射诱导的似然度按角色（查询支持与反事实）进行结构化对比，而非简单地对全部轨道取最大（Max），Relation-Orbit在保持10%选择性风险的前提下显著扩大了有效测试覆盖范围，证明了结构化轨道对比在细粒度方向声明选择验证中的有效性。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable on fine-grained left-right claims, and raw claim likelihoods need not reliably rank verification errors. After a horizontal-reflection intervention is fixed, how should its induced likelihood measurements be combined into a selective verification signal? We introduce Relation-Orbit, a closed-form contrast with no learned fusion parameters that assigns eight normalized likelihoods to query-supporting and counterfactual roles determined by reflection, inverse relation, and entity exchange. A claim is asserted only when the signed contrast exceeds a threshold selected on held-out data using pointwise Clopper-Pearson upper confidence bounds. On VSR and GQA across four frozen VLMs, Relation-Orbit yields higher mean test coverage at a 10% selective-risk calibration target than an all-eight Orbit-Max baseline in all eight dataset-backbone settings; gains over a nearly abstain-all one-sided intervention score are reported separately. A separate LLaVA-1.5/COCO evaluation, reduced-orbit controls, and a two-sided partition diagnostic further characterize the structural advantage.

</details>


### [65] [sensVLA: Spatially-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Model for Autonomous Wheel Loader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17021)
*Gopi Krishna Erabati,Bjarne Johannsen,Angus Stewart,Vardeep Singh Sandhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出sensVLA，一种面向自主轮式装载机控制的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构。它将Qwen3-2B视觉语言模型与全可训练Transformer动作专家相结合，通过流匹配速度回归训练。sensVLA将来自前后激光雷达融合的鸟瞰图（BEV）特征通过专用交叉注意力通路直接路由到动作专家，而VLM处理前后RGB视图以提供任务条件语义上下文。该设计将空间定位与语言推理解耦，同时在决策时保持两流交互。在真实轮式装载机数据集上，sensVLA与强相机基线达到每步聚合性能持平，在装载中心场景下纵向速度RMSE降低28%、位移误差降低9%，且在相机流损坏或移除时退化减少29%，证明显式空间定位提升了重型设备自主控制的精度与容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主轮式装载机控制需要同时推理任务语义、视角视觉、本体感知和3D场景几何。现有方法缺乏将语言/语义推理与显式3D空间定位有效整合的架构，且对传感器故障的鲁棒性不足。

Method: sensVLA采用双流架构：(1) Qwen3-2B VLM消费前后RGB视图提供任务条件语义；(2) 全可训练Transformer动作专家通过流匹配速度回归预测6个动作维度（纵向速度、转向、车身位移、臂速率、铲斗速率）。BEV特征经激光雷达融合后通过专用交叉注意力通路直接接入动作专家，实现空间定位与语义推理的解耦但在决策时保持交互。

Result: 在真实轮式装载机数据集上，sensVLA与强相机基线达到聚合每步性能持平；在装载中心场景中，纵向速度RMSE降低28%，位移误差降低9%；相机流损坏或移除时退化比基线少29%。

Conclusion: 显式空间定位（BEV/激光雷达通路）与语义推理（VLM/RGB通路）的解耦设计能够有效提升重型设备自主控制的精度与传感器容错能力，为VLA架构在工业场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous wheel-loader control requires joint reasoning over task semantics, egocentric vision, proprioception, and 3D scene geometry. We present sensVLA, a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architecture that combines a Qwen3-2B Vision-Language Model (VLM) with a fully trainable transformer action expert trained by flow-matching velocity regression. sensVLA routes Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) features, extracted from fused front and rear lidar, directly to the action expert through a dedicated cross-attention pathway, while the VLM consumes front and rear RGB views to provide task-conditioned semantic context. This design decouples spatial grounding from linguistic reasoning while preserving interaction between both streams at decision time. The expert predicts six action dimensions: longitudinal velocity, steering, body-frame displacement, arm rate, and bucket rate. On a real-world dataset from a wheel loader, sensVLA reaches aggregate per-step parity with a strong camera-only baseline and reduces longitudinal velocity RMSE by 28% and displacement error by 9% on loading centric scenarios. It also degrades 29% less when the camera stream is corrupted or removed, evidencing that explicit spatial grounding improves accuracy and fault-tolerance for heavy equipment autonomy.

</details>


### [66] [Bi-FlowGS: Bridging Generative View Completion and Gaussian Geometry through Bidirectional Flow Co-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17039)
*Yuetong Wang,Jinsheng Quan,Yi Yang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Bi-FlowGS 是一种面向稀疏视角 3D 场景重建的方法，利用光流在生成式视频补全与 3DGS 几何正则化之间建立双向桥接。它通过 V2G（视频到几何流蒸馏）将恢复视频中的时序对应先验蒸馏到高斯几何中以缓解几何作弊，并通过 G2V（几何到视频流引导恢复）用当前几何引导视频恢复，二者构成隐式双向协同精化过程，迭代提升渲染质量与几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下 3DGS 重建本质上是欠约束的，存在"几何作弊"问题：错误的位姿或深度可被不透明度、尺度和外观掩盖，导致看似合理的渲染与错误几何并存。现有正则化方法仅约束已观测视角；基于视频扩散的方法虽补全未观测视角，但主要将其用作RGB伪监督，未充分利用运动与时序先验，且缺乏显式几何监督。

Method: 提出 Bi-FlowGS，以光流为桥梁连接生成式视角补全与高斯几何正则化。包含两个可插拔模块：（1）V2G（Video-to-Geometry Flow Distillation）——将恢复视频中的时序对应先验蒸馏进高斯几何以缓解几何作弊；（2）G2V（Geometry-to-Video Flow-Guided Restoration）——用当前 3DGS 几何引导时序一致的视频恢复，提供更可靠的生成监督。V2G 与 G2V 形成隐式双向协同精化过程，使恢复视频与优化的 3DGS 场景迭代互促。

Result: 实验在广基线和无界 360° 基准上证明了渲染质量与几何一致性的提升。

Conclusion: 通过光流实现视频生成与几何正则化的双向闭环，比单纯以RGB伪监督和仅观测视角约束的方法更有效，为稀疏视角 3DGS 重建提供了一种同时利用时序运动先验和显式几何约束的可行方案。

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D scene reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is inherently underconstrained. Plausible renderings can also coexist with erroneous Gaussian geometry, as errors in positions or depths may be concealed by opacity, scale, and appearance; we term this failure mode Geometry Cheating. Existing regularization methods constrain geometry but remain limited to observed views, while video-diffusion-based methods complete unseen views yet mainly use them as RGB pseudo-supervision, underusing motion and temporal priors and lacking explicit geometry supervision. We present Bi-FlowGS, which uses optical flow to bridge generative view completion and Gaussian geometry regularization. Our plug-and-play Video-to-Geometry Flow Distillation (V2G) distills temporal correspondence priors from restored videos into Gaussian geometry to alleviate Geometry Cheating. Conversely, Geometry-to-Video Flow-Guided Restoration (G2V) uses the current 3DGS geometry to guide temporally consistent video restoration, providing more reliable generative supervision. Together, V2G and G2V form an implicit bidirectional co-refinement process, enabling restored videos and the optimized 3DGS scene to iteratively improve each other. Experiments demonstrate improved rendering quality and geometric consistency across wide-baseline and unbounded 360° benchmarks.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond In-Distribution Metrics: A Systematic Out-of-Distribution Evaluation of Congenital Heart Disease Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17068)
*Aniketh Vijesh,Shrisharanyan Vasu,Abhijit Ramesh,Clare Pomeroy-Ward,Harikrishnan Anil Maya,Sarin Xavier,Mahesh Kappanayil,Gilad Gressel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次系统评估先天性心脏病（CHD）分割中跨域（out-of-distribution, OOD）泛化能力，以ImageCHD作为外部目标队列，比较多种架构与训练策略；发现分布内性能不能反映跨队列鲁棒性，SwinUNETR优于nnU-Net，少量目标域标注即可大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHD诊断和手术规划需要患者特异性三维解剖模型，手动分割耗时，深度学习方法虽可自动化但通常仅在分布内评估，忽视了扫描仪、协议、机构、人群和成像模态等临床相关的分布偏移。

Method: 使用ImageCHD作为外部目标队列，系统比较代表性分割架构（如nnU-Net、SwinUNETR），在联合CT与CMR训练、仅CT训练、自监督预训练（MAE、JEPA）以及有限目标域适应等设置下评估OOD泛化性能。

Result: 分布内验证中nnU-Net Dice最高（0.77），但在ImageCHD上降至0.51；SwinUNETR泛化更好，达到0.67。MAE和JEPA预训练收益有限，说明架构贡献更大。引入11例标注ImageCHD后，所有SwinUNETR变体Dice超过0.76。

Conclusion: 常规分布内评估会掩盖临床重要的泛化失败，应明确进行跨数据集测试作为CHD分割评估的关键环节。

Abstract: Congenital heart disease (CHD) diagnosis and surgical planning often require patient-specific 3D anatomical models, but manual segmentation is labor-intensive, particularly in complex anatomies. Although deep-learning methods can automate this process, they are typically evaluated in-distribution, despite clinically relevant shifts in scanner, protocol, institution, population, and imaging modality. We present, to our knowledge, the first systematic evaluation of out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization in CHD segmentation, using ImageCHD as a held-out target cohort. We compare representative segmentation architectures under combined CT and CMR training, CT-only training, self-supervised pretraining, and limited target-domain adaptation.
