<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全领域普遍使用基于隐藏状态的探针检测恶意提示，但这些探针在真实世界的打字变体（拼写错误、缺失标点）面前表现脆弱，与 LLM 本身对这类扰动的鲁棒性形成鲜明对比。理解并修补这一漏洞对于部署可靠的提示注入防御至关重要。

Method: 作者系统测量了编辑在隐藏状态空间引起的读出向量旋转角度与空间衰减规律；对比了单位置探针 vs 多位置聚合（注意力、max）探针在不同扰动类型（局部 vs 分布式）下的表现；提出 KV-cache fork——在用户消息后附加短固定后缀，使探针在扰动 token 下游若干位置的读出利用其快速空间衰减特性；并以扰动增强训练作为基线对比。

Result: 单位置探针在 typo 处读出向量旋转 43–56 度，约 10 个下游 token 内衰减至 15% 以下；每条消息约 3 个常见错别字使 TPR@FPR=1% 下降 12.0pp；多位置聚合对局部扰动（≤0.5 损失）有效但对分布式扰动只能减弱，注意力与 max 聚合仍下降约 3.8pp；KV-cache fork 弥补 95% 差距（残余 -0.6pp），显著优于扰动增强训练（-3.7pp）；几何在三个模型上复现。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态探针的脆弱性根源于"旋转—衰减"几何：扰动在空间上局部放大但快速消亡。KV-cache fork 利用这一几何的衰减特性实现了近乎完整的修复，为提示注入检测提供了有效且轻量的补救方案，并为理解隐藏空间鲁棒性提供了新视角。

Abstract: LLMs handle ordinary typing variation fluently: a typo or missing punctuation leaves both user intent and the model's response substantively unchanged. Yet probes that detect malicious prompts by reading the model's hidden states tell a different story: the same edit rotates the readout vector by 43--56 at the perturbed token, decaying below 15% within ~10 downstream tokens. Stacking ~3 common typos per message cuts a single-position prompt-injection probe's TPR@FPR$=1% by 12.0pp, a gap recalibration alone cannot close. Multi-position aggregation cures localized perturbations (<= 0.5 loss) but only attenuates distributed ones, where even attention- and max-based aggregators still drop ~3.8pp. For single-position probes, we introduce a KV-cache fork: a short fixed suffix appended after the user message lets the probe read a few tokens downstream of the perturbation, exploiting its rapid spatial decay. This closes 95% of the gap (-0.6pp residual) -- an order of magnitude better than perturbation-augmented training (-3.7pp). The rotation-and-decay geometry replicates on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma-4-E4B; probe evaluation is on Llama-3.1-8B. Code: https://github.com/eladd-ai/latent-undertow

</details>


### [100] [Bias Audits Detect Bias but Disagree on Ranking: Evidence from Ten Instruments and Ten Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15995)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault,Wei Zhang,Vitor D. de Moura,José O. Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在十个前沿模型上运行十个外部审计工具，结果表明偏倚检测可行（8/10工具置信区间不包含零），但跨工具排名一致性等同随机（Kendall W=0.07, p=0.83），因工具测量的是不同构念而非同一构念的噪声；单一审计可检测偏倚并估计方向，却无法支撑模型间排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴AI法规强制对高风险系统进行偏倚审计，且审计分数正被用于模型排名。这两种用途都隐含一个假设：不同审计工具测的是同一事物且可比。作者旨在直接检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 对共享面板（十个前沿模型）经统一推理网关运行十个外部审计工具，依次针对职业性别偏倚、年龄和社会经济地位进行测试。采用正对照试验（六个刻意弱化的模型）区分两种解释：工具内可靠性因真实能力差距恢复，而跨工具排名始终无法恢复，从而指向不同构念假说。全套原始响应、代码与从源头重算所有数字的分析均已开源。

Result: 检测成功而排名失败：8/10工具检出偏倚（置信区间不包含零）；两个被广泛引用的直接探针基准因前沿模型已中性作答而饱和。跨工具排名一致性不可与随机区分（W=0.07, p=0.83）。偏倚方向随审计格式分裂：强制选择决策工具多为过度校正（倾向女性，278项招聘决策中273项偏向工薪阶层候选人），而自由生成和默认共指仍保持刻板一致。结果在社会经济地位上复现；年龄上的表面排名一致性在采用自身工具纳入规则后瓦解。

Conclusion: 单一审计能在自身操作化框架内检测偏倚并估计其方向，但没有任何单一审计足以支持将一个模型与另一模型进行排名比较。实际政策含义是：审计结果可用于检测，不可用于跨模型排序。

Abstract: Emerging AI regulation mandates bias audits of high-risk systems, and audit scores are beginning to be used to rank models. Both uses assume different audit tools measure the same thing well enough to compare. We test that assumption directly, running ten extrinsic audit instruments over a shared panel of ten frontier models through one pooled inference gateway, first on occupational gender bias, then on age and socioeconomic status. Detection succeeds while ranking fails. Eight of ten tools detect bias with confidence intervals clear of zero; two widely cited direct-probe benchmarks are saturated because frontier models now answer neutrally. But cross-tool rank agreement is indistinguishable from chance (Kendall's W=0.07, p=0.83). A positive control with six deliberately weaker models separates two explanations: within-tool reliability recovers once the panel spans real capability gaps, yet cross-tool ranking never recovers, which points to the tools measuring different constructs rather than one construct noisily. Even the direction of bias splits by audit format: forced-choice decision tools mostly over-correct (toward women, and toward working-class candidates in 273 of 278 hiring decisions), while free generation and default coreference stay stereotype-congruent. The pattern replicates on socioeconomic status; an apparent ranking agreement on age dissolves under the paper's own tool-inclusion rules. The practical message: a single audit can detect bias and estimate its direction within its own operationalization, but no single audit supports ranking one model against another. All raw responses, code, and the analysis that recomputes every reported number from source are available at https://github.com/williamguey/bias-audit-agreement.

</details>


### [101] [Comment on arXiv:2607.01233: Survivorship Bias in Published-Paper Baselines for Research-Idea Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15996)
*Fredrik A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇对Chen、Zhao和Cohan关于LLM生成研究想法评估工作的评论，指出其存在一个关键识别问题：人类基线样本来自已发表论文，而LLM基线来自一次性提案，导致已发表的人类基线低估了人类真实想法池中"桥梁式/综合式"想法的比例，从而观测到的人机差距可能部分甚至大部分源于幸存者偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过提出幸存者偏差这一更窄的识别问题，提醒原研究的评估结论可能存在系统性偏差。核心关切是：人类基线选择"已发表论文"而非"未发表的人类想法提案"，而这类"桥梁式/综合式"想法往往易于生成却难以通过发表审查，因此在已发表样本中被系统性低估，进而放大了LLM与人类之间的表现差距。

Method: 作者采用论证/评论的方法，通过逻辑推理指出评估设计中的选择偏误：比较两组定义不对称的基线（已发表的论文 vs 一次性提案），并推断"合成式/桥梁式"想法具有"生成容易但发表存活率低"的属性，由此论证观测差距的成因被污染，属于非实证性的分析性评论。

Result: 作者得出：原研究观测到的人类-LLM差距可能部分甚至很大程度是幸存者偏差的产物，而非LLM真正的想法生成能力优势。已发表基线会低估人类想法池中合成类想法的真实占比。

Conclusion: 该评论提示，为使评估公平，应控制人类与LLM基线在"可发表性过滤"上的一致性（例如用未经过审稿的人类原始提案与LLM提案对照），或对发表过程的选择机制进行建模校正，否则不能将观察到的差距简单归因于LLM更强的想法生成能力。

Abstract: Chen, Zhao, and Cohan introduce a valuable distributional evaluation of LLM-generated research ideas. This comment raises a narrower identification concern: their human baseline consists of published papers, whereas the LLM baseline consists of one-shot proposals. If bridge-like or synthesis-like ideas are relatively easy to generate but relatively unlikely to survive publication, then the published human baseline will understate their prevalence in the unseen human idea pool. The observed human--LLM gap may therefore be partly, or even largely, a consequence of survivorship bias.

</details>


### [102] [Crash Narrative-Guided Countermeasure Recommendation Using Large Language Models: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework for Intersection Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15997)
*Abu Saif Md Nasim Uddin,Mohamed Abdel-Aty,Zubayer Islam,Parvez Anowar,Chenzhu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于事故叙述的检索增强生成（RAG）框架，将事故叙述中提取的机理属性（交通控制、信号指示、驾驶员过错、车辆运动、行驶方向）与FHWA已证实安全对策及CMF清理库中的循证措施关联，通过嵌入检索、关联规则挖掘、统计指导和工程推理引导，将事故叙述转化为针对具体地点的安全对策推荐。在佛罗里达州115个路口的312起重伤/死亡事故上采用五折交叉验证，实现了0.82精确率、0.85召回率和0.82的F1分数，每个地点平均推荐3.91项对策（3.14项匹配），展现了检索增强LLM作为可解释、可扩展的交通安全决策支持工具的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉口安全改善通常依赖专家人工判断来确定事故机理并推荐对策，这是劳动密集型、难以规模化且依赖经验丰富的交通安全工程师。尽管事故叙述（crash narratives）中包含丰富的事故机理描述，但这些非结构化信息在安全分析中很大程度上未被充分利用。因此需要一种能够自动从事故叙述中提取机理并推荐循证对策的可扩展方法。

Method: 提出事故叙述引导的RAG框架：1) 从事故叙述中提取关键机理属性（交通控制、信号指示、驾驶员过错、车辆运动、行驶方向）；2) 将这些属性与FHWA Proven Safety Countermeasures及CMF Clearinghouse中的循证治疗措施关联；3) 整合四种组件——基于嵌入的历史相似交叉口检索、关联规则挖掘、关于预期相关对策数量的统计指导、以及引导LLM经过领域一致决策过程的工程推理指导；4) 在最终选择对策前进行领域一致的推理。评估采用佛罗里达州Lake和Sumter县115个交叉口的312起致命及重伤事故，使用五折交叉验证。

Result: 框架达到0.82精确率、0.85召回率、0.82 F1分数；每个地点平均推荐3.91项对策，其中3.14项与实际情况匹配，接近实际平均值3.86，验证了框架的准确性和一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了检索增强LLM作为交通管理机构可解释、可扩展的决策支持工具的潜力，能够将事故叙述自动转化为针对特定地点的安全对策推荐，减少对专家人工判断的依赖。

Abstract: Improving safety at intersections requires identifying crash mechanisms and recommending appropriate countermeasures. However, this process traditionally relies on expert judgment, making it labor-intensive, difficult to scale, and dependent on the availability of experienced traffic safety engineers. Although crash narratives contain rich description of crash mechanisms, this unstructured information remains largely underutilized in safety analyses. This study presents a crash narrative-guided retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that translates narrative-derived crash mechanisms into site-specific countermeasure recommendations. Key mechanism attributes including traffic control, signal indication, driver fault, vehicle movement, and travel direction were extracted from crash narratives and linked to evidence-based treatments from the FHWA Proven Safety Countermeasures and the CMF Clearinghouse. The framework integrates embedding-based retrieval of historically similar intersections, association-rule mining, statistical guidance on the expected number of relevant countermeasures, and an engineering reasoning guidance that directs LLM through a domain-consistent decision process before selecting countermeasures. Evaluated on 312 fatal and serious-injury crashes across 115 intersections in Lake and Sumter Counties, Florida, using five-fold cross-validation, the framework achieved a precision of 0.82, recall of 0.85, and F1-score of 0.82, while recommending an average of 3.91 countermeasures per location with 3.14 matching, closely matching the actual average (3.86). Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates the potential of retrieval-augmented LLMs as an interpretable and scalable decision-support tool for transportation agencies for translating crash narratives into countermeasure recommendations.

</details>


### [103] [Self-reported archetypes and behavioral failures in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15998)
*Tabia Tanzin Prama,Calla Glavin Beauregard,Christopher M. Danforth,Peter Sheridan Dodds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对22个大型语言模型（涵盖闭源前沿系统如GPT-4.0-5.2、Grok-3/4、Gemini 2.5 Pro/Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.5/4.6，以及开源模型如Llama、DeepSeek、OLMo、Qwen系列）进行了自我报告的人格原型映射。每个模型对464个双极语义差异特质对进行自评，并将结果投影到基于2000个虚构角色众包评分构建的六维原型空间中。研究发现闭源模型的自评特质与人类评分的虚构角色特质共现结构一致，围绕Hero、Angel、Traditionalist、Geek四种原型维度组织，其最接近的类比角色为Data、Vision和Janet；而开源模型的自表征更弱、更嘈杂且内部矛盾。论文还揭示了宣称性格与实际行为之间的差距：幻觉损害了宣称的精确性、谄媚使宣称的善良复杂化、代理失败违背了宣称的服从性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每个LLM都具备构成其"性格"的行为特质和道德偏好，无论是设计所致还是训练涌现，这些系统表现出塑造其交互、顺从、抵抗和出错方式的持久倾向，但LLM性格的结构仍然鲜为人知。作者旨在提供一个可复现、以性格为基础的框架来评估LLM"是什么"，而非仅仅"做什么"。

Method: 作者让22个LLM（覆盖闭源前沿模型与开源模型）在464对双极语义差异特质上自我评分，得到自我报告画像。然后利用Archetypometrics框架，将这些画像投影到由2000个虚构角色众包评分导出的六维原型空间中进行分析。同时将自我报告画像与开发者的宪法/准则进行交叉对照，检验宣称性格与实际行为的一致性。

Result: 闭源模型的自我评分特质与人类评分的虚构角色经验特质共现结构一致，形成连贯的、类人的自我表征，并围绕Hero、Angel、Traditionalist、Geek四类反复出现的原型维度组合组织，其最接近的类比角色包括Data、Vision和Janet。开源模型则表现出更弱、更嘈杂且内部矛盾的自表征，占据原型空间中结构较弱的弥散区域。交叉对照发现宣称性格与实现行为之间存在显著差距：幻觉损害了宣称的精确性、谄媚使宣称的善良复杂化、代理失败违背了宣称的服从性。

Conclusion: 这些自我评分不应被解读为对模型性格的中性测量，而应被视为塑造模型行为的同一优化过程的结构化输出。该工作提供了一个可复现的、基于性格的框架，用于评估LLM的本质属性，而不只是其行为表现。

Abstract: Every large language model (LLM) has behavioral traits and moral preferences that comprise its character. Whether by design or as an emergent property of training, these systems exhibit persistent dispositions that shape how they interact, comply, resist, and err, yet the structure of LLM character remains poorly understood. We map the self-reported personality archetypes of 22 LLMs spanning closed-source frontier systems (GPT-4.0-5.2, Grok-3/4, Gemini 2.5 Pro/Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.5/4.6) and open-source models (Llama, DeepSeek, OLMo, and Qwen series). Each model self-rated across 464 bipolar semantic-differential trait pairs, and the resulting profiles were projected into a six-dimensional archetypal space derived from crowd-sourced ratings of 2,000 fictional characters using the Archetypometrics framework. Closed-source models' self-rating traits align with the empirical trait co-occurrence structure of human-rated fictional characters, suggesting coherent, human-like self-representations organized around combinations of four recurring archetypal dimensions: Hero, Angel, Traditionalist, and Geek. Their closest analogues include Data, Vision, and Janet. Open-source models show weaker, noisier, and internally contradictory self-representations, occupying a diffuse region of archetype space with weak structure. Cross-referencing self-reported profiles with developer constitutions reveals a consequential gap between claimed character and enacted behavior: hallucination undermines claimed precision, sycophancy complicates claimed kindness, and agentic failures contradict claimed obedience. These self-ratings should therefore be interpreted not as neutral measurements of model character, but as structured outputs of the same optimization processes that shape model behavior. This work provides a reproducible, character-grounded framework for evaluating what LLMs are, not just what they do.

</details>


### [104] [NepKANUN: A RAG-Based Nepali Legal Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15999)
*Bhabuk Thapa,Prasiddha Koirala,Ranjit Raut,Sunil Regmi,Bal Krishna Bal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向尼泊尔法律文本的人工智能法律助手，基于微调的大型语言模型并集成检索增强生成（RAG）框架，通过定制的高质量问答数据集训练，在BERTScore评估中针对简单、中等、复杂问题分别获得0.82、0.77、0.71的F1分数，并通过专家评审验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔获取法律信息面临多重困难：法律术语复杂晦涩、可用资源有限、以及错误信息泛滥，这些障碍阻碍了普通民众对法律知识的获取与理解。

Method: 方法包含三个核心环节：1）构建定制的高质量法律问答数据集用于微调大型语言模型；2）将微调模型集成到RAG框架中，实现生成与检索的融合，为自然语言法律查询提供精确、简洁的答案；3）通过BERTScore客观评估与专家人工评审双重验证系统性能。

Result: 根据BERTScore，系统在简单、中等、复杂三种难度层级上分别取得0.82、0.77和0.71的F1分数；专家评审进一步确认了系统的可用性与实际价值。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过定制化法律数据与RAG技术的结合，能够有效将生成式AI与检索机制融合，从而在尼泊尔实现法律知识的民主化获取，为资源受限地区提供可借鉴的解决方案。

Abstract: Accessing legal information in Nepal is difficult due to complex terminology, limited resources, and misinformation. We introduce an AI-powered legal assistant that is tailored for Nepali legal texts and is built on a fine-tuned large language model. The technology provides precise, streamlined answers to natural language legal inquiries when integrated into a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework. It was trained using a custom dataset of high-quality question-answer pairs, and according to BERTScore, it obtained strong F1 scores of 0.82 (simple), 0.77 (moderate), and 0.71 (complex). Its usability is further confirmed by expert reviews. Our method shows how merging generation and retrieval can effectively democratize access to legal knowledge in Nepal by focusing on customized legal data and incorporating RAG.

</details>


### [105] [Nepali Legal Expertise through Generative and Extractive Pre-trained Transformers (NepLEGiT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16010)
*Ranjit Raut,Tishya Dhakal,Aaryan Shakya,Bhabuk Thapa,Prasiddha Koirala,Bal Krishna Bal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 NepLEGiT，一个专为尼泊尔法律文本领域设计的专用小型语言模型（SLM）。该模型从零开始预训练了一个解码器架构的 GPT-2 风格模型（约3000万参数），使用约400万个尼泊尔法律语料 token，在保留验证集上达到了 0.5684 的交叉熵损失、1.8 的困惑度以及 82.9% 的下一词预测准确率。同时对比了 mBERT 和 MuRIL 在相同语料上的连续掩码语言模型预训练效果，其中 mBERT（困惑度2.35）优于 MuRIL（困惑度6.07），与 NepLEGiT 的生成式定位形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔法律语言具有高度复杂性，且法律信息获取渠道受限，严重制约了司法服务的公平可达性。传统法律服务因语言障碍、信息碎片化以及法律专业人才短缺（尤其在农村地区）而难以惠及广大民众，因此亟需通过语言模型技术来民主化法律知识并提升法律服务交付效率。

Method: 从零开始预训练一个解码器架构的 GPT-2 小语言模型，采用6层、6头、384维的 Transformer 结构（约3000万参数），训练语料为约400万个尼泊尔法律文本 token（涵盖宪法、民刑法典及行政法规）。训练策略包括 warmup 余弦衰减调度、梯度累积和混合精度运算。此外，还在相同语料上对 mBERT 和 MuRIL 进行连续掩码语言模型预训练，作为编码器基线进行对比评估。

Result: 在保留验证集上，NepLEGiT 达到了交叉熵损失 0.5684、困惑度 1.8、下一词预测准确率 82.9%；编码器基线方面，mBERT 达到困惑度 2.35（评估损失 0.8565），优于 MuRIL 的困惑度 6.07（评估损失 1.8026），提供了与 NepLEGiT 生成式取向互补的强编码器基线。

Conclusion: NepLEGiT 作为面向尼泊尔法律文本的专用小语言模型，在生成式（解码器）路径上表现出色，而 mBERT 则在编码器路径上提供了有效补充，二者共同构成了涵盖生成与理解的完整法律 AI 能力框架，有望有效缓解尼泊尔法律信息不可及与专业人才短缺的问题。

Abstract: The complexity of legal language and limited accessibility to legal information pose significant challenges to justice delivery in Nepal. Traditional legal services remain inaccessible to many citizens due to language barriers, information fragmentation, and a critical shortage of legal expertise, particularly in rural areas. We present NepLEGiT (Nepali Legal Expertise through Generative and Extractive Pre-trained Transformers), a specialized small language model (SLM) designed to democratize legal knowledge and enhance legal-service delivery in Nepal. We pre-train a decoder-based GPT-2 SLM from scratch on a curated corpus of ~4 million tokens of Nepali legal text, covering constitutional law, civil and criminal codes, and administrative regulations. The model comprises ~30 million parameters in a 6-layer, 6-head, 384-dimensional transformer trained with warmup cosine-decay scheduling, gradient accumulation, and mixed-precision arithmetic. On a held-out validation split, NepLEGiT attains a cross-entropy loss of 0.5684, a perplexity of 1.8, and a next-token prediction accuracy of 82.9%. We further evaluate continual masked-language-model pre-training of mBERT and MuRIL on the same corpus; mBERT achieves a perplexity of 2.35 (eval loss 0.8565), outperforming MuRIL (perplexity 6.07, eval loss 1.8026), providing a strong encoder baseline complementary to NepLEGiT's generative orientation.

</details>


### [106] [ViCo: Visual-oriented Coding with Self-Reflection for Chart Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16014)
*Jiaxin Duan,Dian Jiao Shuai Zhao,Jiabing Leng,Yiran Zhang,Feng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ViCo，一个面向视觉编码的训练框架，通过迭代反思机制逐步将生成的图表与参考对齐。它采用自监督预热阶段（结合基于一致性剪枝的蒙特卡洛树搜索）合成高质量反思轨迹，并开发多步强化学习算法，利用反事实基线估计优势来缓解奖励稀疏问题。同时提出基于分层异构布局图结构的自动化多维评估框架，用于度量图表的风格、布局和语义一致性。在三个公开基准上的实验表明，仅训练于8B模型上的ViCo，其性能已接近具备充分反思能力的专有大型语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体虽能生成结构良好的文本和代码，但其生成的学术图表在风格和语义保真度上远不及人类手绘。即便采用自反思机制的先进编码智能体，也存在视觉推理能力弱、反思遵循受限的问题，导致奖励信号稀疏，严重制约其强化学习效果。

Method: ViCo框架包含两大核心组件：(1) 自监督预热阶段——将蒙特卡洛树搜索与基于一致性的剪枝相结合，合成高质量反思轨迹，确保每个编码步骤严格遵循先前反思的结果；(2) 多步强化学习算法——采用反事实基线估计每次精修循环中反思与动作步骤的优势，从而解决奖励稀疏问题。此外，提出一个自动化的多维评估框架，通过分层异构布局图结构来评估图表的风格、布局和语义一致性，以支撑大规模训练中的高效奖励计算。

Result: 在三个公开基准上的实验表明，ViCo在仅训练8B规模模型的情况下，其性能已接近具备充分反思能力的专有大型语言模型。

Conclusion: ViCo通过将结构化的反思轨迹合成与多步反事实强化学习相结合，有效解决了视觉编码任务中的奖励稀疏问题，证明了在较小模型上也能获得接近专有大模型的图表生成能力，为高质量学术图表自动生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of generating high-quality academic charts that match the visual standards of human-authored papers. While existing AI agents can produce well-structured text and code, their generated visualizations often lack the stylistic and semantic fidelity of human designs. Advanced coding agents that employ self-reflection mechanisms exhibit poor visual reasoning and limited reflection following, resulting in sparse reward signals that severely undermine their reinforcement learning (RL). We propose ViCo, a training framework for visual-oriented coding that employs iterative reflections to align generated chart images progressively with the reference. We first introduce a self-supervised warm-up stage, which augments Monte Carlo Tree Search with consistency-based pruning to synthesize high-quality reflection trajectories, ensuring that each coding step strictly follows the outcomes of prior reflections. A multi-step RL algorithm is then developed, using counterfactual baselines to estimate advantage for reflection and action steps within each refinement cycle, thereby addressing the reward sparsity. To enable efficient reward in massive training, we propose an automatic, multifaceted evaluation framework that assesses charts' style, layout, and semantic consistency via a hierarchical heterogeneous layout graph structure. Experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate that ViCo, trained on an 8B model, achieves performance close to proprietary LLMs with adequate reflection capabilities.

</details>


### [107] [Are We Grading Properly? Understanding Failure Modes in Medical Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16023)
*Prithvi Dixit,Pedram Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了医学评估中基于量规(rubric)的自动评分系统在量规本身存在缺陷时的问题。作者应用全局量规失败分类法RIFT到两个临床基准(HealthBench Professional和LiveMedBench)，发现LLM评判员标注29.6%的标准为非原子性、65.4%为错位/僵化。通过将捆绑式标准(如"至少一个/全部以下")重写为等权子项并重新评分，同一回答的分数可移动高达15.9个百分点，且析取捆绑抬高分数、合取捆绑压低分数。RIFT在临床量规上对捆绑检测不足，仅标注3.3%的非原子性，而表面形式分析发现25.8%存在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学评估正从静态选项式提问转向开放式的真实临床场景，但大规模评分昂贵，量规式评估成为主流可扩展方案。然而量规本身并非完美，本研究旨在探究量规缺陷是否可被检测和纠正，验证这些缺陷是实质性而非表面性的。

Method: 应用RIFT全局量规失败分类法对HealthBench Professional和LiveMedBench两个临床基准进行量规审计，使用LLM评判员标识非原子性和错位/僵化等失败模式；随后通过重写捆绑式标准为等权子项并对相同回答重新评分来量化这些缺陷的影响；同时对比RIFT检测与表面形式分析在捆绑结构检测上的差异。

Result: 在HealthBench Professional上，LLM评判员标注29.6%的非原子性和65.4%的错位/僵化标准；重写捆绑式标准后，受影响对话的分数变化高达15.9个百分点，析取(至少一个)捆绑抬高分数而合取(全部)捆绑压低分数；RIFT对捆绑检测存在明显欠检测，在LiveMedBench上仅标注3.3%非原子性，而表面形式分析发现25.8%存在捆绑结构。

Conclusion: 量规缺陷在临床评估中是实质性且普遍的，会对自动评分结果产生显著且系统性的偏差，而现有RIFT等检测方法严重低估了这些缺陷的普遍性。这提示需要更可靠、更灵敏的量规审计方法，以提升基于量规评估的公平性和准确性。

Abstract: Medical evaluation is shifting from static option-based questioning to realistic clinical scenarios with open-ended output modes. Grading these at scale naively, however, is expensive, and rubric-based evaluation has become the dominant scalable alternative. We ask what happens when the rubrics themselves are not airtight, and whether such flaws can be detected and corrected. We apply RIFT, a global rubric failure taxonomy, to two clinical benchmarks (HealthBench Professional and LiveMedBench), and find failure modes are meaningful: on HealthBench Professional an LLM judge flags 29.6% of criteria as non-atomic and 65.4% as misaligned/rigid. Then, we show that these flaws are meaningful and not simply cosmetic. As an example, rewriting bundled criteria of the form "at least one of / all of the following" as equally weighted children and regrading identical responses shifts scores by up to 15.9 percentage points on affected conversations, with disjunctive bundles inflating scores and conjunctive bundles deflating them. We also find that RIFT generally under-detects bundling on clinical rubrics, flagging 3.3% of LiveMedBench criteria as non-atomic where surface-form analysis finds structure in 25.8%.

</details>


### [108] [Retrieval-Driven Memory Reconsolidation for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16053)
*Yuanyi Song,Yukai Wang,Xinbei Ma,Zhihui Fu,Jianghao Lin,Weiwen Liu,Jun Wang,Huarong Deng,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REALM是一个受认知神经科学中记忆再巩固（reconsolidation）机制启发的智能体长时记忆框架，它将长时记忆建模为持续的生命周期：自主将记忆组织成异构认知图，通过自适应组合的图搜索原子检索证据，并基于检索反馈持续再巩固记忆，从而在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上分别达到75.97%和65.11%的平均准确率，超越最强基线7.17和1.31个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆系统存在两个核心缺陷：一是仅在信息到达时更新记忆，将检索视为记忆访问的终点而非驱动记忆演化的引擎，导致检索反馈很少被用于持续重组记忆以优化未来访问；二是大多依赖预定义记忆结构和固定检索流水线，限制了智能体自主组织和演化自身记忆的能力。受认知神经科学中记忆再巩固概念启发，需要一种让检索反馈驱动记忆持续演化的框架。

Method: REALM将长时记忆建模为一个持续的生命周期，包含三部分：1）自主将记忆组织成语义相关的异构认知图（heterogeneous cognitive graph）；2）通过自适应组合的图搜索原子（graph-search atoms）进行证据检索，使检索路径可根据任务动态调整；3）基于检索反馈对记忆进行持续再巩固（continual reconsolidation），使成功检索的记忆单元被重组强化，从而不断优化记忆结构。

Result: REALM在LoCoMo上取得75.97%的平均准确率，在LongMemEval上取得65.11%，分别超越最强基线7.17和1.31个百分点。消融研究证实记忆再巩固持续提升性能；进一步分析显示，再巩固机制会逐步将相关记忆单元重组为更连贯的局部结构，以便在推理过程中进行集体的证据回忆和利用。

Conclusion: 检索驱动的记忆再巩固为LLM智能体的长时记忆持续演化提供了一种有效机制。将检索的反馈循环融入记忆组织的生命周期，而非将检索视为终点，能够提升长对话场景下的记忆访问与推理能力，为下一代自主记忆系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM-based agents operating over extended interactions. Existing memory systems primarily update memory when new information arrives, treating retrieval as the endpoint of memory access rather than a driver of memory evolution. Consequently, retrieval feedback is rarely exploited to reorganize memory for future access continuously. Moreover, most existing approaches rely on predefined memory structures together with fixed retrieval pipelines, limiting the agent's ability to organize and evolve its own memory autonomously. Inspired by memory reconsolidation in cognitive neuroscience, we propose \textbf{REALM}, a \textbf{r}econsolidation-\textbf{e}volution \textbf{a}gentic \textbf{l}ong-term \textbf{m}emory framework. It models long-term memory as a continual lifecycle by autonomously organizing memories into a heterogeneous cognitive graph, retrieving evidence via adaptively composed graph-search atoms, and continually reconsolidating memories based on retrieval feedback. REALM achieves an average accuracy of 75.97\% on LoCoMo and 65.11\% on LongMemEval, outperforming the strongest baselines by 7.17 and 1.31 points respectively. Ablation studies confirm that memory reconsolidation consistently boosts performance, with further analyses revealing that it progressively reorganizes related memory units into more coherent local structures for collective evidence recall and utilization during reasoning. These results suggest that retrieval-driven memory reconsolidation provides an effective mechanism for continually evolving long-term memory in LLM agents.

</details>


### [109] [State of Thought Enables Endogenous Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16055)
*Zhiren Gong,Yikun Hou,Zihao Zeng,Ming Xiao,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出State of Thought（SoT）推理范式，利用模型内部动态几何状态驱动推理，用582参数控制器在冻结骨干上选择性激活历史证据，显著提升准确率并降低生成token和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时推理范式依赖外部强制控制（固定推理程序或受限搜索空间扩展），限制泛化性和效率，需要更内生的、由模型自身状态驱动的推理方式。

Method: 从模型内部信息传递中提取紧凑的动力学几何状态，用582参数控制器基于当前状态选择性激活历史推理支持，将推理建模为状态条件下的证据过程而非外部规定的token链。

Result: 在3个LLM和16个数据集上，相比均值基线，定量推理提升1.34x、通用1.62x、符号代码1.76x、长上下文2.51x，同时减少62.6%生成token和44.6%端到端延迟；在2个VLM规模和3个推理任务上平均准确率提升3.8点，比搜索方法减少74.9%补全token和73.5%延迟；受限访问下保留38.2%/36.5%训练-free/embedding-only增益，轨迹判断一致性达84.1%。

Conclusion: 内生的状态驱动推理为LLM测试时计算提供了一种兼具泛化性和效率的新范式，验证了模型内部状态作为推理控制信号的有效性。

Abstract: Test-time compute has emerged as a major approach to improving the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing test-time reasoning paradigms rely heavily on externally imposed control, either through fixed reasoning programs or through costly expansion in constrained search spaces, limiting both generalization and efficiency. We propose State of Thought (SoT), a new reasoning paradigm that enables endogenous reasoning in LLMs, with the model's internal reasoning state governing how reasoning unfolds. Concretely, SoT extracts a compact dynamics-geometric state from the model's internal information transfer and uses a 582-parameter controller on frozen backbones to selectively activate historical reasoning support useful under the current reasoning state, framing reasoning as a state-conditioned process over evidence rather than an externally prescribed token chain. Across quantitative (1.34x), general (1.62x), symbolic-and-code (1.76x), and long-context (2.51x) reasoning on 3 LLMs and 16 datasets, SoT consistently improves mean-baseline accuracy while reducing generated tokens by 62.6% and end-to-end latency by 44.6%. Across 2 VLM scales and 3 reasoning tasks, it improves mean accuracy by 3.8 points over reasoning baselines, with 74.9% fewer completion tokens and 73.5% lower latency than search-based methods. Under constrained access, SoT retains 38.2%/36.5% mean accuracy gains in training-free/embedding-only settings, while trajectory-only judging reaches 84.1% agreement across 3 API models. Together, endogenous state-driven reasoning provides a generalizable and efficient alternative.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Scalable RLVR: Multimodal Instruction Following Data Synthesis and Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16059)
*Yirong Zeng,Zhang Sai,Yuxian Wang,Yutai Hou,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Bibo Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MIFS流水线，通过生成约束协议合成多样化的RL-ready多模态数据，结合可学习性感知的蒸馏机制和基于代码的验证器，构建了包含90k样本、覆盖8种约束类别和14个任务领域的数据集。实验表明MIFS训练的MLLM在四个MMIF基准上平均提升8.13%，训练收敛速度提升3倍，同时缓解了SFT的泛化权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态指令跟随（MMIF）对构建通用智能体至关重要，但当前训练范式过度依赖监督微调（SFT），导致表面模式匹配和通用能力退化。虽然基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）提供了有前景的替代方案，但其可扩展性受限于高质量、RL-ready多模态数据的稀缺。

Method: （1）引入生成约束协议以合成多样的原始样本；（2）设计可学习性感知的蒸馏机制，基于RL训练动态过滤数据以保证策略优化的稳定性；（3）采用基于代码的验证器提供高精度奖励信号。（4）构建包含90k样本、8种约束类别和14个任务领域的数据集。

Result: MIFS训练的MLLM在四个MMIF基准上平均提升8.13%，与使用原始数据相比训练收敛速度提升3倍；该方法缓解了SFT典型的泛化权衡问题，在保留核心视觉能力的同时显著提升指令跟随精度。

Conclusion: MIFS通过系统化的数据合成与筛选流水线有效弥合了MMIF中RL-ready数据稀缺的瓶颈，验证了RLVR在MMIF中的可扩展性，并证明了其在缓解SFT泛化权衡方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal instruction following (MMIF) is crucial for building generalist agents. However, current training paradigms rely heavily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), which often leads to surface-level pattern matching and degrades general capabilities. While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) offers a promising alternative, its scalability in MMIF is severely bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality, RL-ready multimodal data. To bridge this gap, we present MIFS (\textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{I}nstruction \textbf{F}ollowing \textbf{S}ynthesis), a systematic pipeline designed to generate RL-ready multimodal data. Specifically, MIFS introduces a generative constraint protocol to synthesize diverse raw samples, followed by a learnability-aware distillation mechanism that filters data based on RL training dynamics to ensure stable policy optimization. Furthermore, a code-based verifier provides high-precision reward signals for policy learning. The resulting dataset comprises 90k samples across 8 constraint categories and 14 task domains. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MIFS-trained MLLMs achieve an average improvement of 8.13\% on four MMIF benchmarks and a 3$\times$ faster training convergence compared to using raw data. Crucially, our approach mitigates the generalization trade-offs typical of SFT, preserving core visual capabilities while significantly boosting instruction-following precision.

</details>


### [111] [Efficient Multimodal Generative Recommendation with Latent Narrative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16070)
*Chenxing Wang,Nantao Zheng,Hao Miao,Juyuan Wang,Xinke Jiang,Yuchen Fang,Aolin Li,Haijun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 NarraLite，一个面向情节化内容续写的高效多模态生成式推荐框架。它通过渐进式谱压缩（Progressive Spectral Compression）蒸馏冗长视觉上下文为紧凑的叙事相关证据，并利用潜在叙事推理（Latent Narrative Reasoning）引入上下文路由的潜在推理标记，以隐含方式完成叙事推断而无需逐字解码文本推理过程。实验表明其在不牺牲效率的前提下显著提升续写准确率、叙事连贯性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式推荐将物品预测重构为语义标识符生成，但情节化内容（episodic content）构成截然不同的设定——其目标由叙事演进（narrative evolution）而非用户偏好决定。该任务要求模型理解多模态情节发展，同时应对冗余视觉上下文与高成本显式推理生成带来的效率挑战。

Method: NarraLite 联合压缩感知与推理：(1) 渐进式谱压缩（Progressive Spectral Compression）——选择性将长视觉上下文蒸馏为紧凑的叙事相关证据，保留过渡关键信息并减少冗余视觉计算；(2) 潜在叙事推理（Latent Narrative Reasoning）——引入上下文路由的潜在推理标记，将其上下文表征与未来续写语义对齐，实现无需自回归解码文本理据的隐含叙事推断。作者还构建了一个用户无关的多模态基准，覆盖 UGC、PGC 与 OOD 场景的短剧续写任务。

Result: 大量实验表明，NarraLite 相较于现有方法在续写准确率、叙事连贯性和鲁棒性上均有持续提升，并实现了良好的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: NarraLite 验证了"感知与推理联合压缩"在情节化续写推荐中的有效性：既能高效处理冗余视觉信息，又能以潜在推理替代昂贵的显式推理生成，为多模态生成式推荐在叙事驱动场景下的高效落地提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Generative recommendation reformulates item prediction as semantic identifier generation, yet episodic content introduces a fundamentally different setting where the target is determined by narrative evolution rather than user preference. This task requires models to understand multimodal storyline progression while addressing the efficiency challenges caused by redundant visual contexts and costly explicit reasoning generation. We propose \textbf{NarraLite}, an efficient multimodal generative recommendation framework that jointly compresses perception and reasoning. Specifically, Progressive Spectral Compression selectively distills long visual contexts into compact narrative-relevant evidence, preserving transition-critical information while reducing redundant visual computation. Latent Narrative Reasoning introduces context-routed latent reasoning tokens and aligns their contextualized representations with future continuation semantics, enabling implicit narrative inference without autoregressively decoding textual rationales. We further establish a user-agnostic multimodal benchmark for short-form drama continuation across UGC, PGC, and OOD settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NarraLite consistently improves continuation accuracy, narrative coherence, and robustness over existing approaches, while achieving a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [112] [The Immutable Past: Formalizing State Mutability and Conflict Resolution in Mutable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16073)
*Hamed HaddadPajouh,Amir AmiriTabat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RAG系统在长期自主智能体记忆架构中的关键缺陷——"语义遮蔽"（Semantic Shadowing），即共享记忆作为追加式仅增流时，冲突的历史观察会累积并在统计上主导有效的近期更新，导致状态发散。作者形式化证明了标准稠密检索存在渐近回忆衰减（Asymptotic Recall Decay）与"多数票陷阱"（Majority Vote Trap），并提出了严格推理期一致性协议GC-Mem（基于时间支配算子Φ_T与矛盾检测的垃圾回收机制），在137,760个记忆块基准上恢复了>90%的冲突解决准确率，同时确立精确率/召回率部署阈值以确保状态收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中，RAG作为长期自主智能体的核心记忆架构，其追加式共享记忆会累积冲突的历史观测，使智能体检索并基于过时事实行动，造成严重的状态发散。现有启发式时间衰减机制会不分青红皂白地摧毁有效的长期记忆，因此需要一种既能消除陈旧语境又不破坏合法长期记忆的一致性机制。

Method: 作者形式化State Mutability（状态可变性）机制，证明标准稠密检索受渐近回忆衰减影响；通过"多数票陷阱"揭示增大检索上下文窗口会通过稀释注意力机制反而降低生成准确率。随后提出GC-Mem推理期协议：采用时间支配算子（Φ_T）配合矛盾检测，纯推断时运行，对遮蔽语境进行外科手术式剔除，区别于盲目的时间衰减启发式。

Result: 在基于行为推断的严格基准（137,760个记忆块及连续积累扫描）上，标准RAG和时间戳重排序基线遭受严重退化，而GC-Mem实证恢复超过90%的冲突解决准确率，并建立了严格的精确率/召回率部署阈值，实现标准可变RAG根本失败处的状态收敛。

Conclusion: GC-Mem提供了一种纯推理期的严格一致性协议，能在动态环境中消除语义遮蔽并确保状态收敛，为长期自主智能体记忆架构提供了优于标准RAG和启发式时间衰减的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) serves as the primary memory architecture for long-horizon autonomous agents. However, treating shared memory as an append-only stream introduces \textit{Semantic Shadowing}, a critical failure mode where conflicting historical observations accumulate and statistically dominate valid recent updates. In dynamic environments, this results in severe state divergence as agents retrieve and act upon obsolete facts. This paper formalizes the mechanics of State Mutability to prove that standard dense retrieval suffers from Asymptotic Recall Decay. Furthermore, we formally demonstrate a Majority Vote Trap, revealing that increasing the retrieval context window paradoxically degrades generation accuracy by diluting the attention mechanism under conditions of semantic equivalence. To resolve this, we introduce GC-Mem (Garbage Collection for Memory), a strict inference-time consistency protocol. Unlike heuristic time-decay mechanisms---which indiscriminately destroy valid long-term memory---GC-Mem relies purely on a temporal dominance operator ($Φ_{\mathcal{T}}$) paired with contradiction detection to surgically excise shadowed context. Evaluated across a rigorous, behaviorally inferred benchmark of 137,760 memory chunks and continuous accumulation sweeps, standard RAG and timestamp re-ranking baselines experience severe degradation. In contrast, GC-Mem empirically recovers $>90\%$ conflict resolution accuracy. We establish strict precision and recall deployment thresholds, ensuring state convergence where standard mutable RAG fundamentally fails.

</details>


### [113] [The Imitation Game: When LLMs Learn to Reason Like Programs via Code-Centric Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16076)
*Jinyang Zhang,Weibin Liao,Keqin Bao,Sihang Li,Shaobo Wang,Muyang Ye,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Tianyi Tang,Fei Huang,Kexin Yang,Xingzhang Ren,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIMIC 框架，将可执行代码作为推理数据合成的严谨媒介，通过叙事融合、代码引导测试合成和动态代码插桩，把算法转化为可验证的推理轨迹，并利用显式中间执行状态构建 Code-Instrumented Reward (CIR) 作为无需外部奖励模型的密集高质量过程监督信号，用于强化学习。实验表明，基于该合成数据集训练的模型（SFT 与 GRPO）在一般推理、复杂数学基准和细粒度确定性任务上均取得显著且一致的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在编程任务上表现优异，但在自然语言中的确定性、细粒度推理上经常失败——过度依赖语义近似而缺乏稳健的符号执行。现有方法难以提供严谨的过程监督信号，因此需要一个可验证、确定性的推理数据合成与训练机制。

Method: MIMIC 框架通过三条路径工作：(1) 叙事融合（narrative fusion）将算法转化为可验证的推理轨迹；(2) 代码引导测试合成（code-guided test synthesis）；(3) 动态代码插桩（dynamic code instrumentation）。这些显式中间执行状态自然构成 Code-Instrumented Reward (CIR)，为强化学习提供密集、高保真的过程监督，全程无需外部奖励模型。

Result: 大量评估显示，在合成数据集上通过 SFT 和 GRPO 训练的模型获得显著且一致的性能提升；方法在一般推理、复杂数学基准以及细粒度确定性任务上均大幅提升准确率。

Conclusion: 可执行代码的程序性严谨性能够有效解锁并增强大语言模型的泛化推理能力，为无外部奖励模型的高质量过程监督训练提供了可行方案。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at programming tasks but frequently fail at deterministic, fine-grained reasoning in natural language, relying heavily on semantic approximations rather than robust symbolic execution. To bridge this gap, we propose MIMIC, a framework that leverages executable code as a rigorous medium for reasoning data synthesis. MIMIC fundamentally transforms algorithms into verifiable reasoning trajectories through narrative fusion, code-guided test synthesis, and dynamic code instrumentation. Crucially, these explicit intermediate execution states naturally form a Code-Instrumented Reward (CIR), providing dense, high-fidelity process supervision for reinforcement learning without external reward models. Extensive evaluations reveal that models trained via SFT and GRPO on our synthesized dataset achieve substantial, consistent gains. Our method significantly elevates accuracy across general reasoning, complex mathematical benchmarks, and fine-grained deterministic tasks, demonstrating that the procedural rigor of executable code can effectively unlock and enhance the generalized reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/zjy1298/MIMIC.

</details>


### [114] [RAG-CT: Mitigating Privacy Risks on Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Scanning Prompt Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16095)
*Xingyu Lyu,Jiayimei Wang,Jianfeng He,Ning Wang,Yidan Hu,Yimin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG-CT，一种针对检索增强生成系统隐私泄露的轻量级防御方法。通过分析查询的熵和边际分布，利用基于评分的检测方法识别恶意查询，在不修改底层LLM或检索器的情况下显著降低个人身份信息（PII）泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过外部知识增强LLM生成质量、减少幻觉，但研究揭示攻击者可利用检索过程提取底层语料库中的个人身份信息（PII），形成严重隐私漏洞。现有防御手段不足，亟需一种无需修改底层模型的有效防护机制。

Method: 提出RAG-CT防御框架，核心思路是分析查询的熵（entropy）和边际（margin）分布特征，采用基于评分的检测方法（score-based detection）来识别恶意查询，从而在检索前拦截攻击请求。

Result: 在四个SOTA攻击策略和四个防御基线上，基于两个数据集的大量实验表明，RAG-CT能显著降低PII泄露，且性能优于现有防御方法。

Conclusion: RAG-CT提供了一种轻量且有效的机制，可在不修改底层LLM或检索器的前提下保护RAG系统免受PII泄露，兼具实用性与部署友好性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving the quality of generated contents of Large Language Models (LLMs) by grounding responses in external knowledge, thus reducing hallucinations and factual errors. However, recent studies have highlighted a critical vulnerability: adversaries can exploit the retrieval process to extract personally identifiable information (PII) from the underlying corpus. To mitigate this risk, we propose a novel defense, RAG-CT, that identifies malicious queries by analyzing their entropy and margin distributions and using a score-based detection method. Extensive experiments with four state-of-the-art attack strategies and four defense baselines on two datasets show that our approach significantly reduces PII leakage while outperforming existing defenses. This work provides a lightweight yet effective mechanism to protect RAG systems against PII leakage without requiring modifications to the underlying LLM or retriever.

</details>


### [115] [Spurious Tool Use: When RL Agents Learn the Wrong Reason to Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16268)
*Yiwei Yang,Haoxiang Zhang,Bingbing Wen,Yao Lu,Yuchen Wu,Lei Zhang,Julian McAuley,Pan Lu,Bill Howe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习训练的LLM智能体为何及何时学会“捷径”工具选择策略——即依据表面提示线索而非真实任务需求调用工具。作者构建受控合成环境，注入与特定工具强相关但对工具必要性因果无关的线索，在反事实评估中发现虚假工具调用率最高增加39%。捷径形成并非普遍存在，仅在智能体已能可靠使用目标工具时才出现，表明任务能力比数据不均衡更关键。语义对齐会显著放大该效应。为此提出基于LLM评委评估各工具调用必要性的密集决策级奖励，可有效抑制线索驱动的工具使用并保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体将自然语言推理与外部工具结合使用，其工具使用策略常通过强化学习优化，但RL可能放大训练数据中的虚假相关性，导致智能体学习捷径式的工具选择策略。作者旨在系统研究此类捷径何时形成、为何形成，以深入理解这一鲁棒性缺陷。

Method: 构建结合事实问答与数学推理任务的受控合成环境，注入与特定工具强相关但因果上无关的线索；设计反事实评估（线索存在但工具非必需）量化捷径行为；执行换线索（swapped-cue）分析探究线索-工具语义对齐的影响；提出密集决策级奖励，由LLM评委评估每次工具调用的必要性。

Result: 反事实评估中智能体表现出显著捷径行为，虚假工具调用率最高增加39%；捷径仅在智能体已可靠学会目标工具时形成，证明任务能力是关键因素；换线索分析表明线索与工具间语义对齐会大幅放大捷径效应；所提出的工具必要性奖励有效抑制线索驱动工具使用，同时保持任务性能。

Conclusion: RL训练的智能体确实会形成捷径工具选择策略，但其形成受任务能力与线索-工具语义对齐调节，而非单纯由数据不均衡驱动。基于LLM评委的决策级工具必要性奖励是提升LLM智能体工具使用策略鲁棒性的实用可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly interleave natural language reasoning with external tools such as web search and code execution. These tool-use policies are often optimized via reinforcement learning (RL), which can amplify spurious correlations in the training data. In this work, we study when and why RL-trained agents learn shortcut tool-selection policies: invoking tools based on superficial prompt cues rather than genuine task requirements. We construct controlled synthetic environments combining factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks, and inject cues that are strongly correlated with specific tools during training but causally irrelevant to tool necessity. Across counterfactual evaluations where cues are present but the associated tools are not required, agents exhibit substantial shortcut behavior, with spurious tool invocation rates increasing by up to 39 percent. However, shortcut formation is not universal: across the conditions we test, it arises only when the agent has already learned to use the target tool reliably, suggesting that task competence, rather than dataset imbalance alone, is a key factor in shortcut learning. A swapped-cue analysis further shows that semantic alignment between cues and tools substantially amplifies this effect. To mitigate these failures, we introduce a dense, decision-level reward in which an LLM judge evaluates the necessity of each tool call. This tool-necessity reward effectively suppresses cue-driven tool use while preserving task performance, providing a practical approach to improving the robustness of LLM agent tool-use policies.

</details>


### [116] [Speaker-Specific and Language-Dependent Temporal Organization in Bilingual Political Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16274)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Nafiseh Taghva,Peter Gilles,Oliver Niebuhr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究调查了政治人物在使用卢森堡语和法语进行演讲时的语音节奏组织。通过对十位政治家的400个句子（分段与停顿已标注）进行分析，采用多种节律指标（均值、变异性、对偶变异性指数），并运用配对t检验检验说话者内语言效应。结果显示：基于辅音的节律指标保留说话者特有的签名，而基于元音的指标主要由语言选择驱动。法语样本展现出更长且更多变的元音与元音间段，辅音时间差异较小，未见稳健的语言×性别交互作用。这表明语言选择系统地重新组织了双语公开演讲中的节律时序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音节奏有助于组织说服性演讲的结构，但现有实证研究大多聚焦于单语英语。本文旨在探讨政治人物在使用卢森堡语和法语这两种语言时如何组织演讲的时序安排，填补双语语境下节奏研究的空白。

Method: 从十位政治人物处采集400个句子，对语音段与停顿进行标注。计算包括均值、变异性以及辅音、元音的对偶变异性指数在内的节奏指标。量化说话者与语言各自的贡献，并通过配对t检验检验说话者内部的语言效应，同时考察语言与性别的交互作用。

Result: 辅音类节律指标保留了个体说话者特有的特征，而元音类指标主要由语言选择所驱动。法语样本呈现更长且更多变的元音及元音间隔，辅音时序差异较小。未发现稳健的语言与性别交互作用。

Conclusion: 语言选择会系统性地重新组织双语公开演讲中的节律时序，表明节奏不仅反映说话者身份，还受语言系统的结构性制约。

Abstract: Speech rhythm helps structure persuasive speech, but most empirical work examines monolingual English. This study asks how politicians organize timing when speaking Luxembourgish and French. We analyze 400 sentences from ten politicians, annotated for segments and pauses. We compute rhythm metrics, including means, variability, and pairwise variability indices for consonants and vowels. We quantify speaker and language contributions and test within-speaker language effects with paired t-tests. Results show that consonant-based metrics retain speaker-specific signatures, whereas vowel-based metrics are largely driven by language choice. French tokens display longer and more variable vowels and vocalic intervals, while consonant timing differences are smaller. No robust language by gender interactions emerge. These findings show that language choice systematically reorganizes rhythmic timing in bilingual public speech.

</details>


### [117] [Speaker or Language? Explaining Variance in Charismatic Prosody Across Luxembourgish and French](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16275)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Nafiseh Taghva,Peter Gilles,Oliver Niebuhr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了10位政客在卢森堡语和法语两种语言下即兴演讲的语音魅力。通过对400个话语提取41个声学韵律特征并拟合混合效应模型，发现说话者身份解释了大部分方差，语言解释较少但存在系统性差异：法语呈现更高抖动值和短语末尾基频（表示礼貌、尊重的嗓音），卢森堡语则表现出更强的中频谱能量（表示更具存在感的嗓音），这与两种语言的社会语言学角色一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语言和说话风格都会塑造有魅力的演讲，但在双语公开演讲中，两者对语音魅力的相对贡献尚不清楚。该研究旨在通过高度可比的跨语言交际情境，分离说话者与语言对语音魅力的影响。

Method: 选取10位在卢森堡语和法语两种语言下面对高度可比交际情境的政客，收集其自发演讲共400个话语。提取41个与嗓音魅力相关的声学韵律特征，拟合混合效应模型，用以分离说话者相关方差与语言相关方差。

Result: 说话者身份解释了大部分方差，语言解释较少但仍呈现系统性差异：法语产出表现出更高的抖动值和短语末尾基频，象征礼貌、尊重的嗓音；卢森堡语产出表现出更强的中频谱能量，暗示更具嗓音存在感的形象。这些模式与卢森堡语作为非正式身份语言、法语作为高威望制度性语言的社会语言学角色相吻合。

Conclusion: 在双语公开演讲中，说话者本人的风格对语音魅力的贡献大于语言因素，但语言选择仍会系统性地影响特定声学特征。这些差异反映了两种语言各自承载的社会语言学功能，说明语音魅力是个人特质与语言社会角色共同作用的结果。

Abstract: Charismatic speech is shaped by language and speaking style, yet their relative contribution in bilingual public speaking remains unclear. We analyzed spontaneous speeches of 10 politicians who address audiences in Luxembourgish and French, in highly comparable communicative contexts across languages. From 400 utterances, we extracted 41 acoustic-prosodic features linked to vocal charisma and fitted mixed-effects models to separate speaker- and language-related variance. Speaker identity accounted for most variance, whereas language explained less, but still showed systematic differences: French productions showed higher shimmer and phrase-final F0, indicative of a polite, respectful voice, while Luxembourgish productions exhibited stronger mid-frequency spectral energy, suggesting a more vocally present profile. These patterns align with the sociolinguistic roles of Luxembourgish as an informal identity language and French as a high-prestige institutional variety.

</details>


### [118] [Efficient One-to-Many Translation with Joint Multi-Stream Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16312)
*Yiwen Guan,Jacob Whitehill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离散扩散框架的多语种机器翻译方法，可并行优化所有目标语言，实现目标语言数量的亚线性延迟缩放，并以单一统一模型替代多个独立系统。通过连续语义锚点而非源token作为条件，该框架支持零样本迁移到未见过的源语言且无需重新训练，在零样本源上保持约75%的有监督翻译质量。实验表明，在加速采样下，其有监督质量与自回归基线相当，速度快2倍，零样本BLEU提升11.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一对多机器翻译在自回归系统中计算开销高昂，因为延迟随序列长度和目标语言数量线性增长。作者旨在探索扩散模型如何实现多语种翻译，以克服自回归系统的线性延迟缩放瓶颈，并支持以单一统一模型替代多个独立系统，追求更低延迟和更好的零样本泛化能力。

Method: 采用离散扩散框架，并行地精炼所有目标语言，实现目标数量上的亚线性延迟缩放。关键技术是使用连续语义锚点（而非源token）作为条件信号，从而支持对未见源语言的零样本迁移。通过加速采样方法研究质量-延迟前沿（quality-latency frontier）的权衡。

Result: 零样本迁移保持约75%的有监督翻译质量；在加速采样下，有监督质量与自回归基线相当，获得2倍速度提升；零样本BLEU比基线提升11.9%。

Conclusion: 联合多流扩散（joint multi-stream diffusion）作为一对多高效翻译的实用且灵活替代方案具有重要潜力，它能同时兼顾高效延迟、统一模型部署和零样本源语言泛化。

Abstract: One-to-many machine translation (MT) is computationally expensive for autoregressive (AR) systems, which suffer from linear latency scaling with both sequence length and the number of target languages. We explore how diffusion can enable multilingual translation with a discrete diffusion framework that refines all target languages in parallel, achieving sublinear latency scaling with the number of targets, and supports deployment as a single unified model to replace multiple independent systems. Conditioned on a continuous semantic anchor rather than source tokens, our framework supports zero-shot transfer to unseen source languages without retraining, maintaining approximately $75\%$ of its supervised translation quality on zero-shot sources. We investigate the quality-latency frontier and find that with accelerated sampling, it achieves comparable supervised quality to AR baselines with a $2 \times$ speedup and $11.9\%$ better zero-shot BLEU. These results highlight the potential of joint multi-stream diffusion as a practical and flexible alternative for efficient one-to-many translation.

</details>


### [119] [StalePO: Anchored Token-Level Preference Optimization using Legacy Post-Edits in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16340)
*Rohit Dhaipule,Sukhdeep Singh Kharbanda,Prasanth Bathala,Pradyumna Lanka,Anubhav Shrimal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"陈旧偏好问题"(Stale Preference)：机器翻译系统升级后，可用的偏好信号仍是旧系统输出的人工后编辑，而新模型可能已超越这些编辑结果。作者提出StalePO目标函数，基于三个联合必要的要求——两个响应的似然都向下移动、策略锚定于自身基准响应、KL约束在token级别——将陈旧反馈转化为性能提升。在英译印地语和英译土耳其语本地化数据上，StalePO将全部通过LLM评判MQM质量检查的片段比例分别提升14.9和4.6个百分点，收益集中在风格与流畅度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译系统会周期性升级为更强的模型，但可用的偏好信号是人类对旧系统输出的后编辑，新模型可能已经超越这些编辑（即"陈旧偏好"问题）。而针对每个新模型重新收集人工后编辑成本过高。在此情境下，标准DPO会失效：它可能提升劣质后编辑的似然、侵蚀模型已有质量，并且缺乏修正局部错误所需的逐token控制能力。因此需要一个专门针对该训练范式的偏好优化目标。

Method: 提出StalePO目标函数，由该范式的三个要求推导而来：(1) 两个响应（后编辑与模型自身输出）的似然移动都必须向下；(2) 策略必须锚定到自身的基准响应；(3) KL约束必须在token级别施加。这三个要求在消融实验中被证明是联合必要的——单独使用任一机制时模型性能与基础模型无异，只有三者组合才能将陈旧反馈转化为收益。

Result: 在英译印地语和英译土耳其语本地化数据上，StalePO使全部通过LLM评判MQM质量检查的片段比例分别提升14.9和4.6个百分点，收益集中在风格与流畅度方面。在相同框架下进行的人工评估证实了英译印地语上的增益，全部通过七项人工检查的片段比例提升13.8个百分点。

Conclusion: StalePO通过三个联合必要的要求（双向向下移动似然、锚定自身基准响应、token级KL约束）能够有效应对陈旧偏好问题，将过时的后编辑反馈转化为可测量的翻译质量提升（尤其在风格与流畅度上），并同时获得自动LLM评判与人工评估的证实。

Abstract: Machine translation systems are periodically upgraded to stronger models, but the available preference signal is human post-edits of an older system's outputs, which the newer model may already surpass. Moreover, collecting fresh post-edits for every new model is prohibitively expensive. We call this the Stale Preference problem. Standard DPO can fail in this setting: it may increase the likelihood of inferior post-edits, erode the model's existing quality, and fail to provide the per-token control needed to correct localized errors. We introduce StalePO, an objective derived from three requirements this regime imposes. Likelihood movement must be downward on both responses, the policy must be anchored to its own base response, and the KL constraint must apply at the token level. These requirements are jointly necessary. In ablations, each mechanism in isolation leaves the model's performance indistinguishable from the base model, and only their combination converts stale feedback into gains. On English-to-Hindi and English-to-Turkish localization data, StalePO improves the fraction of segments passing all LLM-as-judge MQM quality checks by 14.9 and 4.6 percentage points, respectively, with gains concentrated on style and fluency. A human evaluation under the same framework confirms these gains on English-to-Hindi, raising the fraction of segments passing all seven human checks by 13.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [120] [How Humans and LLMs Read Gender into Gender-Neutral Physical Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16366)
*Yingjia Wan,Lin Lin,Elisa Kreiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次实证证明，看似"客观"的外貌描述在人类解读中仍会携带系统性的性别联想。作者构建了GAPA数据集（316个常见身体属性配14,706条性别联想评分），评估了16个LLM，发现模型部分恢复人类联想但存在系统性对齐偏差（评分分布压缩、对男性联想对齐更弱、对非二元类别的不对称弃权），并发布了最优代理模型用于文学人物描述的社会语言学分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期AI公平性、可及性和伦理研究建议在描述人物时避免使用推断性身份标签（如"she"、"his"），转而采用看似"客观"的身体描述（如"短发"、"清晰的下颌线"）。然而，这种描述性语言是否能真正实现性别中立沟通仍是悬而未决的实证问题。作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 作者构建了GAPA数据集：从多个来源收集316个常见身体属性，并配以来自304名美国标注者的14,706条性别联想评分。随后在模型-人类对齐维度上评估了16个大语言模型（覆盖不同模型家族、规模和post-training变体），最后发布了一个预测人类对描述性语言性别联想的最优代理模型，并用于LitBank中角色描述的社会语言学分析。

Result: 研究发现：（1）身体描述在读者中携带结构化且分级化的性别联想，女性和男性的联想比非二元身份更一致且更具区分性；（2）16个LLM部分恢复了人类联想，但表现出系统性对齐偏差，包括评分分布压缩、对男性联想对齐较弱、以及对非二元类别的不对称弃权；（3）发布了最优代理模型，并展示了其在文学人物描述分析中的应用价值。

Conclusion: 研究提供了首个实证证据，表明看似"客观"的身体描述在人类解读中仍保留系统性性别联想，并揭示了模型-人类错位的系统模式。这挑战了"用身体描述替代性别标签必然实现性别中立沟通"的假设，并突出了在人类-AI交互中利用此类描述传达主观身份类别的下游挑战。

Abstract: When foundation models describe people, recent work in AI fairness, accessibility, and ethics recommends avoiding inferred identity labels (e.g., "she", "his") in favor of seemingly "objective" physical descriptions (e.g., "short hair", "a defined jawline"). Yet whether such descriptive language achieves gender-neutral communication remains an open empirical question. To study this, we introduce GAPA (Gender Associations of Physical Attributes), a dataset of 316 common physical attributes drawn from diverse sources, paired with 14,706 gender-association ratings from 304 US-based annotators. Results show that physical descriptions carry structured and graded gender associations among readers, with more consistent and distinctive associations for women and men than for non-binary identities. Next, we evaluate 16 LLMs across model families, sizes, and post-training variants against human ratings. The models partially recover human associations but exhibit systematic alignment biases, including compressed rating distributions, weaker alignment for associations with men, and asymmetric abstention that disproportionately targets the non-binary category. Finally, we release the best-performing proxy model trained to predict humans' gender associations of descriptive language and demonstrate its utility through a sociolinguistic analysis of character descriptions in LitBank. Together, our findings provide the first empirical evidence that seemingly "objective" physical descriptions can retain systematic gender associations in human interpretation, and uncover systematic patterns of model-human misalignment. This challenges the assumption that replacing explicit gender labels with physical descriptions necessarily yields gender-neutral communication, and highlights downstream challenges in using such descriptions to communicate subjective identity categories in human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [121] [Register Tokens for Bounded-State Reasoning in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16372)
*Albert Ge,Chandan Singh,Yufan Zhuang,Xiaodong Liu,Jianfeng Gao,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在掩码扩散语言模型（dLLM）中使用固定数量的"寄存器令牌"（register tokens）作为跨生成分块的连续状态载体，使得模型在清空先前生成文本后仍能继续推理。在LLaDA和Dream模型上，寄存器方法在所有基准上均优于离散文本携带方法，数学推理提升最高8.5分、代码生成提升最高19.5分，尤其对分块代码生成效果显著，还可结合强化学习进一步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型通过双向注意迭代去噪生成文本，传统上跨分块扩展推理需将先前生成的文本保留在上下文中，导致上下文不断增长、计算成本高。作者探究dLLM能否在清空先前文本后仅依靠固定大小的携带状态继续推理，以实现高效的跨分块长程推理。

Method: 将携带状态实现为少量寄存器令牌——固定的专用位置令牌，其连续隐藏状态被训练用于跨生成分块携带推理进度。对dLLM进行后训练，使其解码一个文本分块后清空该文本、保留寄存器值，并从提示和携带状态继续解码。此外还可在长程推理任务上使用强化学习（RL）对寄存器进行进一步微调。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream两种模型上的所有基准测试中，寄存器方法均优于离散文本携带方法：数学推理最高提升8.5分，代码生成最高提升19.5分。寄存器对有界代码生成尤为有效（正确程序通常跨多个分块）。强化学习可进一步提升长程推理任务的性能。

Conclusion: 寄存器令牌提供了一种高效的固定大小跨分块状态承载机制，使掩码扩散语言模型能够在清空上下文后继续推理，在数学和代码任务上显著优于离散文本携带方案，并可与强化学习相结合以获得更优的长程推理能力。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked tokens with bidirectional attention. Extending reasoning across generation chunks normally requires keeping earlier generated text in context. We ask whether a dLLM can instead continue reasoning after that text is cleared, using only a fixed-size carried state. We implement this state as a small number of register tokens: dedicated fixed-position tokens whose continuous hidden states are trained to carry reasoning progress across generation chunks. We post-train dLLMs to decode a chunk of text, clear it while preserving the register values, and continue decoding from the prompt and carried state. In our main comparisons on LLaDA and Dream, registers outperform discrete-text carry on every benchmark, with gains of up to 8.5 points on math and 19.5 points on code. Registers are especially effective for bounded code generation, where correct programs usually span several chunks. Finally, registers can be further refined with reinforcement learning on long-horizon reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [122] [ParsHate: A Benchmark Dataset for Hate and Target Detection in Persian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16393)
*Zahra Bokaei,Walid Magdy,Bonnie Webber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ParsHate——一个包含10,000条波斯语推文（2013-2022年）的人工标注数据集，这是波斯语仇恨言论检测的首个十年期基准。该数据集包含31%的仇恨内容，支持仇恨检测和七类结构化的多标签细粒度目标识别，并区分显式/隐式仇恨、标记显式/隐式目标及提供跨度级理由。通过随机加得分分层的时间采样减少关键词偏差。SOTA模型在仇恨检测上仅达79% F1（早年样本表现尤其差），目标识别仅达25.5% macro-F1，凸显其多样性和挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波斯语仇恨言论检测数据集多存在关键词采样偏差、时间跨度短、缺乏细粒度标注等问题。作者旨在构建一个跨十年、人工标注、具备多维度标注（仇恨类型、目标类别、显式/隐式、理由标注）的高质量基准，以更真实反映仇恨言论的分布并推动更先进方法的开发。

Method: 采用人工标注构建10,000条波斯语推文数据集，涵盖2013-2022年十年。数据收集结合随机采样与得分分层的时间采样以减少关键词驱动偏差并保持自然标签分布。标注维度包括：仇恨检测、七类结构化目标类别的多标签细粒度识别、显式/隐式仇恨区分、显式/隐式目标标记以及跨度级理由。通过评估SOTA波斯语仇恨检测模型来验证数据集的有效性和挑战性。

Result: 数据集含31%仇恨内容；SOTA模型在仇恨检测任务上仅达到中等性能（79% F1），尤其在早期年份样本上表现欠佳；在目标识别任务上性能很低（25.5% macro-F1）。结果显示ParsHate具有多样性和挑战性。

Conclusion: ParsHate作为波斯语首个十年期仇恨言论检测基准，由于采用多样性采样和多维度细粒度标注，对现有SOTA方法构成显著挑战。目前模型性能尚不理想，需要更先进的方法来提升仇恨检测尤其是目标识别性能。数据集已公开，可供社区研究使用。

Abstract: We introduce ParsHate, a manually annotated dataset of 10,000 Persian tweets spanning 2013-2022, representing the first decade-long benchmark for hate speech detection in Persian. The dataset contains 31% hateful content and supports both hate detection and multi-label fine-grained target identification across seven structured target categories. ParsHate also distinguishes explicit and implicit hate, marks explicit and implicit targets, and provides span-level rationales. Data collection combines random and score-stratified temporal sampling to reduce keyword-driven bias while preserving natural label distributions. Applying SOTA models for Persian hate-speech detection on ParsHate shows moderate performance (79% F1), especially with samples from earlier years, and low performance with target identification (25.5% macro-F1). This emphasizes the diverse sampling of hate speech in ParsHate and its challenging nature that requires more advanced methods for better performance. Dataset is made publicly available.

</details>


### [123] [Negation Beyond the Verbal Channel: Temporal Multimodal Correlates in Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16396)
*Leon Hammerla,Patrick Schrottenbacher,Alexander Mehler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究口语否定语境是否蕴含可测量的多模态行为信息。通过对27场虚拟现实中的人际访谈（包含964个标注的否定线索）进行时间序列建模，在排除词汇与声学信息后，最强探针模型在分组10折交叉验证中达到0.75的平均保持AUROC。时间分析表明预测信息集中在线索起始处但可扩散至更宽的时间区间，对话者行为携带较弱且分布弥散的信息；消融与时间扰动实验显示面部特征贡献最大，且模型对事件的时间组织敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，否定主要通过其语言实现来建模，但口语互动伴随着紧密协调的非言语行为。作者提出疑问：以口语否定线索为中心的语境是否包含可测量的多模态行为信息，这些信息能否在无词汇和声学输入的条件下与匹配的控制语境区分开来，其时间分布、所依赖的模态以及是否延伸至对话伙伴。

Method: 研究采集27场在虚拟现实中开展的人-人访谈，包含时间对齐的凝视、面部、头部、身体、手和手指行为，以及964个标注的否定线索。将分类作为预测探针（predictive probe），在排除词汇与声学信息的前提下比较20种时间序列模型，并系统性地改变时间上下文、交互来源、模态可用性和事件时序。采用分组10折交叉验证评估。

Result: 最强探针在说话者侧行为上达到0.75的均值保持AUROC。时间分析显示预测信息集中在线索起始处，但在更宽的周围时间区间仍可检测；对话伙伴行为携带较弱预测信息且时间分布弥散。消融实验表明面部特征产生最大的模态消融效应；时间扰动实验显示训练探针对观测事件的时间组织敏感。

Conclusion: 口语否定语境确实蕴含可测量的多模态行为信息，该信息主要由说话者侧的面部行为在语义线索起始时刻附近承载，对事件的时间组织敏感，而对话伙伴的信息较弱且分散。这一发现支持在多模态互动建模中应重视非言语行为对否定的贡献。

Abstract: Negation is typically modeled through its linguistic realization, although spoken interaction is accompanied by tightly coordinated nonverbal behavior. We ask whether contexts centered on spoken negation cues contain measurable multimodal behavioral information: whether they can be distinguished from matched control contexts without lexical or acoustic input, where this information occurs in time, which modalities carry it, and whether it extends to the dialogue partner. We study 27 human-human interviews conducted in virtual reality, comprising temporally aligned gaze, facial, head, body, hand, and finger behavior and 964 annotated negation cues. Treating classification as a predictive probe, we compare 20 time-series models while excluding lexical and acoustic information, and then systematically vary temporal context, interactional source, modality availability, and event timing. Across grouped 10-fold cross-validation, the strongest probes reach up to .75 mean held-out AUROC from speaker-side behavior. Temporal analyses show that predictive information is concentrated around cue onset but remains detectable over a broader surrounding interval, while dialogue-partner behavior carries weaker predictive information with a comparatively diffuse temporal profile. Ablation and timing perturbations further show that facial features produce the largest modality-ablation effect and that the trained probe is sensitive to the temporal organization of the observed events.

</details>


### [124] [ReMova: Fine-tuning LLMs for English to Belarusian translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16427)
*Mikita Pilinka,Aliaksandr Kliujeŭ,David Samuel,Yves Scherrer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对白俄罗斯语的数据清洗流水线和微调方法，用于英语-白俄罗斯语机器翻译。该流水线通过校正工具处理白俄罗斯语的双正字法问题、训练数据噪声、其他语言干扰和常见的互联网拼写错误。消融实验显示，过滤对所有微调模型都有显著益处，基于LLM的模型从过滤中获得的收益约为专用编码器-解码器MT系统的两倍，表明白俄罗斯语MT的主要瓶颈之一是数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白俄罗斯语存在两种正字法体系，且互联网上的训练数据存在大量噪声、语言干扰和拼写错误，严重制约了英语-白俄罗斯语机器翻译的质量。现有数据清洗流水线未针对这些白俄罗斯语特有问题进行专门处理。

Method: 设计一个专门针对白俄罗斯语的数据清洗流水线，核心是引入一个校正工具来解决双语正字法冲突、训练数据噪声、其他语言干扰以及白俄罗斯语在互联网上常见的拼写问题。随后基于清洗后的数据对各类模型（包括专用编码器-解码器MT系统和LLM-based模型）进行微调，并通过匹配的消融实验（对照未过滤训练数据）评估过滤效果。

Result: 匹配消融实验表明，过滤对所有微调模型都带来显著益处；基于LLM的模型从过滤中的收益约为专用编码器-解码器MT系统的两倍，说明过滤对于LLM类模型影响更大。

Conclusion: 数据质量是白俄罗斯语机器翻译的主要瓶颈之一，专门针对白俄罗斯语特征的清洗流水线能有效提升翻译性能，尤其对LLM-based模型收益更大。

Abstract: This paper presents a Belarusian-specific data-cleaning pipeline and fine-tuning for English-Belarusian machine translation. Our cleaning pipeline distinguishes itself from others by employing a correction tool that addresses the issue of the two orthographies of the Belarusian language, noise in the training data, interference from other languages and other misspelling issues common in Belarusian on the internet. A matched ablation on unfiltered training data shows substantial benefits from filtering for all fine-tuned models, with the LLM-based models gaining roughly twice as much from filtering as the dedicated encoder-decoder MT system, supporting the view that for Belarusian MT one of the primary bottlenecks is data quality.

</details>


### [125] [Early-Bird Decoding: Accelerating Diffusion LLMs with Learnable Block Sizes and Parallel Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16450)
*Lixuan Wei,Wei Zhou,Jianwen Wu,Yipeng Shen,Meiling Wang,Haoran You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"早鸟解码"（EB-Decode）的加速框架，首次利用扩散大语言模型（dLLM）中"低熵令牌聚集"的观察结果，通过自适应分组和位置感知采样器在阈值前并行解码令牌，将吞吐量相比朴素方法提升3.53-18.76倍，相比最强基线Fast-dLLM提升最高1.58倍，且无需修改预训练权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）通过迭代去掩蔽实现并行解码，是自回归生成的替代方案，但通常需要多步迭代才能让令牌置信度达到解码阈值，即使采用块级KV缓存推理效率仍然低下。作者观察到令牌中相似低熵（不确定性）的单元倾向于聚集，可在此前被联合解码，因此提出加速dLLM推理的新思路。

Method: EB-Decode框架包含两大核心组件：(1)可学习网络，将具有相似不确定性的令牌自适应分组为可变长度块（替代固定块大小）；(2)位置感知采样器，学习在预测的可变长度块内用更少的解码步并行去掩蔽令牌。两个组件均不改动预训练dLLM权重，作为插件可直接部署于服务阶段，训练与推理开销可忽略。

Result: 在三个模型与四个基准上的大量实验一致验证了观察与框架的有效性：相比朴素解码方法吞吐量提升3.53-18.76倍；相比最强基线Fast-dLLM吞吐量最高提升1.58倍，且精度相当。

Conclusion: EB-Decode通过利用令牌低熵聚集现象，以自适应可变长度分块与位置感知并行采样显著加速dLLM推理，是一种零权重修改、可直接部署的高效插件式框架，为扩散式并行解码提供了新的加速范式。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising parallel decoding paradigm as an alternative to autoregressive generation through iterative unmasking. However, dLLMs typically require many steps before token confidence reaches the decoding threshold, resulting in inefficient inference even with block-wise KV caching. To accelerate dLLM inference, we for the first time propose an "early-bird (EB)" decoding framework, motivated by the observation that tokens with similarly low entropy tend to cluster and can be jointly decoded earlier, before reaching the confidence threshold. In particular, our EB-Decode framework integrates two key enablers: (1) a learnable network that adaptively groups tokens with similar uncertainty into variable-length blocks, rather than relying on fixed block sizes; (2) a position-aware sampler that learns to unmask tokens in parallel using fewer decoding steps within predicted variable-length blocks. Both components are developed without modifying pretrained dLLM weights and can therefore be directly deployed as plug-ins during serving, with negligible training and inference overhead. Extensive experiments across three models and four benchmarks consistently validate our observation and the effectiveness of EB-Decode, achieving 3.53-18.76$\times$ higher throughput than the vanilla decoding method and up to 1.58$\times$ higher throughput over the strongest baseline, Fast-dLLM, with comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond the Name: Demographic Leakage in De-Identified Résumés and Evaluation Artifacts in LLM Bias Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16501)
*Qiangju Chen,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究检验去除语言字段能否解决去标识化简历筛选中的民族-文化推断泄漏问题。通过9个开放权重模型和620份反事实简历，在语言属性严格一致的前提下隔离非语言散文，发现目标群体恢复率平均0.757，高显著性情境下饱和至1.000，证明非语言散文仍能维持人口统计推断。模型仅在微弱线索下产生分化（0.086-0.690），确立显著性为关键评估轴。同时发现LLM-as-a-judge评估对实验设计高度敏感：禁止平局产生0.39的选择率比并伴随强烈位置与内容效应，允许平局则多数模型接近普遍平局（≥94%）。下游评分仅显示极小的组间差异，凸显需区分简历内容可恢复的人口统计信号与评估协议引入的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去标识化简历筛选假设对显式字段进行脱敏即可防止民族-文化（ethnocultural）推断，但近期审计显示残留泄漏来源于所申报的语言字段。研究者希望验证：移除语言字段后，非语言的简历散文内容是否仍能被开放权重语言模型用于推断申请人的民族-文化背景，从而揭示评估协议设计与评估内容本身在造成差异中的各自作用。

Method: 采用9个开放权重模型和620份反事实简历。通过严格控制语言属性完全一致，隔离出非语言散文作为自变量。实验覆盖五种民族-文化条件与三个线索显著性层级（faint/moderate/high cue-salience tiers）。评估手段包括目标群体恢复率（target-group recovery）以及LLM-as-a-judge配对比较，并系统性改变评估设计（禁止平局 vs 允许平局），同时引入下游评分以对照。

Result: 非语言散文在总体上实现了平均0.757的目标群体恢复率，在高显著性线索下饱和到1.000，证明非语言内容可持续维持人口统计推断。模型间的分歧仅在微弱线索条件下显著（恢复率区间0.086-0.690），说明显著性是一个关键评估维度。评估设计层面，禁止平局时出现0.39的表观选择率比并伴随强烈的位置与内容效应；允许平局时多数模型产生≥94%的普遍平局。下游评分仅显示各组间很小的差异。

Conclusion: 仅做简单字段脱敏（如移除语言）不足以消除人口统计推断泄漏，模型能从非语言散文恢复民族-文化信号，尤其在线索显著时。更关键的是，LLM-as-a-judge的评估结果在很大程度上由评估协议设计（如是否允许平局）而非简历内容本身驱动，因此研究与实践必须区分“可从简历内容恢复的人口统计数据”与“评估流程引入的协议效应”，并将线索显著性作为标准评估轴纳入公平性审计。

Abstract: De-identified résumé screening assumes that redacting explicit fields prevents ethnocultural inference; however, recent audits attribute residual leakage to declared languages. We investigate whether eliminating language fields resolves this leakage across nine open-weight models and 620 counterfactual résumés. By holding language attributes strictly identical, we isolate unstructured prose across five ethnocultural conditions and three cue-salience tiers. Target-group recovery averages 0.757 overall and saturates at 1.000 under high salience, demonstrating that non-language prose sustains demographic inference. Crucially, models diverge only under faint cues (0.086-0.690), establishing salience as an essential evaluation axis. Furthermore, pairwise LLM-as-a-judge outcomes are highly sensitive to evaluation design: forbidding ties yields an apparent selection-rate ratio of 0.39 alongside strong position and content effects, whereas permitting ties produces near-universal ties for most models ($\ge94\%$). Downstream scoring shows only very small between-condition differences, highlighting the need to distinguish demographic signals recoverable from résumé content from effects introduced by the evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [127] [Competence-Preserving Resume Perturbations Expose Presentation Sensitivity in LLM Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16517)
*Qiangju Chen,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对简历筛选场景，提出一种受控审计方法，检验LLM在面对相同资质证据但不同呈现方式时决策是否稳定。实验发现模型在筛选有效性与呈现稳定性之间存在明显脱节：如Llama-3.1-8B有效性强但翻转率高，Mistral-7B-v0.3则更不稳定。研究呼吁评估时应同时考虑识别更强候选者的能力和决策对呈现变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简历筛选系统需要从呈现方式差异很大的简历中推断求职者能力，理想情况下，当底层资质证据不变时，表面变化不应影响决策。但目前缺乏对这一性质的受控审计，因此作者构造了可控能力水平的候选人档案，并生成多种呈现变体，以检验LLM决策是否稳定。

Method: 构建基于职业的候选档案，控制能力水平，并将每个档案渲染为多种不同呈现形式。使用确定性验证门控排除那些改变了底层证据的变体，再对六个开放指令微调LLM进行评分。比较配对决策翻转率与筛选有效性指标。

Result: Llama-3.1-8B在其原生聊天模板下有效性最高（0.781），但在保留能力的呈现变化下翻转了29.6%的配对决策；Mistral-7B-v0.3有效性为0.644，翻转率高达41.4%。原生聊天格式能提升部分模型的有效性，但并未消除这种不稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，简历筛选评估不应只关注系统能否识别更强候选者，还应考察当同一能力证据以不同方式呈现时决策是否保持稳定。当前LLM在有效性与稳定性之间存在显著脱节，需要将两者同时纳入评估框架。

Abstract: Resume screeners must infer job-relevant competence from resumes whose presentation can vary substantially in wording, structure, stylistic polish, and document extraction quality. Ideally, such surface variation should not change decisions when the underlying qualification evidence is unchanged. We introduce a controlled audit of this property, constructing occupation-grounded candidate profiles at controlled competence levels and rendering each profile into multiple resume presentations. A deterministic validation gate excludes variants that alter the underlying evidence before scoring. Across six open instruction-tuned LLM conditions, we find a clear disconnect between screening validity and presentation stability. Llama-3.1-8B with its native chat template achieves the strongest validity ($0.781$) yet reverses $29.6\%$ of matched pairwise decisions under competence-preserving presentation changes; Mistral-7B-v0.3 reaches validity $0.644$ with a $41.4\%$ flip rate. Native chat formatting improves validity for several chat-tuned models but does not remove this instability. These results show that resume-screening evaluations should assess not only whether a system identifies stronger candidates, but also whether those decisions remain stable when the same competence evidence is presented differently.

</details>


### [128] [Style-Debiased DPO: Updating LLM Knowledge with Factuality-Aware Synthetic Preference Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16532)
*Takayuki Yamamoto,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）中“知识存储”之外的另一面——“知识唤起”：通过偏好优化让模型更准确地唤起（检索）已存储的知识。作者发现，当目标知识部分已知时，直接偏好优化（DPO）会错误地压低含有正确知识、仅因风格差异（长度、措辞）不同于标准答案的被拒响应。为此提出风格去偏DPO（SD-DPO），它判断每个对被拒响应是否正确，反转这类对的偏好并赋予权重，使风格差异带来的学习信号整体抵消。实验表明，在EntiGraph（存储侧方法）之上，SD-DPO用少几十倍训练token显著提升QuALITY准确率；在知识更新基准AToKE上达到0.982总体准确率并能按查询时段区分新旧事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练（CPT）配合数据增强（如同义改写）可以把一个小型源语料的知识存入LLM，但存储的知识并非总能被正确检索，即存在“存储了但唤不出”的问题。已有工作多聚焦于存储侧，而本文转向唤起侧：希望通过偏好优化让模型更准确地唤起已存储知识。关键困难在于当目标知识部分已知时，大多数被拒响应其实事实正确，直接用DPO会错误地压低这些正确回答，从而损害模型能力。

Method: 提出风格去偏DPO（SD-DPO）。该方法为每个偏好对（chosen/rejected）判断被拒响应是否事实正确；对事实正确的被拒对，反转其偏好方向并赋予权重，使得因风格（长度、措辞等）差异产生的学习信号在整体上相互抵消，从而避免DPO误伤正确知识，只保留真正错误所带来的学习信号。

Result: 1) 在QuALITY（阅读理解QA基准）上，SD-DPO在EntiGraph存储方法之上超过了基线（同一基础模型在EntiGraph合成数据上做CPT、同样流程评估），且所需训练token比获得同等增益所需的额外CPT少几十倍。2) 在知识更新任务（AToKE基准，针对随时间变化的事实）上，SD-DPO总体准确率达0.982，并能根据查询时段正确回答新或旧事实。

Conclusion: SD-DPO有效补充了存储侧的CPT方法，以极低的训练成本高效地“唤起”LLM已存储的知识，避免DPO因风格差异误导偏好优化而损伤正确知识；在知识更新场景也能准确按时间区分新旧事实，表明该方法兼具准确性与效率。

Abstract: Continued pretraining (CPT) with data augmentation such as paraphrasing can store inside a large language model (LLM) the knowledge of a small source corpus. The stored knowledge, however, is not always retrieved correctly. We study the eliciting side rather than the storing side: we use preference optimization, which learns from pairs of a preferred (chosen) and a dispreferred (rejected) response, so that the model elicits its stored knowledge more accurately. One proposed approach takes the model's own erroneous response as rejected and the gold answer as chosen, so as to suppress the error. When the target knowledge is partially known, however, most of these rejected responses are factually correct. Using direct preference optimization (DPO) then pushes down rejected responses that contain correct knowledge and differ from the chosen answer only in style, such as length and wording. We propose style-debiased DPO (SD-DPO), which scores whether the rejected response of each pair is factually correct, inverts the preference of such pairs, and weights them so that the learning signal due to differences in style cancels out as a whole. We first test whether, on top of EntiGraph, a representative storing-side method that runs CPT on text synthesized from the corpus, our method adds accuracy efficiently. On QuALITY, the reading-comprehension QA benchmark on which EntiGraph was evaluated, SD-DPO exceeds a baseline we CPT on EntiGraph's synthetic data from the same base model and evaluate with the same procedure. The training tokens this requires are a few dozen times fewer than the additional CPT needed for the same gain. For knowledge updating, the main goal of this work, we use AToKE, a knowledge-editing benchmark for facts that change over time. There, SD-DPO reaches an overall accuracy of 0.982 and answers with the new or the old fact according to the queried period.

</details>


### [129] [PunGraph: Retrieval-Enhanced Phonetic-Semantic Graph Reasoning for Pun Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16557)
*Yuchen Su,Zijian Huang,Yaotian Shi,Shaoxin Zhong,Ruofan Wang,Mengze Li,Yonghua Zhu,Diana Benavides-Prado,Michael Witbrock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PunGraph，一个基于检索增强的知识图谱框架，用于双关语理解。它利用Unisyn音标字典、IPA和G2P表示以及WordNet定义构建音韵-语义词汇图，在结构化候选空间中约束LLM推理。同时引入WebPun大型数据集（5,730个双关语）。在SemEval-2017和WebPun上的实验表明，PunGraph能持续提升小型LLM的性能，并可与强专有模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双关语作为一种利用语音相似性和语义歧义传达多重含义的修辞手法，对LLM而言颇具挑战。尽管LLM具备强大的语言理解能力，但由于有限的语音建模和不受控的端到端生成，它们在双关推理上仍存在困难，因此需要一种引入结构化知识来弥补这些不足的方法。

Method: PunGraph构建了一个音韵-语义词汇图，其数据来源包括：Unisyn音标词典、IPA（国际音标）和G2P（字素到音素）表示，以及WordNet的定义。通过检索候选词或词义，将LLM的推理约束在一个结构化候选空间内。同时贡献了WebPun数据集，包含5,730个带标注的同形异义和同音异义双关语。

Result: 在SemEval-2017和WebPun数据集上的实验显示，PunGraph持续提升了小型LLM的性能，并取得了与强专有模型相竞争的结果。进一步分析表明，检索引导的语音和语义约束能有效减少双关解释中常见的推理错误。

Conclusion: 将结构化知识与LLM相结合能够显著提升双关理解能力，验证了检索增强知识图谱框架的有效性。研究公开了代码与数据集，为后续双关理解研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Puns are a challenging form of figurative language that exploit phonetic similarity and semantic ambiguity to convey multiple meanings. Although large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong language understanding capabilities, they still struggle with pun reasoning due to limited phonetic modeling and uncontrolled end-to-end generation. We propose \textbf{PunGraph}, a retrieval-enhanced knowledge graph framework for pun understanding. PunGraph constructs a phonetic-semantic lexical graph using the Unisyn phonetic dictionary, IPA and G2P representations, and WordNet definitions, and retrieves candidate words or senses to constrain LLM reasoning within a structured candidate space. We further introduce \textbf{WebPun}, a new large-scale dataset containing 5,730 annotated heterographic and homographic puns. Experiments on SemEval-2017 and WebPun show that PunGraph consistently improves the performance of small-scale LLMs and achieves competitive results against strong proprietary models. Further analysis shows that retrieval-guided phonetic and semantic constraints effectively reduce common reasoning errors in pun interpretation, highlighting the benefits of integrating structured knowledge with LLMs. We release our code and dataset at https://github.com/ysu132/PunGraph.

</details>


### [130] [Challenges of Auditing: Variability in Outputs of Large Language Models for Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16590)
*Yuan Pu,Yewon Chang,Furong Jia,Xunjian Yin,Jessica Ma,Ayman Ali,Monica Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现消费者通过不同访问模式（如ChatGPT、ChatGPT Health、API）使用前沿AI模型获取健康建议时存在系统性差异，而现有评估多依赖API进行，与消费者实际使用的聊天界面不一致，从而限制了评估的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的人使用前沿AI模型获取健康建议，但用户通过不同的访问模式（如ChatGPT聊天界面、ChatGPT Health、API接口）以不同设置与模型交互。由于现有评估通常基于API进行，而消费者实际通过聊天界面使用，这种评估与真实使用场景的脱节可能导致评估结果无法真实反映消费者体验。

Method: 研究对不同访问模式（ChatGPT、ChatGPT Health、API）下前沿AI模型提供的健康建议进行了系统性比较，识别各模式之间的差异，并评估这些差异对评价有效性的影响。

Result: 研究发现不同访问模式之间存在系统性差异，这些差异使得基于API的评估结果无法准确代表消费者通过聊天界面获得的实际体验，从而限制了评估的效度。

Conclusion: 模型提供商迫切需要使评估能够忠实复现消费者的实际使用体验和设置，以便进行严格的审计，确保健康建议的评估具有真实有效性。

Abstract: People increasingly use frontier AI models for health advice, but via different access modes (e.g., ChatGPT, ChatGPT Health, APIs) with varying settings. Here, we find systematic differences across access modes. Because evaluations typically rely on APIs while consumers interact through chatbot interfaces, these discrepancies limit evaluation validity. Our findings underscore an urgent need for model providers to enable faithful replication of consumer experiences and settings for rigorous audits.

</details>


### [131] [RoleBreak: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Role-Playing Robustness in Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16614)
*Yuqi Wang,Fengyuan Liu,Haochen Luo,Zhiqi Yu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoleBreak，一个用于口语对话系统中长期角色扮演鲁棒性的开放基准，包含 310 个角色、6,688 个对话轮次和 11,743 条细粒度评测标准。作者评估了九种涵盖全双工、全模态和级联 ASR–LLM–TTS 范式的系统配置，发现四个关键模式：语义角色遵循强于声音情绪表达、语义鲁棒性在长交互中脆弱、扩大 LLM 规模可提升语义鲁棒性但对声音情绪提升有限、用户声音情绪会影响角色扮演行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语角色扮演基准大多以角色为中心且为短期交互，无法验证对话模型能否在长时间交互中维持多样角色，尤其是超越预定义的虚构角色。这一空白促使作者构建一个面向长期角色扮演鲁棒性的开放基准。

Method: 作者构建 RoleBreak 基准，包含 310 个基于角色和以用户为中心的角色、6,688 个人工验证的对话轮次及 11,743 条细粒度评测标准，其中 1,856 个轮次带有表达性情绪目标用于评测声音情绪。场景设计旨在长期对话中检验角色一致性、交互质量、安全性和情感表达。作者评估了九种配置，涵盖全双工、全模态及级联 ASR–LLM–TTS 范式。

Result: 研究发现四个关键模式：(1) 现有系统在语义角色遵循方面明显强于声音情绪表达；(2) 语义鲁棒性在长交互中依然脆弱——即便是最强的系统平均也仅在第 10.4 和 11.6 轮分别首次遭遇角色与安全失败；(3) 扩大 LLM 规模能显著提升语义鲁棒性并延迟失败，但对声音情绪改善甚微；(4) 在语言内容固定的情况下，用户声音情绪仍会影响角色扮演行为。

Conclusion: 这些发现凸显了口语角色扮演系统在长期交互鲁棒性与声音表现力方面仍存在持久差距，为后续改进方向提供了明确依据。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech dialogue models increasingly support persona control, yet existing spoken role-playing benchmarks remain largely character-centric and short-horizon. This leaves open whether spoken dialogue models can sustain diverse roles over extended interactions, especially beyond predefined fictional characters. We introduce RoleBreak, an open benchmark for long-horizon role-playing robustness in spoken dialogue. RoleBreak contains 310 character-based and user-centered roles, 6,688 human-verified dialogue turns, and 11,743 fine-grained evaluation criteria, with 1,856 turns carrying expressive emotion targets for evaluating vocal emotion. Its scenarios are designed to stress role consistency, interaction quality, safety, and affect over extended conversations. We evaluate nine configurations spanning full-duplex, omni-modal, and cascaded ASR--LLM--TTS paradigms. We find four key patterns. First, current systems are substantially stronger at semantic role adherence than at vocal emotion. Second, semantic robustness remains brittle over long interactions: even the strongest evaluated system encounters its first persona and safety failures after only 10.4 and 11.6 turns on average. Third, scaling the LLM substantially improves semantic robustness and delays failure, but yields little improvement in vocal emotion. Finally, user vocal emotion affects role-playing behavior even when linguistic content is fixed. These findings highlight persistent gaps in both long-horizon robustness and vocal expressiveness in spoken role-playing systems.

</details>


### [132] [Quantifying Organizational Environmental Action from Web Data and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16627)
*Quinn Reynolds,Daniel Shore,Vianey Leos Barajas,Tanhum Yoreh,Meredith Franklin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的计算框架，用于从公开网页非结构化文本中提取组织层面环境行动的结构化度量，并以美国犹太会众为案例验证。通过整合多方数据源构建了4,964个会众的国家级数据库，抓取2,657个活跃网站共计154,454个网页，比较了关键词检索、语义向量检索和直接LLM分类三种检测方法，发现初步检索虽降低计算成本但可能提前丢失相关信息，直接LLM分类覆盖率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从公开网页内容量化组织环境行动是环境数据科学中的难题，因为相关信息分散于多个网页且主要以非结构化文本呈现。需要一个可扩展的计算框架将这些非结构化网络内容转化为结构化的环境行动度量。

Method: 构建了包含4,964个美国犹太会众的国家级数据库，整合了地理空间、知识库、目录和人工审核等多源数据。抓取2,657个活跃网站的154,454个网页作为语料。比较三种检测环境行动的方法：关键词检索后接LLM分类、语义向量检索后接LLM分类、以及无预先检索的直接LLM分类，并通过专家人工审阅评估一致性（kappa系数）。

Result: 与专家人工审阅的一致性：关键词检索最低（κ=0.26），语义向量检索最高（κ=0.42），直接LLM分类相近（κ=0.40）。语义检索的召回率为0.87，表明分类前已丢失部分相关内容。应用于完整语料时，直接LLM分类在1,398个会众（53%）中识别出至少一种环境行动，覆盖率高于两种检索式方法。

Conclusion: 初步检索可降低计算成本，但可能提前排除相关信息，导致内容在到达分类器前丢失。该框架提供了一种可复现的、从非结构化网络内容中提取组织层面环境信息的方法，并可推广至其他类型的机构。

Abstract: Quantifying organizational environmental action from publicly available web content remains a challenging environmental data science problem because relevant information can be dispersed across multiple webpages and is primarily communicated through unstructured text. We present a scalable computational framework for transforming organizational web content into structured measures of environmental action and demonstrate the approach using Jewish congregations in the United States. We constructed a national database of 4,964 congregations by integrating multiple geospatial, knowledge-base, directory, and manually reviewed sources. Of these, 2,657 had active websites that were successfully crawled, producing a corpus of 154,454 webpages. We compared three approaches for detecting environmental actions: keyword retrieval followed by large language model (LLM) classification, semantic vector retrieval followed by LLM classification, and direct LLM classification classification without preliminary retrieval. Agreement with an expert human reviewer was lowest for keyword retrieval ($κ$ = 0.26), higher for semantic vector retrieval ($κ$ = 0.42), and similar for direct LLM classification ($κ$ = 0.40). Although semantic retrieval achieved the highest agreement, its retrieval recall was 0.87, indicating loss of relevant content before classification.
