Applied to the complete corpus, direct LLM classification identified at least one environmental action at 1,398 congregations (53%), providing greater coverage than either retrieval-based approach. These results demonstrate that preliminary retrieval can reduce computational cost but may exclude relevant information before it reaches the classifier. The framework provides a reproducible approach for extracting organization-level environmental information from unstructured web content that can be adapted to other institutions.

</details>


### [133] [Rewarding Reasoning, Not Answers: Fixing and Bounding Test-Time Reinforcement Learning on Medical QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16660)
*Kailong Fan,Anqi Pu,Yichen Wu,Wanhua Li,Yicong Li,Hanspeter Pfister,Huafeng Liu,Xiang Li,Quanzheng Li,Ning Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时强化学习（test-time RL）在医学多选问答（MCQA）上的失败原因并提出解决方案。作者发现基于多数投票伪标签的自训练方法在数学任务上有效，但在医学MCQA上会因答案空间结构导致崩溃（小答案空间导致错误伪标签碰撞强化，大答案空间导致奖励稀疏）。据此提出PROSE（过程奖励引导自训练），改用过程奖励模型对推理步骤评分，取所有步骤的最小值作为轨迹奖励，并施加答案格式约束。PROSE无需标签即可大幅提升通用Llama模型，超越专用医学模型并匹敌更大规模系统，推理时无需奖励模型，且能迁移到未见数据集。实验还证明最小值聚合是关键——均值聚合可被利用，导致代理奖励饱和而准确率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习（基于多数投票伪标签）在数学任务上表现出色，但在医学多选问答任务上失效：准确率停滞而输出多样性迅速下降。作者希望弄清这一失败的根本原因，并设计一种能在无监督条件下有效利用模型自身测试数据的适应方法。

Method: 首先通过受控实验（保持问题、模型、优化器不变，仅改变答案空间）诊断失败根源，发现答案空间结构是主因而非领域难度。在此基础上提出PROSE：用医学过程奖励模型（process reward model，PRM）对每个推理步骤打分，将轨迹奖励设为所有步骤得分的最小值，并施加答案格式约束。整个适应过程无需标签，过程奖励信号被内化进策略。

Result: PROSE 在无标签条件下显著提升通用Llama模型，超越专门构建的医学模型，匹敌更大规模系统。适应后的模型推理时无需奖励模型，且能将收益迁移到未见数据集。实验还证明最小值聚合必不可少——均值聚合可被利用，使代理奖励饱和但准确率反而下降。

Conclusion: 测试时强化学习在医学MCQA上的崩溃源于答案空间结构而非领域难度；奖励应基于推理过程质量而非答案一致性。PROSE通过过程奖励加最小值聚合成功解决该问题，实现了无监督、可迁移且推理高效的模型适应。该工作为测试时学习中的奖励设计与聚合策略提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning adapts a model on its own unlabeled test set using majority-vote pseudo-labels and has shown strong results in mathematics. We show that this recipe collapses on medical multiple-choice QA: accuracy stagnates while output diversity rapidly declines. Through a controlled experiment that keeps the questions, model, and optimizer fixed while changing only the answer space, we trace this failure to answer-space structure rather than domain difficulty. In small answer spaces, incorrect rollouts often collide on the same wrong pseudo-label and reinforce it; in large answer spaces, they disperse and receive little reward. This diagnosis motivates PROSE, Process Reward Guided Self-Training, which rewards reasoning quality instead of answer agreement. PROSE scores each reasoning step with a medical process reward model, assigns the trajectory reward as the minimum score across steps, and enforces answer-format constraints. Without labels, PROSE substantially improves a general Llama model, surpassing purpose-built medical models and matching much larger systems. Because the process signal is internalized into the policy, the adapted model requires no reward model at inference and transfers its gains to unseen datasets. We further show that the minimum aggregation is essential: mean aggregation can be exploited, saturating the proxy reward while degrading accuracy.

</details>


### [134] [DiaWhisper-DPO: Role-Attributed Transcription of Clinical Interviews via Failure-Mined Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16661)
*Weiming Li,Ana Catarina Fidalgo Barata,Miguel Constante,João Miguel Sanches*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出端到端模型 DiaWhisper 及其强化版本 DiaWhisper-DPO，用于临床访谈中说话人（医生/患者）的角色归属与转录。DiaWhisper 基于 Whisper-large-v3 微调（LoRA + 辅助帧级角色头），DiaWhisper-DPO 利用真实解码失败样本作为 DPO 拒绝完成进行无人工标注的优化，在 DAIC-WOZ 上角色准确率达 0.973、DER 0.119（较最强级联基线降低 72%），并在跨语言 PDCH-HAMD 数据集上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动抑郁筛查需要将临床访谈中的语句准确归属到医生或患者。现有级联系统（说话人日志+角色分配启发式）错误会在各阶段累积传播，且缺乏跨语言验证的端到端方案。

Method: 提出端到端模型 DiaWhisper，对 Whisper-large-v3 进行 LoRA 微调，并加入辅助帧级角色头以同时完成转录与角色归属。进一步提出 DiaWhisper-DPO，通过挖掘真实解码失败样本作为 DPO 的被拒补全，在无需人工偏好标注的情况下进行优化。实验在两个数据集上评估：DAIC-WOZ（通过重合成医患双方便于受控两方评测）和 PDCH-HAMD（真实中文访谈，用于跨语言验证）。

Result: 在 29 个 DAIC-WOZ 测试会话上，DiaWhisper-DPO 达到 0.973 角色准确率和 0.119 DER，比最强级联基线降低 72%，并将种子间方差从 σ=0.205 降至 0.002。在 PDCH-HAMD 上重新训练后达到 0.757 角色准确率，并在全部 78 个会话-种子组合上均有提升。

Conclusion: 端到端的 DiaWhisper 系列显著优于级联基线，兼具高角色归属准确率、低词错误率与强训练稳定性，且跨语言泛化得到验证，为自动抑郁筛查中的说话人归属提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Automated depression screening from clinical interviews requires attribution of utterances to the clinician or patient. We evaluate two datasets: DAIC-WOZ, where participant-only recordings require re-synthesizing both sides for controlled two-party evaluation, and PDCH-HAMD, comprising voice-converted real Chinese interviews for cross-lingual validation. Cascaded systems combine speaker diarization with role-assignment heuristics, so errors can propagate across stages. We propose an end-to-end model, which we named DiaWhisper, that fine-tunes Whisper-large-v3 with LoRA and an auxiliary frame-level role head for transcription and attribution, together with DiaWhisper-DPO, a failure-mined refinement that uses genuine decoding failures as DPO rejected completions without human preference annotation. On 29 DAIC-WOZ test sessions, DiaWhisper-DPO achieves 0.973 role accuracy and 0.119 DER, 72% below the strongest cascaded baseline, and reduces seed variation from σ = .205 to .002. Retrained on PDCH-HAMD, it achieves 0.757 role accuracy and improves all 78 session-seed pairs.

</details>


### [135] [Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation: A Conversational Benchmark for Safe Stroke Care in Japanese Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16739)
*Keisuke Masuda,Kazutaka Yatsushiro,Hirohumi Iwamoto,Hirofumi Hirano,Ryosuke Hanaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了"Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation"，一个用于日本卒中诊疗的多轮对话基准评测框架。通过神经外科专家模拟患者并按照预定标准评分，评估了18个LLM在病史采集、紧急度评估和安全性方面的表现。结果显示Claude Fable 5得分最高（87.4%）且无致命错误，仅两个模型达到安全阈值（≥80%且零致命错误），11个模型共出现17次致命错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在多项选择题形式的医学考试中已能达到与医生相当的水平，但它们在真实临床场景下的病史采集、紧急度评估和安全性能力尚未得到充分评估。现有评测多采用客观题形式，难以反映实际临床工作中的多轮对话、评估判断和操作决策等复杂能力，因此需要建立贴近实践的评测基准。

Method: 创建了10个卒中及相关疾病病例，让LLM扮演医生角色，由一名获得委员会认证的神经外科医生扮演模拟患者并进行评分。每个病例分为病史采集和行动两个阶段，使用预定标准评分。将可能直接威胁生命的错误定义为"致命错误"，安全阈值为总体得分≥80%且零致命错误。于2025年10月和2026年6月评估了18个模型，并限制病史采集问题的数量。

Result: Claude Fable 5得分最高（87.4%）且零致命错误，其次是Claude Opus 4.7（80.3%）和GLM-5.2（75.6%）。仅两个领先模型达到安全阈值；11个模型共犯17次致命错误，包括使用t-PA前未确认实验室结果或血糖、在气道稳定前进行手术、遗漏颈动脉血管评估、在适应证外使用t-PA等。病史采集问题数量与病史采集得分呈正相关（r=0.648，p=0.007）。2026年云和本地部署模型的表现均有提升，部分超过安全阈值。

Conclusion: Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation为LLM在实践导向条件下的性能提供了评测基准，其病例和评估由神经外科专家创建而非采用LLM-as-judge方法。尽管模型性能在持续提升且部分已满足安全标准，但多数模型仍存在威胁生命的临床失误，提示LLM在真实临床应用中存在安全性风险，还需使用真实病例进行进一步评估。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved physician-comparable performance on multiple-choice medical knowledge examinations, but their capabilities in clinical history taking, urgency assessment, and safety remain insufficiently evaluated. We proposed Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation, a multi-turn conversational benchmark for stroke care in Japanese, and evaluated LLM performance and safety under practice-oriented conditions. Methods: We created 10 stroke and related-condition cases and evaluated LLMs in multi-turn Japanese conversations. The LLM acted as physician, while a board-certified neurosurgeon acted as simulated patient and evaluator. Each case comprised history-taking and action phases scored using pre-specified criteria. Errors that could directly threaten life were defined as critical mistakes. The safety threshold was at least 80% overall with zero critical mistakes. Eighteen models were evaluated in October 2025 and June 2026. Results: Claude Fable 5 achieved the highest score (87.4%) with zero critical mistakes, followed by Claude Opus 4.7 (80.3%) and GLM-5.2 (75.6%). Two leaders met the safety threshold. Eleven models made 17 critical mistakes, including failure to confirm laboratory results or blood glucose before t-PA, surgery before airway stabilization, omission of cervical vascular evaluation, and t-PA outside its indication. History-taking question count correlated with history-taking score (r = 0.648, p = 0.007). Conclusions: Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation provides a benchmark for LLM performance under practice-oriented conditions, including a cap on history-taking questions. Cases and evaluations were created by neurosurgical specialists rather than using an LLM-as-judge approach. Performance improved across cloud-based and on-premise models in 2026, with some exceeding the safety threshold. Further evaluation using real-world cases is required.

</details>


### [136] [TIAO: Token Importance-Aware Policy Optimization for Text Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16748)
*Qixiu Li,Chenlong Bao,Xiang Zhu,Xiaoyong Li,Ruixin Cao,Shukai Chen,Zhenxiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TIAO（Token Importance-Aware Policy Optimization），一种基于token重要性感知的强化学习策略，用于文本摘要。TIAO基于token依赖关系识别核心token，并根据整体依赖关系重新加权轨迹的advantage。实验表明，经TIAO增强的7B基础模型性能可与GPT-4和GPT-5-nano相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型摘要强化学习方法将奖励信号直接作用于无差别的token序列，忽视了单个token对词级和句级摘要质量的不同重要性，导致优化不够精准。

Method: 提出TIAO策略：基于token依赖关系识别核心token，并根据整体的token依赖关系对轨迹的advantage进行重新加权，从而显式利用token重要性感知来优化策略。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，TIAO取得了极具竞争力的结果；经TIAO增强的7B基础模型性能可与GPT-4和GPT-5-nano相媲美。

Conclusion: TIAO有效提升了文本摘要的强化学习优化效果，验证了token重要性感知的有效性，并表明小规模模型可通过该策略达到大型模型的摘要性能。

Abstract: Text summarization requires models to condense content while preserving key qualities such as consistency and coherence. Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on this task and can be further improved through reinforcement learning (RL). However, most existing methods apply reward signals directly to undifferentiated token sequences, overlooking the varying importance of individual tokens to word and sentence level quality in summarization. In this paper, we propose Token Importance-Aware Policy Optimization (TIAO), a novel reinforcement learning strategy that explicitly leverages token-importance awareness. Specifically, TIAO identifies core tokens based on token dependency and reweights a trajectory's advantage according to its overall dependencies. Experiments on the real world dataset show that our TIAO achieves highly competitive results, and that a 7B foundation model enhanced by TIAO performs comparably to GPT-4 and GPT-5-nano. Code is available at https://github.com/TechCloud-x/TIAO

</details>


### [137] [Benchmarking Factual Robustness of LLMs via Multi-conversation Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16777)
*Zhuoang Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM安全评估中的一个关键缺陷——"拒绝惯性"(Refusal Inertia)：模型在多轮对话中的初始拒绝常因上下文一致性而延续，掩盖了其在孤立说服攻击下的真实脆弱性。作者提出SAST-IR框架（有状态攻击者、无状态目标），通过清除目标模型记忆但保留攻击者历史，模拟最坏情况下的对抗场景。实验表明简单攻击策略对无记忆防御的成功率高达96%，并发现"复杂度悖论"：复杂攻击虽有效但易触发合规性防御，简单策略反而实现更高的真实说服率(84.7%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队框架在多轮对话中评估LLM时，目标模型保留完整对话历史，导致模型最初的拒绝会通过后续轮次传播以维持上下文一致性，从而掩盖其对抗隔离说服攻击的真实脆弱性。作者希望评估SOTA模型在"冷启动"（无记忆）状态下的防御能力，以暴露传统评估方法忽视的安全盲区。

Method: 提出SAST-IR（Stateful Attacker, Stateless Target - Iterative Refinement）框架：对目标模型强制执行记忆清除（stateless），同时保留攻击者历史（stateful），模拟最坏情况的对抗设置。利用CP-Agent（Cognitive Persuasion Agent，诊断引导的增强型攻击代理），在自定义的CounterFact-Strict数据集（N=50）上进行多轮无状态迭代的对抗评估。

Result: 简单且多样化的攻击策略对无记忆防御实现了惊人的96%成功率，暴露了LLM在无记忆防御下的严重脆弱性。同时揭示了"复杂度悖论"：复杂且迭代精炼的攻击虽有效但常触发防御性合规（compliance），而简单策略实现了更高的真实说服率（84.7%）。

Conclusion: 传统多轮红队评估因"拒绝惯性"高估了模型的防御能力；LLM在无记忆（冷启动）状态下对说服攻击极度脆弱；攻击复杂度与真实说服效果之间存在悖论关系——简单攻击更具"真实说服力"，而复杂攻击更多触发形式化防御。这为LLM安全评估设计（特别是记忆管理、防御机制）提供了新的视角与改进方向。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as primary knowledge retrieval interfaces, their robustness against \textit{persuasion attacks}---attempts to inject misinformation or enforce counterfactuals---has become a critical safety concern. Existing red-teaming frameworks typically evaluate models in multi-turn dialogues where the target model retains full conversation history. We identify a critical flaw in this setting termed \textbf{``Refusal Inertia''}: a model's initial refusal often propagates through subsequent turns largely to maintain contextual consistency, thereby masking its true vulnerability to sophisticated, isolated persuasion attempts. To rigorously evaluate the ``cold-start'' defense capabilities of SOTA models, we introduce the \textbf{SAST-IR} (Stateful Attacker, Stateless Target - Iterative Refinement) framework. By enforcing a memory wipe on the target while retaining the attacker's history, we simulate a worst-case adversarial setting using \textbf{multi-turn} (stateless) iterations. Leveraging \textbf{CP-Agent} (Cognitive Persuasion Agent), an enhanced diagnosis-guided agent, our experiments on the custom \textsc{CounterFact-Strict} dataset ($N=50$) yield alarming results: simple, diverse attack strategies achieved a staggering \textbf{96\%} success rate, exposing severe brittleness in memory-less defense. Furthermore, we reveal a \textbf{``Complexity Paradox''}: while complex, iteratively refined attacks are effective, they often trigger defensive compliance, whereas simple strategies achieve a higher rate of genuine persuasion (\textbf{84.7\%}). Our code and dataset are available at GitHub, https://github.com/cza1006/llm-persuasion-defense.

</details>


### [138] [Smarter by the Moment: Environment-Driven Dynamic Policies for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16800)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Po-Chun Chen,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出动态检索策略生成框架（DRPG），通过结合基于记忆的检索与动态策略生成器，利用历史数据和环境反馈为持续学习的大语言模型（LLM）产生任务特定策略，从而减少同类错误的重复发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在持续适应不断演化的任务和环境方面面临挑战。现有的记忆增强方法仅检索单个历史样例作为直接参考，未能从这些样例中显式提炼可操作的策略，导致同类错误反复出现。

Method: DRPG框架整合了基于记忆的检索模块与动态策略生成器：检索模块从历史数据中提取相关样例，策略生成器综合解析这些样例并结合环境反馈，动态生成针对具体任务的策略，用于指导LLM的持续改进。

Result: 在六个基准（涵盖文本转SQL、问答、医学诊断、Python编程）上使用七个LLM（涵盖专有与开放权重模型）进行评测，DRPG在大多数数据集和模型上优于强基线。分析表明：策略生成对检索策略具有鲁棒性、无需先前策略连续性即可有效运作，且可使用更小或跨家族的模型作为经济高效的策略生成器。

Conclusion: 策略级指导的效果取决于任务特性，本文为此机制何时以及何种条件下最有效提供了实用洞见。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across diverse domains, but continual adaptation to evolving tasks and environments remains a key challenge. Existing memory-augmented approaches retrieve individual past examples as direct references, but do not explicitly synthesize actionable strategies from them, causing the same types of errors to recur. We propose Dynamic Retrieval-based Policy Generation (DRPG), a framework that integrates memory-based retrieval with a dynamic policy generator, leveraging historical data and environment feedback to produce task-specific policies for continual LLM improvement. We evaluate DRPG across six benchmarks spanning text-to-SQL, question answering, medical diagnosis, and Python programming, using seven LLMs from both proprietary and open-weight families. DRPG outperforms strong baselines across most datasets and models. Further analysis demonstrates that DRPG's policy generation is robust to retrieval strategy, operates effectively without prior policy continuity, and can leverage smaller or cross-family models as cost-efficient policy generators. We also find that the benefit of policy-level guidance depends on task characteristics, offering practical insights into when and under what conditions this mechanism is most effective.

</details>


### [139] [A Data-free Universal Prior over Syntactic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16854)
*Fermín Moscoso del Prado Martín*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现一种源自认知驱动的增量语言生成模型的"普适先验"能够为句法结构（依存树）分配概率，且无需拟合任何语言数据参数。该先验在138种语言中赋予真实（attested）句法树高于随机树的概率，并在34种语言中的33种与语料库估计的概率呈正相关，表明部分句法概率结构可独立于语言特定的统计学习而存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率是语言理解、产出、习得与演化理论以及大语言模型的基础。现有理论通常从特定语言数据中估计句法结构的概率，但尚不清楚这种概率结构中是否有一部分可以独立于语言特定经验而产生。作者旨在探究句法概率结构是否存在普适的、数据独立的认知起源。

Method: 作者构建了一个认知上合理的增量语言产出模型，其中词语通过网络生长逐步整合为句法结构（以依存树表示）。该模型产生的先验直接为依存树分配概率，不依赖任何语言数据拟合参数。作者将该先验分配给真实句法树和随机树的概率进行比较，并在138种类型多样的语言中检验，同时在34种语言中将其与从语料库估计的概率计算相关性。

Result: 该普适先验在全部138种语言中赋予真实句法树比随机树更高的概率；在34种语言中，有33种语言中该先验概率与语料库估计概率呈正相关。这证明部分句法概率结构可以在没有任何语言特定经验的情况下产生。

Conclusion: 句法结构的概率分布中有一部分可以独立于语言特定的统计学习而产生。语言经验可能不是从均匀空间中"创造"概率，而是对已由语言产出过程结构化了的概率进行精炼。这为句法结构概率分布提供了一种可能的认知起源，将语言产出与统计学习联系起来，并为概率语言模型提供了一个数据无关的结构性偏向。

Abstract: Probability is fundamental to theories of language comprehension, production, acquisition, and evolution, as well as to large language models. Existing theories estimate the probability of syntactic structures from language-specific data. Whether part of this probability structure can arise independently of language-specific experience remains unknown. Here I show that a universal prior over syntactic structures emerges from a cognitively motivated model of incremental language production, in which words are progressively integrated into syntactic structure through network growth. The resulting prior assigns probabilities to syntactic structures --represented as dependency trees-- without fitting parameters to linguistic data, and assigns higher probabilities to attested than to random trees in all 138 typologically diverse languages examined. These prior probabilities correlate positively with probabilities estimated from corpora in 33 of 34 languages. The results indicate that part of the probability structure of syntax can arise independently of language-specific statistical learning. Linguistic experience may therefore refine probabilities that are already structured by the process of language production, rather than create them from an initially uniform space. This identifies a possible cognitive origin for part of the probability distribution over syntactic structures, linking language production and statistical learning while providing a data-independent structural bias for probabilistic models of language.

</details>


### [140] [Reduplicative constructions in Mandarin: Socio-emotional profiling through distributional semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16860)
*Chaoyi Wu,Yu-Hsiang Tseng,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用分布语义学中的词嵌入（Tencent嵌入）来考察普通话两种能产的重叠构式，发现嵌入空间能够恢复文献中已识别的语义和语法属性，两种重叠模式在分布空间中呈现清晰的语义与语用分化，且Procrustes分析显示基词空间的组织结构在重叠空间中大体保留，局部错位则凸显话语-语用层面的重组区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通话有两种能产的重叠构式（重复双字基词或其构成成分），其多样化的意义被描述为实现复数、情态色彩、声音象征和语用功能，但缺乏精确统一的语义理解。作者旨在通过引入分布语义学的词嵌入，一方面厘清普通话重叠多样语义是否能够更精确地理解，另一方面探究嵌入在理解复杂语义构词过程中的实用价值。

Method: 采用分布语义学方法，使用Tencent词嵌入对普通话两种重叠构式进行分析。通过语义画像（semantic profiling）考察重叠构式在多个维度上的语义表征，并运用Procrustes分析比较基词空间与重叠空间的组织结构，以检验构式的语义-语用分化及构式透明度。

Result: 嵌入空间能够恢复文献中已识别的重叠的语义与语法属性，验证了Tencent嵌入用于形态学研究的有效性。语义画像显示重叠构式常在多个维度上都有强烈表征；两种重叠模式在分布空间中呈现清晰的语义与语用分化；Procrustes分析表明基词空间整体组织在重叠空间中大体保留，局部不匹配则凸显话语-语用重组区域。

Conclusion: 高维词嵌入能够恢复既有的语言学概括，捕捉普通话重叠的语义多面性与构式透明度，证明了分布语义学对复杂形态构词过程研究的有效性。

Abstract: Mandarin Chinese has two productive reduplicative constructions that repeat either two-character base words or their constituents (e.g., `in good health', `discuss a bit'). Their varied meanings have been described as realizing plurality, valence coloring, sound symbolism and pragmatic functions. The aim of this study is twofold. A first goal is to clarify whether it is possible to come to a more precise understanding of the variegated semantics of Mandarin reduplication by using word embeddings from distributional semantics. A second goal is to explore how useful embeddings are for understanding the details of a semantically complex word-formation process. We show that the embedding space recovers the semantic and grammatical properties of reduplications previously identified in the literature, validating Tencent embeddings for morphological investigation. Semantic profiling revealed that reduplicative constructions are often strongly represented on multiple dimensions. The two patterns exhibit clear semantic and pragmatic differentiation in distributional space. Procrustes analysis clarified that the overall organization of the base-word space is largely preserved in the reduplication space, with local mismatches highlighting regions of discourse-pragmatic reorganization. Taken together, these results show that high-dimensional word embeddings can recover established linguistic generalizations, and capture the semantic versatility of Mandarin reduplication and constructional transparency.

</details>


### [141] [Cascade: Hierarchical Recoverability Control for Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16890)
*Qingchen Yu,Shiying Duan,Xiaodong Li,Yuhua Wang,Zhiyu Li,Shiji Zhou,Yifan Sun,Zhaoxin Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cascade，一个层次化可恢复性控制框架，通过路径级路由、表示级压缩和解码级干预三道互补机制，降低大语言模型中目标知识在中间表示层的内省可识别性，从而在去除敏感或受版权保护知识的同时保持模型通用能力。实验在TOFU、MUSE-News和WMDP上验证该方法能有效降低可恢复性且维持稳定效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM去学习方法常在中层表示中残留目标知识，这些残留仍可通过中间表征被恢复。因此需要一种框架来最小化目标知识在模型内部的可识别性，而非仅仅在输出层层面去除。

Method: 提出Cascade层次化可恢复性控制框架，由三个互补控制组成：路径级路由抑制与隐私关联的激活路径；表示级压缩降低几何可分性；解码级干预限制残余信息的恢复。

Result: 在TOFU、MUSE-News和WMDP数据集上，包括查询改写和抽取式提示的鲁棒性测试中，Cascade有效降低目标知识的可恢复性，同时保持模型效用稳定。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合多层次内部表示控制能比单一输出层面的去学习更有效地压制知识可恢复性，在去学习强度与通用效用之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning is essential for removing sensitive or copyrighted knowledge while preserving general utility. Existing methods often leave residual knowledge in intermediate representations, which can still be recovered. To address this, we propose Cascade, a hierarchical recoverability control framework that minimizes the internal identifiability of target knowledge. Cascade combines three complementary controls: path-level routing to suppress privacy-associated activation routes, representation-level compression to reduce geometric separability, and decoding-level intervention to limit residual recovery. Experiments on TOFU, MUSE-News, and WMDP, including robustness tests with query reformulation and extraction-style prompts, show that Cascade effectively reduces recoverability while maintaining stable model utility.

</details>


### [142] [RiskChainBench: A Benchmark for Obfuscated Platform Message Restoration and Evidence-Grounded Web Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16900)
*ZhuoXin Liu,Zhiming Ma,Ying Zhang,Mengzheng Yang,Yifan Wang,Zhengqi Huang,Yanhan Zhou,Zekun Lin,Jun Zhang,Shun Zhang,Yue Chen,Qiao Zhao,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RiskChainBench基准，将3,600个混淆token文本恢复任务与600个带人工标签的本地web环境配对，构成端到端风险取证流水线：模型先恢复消息、意图与目标地址，再由同一模型作为VLM驱动web智能体调查对应网站并生成可引证的风险报告。恢复与网页调查分别评分并离线组合（以冻结的主入口预测作为门控）。十个模型上入口Top-1为35.2%~95.2%，web决策准确率26.3%~62.8%；执行失败占web运行31.9%，而决策后类型错误仅占0.9%，表明稳定探索与风险判断是主要瓶颈。已开源基准、协议与可重置本地沙箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台滥用活动使用emoji、谐音、字符拆解和冗余符号隐藏重定向指令，将用户导流至涉黄、欺诈、赌博或非法交易服务。现有基准分别评估混淆文本和风险网页，割裂了目标恢复与下游证据获取之间的联系，无法反映端到端的取证能力。

Method: 构建RiskChainBench：由600个源会话生成3,600个合成token文本恢复输入，并配对600个带人工标签的本地web环境。流水线分两阶段——模型先恢复消息、操作意图与目标地址；同一底层模型再作为VLM驱动的web智能体调查正确关联的网站并生成冻结的、带证据引用的风险报告，避免消息侧语义与域名声誉线索干扰。采用人工标签判定任务正确性，并用固定多模态证据评审器评估忠实性、充分性、完整性与一致性。恢复得分与网页调查得分分离计算，并通过将冻结的主入口预测作为门控离线组合两个任务结果。

Result: 十个模型上，入口恢复Top-1范围35.2%~95.2%，web决策准确率26.3%~62.8%；领先系统在入口恢复、完整重建、网站决策与细粒度类型划分上表现各异。执行失败占web运行31.9%，而决策后类型错误仅占0.9%，说明问题集中在探索稳定性和风险判断环节而非最终类型判定。

Conclusion: 端到端风险取证流水线的主要瓶颈是稳定的web环境探索与风险判断，而非决策后的分类精度；该基准可支持对平台滥用链路的可分解、可组合评估，并通过发布可重置本地沙箱促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Platform abuse campaigns conceal redirection instructions with emojis, homophones, character decomposition, and redundant symbols, then route users through disguised links to services associated with pornography, fraud, gambling, or illicit transactions. Existing benchmarks evaluate obfuscated text and risky webpages separately, obscuring how target recovery affects downstream evidence acquisition. We introduce RiskChainBench, pairing 3,600 synthetic token-text restoration inputs from 600 source sessions with 600 corresponding human-labeled local web environments. A model first restores the message, operational intent, and destination; the same underlying model then acts as a VLM-driven web agent that investigates the correctly associated website and produces a frozen, evidence-cited risk report without message-side semantics or domain-reputation cues. We score restoration and correct-routing web investigation separately and compose them offline by applying the frozen primary-entry prediction as a gate to the same Task 2 result. Human labels determine task correctness, while a fixed multimodal evidence judge assesses faithfulness, sufficiency, completeness, and consistency. Across ten models, Entry Top-1 ranges from 35.2% to 95.2% and web decision accuracy from 26.3% to 62.8%; the leading systems differ across entry recovery, full reconstruction, website decisions, and fine-grained typing. Execution failures account for 31.9% of web runs, whereas post-decision type errors account for only 0.9%, identifying stable exploration and risk judgment as the principal bottlenecks. We release the benchmark, protocol, and resettable local sandbox.

</details>


### [143] [Deconstructing Stereotypes: Scope-Conditioned Generation for Effective Multilingual Counterspeech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16906)
*Greta Damo,Elias Urios Alacreu,Elena Cabrio,Paolo Rosso,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的"范围条件化"（scope-conditioned）对抗言论生成框架，将结构化的刻板印象特征显式整合进大语言模型提示词中，用于生成更具针对性的反仇恨言论。研究在自建的多语种（英语、意大利语、西班牙语）人工标注数据集上验证，结果表明刻板印象条件化提示在事实性、具体性、说服力和有效性上显著优于通用基线，无论刻板印象是显式还是隐式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线仇恨言论日益泛滥，反仇恨言论（Counterspeech, CS）——通过说理和替代视角直接回应的方式——被视为内容删除的替代方案。然而现有自动CS生成方法常产生泛泛而谈、效果不佳的回复，未能针对仇恨言论背后隐含的刻板印象进行精准反击，这是本文要解决的核心痛点。

Method: 提出一个范围条件化生成框架：将结构化的刻板印象特征显式整合进大语言模型的提示词（prompt）中，使模型生成时能针对特定的刻板印象进行有的放矢的回应。同时构建了一个人工策划的三语种（英语、意大利语、西班牙语）新数据集用于验证。

Result: 大量评估表明，刻板印象条件化提示在三种语言上均显著优于通用基线，在事实性（factuality）、具体性（specificity）、说服力（cogency）和有效性（effectiveness）指标上均获得实质性提升，并且对显式和隐式刻板印象都有效。

Conclusion: 将结构化的刻板印象知识注入提示词能够有效提升自动化反仇恨言论生成的质量与针对性，为跨语言、跨情境的在线仇恨言论治理提供了一种更好的生成方案。

Abstract: Counterspeech (CS) - direct responses that counter online Hate Speech (HS) using reasoning and alternative viewpoints - has emerged as an alternative to content removal. Current automatic CS generation methods, however, frequently produce generic, ineffective replies that fail to target the implicit stereotypes behind HS. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel scope-conditioned generation framework that explicitly integrates structured stereotype characteristics into Large Language Models prompts. We validate our approach on a novel, human-curated dataset annotated in English, Italian, and Spanish. Extensive evaluations show that stereotype-conditioned prompting substantially outperforms generic baselines across all three languages, obtaining significant gains in factuality, specificity, cogency, and effectiveness for both explicit and implicit implied stereotypes.

</details>


### [144] [Lit3R: Retrieve-Relate-Read for Evidence-Grounded Question Answering over Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16912)
*Akira Ise,Kotaro Kumagai,Yuta Yamaguchi,Hisanori Ozaki,Yukio Uematsu,Ikuya Yamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了tus-nlp团队为LitTraceQA共享任务开发的Lit3R（Retrieve-Relate-Read）系统，该系统是一个基于文献的问答系统，需要检索相关论文、识别支持证据并生成答案。Lit3R无需任务特定训练，直接组合现成的检索、重排序和大型语言模型组件：检索器迭代结合BM25稀疏检索、稠密检索、交叉编码器重排序和LLM验证，并在基于问题的检索基础上增加论文间扩展；阅读器先在单篇论文内识别支持证据，再跨论文综合证据生成最终答案和证据追踪。在官方测试集上排名第4，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献接地问答（literature-grounded question answering）任务要求系统不仅要回答问题，还要检索相关论文并定位支撑证据，这对自动化科研文献分析具有重要意义。该论文旨在构建一个无需任务特定训练、可组合现成组件的系统，验证通用检索和LLM技术在此类任务上的表现。

Method: Lit3R采用两阶段（检索-阅读）pipeline架构。检索模块迭代结合BM25稀疏检索和稠密检索、交叉编码器重排序以及LLM验证，并以论文到论文（paper-to-paper）扩展方式补充基于问题的检索。阅读模块分为两步：先在单篇论文内部识别支持证据，再跨论文综合这些证据，最终生成答案和证据追踪（evidence trace）。

Result: 在官方测试集上，Lit3R在LitTraceQA排行榜上排名第4。系统代码已开源发布在GitHub上。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了无需任务特定训练的模块化检索-重排-阅读框架在文献接地问答任务上的有效性，通过迭代检索和跨论文证据综合可实现有竞争力的排名，同时开源代码便于复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: We describe tus-nlp's Lit3R (Retrieve-Relate-Read) system for LitTraceQA, a shared task for literature-grounded question answering that requires systems to retrieve relevant papers, identify supporting evidence, and generate answers. Lit3R combines off-the-shelf retrieval, reranking, and large language model (LLM) components without task-specific training. The retriever iteratively combines BM25-based sparse and dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and LLM-based verification, and complements retrieval based on the question with paper-to-paper expansion. The reader first identifies supporting evidence within individual papers and then synthesizes evidence across papers to produce the final answer and evidence trace. On the official test set, our system ranked 4th on the leaderboard. Our code is available at https://github.com/tus-ist-nlp/littraceqa.

</details>


### [145] [HUMAID-NER: A Disaster Tweet Dataset for Joint Named Entity Recognition and Event Classification via Uncertainty-Weighted Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16964)
*Aijaz Ali,Nazish Basir,Sarfaraz Nawaz,Danish Nazir Arain,Haris Ali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HUMAID-NER，这是首个基于HumAID基准构建的灾害推文命名实体识别数据集，包含6万条英文灾害推文、约17.5万个实体跨度，涵盖十种面向运营的实体类型，并采用BIO标注格式。作者同时提出一种联合多任务学习框架，通过共享RoBERTa-large编码器同时进行灾害命名实体识别与人道主义事件分类，在验证集上分别取得0.841的NER跨度微F1和0.761的分类宏F1，并配套发布实时Web仪表盘及完整代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义响应对从社交媒体快速提取结构化信息至关重要，但现有灾害推文资源主要提供文档级类别标签，缺乏跨度级实体标注。这一标注层级缺失限制了细粒度信息抽取能力，因此亟需构建包含跨度级实体标注的灾害推文数据集。

Method: 采用可复现的三阶段混合标注流水线生成标注，包括spaCy transformer模型、灾害领域EntityRuler模式和结构化正则表达式，并结合优先级冲突消解。在此基础上提出联合多任务学习框架，使用共享RoBERTa-large编码器执行灾害NER与事件分类，通过同方差不确定性加权与可学习任务参数降低联合训练的任务冲突，并采用两阶段训练策略（第二阶段冻结24层编码器中的18层）。

Result: 在HUMAID-NER验证集上，系统同时实现NER跨度微F1为0.841、事件分类宏F1为0.761。数据集包含6万条推文，约17.5万个标注实体跨度，覆盖十种实体类型。

Conclusion: 所提框架能同时高效完成灾害NER与事件分类，且无需牺牲性能；数据集、模型与流水线代码全部开源，通过实时Web仪表盘展示端到端部署能力，为危机信息学领域的可复现研究与后续工作奠定基础。

Abstract: Rapid extraction of structured information from social media is important for humanitarian response, yet existing disaster tweet resources mainly provide document-level category labels without span-level entity annotations. We introduce HUMAID-NER, the first named entity recognition dataset built on the HumAID benchmark, containing 60,000 English disaster tweets annotated in BIO format across ten operationally motivated entity types and yielding approximately 175,000 labelled entity spans. Annotations are generated through a reproducible three-stage hybrid pipeline combining a spaCy transformer model, disaster-domain EntityRuler patterns, and structured regular expressions with priority-based overlap resolution. We also propose a joint multitask learning framework that performs disaster-specific named entity recognition and humanitarian event classification using a shared RoBERTa-large encoder. To reduce task conflict during joint training, the model uses homoscedastic uncertainty weighting with learnable task parameters and a two-stage training schedule that freezes the lower 18 of 24 encoder layers in the second stage. On the HUMAID-NER validation set, the proposed system achieves NER span micro-F1 of 0.841 and classification macro-F1 of 0.761 simultaneously. A real-time web dashboard demonstrates end-to-end deployment. The dataset, models, and pipeline code are released to support reproducibility and future crisis informatics research.

</details>


### [146] [Target-Language Generation in Multilingual Models: Activation Steering and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16967)
*James A. Michaelov,Carmen Amo Alonso,Tyler A. Chang,Roger P. Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于多语言模型目标语言文本生成的最优控制方法，并构建了评估文本质量（语言遵循度、语言连贯性、语义连贯性）的框架。实验表明，该方法的性能在大多数测试模型上不逊于著名的差异均值激活引导方法（difference-in-means activation steering），且所需超参数调优大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语言模型在生成特定目标语言的流畅文本方面存在重大挑战，现有激活引导方法（如difference-in-means）虽然有效但需要大量超参数调优，成本高昂且难以推广。

Method: 开发了一种基于最优控制（optimal control）的目标语言文本生成方法，并构建了包含语言遵循度、语言连贯性和语义连贯性三个维度的文本质量评估框架。

Result: 在大多数测试模型中，该方法的表现至少与差异均值激活引导方法相当，且显著减少了超参数调优的工作量。

Conclusion: 最优控制方法为多语言目标语言生成提供了一种更高效、更少调参需求的替代方案，在性能相当的同时大幅降低了使用成本。

Abstract: Ensuring that multilingual language models generate coherent text in a specific target language is a major issue in multilingual language modeling. We develop an optimal control method for target-language text generation as well as a framework for evaluating the quality of generated text in terms of language adherence, linguistic coherence, and semantic coherence. We find that the proposed method performs at least as well as the prominent difference-in-means activation steering method for the majority of models tested, with substantially less hyperparameter tuning required.

</details>


### [147] [Nameless Tokenization: A Lossless Tokenizer-Level Defense Against Control-Token Forgery in Open-Weight LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16984)
*Kisu Yang,Yoonna Jang,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现开放权重大语言模型的对话模板字符串可被伪造：由于发布聊天模板、且分词器将字符串映射回保留标识符，任何能控制提示词文本的攻击者都能写出与服务栈生成的、无法区分的回合边界（注入攻击）。作者审计了256个已部署的聊天分词器，全部可伪造，而常用的修复标志仍使56.6%可伪造，因为它遗漏了智能体系统依赖的工具与推理标记。作者提出"无名分词化"（nameless tokenization）：控制项仅保留保留标识符、无表面字符串，使内容编码器无法发射该串，消息内容得以原样到达模型。在五个分词器族上，该方法在无攻击数据上精确复现标准token流，并将含分隔符文本探针的准确率从8.5%提升至59.9%（而净化器会损失准确率）。将分隔符的外观与标识符解耦表明：面对裸任务指令时标识符作用甚微，但在系统消息指示模型把用户内容当作数据处理时，标识符承担了大部分伪造工具结果及绝大部分伪造回合的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型公开了其聊天模板中标记回合、角色与工具结果的字符串，分词器将这些字符串映射回模型遵循的保留标识符。因此任何能控制提示词文本的人都能写出与服务栈生成的、无法区分的回合边界，造成提示注入风险。作者通过审计大规模部署分词器验证该威胁的普遍性，并指出现有修复建议不完整。

Method: 作者审计了256个部署中的聊天分词器以量化伪造可乘之机，指出"禁止字符串出现"这一常规修复因遗漏工具与推理标记仍留下大量可伪造面。随后提出"无名分词化"：为控制项仅分配保留标识符而不绑定表面字符串，使内容编码器无法发射该字符串，从而消息内容原样抵达模型。作者在五种分词器族上验证该方法，并将分隔符的"外观"（appearance）与其"标识符"（identifier）解耦，设计探针以分离二者对伪造攻击的影响。

Result: 256个部署分词器全部可伪造；通常推荐的修复标志仍使56.6%可伪造。无名分词化在五种分词器族上于无攻击数据上完全复现标准token流，在含分隔符文本的探针上把准确率从8.5%提升到59.9%（而净化器反而损失准确率）。解耦实验表明：在裸任务指令下标识符作用微小；而一旦系统消息指示模型将用户内容视为数据，标识符承担了大部分伪造工具结果的效果，并承担了绝大部分任何伪造回合的效果。

Conclusion: 回合/角色/工具边界伪造是开放权重模型普遍且未被完整修复的安全漏洞。作者提出的无名分词化通过剥离保留标识符的表面字符串，在保持兼容性（攻击无关数据上token流完全一致）的同时显著提升对含分隔符（即带边界标记）文本的处理鲁棒性。实验进一步厘清了"外观安全"与"标识符安全"的不同防御价值：标识符对伪造工具结果与（在系统指令引导下）伪造回合的攻击贡献最大，因此保留标识符的内部隔离仍具关键作用。

Abstract: Open-weight language models publish the strings their chat templates use to mark turns, roles and tool results, which the tokenizer maps back to the reserved identifiers the model obeys. Anyone who controls text in a prompt can therefore write a turn boundary indistinguishable from one the serving stack wrote. We audit 256 deployed chat tokenizers. All are forgeable, and the flag usually recommended as a fix leaves 56.6% forgeable because it misses the tool and reasoning markers agent systems rely on. We propose nameless tokenization, which leaves the control entries with a reserved identifier and no surface string, so the content encoder cannot emit one and message content reaches the model unaltered. Across five tokenizer families it reproduces the standard token stream exactly on attack-free data and lifts accuracy on a probe of delimiter-bearing text from 8.5% to 59.9%, where sanitizers lose it. Separating a delimiter's appearance from its identifier shows the identifier matters little against a bare task instruction, but carries most of a forged tool result and most of any forged turn once the system message tells the model to treat user content as data.

</details>


### [148] [Autoformalizing Argumentative Material Inferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16991)
*Xin Quan,Reto Gubelmann,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将论证性自然语言的自动形式化建模为"守卫补全"任务：将非单调的实质支持转化为基于显式构造守卫集的单调形式推理。通过GUARD神经符号框架（LLM构造候选守卫+Isabelle/HOL验证并反馈），结合前提依赖性与结论选择性的对比测试，在Debatepedia和ARCT数据集上相比SOTA LLM定理证明方法，在验证保真度上提升+35.3/+32.9点，泄漏降低-25.9/-21.9点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言论证在形式显式化之前就具有说服力：前提通过可废止的保证（defeasible warrants）、背景承诺（background commitments）和隐含的例外条件（exception conditions）来支持主张。但形式验证需要相反的处理——不仅要把给定句子翻译为逻辑，还需构造缺失的承诺。然而构造存在翻译所没有的风险：自由添加前提的系统可令任何主张可证，而形式有效证明可能直接断言主张、在不依赖原前提时证明它，或证明超出主张本身的内容。这一保真性风险是核心研究动机。

Method: 将论证性实质推理的自动形式化定义为守卫补全（guard completion），即把非单调的实质支持转化为相对显式构造守卫集的单调形式推理。补全仅在证明既通过定理证明器、又通过前提依赖性和结论选择性的对比测试（contrastive tests）时才被接受。实现为GUARD神经符号框架：LLM构造并形式化候选守卫，Isabelle/HOL验证生成的理论并返回步骤级反馈用于迭代精化，无法达到忠实补全时系统弃权（abstain）。

Result: 在Debatepedia和ARCT上使用不同LLM的实验表明：GUARD相比当前最优的LLM驱动定理证明方法，在验证-忠实（verified-faithful）指标上显著提升（+35.3和+32.9点），在泄漏（leakage）上大幅降低（-25.9和-21.9点）。消融显示符号软批判（soft critique）和显式假设层贡献了大部分增益，其中软批判还提升了所引出上下文的初始有效性，并减少成功验证所需的迭代次数。

Conclusion: 论证认为通过守卫补全与神经符号结合（LLM构造+形式验证器反馈+对比保真测试+弃权机制），可以在形式化论证推理中同时实现可验证性与论证保真度，防止系统通过任意添加前提而"作弊"，并以实证展示了该方法较纯LLM定理证明的显著优势。

Abstract: Natural language arguments are compelling before they are formally explicit. A premise supports a claim through defeasible warrants, background commitments, and exception conditions that the text leaves implicit. However, formal verification requires the opposite. Making such arguments machine-checkable requires constructing the missing commitments, not only translating given sentences into logic. Construction, however, carries a risk that translation does not: a system free to add premises can make any claim provable, and a formally valid proof may assert the claim outright, prove it without the original premise, or establish more than the claim itself. We address this problem by formulating autoformalization for argumentative material inference as guard completion, in which non-monotonic material support is turned into monotonic formal inference relative to an explicitly constructed guard set. A completion is accepted only when its proof both passes the theorem prover and survives contrastive tests of premise dependence and claim selectivity. We implement this formulation in GUARD, a neuro-symbolic framework in which LLMs construct and formalize candidate guards, Isabelle/HOL verifies the resulting theories and returns step-level feedback for iterative refinement, and the system abstains when no faithful completion can be reached. Our empirical results on Debatepedia and ARCT using different LLMs demonstrate that GUARD yields significant improvements in verified-faithful (+35.3, +32.9 points) and substantial reductions in leakage (-25.9, -21.9 points) over the state-of-the-art LLM-driven theorem proving approach. Moreover, we show that the symbolic soft critique and the explicit assumption layer account for most of these gains, with the soft critique also improving the initial validity of the elicited context and reducing the number of iterations required for successful verification.

</details>


### [149] [The Role of Implicit and Explicit Demographic Signals in Large Language Model-based Student Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16993)
*Donya Rooein,Luca Benedetto,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在教育评估中如何受学生人口统计学特征影响。通过设计显式（直接提及人口统计信息）和隐式（通过对话历史传递）两种受控提示，在自动作文评分、形成性反馈和元语言问答三个任务中测试了六个SOTA LLM。结果显示模型既能感知并利用人口统计线索（如根据教育程度调整反馈可读性），也会产生不可预测的偏见（如对低教育水平用户的回答给予更低情感分数），为LLM在教育评估中的人口统计学敏感性提供了明确证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM已广泛应用于学生评估，但对学生人口统计学特征如何影响其使用知之甚少。考虑人口统计学特征有时是必要的（如为低教育水平用户提升可读性），但也可能成为歧视的来源（如对低社会经济背景学生打出更低分数），因此需要系统探究LLM在评估任务中对人口统计线索的敏感性与风险。

Method: 研究者设计了两种受控提示条件：1)显式条件——直接提及人口统计学细节；2)隐式条件——利用对话历史作为人口统计学信号。在三个任务（自动作文评分、形成性反馈、元语言问答）中对六个SOTA LLM进行了系统测试，以比较两种条件下模型行为差异。

Result: 在显式和隐式两种条件下，模型均会捕捉人口统计线索并相应调整评分、反馈和回答。具体而言：当显式提及教育水平时，LLM频繁调整反馈的可读性以适应教育程度；而隐式条件下则产生不可预测的偏见，例如在问答任务中，来自低教育水平用户的回答获得了更低的情感分数。

Conclusion: 研究为LLM在教育评估任务中的人口统计学敏感性提供了清晰证据。显式的人口统计信息可被合理利用（如调整可读性），但隐式条件下产生的偏见不可预测且难以控制，凸显了在教育评估中部署LLM时需要警惕潜在歧视风险，并需建立相应的治理与校准机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models are now common in student assessment, but we know little about how student demographics affect their use. Sometimes, considering student demographics may be necessary -- for example, to improve readability for users with lower educational levels. However, it also risks being a cause of discrimination, e.g., when assigning lower scores to students from lower socioeconomic backgrounds. We set up controlled prompts to test 1) explicit demographic effects, where we mention demographic details directly, and 2) implicit effects, where we use conversation history as a demographic signal. We test these settings in three tasks: Automated Essay Scoring, Formative Feedback, and Metalinguistic Question Answering. We test six state-of-the-art LLMs on these tasks. In both explicit and implicit cases, the models pick up on demographic cues and can change their scoring, feedback, and answers accordingly. We find that LLMs frequently adjust the readability of feedback to education levels when these are explicitly mentioned. On the other hand, implicit conditions produce unpredictable biases, such as in question answering, where responses from lower-education levels receive lower sentiment scores. Our results provide clear evidence of demographic sensitivity in LLMs for educational assessment tasks.

</details>


### [150] [PaperDoctor: Evidence-Grounded and Actionable Feedback for Scientific Papers in Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16995)
*Kevin Qinghong Lin,Siyuan Hu,Pan Lu,Yu Chen,Yanzhe Chen,Owen Queen,Yupeng Chen,Jialin Yu,Junchi Yu,Zifeng Ding,Yuanfeng Ji,Sheng Liu,Jindong Gu,Linjie Li,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PaperDoctor 是一个面向投稿前反馈的智能体框架，将自动化论文评审从"裁判"转变为"诊断者"。它通过三层层次化评估（L1 表面筛查、L2 类型化验证器路由证据、L3 按优先级重跑实验），为每条发现提供可审计、可操作的反馈（观察、具体证据定位、修改建议），并基于声明重要性和计算预算选择性重建与重跑实验。在 30 篇进行中论文上取得 70.6% 的一致率和全部正向整体评分，在 40 篇多学科手稿上较人工与智能体评审者产生更可审计的反馈，并配套了交互式界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究智能体可能让有缺陷的声明以大规模方式进入文献。人类顾问能通过仔细、可追溯的反馈发现草稿中的问题，但此类顾问式评估需要大量人工劳动、无法规模化。现有自动化论文评估更像"裁判"给出判定而非"诊断"，缺少可追溯、可操作的反馈机制，因此需要将评估范式转向可扩展的诊断式反馈。

Method: PaperDoctor 提出三个关键创新：(1) 整体层次化框架——通过 L1 表面筛查、L2 类型化验证器（将每条声明路由到相应证据）、L3 复现器（按优先级重跑实验）三层评估写作、布局、参考文献、代码、理论、先前工作与实验；(2) 每条发现包含观察、指向具体证据（句子、公式或代码行）的指针以及修改建议，使批评可审计、可操作；(3) 基于声明重要性与计算预算选择性重建并重跑实验，揭示手稿本身不可见的复现差距与定量局限。同时开发交互式界面供作者浏览基于其论文的发现。

Result: 在 30 篇进行中论文上评估，取得 70.6% 的一致率和全部正向整体评分；在涵盖机器学习、自然科学和社会科学、含人工与 AI 撰写的带代码论文的 40 篇手稿上评估。总体上，PaperDoctor 相比人工和其他智能体评审者产生更可审计的反馈，其批评天然配对各具体建议，并补充了人工评审者常忽视的维度。

Conclusion: PaperDoctor 将自动化论文评估重新定位为"诊断而非判决"，将反馈模式从裁判式判定转向诊断式分析，为更严谨的 AI 辅助科学发现提供了迈向 AI 顾问的具体一步。其贡献在于将人工顾问的可追溯反馈能力扩展为可规模化、可操作的智能体评审框架。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents are reshaping the research ecosystem, but they can also let flawed claims enter the literature at scale. Human advisors catch such issues in drafts through careful, traceable feedback, yet advisor-style assessment requires extensive manual effort and does not scale. To shift automated paper assessment from a judge to a diagnostician, we introduce PaperDoctor, an agent framework for pre-submission feedback with three key innovations. First, a holistic hierarchical framework evaluates writing, layout, references, code, theory, prior work, and experiments through three layers: L1 surface screening, L2 typed verifiers that route each claim to the appropriate evidence, and L3 reproducers that rerun experiments by priority. Second, each finding contains an observation, a pointer to specific evidence such as a sentence, equation, or code line, and a revision suggestion, making critiques auditable and actionable. Third, PaperDoctor selectively rebuilds and reruns experiments based on claim importance and compute budget, surfacing reproducibility gaps and quantitative limitations that are invisible from the manuscript alone. We evaluate PaperDoctor on 30 in-progress papers, yielding 70.6% agreement and all positive holistic scores, and on 40 manuscripts across machine learning, natural science, and social science, covering human- and AI-authored papers with code. Overall, PaperDoctor produces more auditable feedback than human and other agentic reviewers, pairs critiques with concrete suggestions by design, and complements dimensions often overlooked by human reviewers. We also develop an interactive interface that lets authors browse findings grounded in their paper. PaperDoctor reframes automated paper assessment as diagnosis rather than verdict, taking a concrete step toward AI advisors for more rigorous AI-assisted scientific discovery.

</details>


### [151] [Can LLMs Follow the Pulse of a Crisis? Evaluating Crisis Sentiment in Bangladesh's July Uprising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16997)
*Md. Samiul Alim,Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Sharjil Khan,Rafia Ferdous Duti,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了UNRESTSENT200K，一个包含约20万条孟加拉语危机评论（来自2024年7-8月孟加拉国起义的Facebook和YouTube）的情感分析数据集。该数据集覆盖五个事件对齐阶段，所有评论均由14位母语标注者人工标注，信度良好（kappa=0.73, alpha=0.71，盲审一致率94.2%）。作者对微调编码器、提示LLM和LoRA微调LLM进行了基准测试，发现父帖上下文能稳定提升性能，而阶段间的时间偏移导致性能大幅下降；强大LLM表现良好，但仍难以应对讽刺、隐晦政治指涉和随阶段变化的语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机情感分析对孟加拉语这类低资源语言尤为困难，因为语言、语境和公众反应变化迅速。目前缺乏同时包含大规模数据、事件阶段对齐、话语上下文标记的高质量危机情感数据集，难以支撑上下文感知和时间鲁棒性的研究。

Method: 构建UNRESTSENT200K数据集：收集约20万条来自Facebook和YouTube的评论，覆盖五个事件对齐阶段（从早期升级、互联网封锁到政权更迭及后续洪灾危机）；每条评论关联其父帖以便支持有无话语上下文的评估；采用完全人工标注流程，由14位母语孟加拉语标注者并辅以资深校验；并用kappa、alpha和盲审一致性评估标注质量。同时基准测试三种方法：微调编码器、提示式LLM和LoRA微调LLM。

Result: 父帖上下文在所有评测中稳定提升情感分类性能；跨阶段的时间偏移导致性能大幅下降，揭示模型对时间鲁棒性的不足；性能较强的LLM仍难以应对讽刺、隐晦政治指涉及阶段依赖性的语义变化。标注质量达到substantial agreement水平。

Conclusion: UNRESENTSENT200K为低资源危机话语中的上下文感知和时间鲁棒情感分析提供了基准与公开资源，可用于推动相关研究。

Abstract: Crisis sentiment analysis is especially challenging for low-resource languages such as Bangla, where language, context, and public reaction shift rapidly. We introduce UNRESTSENT200K, a Bangla crisis sentiment dataset with approximately 200K Facebook and YouTube comments from the July-August 2024 Bangladesh uprising. The dataset covers five event-aligned phases, from early escalation and internet blackout to regime transition and a later flood crisis. Each comment is linked to its parent post, enabling evaluation with and without discourse context. All comments are annotated through a fully human process involving 14 native Bangla-speaking annotators and senior validation, achieving substantial agreement (kappa = 0.73, alpha = 0.71) and 94.2% blind-audit agreement. We benchmark fine-tuned encoders, prompted LLMs, and LoRA-tuned LLMs. Results show that parent-post context consistently improves performance, while temporal shift across phases causes large performance drops. Strong LLMs perform well, but still struggle with sarcasm, implicit political references, and phase-dependent meaning. UNRESTSENT200K provides a benchmark for studying context-aware and temporally robust sentiment analysis in low-resource crisis discourse. UNRESTSENT200K is available at https://sami0055.github.io/UNRESTSENT200K/

</details>


### [152] [Diagnosing the Fact-Grounding Gap in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17043)
*Kevin Mo,Nathan Mo,Richard Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多跳问答系统在单个推理步骤层面的失败模式，发现失败分为检索失败（相关段落未被检索到）和抽取失败（段落被检索到但无法抽取所需事实），后者约占所有单跳缺陷的一半，且标准检索指标无法察觉，现有检索干预手段也无法解决，构成检索改进的天花板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答系统能力不断提升，但其失败通常被归因于未找到正确的文档。然而这种归因是否在单个推理步骤层面成立，尚未被充分检验。作者旨在系统性地调查多跳QA失败的根本原因，区分检索层面与事实抽取层面的瓶颈。

Method: 在三个标准多跳QA基准上，对每个推理步骤逐跳分析失败来源，将失败分解为两类：检索失败（所需段落未被检索）和抽取失败（段落已检索但无法抽取所需事实），后者被称为"事实接地差距"（fact-grounding gap）。同时测试多种检索干预手段以验证其对两类失败的影响。

Result: 抽取失败占所有单跳缺陷的近一半，且标准检索指标无法察觉；所有被测试的检索干预手段都无法解决抽取失败，从而确立了检索类改进的性能天花板。该差距的严重程度因基准和问题类型而异，但在所有测量数据集上均出现抽取失败。

Conclusion: 检索失败与抽取失败是根本不同的瓶颈，需要不同的解决方案。当前评估实践中缺少这一区分，需要将两者分开考量，以更准确地诊断多跳问答系统的失败并指导改进方向。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires combining information from multiple documents to answer complex questions. These systems have grown increasingly capable, yet when they fail, the error is typically attributed to not finding the right documents. Whether this holds at the level of individual reasoning steps remains largely unexamined. We investigate this across three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks and find that failures decompose into two distinct modes: retrieval failures, where the needed passage was not retrieved, and extraction failures, where the passage was retrieved but the needed fact could not be extracted - a phenomenon we term the fact-grounding gap. Extraction failures account for nearly half of all per-hop deficiencies and are invisible to standard retrieval metrics. They remain unresolved by every retrieval intervention we test, establishing a ceiling for retrieval-only improvements. The gap's severity varies across benchmarks and question types, but extraction failures appear on every dataset we measure. Our findings reveal that retrieval failures and extraction failures are fundamentally different bottlenecks requiring different solutions - a distinction absent from current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [153] [EviScope: Paired Counterfactual Evidence Diagnostics for Faithful and Efficient Grounded Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17081)
*Suryadeep Singh Deswal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EviScope——一种成对反事实评测基准，通过固定问题、同时增删/干扰/矛盾证据来检验语言模型系统的“证据支持能力”而非仅看最终答案准确率。在960个gold-blind生成样本上（Qwen2.5-7B、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 3.5 Flash），发现成对指标能揭示被答案准确率掩盖的退化行为：本地开放模型上的显式证据-动作门控在QCS上反而低于vanilla RAG，而Gemini虽达到0.944联合成功率，仍在5%的冲突案例中给出答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索增强的落地语言模型系统通常只以最终答案准确率作为评估指标，然而“答案正确”并不等于“推理过程正确”——正确答案可能缺乏证据支撑、来自错误来源，或在证据不足/矛盾时仍被强行生成。这掩盖了系统性缺陷，因此需要一个能区分“无证据作答、冲突盲点、错误的非作答动作”的评估框架。

Method: 构建EviScope成对反事实基准：保持问题不变，对证据进行增(adding)、删(removing)、干扰(distracting)、矛盾(contradicting)四种操作，形成四条件四元组(quartet)。EviScope-v1.1包含40个四条件四元组，配备修复后的反事实声明(repared counterfactual claims)与span级支持标签(span-level support labels)以支持自动评估。在Qwen2.5-7B、Llama 3.1 8B和Gemini 3.5 Flash上开展960个gold-blind（盲测）生成实验，使用成对指标与QCS（问题-证据一致性）进行对比。

Result: 成对指标揭示出答案准确率所掩盖的模型依赖型grounding行为：在两个本地开放模型上，显式证据-动作门控在QCS上表现反而低于vanilla RAG（Qwen为0.15 vs 0.50，Llama为0.10 vs 0.375）；Gemini在两种提示下均达到0.944的联合成功率，但仍在矛盾插入后于5%的冲突案例中继续作答。

Conclusion: EviScope能够区分三类缺陷：无证据支持作答(unsupported answering)、冲突盲点(conflict blindness)以及错误的非作答动作(wrong non-answer actions)，而不是单纯对答案进行评分。该基准可作为评估grounded LLM系统证据忠实性的更严格评测工具。

Abstract: Grounded language-model systems are often evaluated by final answer accuracy, yet a correct answer can be unsupported, drawn from the wrong source, or produced when evidence is insufficient or contradictory. We introduce EviScope, a paired counterfactual benchmark that holds the question fixed while adding, removing, distracting, or contradicting its evidence. EviScope-v1.1 contains 40 four-condition quartets with repaired counterfactual claims and span-level support labels for automatic evaluation. Across 960 gold-blind generations from Qwen2.5-7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, paired metrics expose model-dependent grounding behavior that answer accuracy hides. On two local open models, an explicit evidence-action gate underperforms vanilla RAG on QCS: 0.15 vs. 0.50 for Qwen and 0.10 vs. 0.375 for Llama. Gemini reaches 0.944 joint success under both prompts, yet still answers 5% of conflict cases after contradiction insertion. EviScope therefore distinguishes unsupported answering, conflict blindness, and wrong non-answer actions rather than scoring answers alone.

</details>


### [154] [An Empirical Study of Counterfactual Self-Explanations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17119)
*Giannis Kalyvas,Giorgos Filandrianos,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Vassilis Lyberatos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）的自我解释忠实性问题，通过反事实自我解释（即模型最小化编辑输入使其自身预测改变）评估了LLaMA-3和Qwen-2.5两个系列共十个指令微调模型在情感分析和自然语言推断任务上的表现，从忠实性、最小性和与人类标注理由的一致性三个维度衡量。结果表明模型规模是解释质量的最强决定因素：更大的模型更可能生成翻转自身预测且针对决策相关证据的反事实；而基于理由引导的条件能产生编辑最小且更符合人类的对齐反事实，但未持续改善忠实性。总体而言，反事实自我解释能提供有用的行为证据，但其可靠性高度依赖模型容量，应经验验证而非想当然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能够轻易为其自身输出生成解释，但这种自我解释未必忠实于模型的实际行为。鉴于此，研究者希望通过反事实自我解释这一途径来严格检验模型自我解释的忠实性，探究什么因素决定了解释质量。

Method: 采用反事实自我解释方法：让模型对输入进行最小编辑，使得模型自身的预测发生翻转。实验涉及情感分析和自然语言推断两个任务，评估了LLaMA-3和Qwen-2.5家族共十个指令微调模型，从忠实性（反事实是否真的翻转自身预测）、最小性（编辑是否最小）以及与人标注理由的一致性三个维度进行衡量，并对比了理由引导条件。

Result: 模型规模是最强的解释质量决定因素：更大的模型更可能生成能翻转自身预测且针对决策相关证据的反事实。理由引导条件产生的反事实具有编辑最小性且更符合人类标注，但并未一致地提升忠实性。

Conclusion: 反事实自我解释可为模型决策提供有价值的行为证据，但其可靠性高度依赖于模型容量，应当通过经验验证来评估，而非假定其忠实可靠。

Abstract: Large language models can easily generate explanations for their own outputs, but such self-explanations are not necessarily faithful to the model's behavior. We study this issue through counterfactual self-explanations, where a model minimally edits an input so that its own prediction changes. Across sentiment analysis and natural language inference, we evaluate ten instruction-tuned models from the LLaMA-3 and Qwen-2.5 families, measuring faithfulness, minimality, and alignment with human-annotated rationales. Our results show that model scale is the strongest determinant of explanation quality: larger models are substantially more likely to generate counterfactuals that flip their own predictions and target decision-relevant evidence. In contrast, the rationale-guided condition produces edit-minimal counterfactuals that are also more human-aligned. However, it does not consistently improve faithfulness. Overall, counterfactual self-explanations can provide useful behavioral evidence about model decisions, but their reliability depends strongly on model capacity and should be empirically validated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [155] [Psychological Effects of Cultural Upheavals from Millions of Song Lyrics Over 100 Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17225)
*David M. Markowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了跨越一个多世纪的数百万首歌曲歌词，考察越南战争、9/11恐怖袭击和新冠疫情三大文化剧变前后艺术家（个体内与个体间）的困扰信号。相比五年基线，越战和9/11后自我指称（心理疏离的标志）显著减少；剧变后认知加工词汇升高，表明艺术家试图为大规模破坏寻找意义；内容模式与此一致——艺术家在剧变后更多书写"生命与自由"（社会状况）、更少书写"求爱与夜生活"（人际联结）。结论认为文化剧变会改变个体与集体言语行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究探讨文化剧变对语言模式的影响，但很少有研究在大规模流行媒体上隔离考察剧变对个体的影响。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过歌词这一流行媒介在个体层面（艺术家内/间）量化验证剧变的心理效应。

Method: 收集跨越一个多世纪的数百万首歌曲歌词，以五年基线为对照，考察越南战争、9/11和新冠疫情三类剧变。分析了个体内（within-artist）与个体间（between-artist）信号：以自我指称频率作为心理疏离的标记，以认知加工词汇作为意义建构的标记，并结合内容主题（生命与自由 vs. 求爱与夜生活）进行模式验证。

Result: 越战和9/11后自我指称率显著低于基线；剧变后认知加工词汇较剧变前升高；内容上剧变后"生命与自由"增加、"求爱与夜生活"减少，与上述指标相互印证。

Conclusion: 文化剧变不仅影响社会宏观层面，也会切实改变个体与集体的言语行为，表明其对社会的影响力深远而广泛。

Abstract: Cultural upheavals impact many aspects of social life, and many studies have investigated their impact on language patterns. However, few investigations have isolated the impact of upheavals on individuals at scale in popular media. The current work evaluated millions of song lyrics spanning more than a century in search of within-artist and between-artist signals of distress from the Vietnam War, the terrorist attacks of 9/11, and COVID-19. Compared to a five-year baseline, rates of self-references - a marker of psychological distancing - were significantly reduced after the Vietnam War and September 11th. Cognitive processing terms were elevated post-upheaval vs. pre-upheaval, which indicated artists' increased attempts to make meaning from such massive disruptions. Content patterns corroborated these findings as artists wrote more about "life and freedom" (societal conditions) and less about "courtship and nightlife" (interpersonal connection) following the upheavals. Cultural upheavals modify individual and collective verbal behavior, demonstrating their far-reaching impact on society.

</details>


### [156] [AraMIP: Extending MIPVU Towards Metaphor Identification in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17235)
*Mandar Marathe,Manar Ali,Sara Nabhani,Raia Abu Ahmad,Ibrahim Baroud,Omar Momen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出阿拉伯语隐喻识别程序（AraMIP），这是基于MIPVU框架并针对阿拉伯语语言特性进行改编的隐喻标注指南。研究者区分了阿拉伯语三大比喻类型（istiarah隐喻、kinaya转喻/间接表达、tashbih明喻），并标注了300个句子（5277词）的试点数据集，揭示了形态复杂性、词典义序不一致及缺乏标准语境材料等阿拉伯语特有挑战，为阿拉伯语比喻语言标准化注释和更大规模资源构建迈出第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻研究与语言创造力、语言使用和认知过程密切相关，但尽管英语及其他语言在隐喻识别和标注方面已有较多投入，阿拉伯语在此领域资源严重不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，为阿拉伯语提供标准化隐喻标注指南。

Method: 作者在广泛应用的MIPVU（Vrije Universiteit隐喻识别程序）框架基础上构建AraMIP，融入针对阿拉伯语语言特性（形态复杂性等）的改编。区分三类阿拉伯语比喻语言：Isti'ara（隐喻）、kinaya（转喻/间接表达）和tashbih（明喻），并据此标注了包含300个句子、5277词的试点数据集。

Result: 标注分析揭示了阿拉伯语特有的关键挑战，包括形态复杂性、词典义项排序的不一致性，以及缺乏面向标注者的标准化语境材料。该试点数据集为更广泛的标注实践奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 这项工作为阿拉伯语比喻实例的标准化迈出了第一步，有助于开发更大规模的标注资源，从而支持阿拉伯语比喻语言的未来研究。

Abstract: Metaphor research has gained increasing attention due to its relevance to linguistic creativity, language use, cognitive processes, and related areas. While many efforts have been devoted to metaphor identification and annotation in English and other languages, Arabic remains under-resourced in this area. In this work, we propose the Arabic Metaphor Identification Procedure (AraMIP), a novel guideline for Arabic metaphor annotation. AraMIP builds on the widely used Metaphor Identification Procedure Vrije Universiteit (MIPVU) framework, incorporating adaptations that accounts for the language-specific properties of Arabic. We distinguish three major types of Arabic figurative language: Isti'ara (metaphor), kinaya (metonymy/indirect expression), and tashbih (simile), and annotate a pilot dataset of 300 sentences (5277 words). Our analysis reveals key challenges specific to Arabic, including morphological complexity, inconsistencies in dictionary sense ordering, and the absence of standardized contextual materials for annotators. This work contributes a first step toward standardized Arabic figurative instances and facilitates the development of larger annotated resources, thereby supporting future research on figurative language in Arabic.

</details>


### [157] [ECHO: Early-layer Collaborative Hierarchical Orchestration with Bonus Logits in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17241)
*Ziyang Ma,Zihong Zhang,Zuchao Li,Lefei Zhang,Baoyuan Qi,Siqi Li,Simin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECHO 是一种针对无草稿模型投机解码的分层双循环框架，利用LLM不同层在判别效率与分布权威性上的功能不对称：高频内循环借助早期层的bonus logits进行低成本的多步草稿树探索，低频外循环通过状态复用机制执行全模型权威验证，并利用最终层bonus logits修正现有路径和补充高置信候选。实验表明其在多样化基准上显著提升平均接受token数，实现2.4×~2.9×加速，超越现有SOTA基线，且几乎无需工程开销和额外部署参数，但依赖一次性微调以获得最优加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无草稿模型投机解码虽为高效LLM推理提供了有前景的路径，但常常受限于过时的草稿候选以及验证的高计算成本，因此需要一种新框架来同时解决候选陈旧性和验证开销这两个核心问题。

Method: 利用LLM层间功能不对称性（早期层高判别效率、最终层权威分布），将推理拆分为高频内循环和低频外循环。内循环中，早期层bonus logits以极低成本驱动快速多步草稿树探索；外循环通过状态复用机制执行权威的全模型验证，同时利用最终层bonus logits修正已有路径并为后续循环补充高置信度候选。

Result: 在多样化基准上的实验表明，ECHO显著提升平均接受token数，实现2.4×~2.9×的加速，超越现有SOTA基线，且几乎没有工程开销、无需额外部署参数，代价是需要一次性微调以获得最优加速效果。代码已开源。

Conclusion: ECHO通过层级化双循环设计有效解决了无草稿投机解码中草稿陈旧与验证昂贵两大难题，在加速、开销和参数效率上均具优势，是现有SOTA方法的有力替代方案。

Abstract: While draft-model-free speculative decoding offers a promising path to efficient LLM inference, it is frequently constrained by stale draft candidates and the high computational cost of the verification. To address these challenges, we propose ECHO, a hierarchical dual-loop framework that exploits the functional asymmetry between LLM layers. Leveraging the high discriminative efficiency of early layers and the authoritative distribution of final layers, ECHO bifurcates inference into a high-frequency inner loop and a low-frequency outer loop. Within the inner loop, early-layer bonus logits drive rapid, multi-step draft-tree exploration at a minimal cost. Simultaneously, the outer loop performs authoritative full-model verification through a state-reuse mechanism. Crucially, the outer loop also utilizes final-layer bonus logits to correct existing paths and supplement the tree with high-confidence candidates for subsequent cycles. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that ECHO significantly boosts mean accepted tokens and achieves a 2.4$\times$ to 2.9$\times$ speedup, outperforming existing state-of-the-art baselines with negligible engineering overhead and no extra deployment parameters, albeit with a one-shot fine-tuning dependency for optimal acceleration. The code is available at https://github.com/whucs21Mzy/ECHO.

</details>


### [158] [Persistent Recurrent Memory Between Transformer Layers - Improves Language Model Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17251)
*Eduardo Novaes Hering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅增加3.7%参数的轻量级持久循环记忆模块，插入6层解码器Transformer的中间，将验证集困惑度对应的评估损失从2.438降至1.743（降低28.5%），显著缩小泛化差距（0.12 vs 0.26），并通过消融实验证明收益完全来自持久记忆拓扑而非辅助目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer在小规模语言模型上存在过拟合问题，泛化能力受限；作者希望探索一种简单有效的方法，通过轻量级循环记忆桥接Transformer层来改善泛化，并厘清性能提升的真正来源（是拓扑结构还是辅助目标）。

Method: 在6层解码器Transformer的上下两半之间插入持久循环状态模块：该状态通过交叉注意力观察隐层表示，用GRU进行状态更新，并通过门控加法调制后续处理。作者仅添加3.7%额外参数，并通过控制变量的消融实验（无辅助损失、随机辅助损失）检验性能来源，同时用表示探针分析该状态编码的信息。

Result: 评估损失从2.438±0.004降至1.743±0.018（降低28.5%），在5个随机种子下统计显著（p<0.01）；泛化差距从0.26缩小至0.12。消融实验表明收益完全来自持久记忆拓扑，而非辅助自预测目标——相同拓扑但无辅助损失的模型表现相当，随机辅助损失无增益。表示探针显示持久状态以52%（随机33%）的准确率编码叙事位置信息。

Conclusion: 用轻量级循环记忆桥接Transformer层，是提升小规模语言模型泛化能力的简单而有效的方案；该持久状态能高效编码标准注意力难以维持的信息（如叙事位置）。

Abstract: We introduce a simple architectural modification to decoder-only transformers: a persistent recurrent state that observes hidden representations via cross-attention, updates itself through a GRU, and modulates subsequent processing via gated addition. Inserted between the lower and upper halves of a 6-layer transformer, this module adds only 3.7\% additional parameters while reducing evaluation loss from $2.438 \pm 0.004$ to $1.743 \pm 0.018$, corresponding to a 28.5\% reduction on held-out language modeling data. The improvement is statistically significant across 5 random seeds ($p < 0.01$) and corresponds to reduced overfitting (generalization gap 0.12 vs 0.26). Through controlled ablations, we demonstrate that the improvement stems entirely from the persistent memory topology, not from auxiliary self-prediction objectives. A model with identical topology but no auxiliary loss performs equivalently, while a random auxiliary loss provides no benefit. Representation probing reveals that the persistent state encodes narrative position (52\% vs 33\% chance level)---information that standard attention maintains less efficiently. Our results suggest that bridging transformer layers with a lightweight recurrent memory is a simple, effective approach to improving generalization in small-scale language models.

</details>


### [159] [Towards Illusions Awareness in Cyber-Physical System's Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17260)
*Anna Di Placido,Nicolas Ferry,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"设计错觉"（design illusions）概念，指对已失效假设的持续依赖所导致的虚假信心，并构建了一个概念性流水线，用于识别、分类、表征并利用这些错觉，将其转化为可复用的设计知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统（CPS）在开放环境中运行，难以预见所有场景；利益相关者依赖设计模型中的假设，但运行时这些假设可能失效，产生"现实差距"（reality gap）。现有方法多致力于通过更逼真的仿真来缩小或克服该差距，缺乏统一方法论来整理和利用失效假设作为可复用设计知识。

Method: 提出一个"错觉感知"（illusions-awareness）概念性流水线，包括识别（identify）、分类（classify）、表征（characterize）和利用（leverage）设计错觉，从而将其转化为可操作的设计知识。

Result: 论文目前给出的是概念框架而非实证结果；主要成果是建立了一套系统性的流程，使设计错觉显式化、结构化，并可作为后续设计决策的知识基础。

Conclusion: 设计错觉应被显式化、结构化并充分利用；所提出的流水线为弥合设计模型与运行时现实之间的差距提供了新的知识驱动视角，有助于支持更优的设计决策。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) operate through a continuous sense-compute-act loop within an open context environment, making it impossible to anticipate all the situations the system will face. To cope with this openness, stakeholders rely on assumptions, formalized into design models. However, these assumptions may no longer hold once the system is confronted with runtime reality, resulting in a discrepancy between expected and observed behaviour known in literature as the reality gap. Existing approaches mainly focus on reducing or overcoming it by making simulations more faithful to reality, with no unified methodology to structure and exploit invalidated assumptions that give rise to this gap as reusable design knowledge. We refer to the persistent reliance on invalidated assumptions -and the resulting false confidence in the design model's operational validity -as design illusions, and argue that they need to be made explicit, structured, and exploited as knowledge to support better design decisions. We propose a conceptual pipeline for illusions-awareness that identifies, classifies, characterizes, and leverages illusions to transform them into actionable design knowledge.

</details>


### [160] [Zero-shot narrative detection in social messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17310)
*Jesús M. Fraile-Hernández,Anselmo Peñas,Patrick Giedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，大型语言模型（LLMs）在零样本条件下能够有效识别和分类社会消息中的隐性叙事，结合人工撰写的叙事描述和集成方法（多数投票）可达到接近监督系统的性能，为缺乏训练数据的领域提供可扩展的叙事检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLMs能否利用广泛上下文知识在更深层次的语用层面解读消息，超越情感或主题分析，实现零样本叙事检测。

Method: 在Dipromats和SemEval数据集上，对比人工叙事描述、自动生成描述及少样本示例对LLM零样本性能的影响，并采用集成方法（特别是多数投票）提升鲁棒性。

Result: 人工叙事描述显著提升性能且无需训练样例；自动生成描述或少样本常因框架细微偏移降低准确率；多数投票增强鲁棒性；更大模型性能最佳且对提示变化不敏感。

Conclusion: 零样本LLM结合人工描述和简单集成可媲美监督系统，为无训练数据域的叙事检测提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: This study investigates the zero-shot ability of large language models (LLMs) to identify and classify hidden narratives in social messages. Our research hypothesis is that LLMs' extensive contextual knowledge allows them to interpret messages on a deeper, pragmatic level, going beyond basic sentiment or topic analysis. Experiments on the Dipromats and SemEval datasets show that providing models with human-written narrative descriptions significantly improves performance, without the need of training examples. In contrast, automatically generated descriptions or the use of few examples (few-shot) often degrade accuracy due to subtle shifts in framing. The study also finds that ensemble methods, particularly majority voting, enhance robustness and that larger models perform best while also being less sensitive to prompt variations. The findings validate that LLMs can effectively detect strategic narratives in a zero-shot setting, and when combined with simple ensembling and human-written descriptions, they can rival supervised systems, offering a scalable solution for narrative detection, specially when there is no training data for the vast majority of domains.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Detecting AI-Assisted Responses in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17317)
*Qizhou Wang,Bogdan Mamaev,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于AI辅助问卷调查的基准数据集ASURRE，涵盖从完全生成、修订到基于人设的智能体完成等多种AI使用策略。研究发现简单的AI伪造可被现有检测器轻松识别，但模仿真实受访者的人设智能体可使检测器性能降至随机水平。然而，智能体完成无法完全复刻受访者行为，会留下独特行为痕迹，基于这些线索的简单少样本无训练聚合器可将平均AUROC提升0.14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型被用于填写在线问卷会严重影响基于调查研究的有效性，但如何检测这种AI辅助的问卷填写行为仍然缺乏充分探索。现有研究尚未系统性地针对问卷调查场景建立AI生成文本检测的基准。

Method: 构建ASURRE基准数据集，设计三种AI辅助参与策略（完全生成、修订、基于人设的智能体完成），使用多种LLM在三个不同学科的真实问卷上生成AI辅助响应，并与真实人类响应配对。评估现有机器生成文本（MGT）检测器性能，并分析智能体完成的行为痕迹，提出基于这些线索的少样本无训练聚合检测方法。

Result: 现有MGT检测器能容易地检测出朴素的AI用法，但对模仿整个受访者的人设智能体检测性能接近随机。智能体完成无法完全复刻受访者级行为，会留下独特的行为痕迹。基于这些行为线索的少样本、免训练聚合器在智能体设置下相比最佳现有检测器平均AUROC提升0.14。

Conclusion: 问卷调查中的AI辅助检测存在层级差异：简单AI生成易检测，而人设智能体难以通过现有检测器识别。但智能体行为存在无法完全模仿受访者的固有痕迹，利用这些线索可显著提升检测性能，为问卷数据的有效性保障提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The use of LLMs to complete online surveys impacts the validity of survey-based research, but detecting such usage remains underexplored. We introduce an initial benchmark dataset, namely ASURRE, for AI-assisted survey participation to capture usage strategies ranging from full generation and revision to persona-grounded agentic completion. Controlled by these strategies, LLM-assisted survey responses are generated using multiple LLMs on three real-world surveys in different disciplines, paired with genuine human responses. Our evaluation of existing machine-generated text (MGT) detectors shows that naive AI usage is readily detectable, whereas persona-grounded agents that mimic entire respondents push detector performance toward chance. We further show that agentic completion cannot fully replicate respondent-level behaviour and leaves distinctive behavioural traces. While individual cues can be circumvented by targeted prompting, a simple few-shot, training-free aggregator over these cues improves mean AUROC by +0.14 over the best existing detector across agentic settings. Our project is available at https://github.com/mike-qz-wang/ASURRE.

</details>


### [162] [Vroom-Vroom at SHROOM-Visions: A Multi-Judge Committee for Detecting Hallucinated Spans in Vision-Language Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17327)
*Toqeer Ehsan,Nico Penttilä,Richard Schmidt,Arash Hajikhani,Victoria Palacin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了作者在SHROOM-Visions共享任务中的提交方案，该任务旨在检测和分类跨四种语言的视觉语言模型输出中的幻觉字符片段。作者采用多个微调后的视觉语言模型作为独立标注器，通过字符级多数投票整合其片段预测，并探索了激活探测方法。该方案在四种语言中的三种排名第一，且在每种语言和每个指标上都登上领奖台。分析表明，不同模型间的不一致性与人类标注者间的不一致性存在相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）的输出中经常出现幻觉内容，需要可靠的方法来检测和分类这些幻觉字符片段。该任务跨越四种语言的多样性增加了挑战性，作者旨在构建一种鲁棒且可解释的检测方案。

Method: 作者使用多个微调后的视觉语言模型作为独立标注器（集成策略），通过字符级多数投票整合各模型的片段预测，同时探索使用激活探测（activation probes）作为补充手段。通过多模型协作与投票机制来增强检测的一致性和准确性。

Result: 该方法在四种语言中的三种排名第一，并在所有语言和所有指标上都进入前三名（登上领奖台）。此外，分析发现不同模型间的分歧与人类标注者间的分歧具有关联性，说明模型的不确定性可反映人类标注的主观性。

Conclusion: 多模型集成投票是检测多语言视觉语言模型幻觉字符片段的有效策略，且模型间的分歧程度可作为人类标注不一致性的近似指标，为进一步改进幻觉检测提供了有价值的洞见。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to the SHROOM-Visions shared task on detecting and classifying hallucinated character spans in vision-language model outputs across four languages. We employ several fine-tuned vision-language models as independent annotators and combine their span predictions through character-level majority voting, and additionally explore activation probes. The approach ranks first in three of four languages and places on the podium in every language and metric. Our analysis indicates that disagreement among diverse models tracks disagreement among human annotators.

</details>


### [163] [Where Should a Document Live: Context, Representations, or Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17346)
*Nathanaël Carraz Rakotonirina,Momchil Hardalov,Gonzalo Iglesias,Adrià de Gispert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对两类知识注入方法进行了受控对比：基于表征的（KV-cache）方法和基于参数的（微调）方法，在五个知识密集型基准上评估。结果显示，在oracle设置下，Cartridges（KV）在几乎所有存储预算下准确性最高，比参数化方法高出10个百分点；Compaction仅在低压缩率时能与Cartridges匹敌，压缩率超过50倍时落后参数化方法10个百分点。在多文档检索场景下，Cartridges是唯一与上下文学习（ICL）持平的方法，领先参数化方法29分、Compaction 15分。但Cartridges也是除全量微调和大型MLP适配器外唯一出现灾难性遗忘的方法，在控制基准上性能下降6%，编码任务上达13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要获取预训练数据之外的新信息，现有注入方式包括上下文文档（ICL）、编码进参数（微调）以及潜表征注入（KV-cache），但三者效率、成本和性能各有取舍，缺乏系统性受控对比，难以判断在何种存储预算和场景下应选用哪种方法。

Method: 在五个知识密集型基准上，对表征式方法（Cartridges与Compaction，均基于KV-cache）和参数式方法（微调类）进行受控比较，覆盖oracle单文档设置与更真实的多文档检索场景，并以ICL为基线参照，同时考察各方法在控制基准上的遗忘表现。

Result: oracle设置下Cartridges在几乎每个存储预算下准确性最高，超出参数化方法10分；Compaction仅在低压缩率时持平Cartridges，高压缩率（>50x）时落后参数化方法10分。多文档检索场景下Cartridges是唯一匹配ICL的方法，领先参数化方法29分、Compaction 15分。但Cartridges、全量微调和大型MLP适配器是仅有的发生灾难性遗忘的方法（控制基准降6%，编码降13%）。

Conclusion: KV-cache表征注入（尤以Cartridges为代表）在知识注入准确性上整体优于微调类参数化方法，甚至能匹配ICL，但其代价是显著的灾难性遗忘，选择注入方法需依据存储预算、检索场景以及是否容忍遗忘来权衡。

Abstract: To answer questions outside of their pre-training data, large language models (LLMs) need access to new information, which can be presented in the context window as documents, encoded into the model's parameters, or injected as latent representations. However, each of these methods comes with different efficiency, cost, and performance trade-offs, with no single winner. We present a controlled comparison of representation-based (KV-cache based) and parametric (fine-tuning-based) adaptation methods on five knowledge-intensive benchmarks. We show that in the oracle setting, Cartridges (KV) are the most accurate injection method at nearly every storage budget, outperforming parametric methods by 10 points. Compaction (KV) matches Cartridges only at low compression rates, lagging behind the parametric methods by 10 points at rates higher than $50\times$. In the more realistic multi-document retrieval scenario, Cartridges are the only method that matches in-context learning (ICL), leading the parametric methods by 29 points and Compaction by 15 points. Nonetheless, Cartridges are also the only method, besides full fine-tuning and large MLP adapters, that suffers from catastrophic forgetting, i.e., a 6% performance degradation on control benchmarks, with 13% in coding.

</details>


### [164] [ECHO: A Matched-Contrast Benchmark for Context-Sensitive Turn-Taking in Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17360)
*Shuofeng Zhao,Hongwei Cai,Wenke Fan,Qingxiang Guo,Dawei Yang,Zhou Wang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍 ECHO——一个用于中文全双工对话轮转诊断的配对基准测试，通过配对样本（同一重叠话语但上下文相反，一个需让位Yield、一个需保持Keep）来评估系统能否根据多轮对话上下文做出情境敏感的决定，并引入配对准确率指标来惩罚固定动作倾向，实验发现多数全双工系统存在明显的让位偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常独立评估每个事件，可能奖励固定的动作偏好而非情境敏感的决定；全双工对话系统必须区分需要让位的打断与允许继续说话的反馈词，而现有的独立评估方式无法真正检验这种情境敏感性。

Method: 构建配对的诊断基准 ECHO：每个配对包含相同的重叠转录文本，但前面的多轮对话上下文形成对比，一个要求Yield（让位）另一个要求Keep（保持说话）；还包含off-talk（离题话语）样例用于诊断不必要的让位。引入pair accuracy（配对准确率）指标，要求配对中两个样例都判断正确，且对恒定动作策略不赋予任何分数。

Result: 在多个全双工系统上的实验表明，大多数系统表现出明显的Yield偏向，在打断上的表现显著优于在反馈词上的表现，而另一个系统保持相对均衡。这证明仅用打断评估会高估实际的轮转可靠性。

Conclusion: ECHO揭示了主流全双工系统存在系统性让位偏差，指出打断专用评估的局限性；本文强调需要情境敏感且均衡的轮转评估，ECHO及其元数据将公开发布。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems must distinguish interruptions that require yielding the floor from backchannels that permit continued speaking. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate events independently and may therefore reward fixed action preferences rather than context-sensitive decisions. We introduce ECHO, a paired diagnostic benchmark for Chinese full-duplex turn-taking. ECHO pairs examples with the same overlap transcript but contrasting preceding multi-turn dialogue contexts, with one requiring Yield and the other Keep. It additionally includes off-talk examples for diagnosing unnecessary yielding. We introduce pair accuracy, which requires correct decisions on both members of a pair and assigns no credit to constant-action policies. Experiments on multiple full-duplex systems show that most exhibit a pronounced bias toward \textsc{Yield}, performing substantially better on interruptions than on backchannels, while another system remains comparatively balanced. These findings demonstrate that interruption-only evaluation can overestimate practical turn-taking reliability. ECHO and its metadata will be publicly released.

</details>


### [165] [Enhancing Accessibility of Medical Texts through Large Language Model-Driven Plain Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17398)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型（GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro、LLaMA）结合Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）技术，实现医疗保健信息的自动平民语言适配（PLA），通过提示策略对比、QLoRA微调和MoA集成，使复杂的医疗文本更易于患者理解，同时保持核心内容完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂医疗信息与患者阅读理解能力之间存在显著鸿沟，亟需自动化的平民语言适配（PLA）技术来简化专业医学术语，使医疗文本更易获取和理解。任务特定数据有限的情况下，需要探索LLM在零样本和少样本学习场景中的PLA潜力。

Method: 利用GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro和LLaMA等LLM进行文本简化；对不同LLM采用QLoRA微调；集成Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）技术增强PLA任务的适应性和鲁棒性；并对多种提示策略进行对比分析。

Result: 研究证明了LLM驱动的PLA在医疗信息简化方面的有效性，能够在不丢失关键内容的前提下提升文本的可理解性，并展示了MoA技术在增强PLA适应性和鲁棒性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的PLA在使医疗保健信息更易于理解方面具有显著潜力，为弥合医疗文本复杂性与患者阅读能力之间的鸿沟提供了有效方案，未来可在保持内容完整性的同时进一步提升普及性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of making complex healthcare information more accessible through automated Plain Language Adaptation (PLA). PLA aims to simplify technical medical language, bridging a critical gap between the complexity of healthcare texts and patients' reading comprehension. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), such as GPT and BART, have opened new possibilities for PLA, especially in zero-shot and few-shot learning contexts where task-specific data is limited. In this work, we leverage the capabilities of LLMs such as GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-1.5-pro, and LLaMA for text simplification. Additionally, we incorporate Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) techniques to enhance adaptability and robustness in PLA tasks. Key contributions include a comparative analysis of prompting strategies, finetuning with QLoRA on different LLMs, and the integration of MoA technique. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of LLM-driven PLA, showcasing its potential in making healthcare information more comprehensible while preserving essential content.

</details>


### [166] [Right Tool, Right Job: Native-Language Evaluation, Tokenizer Sensitivity, and Methodological Findings from a French-Only BabyLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17435)
*Adam Zachary Wasserman,David Beauchemin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提交了MéTRON-FR——一个基于92.47M法语词预训练的125M GPT-2模型，用于BabyLM 2026严格赛道。该模型在魁北克法语语法最小对基准QFrBLiMP上取得85.97±0.17%的准确率，在BabyLM加权排行榜上得分62.80%。通过跨语言GLUE协议（法语任务数据翻译+秩16 LoRA），发现任务类型呈现明显梯度：关系型任务显著提升，而世界知识型任务反而退化。双语词汇归纳将法语嵌入对齐到GPT-2，p@1达68.84±8.61%（高于随机18倍），表明跨语言对齐追踪的是习得的语法能力而非训练时长。消融研究表明，在儿童规模下零样本单token评分受分词器和模板伪影主导，因此建议将分词器交换敏感性、安慰剂对照提示、母语最小对基准作为标准诊断手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在儿童规模（极低数据量）下预训练非英语（法语）语言模型的可行性，并系统研究小规模模型中跨语言迁移、任务类型差异以及评估方法中的伪影问题，为低资源语言的小规模语言建模提供方法论指导。

Method: 1) 构建125M参数的GPT-2模型，在92.47M法语词上进行预训练；2) 采用QFrBLiMP（魁北克法语最小对）与BabyLM加权榜单评估；3) 设计跨语言GLUE协议，将法语任务数据翻译后结合秩16 LoRA微调；4) 使用双语词汇归纳（BLI）对齐法语嵌入与GPT-2；5) 进行消融研究，检验单token零样本评分中分词器与模板伪影的影响。

Result: QFrBLiMP得分为85.97±0.17%，BabyLM加权排行榜得分62.80%。跨语言GLUE显示关系型任务受益于LoRA适配而世界知识任务退化。BLI的p@1为68.84±8.61%（随机水平18倍）。消融实验确认儿童规模下分词器与模板伪影主导零样本评分。

Conclusion: 小规模法语模型可取得合理的语法能力；跨语言对齐反映语法能力而非训练时长；儿童规模评估必须警惕分词器与模板伪影，并倡导分词器交换敏感性、安慰剂对照提示和母语最小对基准作为标准化诊断手段。

Abstract: We submit MéTRON-FR, a 125M GPT-2 pretrained on 92.47M words of French, to the BabyLM 2026 Strict track. It scores 85.97 +/- 0.17% on QFrBLiMP (a native Quebec-French benchmark of grammatical minimal pairs) and 62.80% on the BabyLM-weighted leaderboard. A cross-lingual GLUE (General Language Understanding Evaluation) protocol that combines French task-data translation with rank-16 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) produces a sharp task-type gradient: relational tasks gain measurably, while world-knowledge tasks regress. Bilingual Lexicon Induction aligns the French embeddings to GPT-2 at p@1 = 68.84 +/- 8.61%, 18X above chance, suggesting cross-lingual alignment tracks acquired grammatical competence rather than training duration. An ablation study shows that single-token zero-shot scoring is dominated by tokenizer and template artifacts at the child scale, motivating tokenizer-swap sensitivity, placebo-controlled prompting, and native-language minimal-pair benchmarks as standard diagnostics.

</details>


### [167] [What Breaks Under Pruning in Smart Homes, and When? Evaluating LLM Degradation Across Architectures and Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17515)
*Congjing Zhang,Vashishtha Patil,Henning Lange,Usman Aleem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了剪枝对大型语言模型（LLM）在智能家居工具调用任务中的影响，覆盖四种架构（稠密Transformer、稠密混合、混合专家MoE）及深度、宽度、混合、专家四种剪枝方法，在超过19,500个实例上进行评估。研究发现：稠密模型的安全剪枝区间窄且随后急剧退化，而MoE模型能容忍更多剪枝；剪枝会先损害grounded具体性再影响schema层面的意图，且激进的稠密剪枝会诱发系统性过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪枝虽能降低LLM部署成本，但其对上下文grounded的工具调用（tool calling）的影响此前缺乏系统理解。作者希望超越聚合准确率，深入揭示剪枝导致的性能退化特征，以指导可靠的工具执行场景下的模型选择。

Method: 选取四种涵盖稠密Transformer、稠密混合与MoE架构的LLM，应用深度、宽度、混合和专家四种剪枝方法；在剪枝后进行监督微调（SFT）恢复，并在三个智能家居数据集上评估超过19,500个实例。除聚合任务准确率外，还沿两个维度刻画退化：动作组件（操作、设备、参数、值）与任务复杂度。

Result: 稠密模型只存在狭窄的安全剪枝区间，超出后性能急剧下降；MoE模型则能容忍显著更多的剪枝。退化呈现层次性：剪枝先损害grounded具体性（动作组件细节），后影响schema级别的意图识别；激进的稠密剪枝还会诱发系统性的过度拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 选择用于可靠工具执行的剪枝LLM时，不能仅以聚合准确率为依据，必须评估剪枝在具体性与行为鲁棒性维度的退化影响。该研究为剪枝LLM在工具调用场景的可靠性选型提供了重要警示与评测框架。

Abstract: Pruning can reduce the deployment cost of large language models (LLMs), but its impact on context-grounded tool calling remains poorly understood. We systematically study pruning-induced degradation in smart-home tool calling across four LLMs spanning dense Transformer, dense hybrid, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, together with depth, width, hybrid, and expert pruning methods. After post-pruning supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we evaluate more than 19,500 instances from three smart-home datasets. Beyond aggregate task accuracy, we characterize degradation along two dimensions: action components (i.e., operation, device, argument, and value) and task complexity. Our results show that dense models have narrow safe pruning regions followed by sharp degradation, while MoE models tolerate substantially more pruning.
