Crucially, during repairs, RCE rechecks affected checkpoint states, preventing repair-induced regressions from propagating silently. We instantiate PosterVisor in HTML/CSS and editable PPTX generators. On the 100-paper Paper2Poster benchmark, PosterVisor-PPT improves observed mean poster-grounded QA accuracy over PosterGen (64.47% vs. 58.53%) and is preferred by human judges in 72.5% of non-tied pairwise comparisons (95% CI, 61.6-83.4%). A secondary 30-paper study also yields higher VLM Overall and PaperQuiz means. These results support rubric-compiled contracts and stage-conditioned enforcement for controllable poster synthesis.

</details>


### [231] [Self-Emergence Agent Architecture:Behavior-Inertia HMM, Reflexive Metacognition,and Social-Contrastive Self-Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17331)
*Xiaoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自我涌现智能体架构（SEAA），针对LLM智能体的三个人格漂移、非进化反思、缺乏自我-他者边界等结构性局限，通过隐马尔可夫模型（编码行为与认知惯性）、Reflexion式言语元认知回路（直接更新HMM参数而非仅存储文本）、以及多智能体社会环境（初始相同智能体相互对比）三部分形成"社会行为→反馈→自我反思→惯性更新→差异化行为"的闭环。实验表明该闭环可自发打破对称性，使初始相同的智能体分化为稳定不同的人格，并自发产生社会结构（共识枢纽与一致排斥的离群者）。研究采取源于庄子的认识论不可知立场，仅观察可测的行为涌现，不对主观感受质作断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式智能体模拟依赖静态记忆与固定提示词，既无法维持行为惯性，也无法实现内生的自我演化，导致LLM智能体始终存在三类结构性缺陷：人格漂移（无长久行为惯性）、非进化反思（反思结果仅存为文本而不改变自身机制）、以及自我-他者边界缺失。作者旨在构建一个能自发涌现分化而稳定"自我"的统一架构。

Method: SEAA由三部分构成闭环：(i) HMM将长期行为与认知惯性编码为可编辑的状态转移矩阵；(ii) Reflexion式言语元认知回路将反思输出直接用于更新HMM参数本身，而非仅存为文本；(iii) 多智能体社会环境让初始相同的智能体持续与他人行为对比。闭环流程为：社会行为→反馈→自我反思→惯性更新→差异化行为。文章给出三个可证伪假设、可复现实验协议及可操作度量指标。

Result: 无语言模型的原型表明该闭环能自发打破对称性：初始相同的智能体巩固出各自不同且稳定的性格，而匹配对照组则不能。托管LLM实验将这些差异显现为不同的第一人称自我叙事；五智能体商议实验自发发展出社会结构——一个共识枢纽与一个被一致排斥的离群者——而对照组中此结构缺席。

Conclusion: SEAA以统一框架、具体架构（含伪代码）、机制性证据及"显微镜式"沙盒，为研究人工自我的涌现提供工具。基于庄子式认识论不可知立场，研究仅关注可观测的行为涌现，不涉及主观感受质的声称，从而保持科学严谨性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents exhibit strong language-generation and problem-solving capabilities, yet suffer from three structural limitations: personality drift, non-evolutionary reflection, and the absence of a self-other boundary. Existing generative-agent simulations rely on static memory and fixed prompts, maintaining neither behavioral inertia nor endogenous self-evolution. We propose the Self-Emergence Agent Architecture (SEAA), which integrates three components: (i) a Hidden Markov Model (HMM) that encodes long-term behavioral and cognitive inertia as an editable state-transition matrix; (ii) a Reflexion-style verbal metacognition loop whose output updates the HMM parameters themselves, rather than merely being stored as text; and (iii) a multi-agent social environment in which initially identical agents continuously compare their behavior with others'. The three components form a closed loop: social action $\to$ feedback $\to$ self-reflection $\to$ inertia update $\to$ differentiated action. We state three falsifiable hypotheses and provide a reproducible experimental protocol with operational metrics. A language-model-free prototype shows the loop spontaneously breaks symmetry: initially identical agents consolidate distinct, stable personalities whereas matched controls do not. Experiments with a hosted LLM surface these differences as distinct first-person self-narratives, and a five-agent deliberation spontaneously develops social structure---a consensus hub and a unanimously rejected outlier---absent in the control. Following an epistemologically agnostic stance inspired by Zhuangzi, SEAA studies only observable behavioral emergence and makes no claim about subjective qualia. This work contributes a unified framework, a concrete architecture with pseudocode, mechanistic evidence, and a microscope-style sandbox for studying artificial-self emergence.

</details>


### [232] [FlashVector: Agent for Hierarchical Model Serving Stack Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17391)
*Qi Wu,Lohan Lemire,Kai Meng,Zhongmou Cai,Raphael Bargues,Petr Zhitnikov,Zeyuan Cao,Yao Wang,Shujun Bian,Wei Chen,Sean Sheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlashVector是一个自主体(agentic)系统，通过可扩展框架将单一GPU内核优化智能体范式推广到异构技术栈，在Unity广告平台的模型服务全栈中实现端到端性能优化，取得了模型服务器2倍吞吐提升、1.98倍延迟加速，以及特征存储1.6倍吞吐提升的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型服务是生产环境推荐系统中最大的成本驱动因素之一。最大化其吞吐量需要跨越GPU内核、ML框架计算图、模型服务器和按需特征处理等多层深层技术栈，每层都需要专业领域知识。这种跨层专业知识难以获取，且无法随持续增长演变的负载扩展，导致大量成本效率收益未被实现。虽然近期AI智能体在独立GPU内核优化上达到了人类专家水平，但服务栈其余部分的自动调优和优化仍未被探索。

Method: FlashVector的核心贡献是一个可扩展框架，用于将单一内核优化智能体范式泛化到异构技术栈，从而整体性地实现性能改进。该系统能够跨所有层进行优化，不仅涵盖GPU内核和计算图层面，还延伸到模型服务器（NVIDIA Triton的C++代码库）以及按需特征转换服务（Python代码库）等更复杂的系统架构组件。

Result: 在Unity的Vector广告平台部署后，FlashVector在模型服务器上实现了最高2倍的吞吐量提升和最高1.98倍的延迟加速，在特征存储上实现了最高1.6倍的吞吐量提升。优化不仅在内核和计算图层被发现，也出现在服务栈的其他组件中，验证了框架对更复杂系统架构的可扩展性。

Conclusion: FlashVector证明了一个可扩展的智能体框架能够将内核优化范式推广到整个模型服务堆栈，通过自动化跨层优化显著降低推理成本、提升效率，同时具备对异构复杂系统架构的可扩展性，弥补了服务栈自动智能体调优的空白。

Abstract: Model serving is one of the largest cost drivers in production recommender systems. Maximizing its throughput requires navigating a deeply layered hierarchy: GPU kernels, the ML framework computation graph, the model server, and on-demand feature processing -- each demanding specialized domain expertise. Such cross-layer expertise is inherently difficult to acquire, and does not scale with a workload that continuously grows and evolves, leaving significant cost efficiency gains unrealized. While recent AI agents have demonstrated human expert level efficiency in standalone GPU kernel optimization, automated tuning and optimization for the rest of the serving stack remain largely unexplored. We present FlashVector, an agentic system that optimizes performance across all layers of the model serving stack. The key contribution is an extensible framework to generalize the single kernel optimization agent paradigm to heterogeneous technical stacks, and to deliver performance improvements holistically. After deployment in Unity's Vector advertising platform, FlashVector achieved up to 2x throughput increase and up to 1.98x latency speedup on model server, and up to 1.6x throughput increase on feature store. These optimizations were discovered not only at the GPU kernel and computation graph levels, but also across the other components of the model serving stack, such as the model server (NVIDIA Triton's C++ codebase) and the on-demand feature transformation service (Python codebase), demonstrating the extensibility of the framework to more complex system architectures.

</details>


### [233] [Never Stop Thinking: Continuous-Time Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17416)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在未修改的文本模型上，通过轻量级中断-恢复编排器实现了"边听边想、边说边想"的连续时间认知，将实时流水线整体延迟降低19%，在目标场景中降低50%。作者引入ReactiveBench（120个交互场景+可验证流式轨道）来评测，发现LLM裁判偏好可见推理会误导评测；通过五阶段训练研究，证明可验证目标、合理形状的奖励以及on-policy RL能显著提升任务完成率（流式完成率从48%提升到73±5%），并验证了跨规模与跨模型的复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM语音代理遵循"听-思-说"的刚性循环，在每次回复前插入数秒静默延迟。作者希望突破这种延迟瓶颈，并同时回答"连续时间思考是否真正改善代理的实际任务完成能力"这一评测难题。

Method: 1) 提出轻量级中断-恢复编排器，使未修改的文本模型支持边听边想、边说边想的连续时间认知；2) 构建ReactiveBench，包含120个交互场景，使用预注册的二值需求评判，并增加可验证的流式轨道用精确正确性评分；3) 开展五阶段训练研究，分别检验信号来源（可验证目标）、结构（类型形状奖励）、优化器（on-policy RL vs偏好优化）的影响。

Result: 编排器使pipeline延迟整体降低19%，目标场景降低50%。ReactiveBench揭示LLM裁判偏好可见推理会导致评测结论翻转，而训练研究中发现：可验证目标使思考从有害变为有益；均匀奖励会导致优化牺牲未奖励部分；偏好优化只能权衡冲突子目标，而基于类型形状奖励的on-policy RL能同时改善所有正确性维度，将流式完成率从48%提升到73±5%（跨种子），并在更大规模和第二模型上复现。

Conclusion: 编排层使连续时间交互成为可能，但真正让连续时间思考变得"好"的关键在于：可验证信号（而非LLM裁判）、正确的奖励形状（类型化而非均匀）以及正确的优化器（on-policy RL而非偏好优化）。该工作为构建高效且真正有效的连续交互代理提供了方法论。

Abstract: Voice agents built on LLMs follow a rigid listen-think-speak loop that inserts seconds of dead air before every reply. We show that continuous-time cognition (thinking while listening and thinking while speaking) emerges from an unmodified text model under a lightweight interrupt-and-resume orchestrator, cutting live-pipeline latency by 19% overall and by half in the regime the mechanism targets. To measure whether continuous-time thinking improves what agents accomplish, we introduce ReactiveBench: 120 interactive scenarios scored against pre-registered binary requirements, plus a verifiable streaming track scored by exact correctness. ReactiveBench exposes a pitfall with broad consequences: LLM judges reward visible reasoning; a large judged "advantage" of continuous-time thinking reverses sign under an independent judge, and judge-trained models objectively complete fewer requirements when they think. A five-stage training study then locates the right signal at three levels. Its source: verifiable objectives turn thinking from harmful to helpful. Its structure: whatever a uniform reward omits, optimization trades away; brevity everywhere erodes multi-hop tool chaining. Its optimizer: preference optimization can only trade conflicting sub-goals against each other, while on-policy RL over a type-shaped reward improves every correctness axis at once, raising streaming completion from 48% to 73+/-5% across seeds and replicating at larger scale and on a second model. Orchestration makes continuous-time interaction possible; a verifiable signal, correctly sourced, shaped, and optimized, makes it good.

</details>


### [234] [World Model Science: Self-Organized Criticality, Weak Chaos, and Metastable Belief Dynamics in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17419)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从动力学视角系统研究长时程LLM智能体的轨迹行为，综合自组织临界性、弱混沌与亚稳态信念动力学三种框架，在22项跨领域实验中测量应力累积、错误雪崩、时间依赖性、局部-全局失配、有界发散及信念盆地转变等指标，发现局部有效动作可在全局状态保真失败后持续、压力可引发突然崩溃、错误具有长记忆等规律；同时明确拒绝了普适幂律、临界点与共享干预最优等强主张，指出发散有界且信念呈亚稳态而非混沌，呼吁以轨迹级动力学诊断替代单一终端奖励来科学评估智能体世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体需在大量观测、动作、工具调用与中间信念中维持任务状态，但现有评估多依赖终端奖励，缺乏对轨迹内部演化机制的深入理解。作者希望通过引入动力学视角，系统刻画智能体状态在长时间尺度上的演化规律，从而为智能体世界模型的科学评估建立理论基础。

Method: 论文提出将智能体隐含状态与基准锚定的真实状态进行对齐，在此基础上对轨迹施加多种动力学定量诊断：测量应力累积与误差雪崩，分析时间依赖性与局部-全局失配，检验有界发散，追踪信念盆地转变，并进行有限尺度标度分析，同时以显式零模型作为对照。实验覆盖受控谜题、工具使用、具身任务、多跳检索、通用助手推理及生命游戏等22项场景。

Result: 实验发现：局部有效动作可在全局状态保真失败后仍维持；压力累积可触发突然崩溃；错误序列具有长程记忆；依赖深度改变错误传播机制；更大规划视界支持更大幅度的雪崩。与此同时，发散始终保持有界，信念状态呈现亚稳态而非完全混沌特征，且数据不支持普适幂律、临界点存在或存在共享干预最优的更强论断。

Conclusion: 研究表明，轨迹级动力学诊断比终端奖励更能揭示LLM智能体的真实行为状态，为构建"智能体世界模型的科学"提供了方法论基础。作者同时强调结论的边界：不应过度推断临界性、普适幂律等强主张，需以有界发散和亚稳信念为基调解释长时程行为。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must maintain task state across extended sequences of observations, actions, tool calls, and intermediate beliefs. We study these trajectories through three dynamical views: self-organized criticality, weak chaos, and metastable belief dynamics. Our framework aligns agent-implied states with benchmark-grounded states and measures stress accumulation, error avalanches, temporal dependence, local--global mismatch, bounded divergence, belief-basin transitions, and finite-size scaling under explicit null models. Across 22 experiments spanning controlled puzzles, tool use, embodied tasks, multi-hop retrieval, general-assistant reasoning, and Game of Life, we find that locally valid actions can persist after global state fidelity fails, stress can trigger abrupt collapse, error sequences exhibit long memory, dependency depth changes the propagation regime, and larger horizons support larger avalanches. At the same time, divergence remains bounded, belief states show metastable rather than fully chaotic behavior, and stronger claims of universal power laws, critical points, or shared intervention optima are not supported. These results suggest a science of agent world models based on trajectory-level dynamical diagnostics rather than terminal reward alone.

</details>


### [235] [Transformer-Based Token Fusion and Dynamic Graph Planning for Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17421)
*Shaohang Wu,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer-based Token Fusion and Dynamic Graph Planning (TDGP)模型，解决音频-视觉导航(AVN)中被动视觉感知导致的不完整误导性问题。通过Transformer融合多模态线索进行精确局部规划，并设计低层规划层，利用物理碰撞惩罚实时移除地图中碰撞的边并施加惩罚，迫使智能体自动重新规划以补偿视觉信息缺失。实验表明该方法在Replica和MP3D数据集上优于基线模型，且声音增强策略显著提升未听过声学场景的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVN系统缺乏在视觉感知不完整或具有误导性时自适应纠正和重新规划的能力；依赖物理碰撞补偿缺失视觉信息导致导航低效且不安全；现有方法过度依赖被动视觉感知。

Method: 提出TDGP模型，包含高层感知层和低层规划层。高层感知层利用Transformer模型融合多模态线索（视觉与声学）进行精确局部规划；低层规划层使用物理碰撞惩罚，实时移除与地图碰撞的边并施加相应惩罚，强迫智能体自动重新规划以弥补视觉信息不足。

Result: TDGP模型在Replica和Matterport3D (MP3D)数据集上优于基线模型；模型的声学增强策略显著提升了在未见过的声学场景中的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过Transformer多模态融合与动态图规划结合物理碰撞惩罚机制，有效解决了视觉信息缺失下的导航重规划问题，同时提升导航效率与安全性，并增强了在未听过声学场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Navigation (AVN) requires an agent to localize and navigate toward a continuously vocalizing target relying solely on visual observations and acoustic cues. Currently, systems lack the ability to adaptively correct and replan when faced with incomplete or misleading visual perception. Furthermore, relying on physical collisions to compensate for missing visual information results in inefficient and unsafe navigation, whereas existing methods are overly dependent on passive visual perception. To address these issues, we propose the Transformer-based Token Fusion and Dynamic Graph Planning (TDGP) model, which incorporates high-level perception layers and leverages the Transformer model to fuse multimodal cues for precise local planning. Next, a low-level planning layer is designed that uses physical collision penalties to remove edges that collide with the map in real time and apply corresponding penalties, forcing the agent to automatically re-plan to compensate for the lack of visual information. Experiments show that our TDGP model outperforms baseline models on the Replica and Matterport3D (MP3D) datasets, and that the model's sound enhancement strategy significantly improves generalization in unheard acoustic scenarios.

</details>


### [236] [Talking Head Synthesis with Facial Landmark Guidance via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17422)
*Ziheng Yang,Yinfeng Yu,Yongming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人脸关键点引导的空间增强方法，用于音频驱动的数字人（talking-head）生成，通过预测的landmark为表情敏感区域筛选和丰富空间点，并引入全局关键点补偿机制对3DGS属性进行细化，提升口型同步、面部真实感和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然NeRF和3DGS为说话头系统提供了更真实的三维面部几何与外观建模，但语音特征主要描述时间上的声学模式，缺乏显式的面部布局信息，直接以音频驱动三维面部形变会产生口型动作不准确、表情细节薄弱以及局部伪影等问题。

Method: 1) 提出面部关键点引导的空间增强模块：利用预测的关键点提供结构线索，用于选取并丰富表情敏感面部区域周围的空间点；2) 引入全局关键点补偿机制：将完整的关键点集合编码为条件向量，用于细化3DGS属性，向底层形变表征补充全脸结构信息。

Result: 在自驱动（self-driven）和跨驱动（cross-driven）两种实验设置下，所提方法均提升了视觉质量、面部真实感与口型同步性能。

Conclusion: 通过关键点引导的空间增强与全局补偿相结合，有效弥合了语音声学特征与三维面部几何形变之间的语义鸿沟，实现了更精确、更真实的三维说话头生成。

Abstract: Audio-driven digital human generation plays an important role in virtual communication, immersive interaction, and media production. With the development of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), recent talking-head systems have obtained more faithful 3D facial geometry and appearance modeling. A remaining difficulty is that speech features mainly describe temporal acoustic patterns rather than explicit facial layouts. As a result, directly driving 3D facial deformation with audio may produce inaccurate mouth motion, weak expression details, and local artifacts. To address this issue, we propose a facial-keypoint-guided spatial enhancement module. The predicted landmarks provide structural cues for selecting and enriching spatial points around expression-sensitive facial regions. We further introduce a global landmark compensation mechanism, where the full set of keypoints is encoded into a conditioning vector to refine 3DGS attributes. This compensation supplies whole-face structural information to the underlying shape representation. Experiments under self-driven and cross-driven settings show that the proposed method improves visual quality, facial realism, and lip synchronization.

</details>


### [237] [JustFit: 200K-Token LLM Serving on a 24 GiB Laptop with Just-in-Time State Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17475)
*Yuhua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 JustFit，一个基于 MLX 的推理运行时，通过 KVExec（压缩 KV 执行）、PhaseSwap（组件驻留）和 StateTrans（保状态的服务切换）三种机制协同，在不依赖模型权重量化的前提下实现重建融合与按需物化/释放，从而在 24 GiB 内存笔记本上大幅扩展本地大模型的服务上下文容量并支持推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力强大的开源权重模型使得本地编码与推理极具吸引力，但其上下文和运行状态会严重占用笔记本有限的内存，需要一种能在低内存设备上高效执行大模型的方法。

Method: 提出 MLX 推理运行时 JustFit，包含三种核心机制：KVExec 用于压缩 KV 缓存执行、PhaseSwap 用于组件驻留管理、StateTrans 用于保状态的服务切换；三者融合重建过程并协调"及时物化与释放"，且与模型权重量化相互独立。

Result: 在 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook 上运行 Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4：全量执行测试中三次独立运行完成 196,608 输入与 16,384 输出 token，将单请求上下文从 mlx-vlm 基线的 30,720 位提升至 212,992 位（6.93 倍）；另一次双请求运行合计保留 229,376 位。性能测试中 32K 输入/64 输出探针达 19.11 tokens/s；重复 32K+6K 负载的中位峰值进程占用 16,374 MiB。集成运行时在 AIME 2026 上正确解答 29/30 题。

Conclusion: 紧凑的状态管理与时序感知执行能够显著扩展本地服务容量并支持长时间生成的推理，证明了在消费级低内存设备上运行大规模开源模型的可行性。

Abstract: Capable open-weight models make local coding and reasoning attractive, but their context and execution state strain laptop memory. We present JustFit, an MLX-based inference runtime that combines KVExec for compressed KV execution, PhaseSwap for component residency, and StateTrans for state-preserving serving transitions. These mechanisms fuse reconstruction and coordinate just-in-time materialization and release, independently of model-weight quantization. In full-execution capacity tests on a 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook running Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4, three independent runs complete 196,608 input and 16,384 output tokens, increasing completed single-request context from the mlx-vlm baseline's 30,720 positions to 212,992 (6.93x); a separate two-request run retains 229,376 positions in aggregate. In separate performance tests, a 32K-input, 64-output probe reaches 19.11 tokens/s, and a repeated 32K+6K workload has a median peak process footprint of 16,374 MiB. The integrated runtime answers 29 of 30 AIME 2026 problems correctly, showing how compact state and lifetime-aware execution expand local serving capacity while supporting extended generated reasoning.

</details>


### [238] [LimiX-2: A Contextual Mechanism Network Towards General Structured-Data Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17488)
*Xingxuan Zhang,Gang Ren,Hao Yuan,Hao Zou,Hongze Tan,Hui Wang,Jianhao Song,Jiansheng Li,Jiayao Zhang,Jinghan Zhang,Kaifang Li,Lang Mo,Li Mao,Mingchao Hao,Nuo Xu,Rui Ding,Ruiji Zhang,Shuyang Li,Siyu Mei,Tianyang Zhang,Weiyang Mu,Yancheng Dong,Yongxian Wei,Yuan Xue,Yuanrui Wang,Yue He,Zijia Yang,Ziyun Li,Dongzhe Li,Fuqiang Wang,Jiandong Liu,Jiawei Chen,Jiaxin Du,Kaijie Cheng,Kehan Li,Lei Sun,Linjun Zhou,Ningbo Dai,Qi Wang,Renzhe Xu,Shaoxing Du,Shumeng Yang,Wang Lu,Wenjing Chu,Xiannan Huang,Xiaoyu Lin,Xing Ai,Xinyan Han,Xuanyue Li,Xuanyue Su,Xukun Zhang,Yan Lu,Yaxin Zhang,Yi Qin,Yifei Huang,Yihan Xu,Yongle Lv,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yushan Han,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LimiX-2，一个基于Contextual Mechanism Networks（CMNs）范式的表格基础模型。它通过Context-Conditional Masked Modeling（CCMM）预训练，将上下文学习的组织原则从目标中心预测p(y|x, D)转向机制导向的联合建模p(x,y|D)，并使用结构因果模型生成的合成数据预训练。在TabArena、TALENT和BCCO基准上超越现有数据集专用模型和表格基础模型，同时其特征注意力编码了直接因果关系，具备因果骨架恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格基础模型（如PFN）采用目标中心预测目标p(y|x, D_context)，这种组织原则忽略了数据生成背后的联合结构和机制，限制了模型对表格数据的理解能力与因果感知能力。作者希望将上下文学习范式从单纯预测转向机制导向的联合建模，以提升表格式基础模型的泛化性能和可解释性。

Method: LimiX-2基于LimiX家族中先前建立的缩放定律进行模型与数据缩放，采用CMNs范式，以p(x,y|D_context)（上下文依赖的联合结构表示）替代传统p(y|x,D_context)预测目标，通过CCMM预训练算法，利用涵盖多种图结构、功能机制和观测过程的结构因果模型（SCMs）生成合成数据集进行预训练。

Result: 在TabArena、TALENT和BCCO基准上的评估显示，LimiX-2优于当前数据集专用模型和表格基础模型。此外，CMN范式提升了模型的因果意识：其特征注意力编码了直接因果关系，能够实现准确的因果骨架恢复。

Conclusion: LimiX-2验证了机制导向的联合建模范式（CMNs+CCMM）在表格基础模型上的有效性，不仅带来更优的预测性能，还赋予模型因果结构学习能力，为表格数据建模与因果发现提供了统一的新思路。

Abstract: We introduce LimiX-2, a new model in the LimiX family, developed through model and data scaling guided by our previously established scaling laws. LimiX-2 adopts the Contextual Mechanism Networks (CMNs) paradigm and is pretrained with Context-Conditional Masked Modeling (CCMM). CMNs shifts the organizing principle of in-context learning from target-centric prediction to mechanism-oriented joint modeling. Rather than centering the network on the $p(y \mid x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$ objective of conventional tabular PFNs, it is designed around learning $p(x, y \mid D_{\mathrm{context}})$, a context-dependent representation of the joint structure underlying data generation. Pretraining uses synthetic datasets generated by structural causal models (SCMs) spanning diverse graph structures, functional mechanisms, and observation processes. Evaluations on TabArena, TALENT, and BCCO show that LimiX-2 outperforms current dataset-specific models and tabular foundation models. Beyond predictive performance, the CMN paradigm also promotes causal awareness in LimiX-2: its feature attention encodes direct causal relationships, enabling accurate causal skeleton recovery.

</details>


### [239] [Verifiable Social Reasoning for LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17496)
*Amir Taubenfeld,Zorik Gekhman,Avigail Grinstein-Dabush,Itay Laish,Ariel Goldstein,Marian Croak,Avinatan Hassidim,Yossi Matias,Amir Feder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Fuse，一个用于研究用户中介社交推理的多智能体仿真框架。在Fuse中，带隐藏动机的目标智能体与其他智能体（包括代表用户的智能体）交互，用户随后向被测助手咨询以推断目标动机，从而通过构造提供可验证的真值。通过一项包含24k标注的人类研究验证了仿真保真度。作者将Fuse应用于12个LLM，系统性地隔离关键因素，发现：(i)用户中介加剧了社交推理的固有难度；(ii)LLM对偏颇的用户框架表现出系统性敏感；(iii)模型可能需要比人类更多的细节才能得出正确预测；(iv)更长的对话并不总能提升性能。作者开源了Fuse及含21k示例的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM助手在日常社交建议中被广泛使用，但评估其在咨询场景中的社交推理能力面临两大挑战：一是需要助手从主观用户叙述中学习社交情境的设置；二是意图等社交属性通常缺乏可验证的真值。作者旨在构建一个能提供可验证真值、同时贴近真实咨询场景的评估方法。

Method: 提出Fuse多智能体仿真框架：目标智能体持隐藏动机与其他智能体（包括用户代表）交互，用户随后咨询被测LLM助手以推断目标动机，由此通过构造获得可验证的ground truth。通过含24k标注的人类研究验证仿真保真度，并将Fuse应用于12个LLM进行系统性因素隔离分析。

Result: 实证发现四点：（i）用户中介会加剧社交推理的固有难度；（ii）LLM对偏颇的用户框架呈现系统性敏感；（iii）模型有时需要比人类更多的细节才能达到正确预测；（iv）更长的对话并非总能改善性能，尽管它提供了澄清性提问的机会。并开源了Fuse框架和含21k示例的数据集。

Conclusion: Fuse提供了首个通过构造具备可验证真值的用户中介社交推理评估框架，验证其仿真保真度并系统揭示了LLM在咨询式社交推理中的若干关键局限与行为模式，为构建更稳健的社交AI助手提供了分析工具与基准数据。

Abstract: LLM assistants are widely used for daily social advice, yet evaluating their social reasoning in such consultation settings remains challenging since (i) it requires setups where the assistant learns about social situations from subjective user narratives, and (ii) social properties, such as others' intentions, typically lack verifiable ground truth. To address these challenges, we introduce Fuse, a multi-agent simulation framework for studying user-mediated social reasoning. In Fuse, a target agent with a hidden motive interacts with other agents including one representing the user, who then consults the evaluated assistant to infer the target's motive, providing verifiable ground truth by construction. Simulation faithfulness is validated through a human study with 24k annotations. We apply Fuse to 12 LLMs and demonstrate its analytical utility by systematically isolating key factors, showing that (i) user mediation compounds the inherent difficulty of social reasoning; (ii) LLMs exhibit systematic sensitivity to biased user framing; (iii) models can require more details than humans need to reach a correct prediction; and (iv) longer conversations do not always improve performance despite providing opportunities for clarifying questions. We open-source Fuse and a dataset with 21k examples.

</details>


### [240] [ScienceBuddy: Recursive-in-Recursive Self-Improvement for Interactive Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17523)
*Shuhan Xue,Jianyuan Zhong,Ziyuan Nan,Wenbin Li,Zhaochen Yu,Jinchao Ding,Qiang Gao,Pengyu Zhan,Yuntong Zhang,Tian Cheng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceBuddy是一个交互式科学研究工作空间，它通过"递归中的递归自我改进"范式，将研究者请求、反馈和执行证据转化为任务与评估标准，实现持续学习的科学智能体，让科学AI在研究者的日常工作中不断完善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将持续改进的科学智能体引入研究者的日常工作流程，推动"发现智能"——即通过与研究者的持续协作来不断进化并支持研究本身的科学AI，解决现有科学AI缺乏持续学习能力和与研究者深度融合的问题。

Method: 采用递归中的递归自我改进范式：内层递归在模型固定的情况下改进"护栏"（harness），外层递归在改进后的护栏下训练模型。护栏进化塑造训练体验，模型学习为护栏适应创造新机会，同时将研究者的请求、反馈和执行证据转化为持续学习的任务与评估标准。

Result: 提供了研究者交互、护栏改进和模型学习的案例研究，基准案例涵盖四个科学任务家族；系统已作为研究产品发布供科学界使用。

Conclusion: 通过发布ScienceBuddy，将该范式提供给科学社区，向"发现智能"迈出一步：科学AI能够通过与研究者的持续协作不断进步，并与它所支持的研究共同进化。

Abstract: We introduce and release ScienceBuddy, an interactive scientific research workspace that brings continually improving scientific agents into researchers' everyday workflows. ScienceBuddy supports researchers in carrying out scientific tasks while transforming their requests, feedback, and execution evidence into tasks and evaluation rubrics for continual learning. At its core is recursive-in-recursive self-improvement, a paradigm that couples harness evolution with model reinforcement learning: the inner recursion improves the harness with the model fixed, while the outer recursion trains the model under the improved harness. Harness evolution shapes training experience, and model learning creates new opportunities for harness adaptation. We present case studies of researcher interaction, harness refinement, and model learning, with the benchmark cases spanning four scientific task families. By releasing ScienceBuddy as a research product, we make this paradigm available to the scientific community and take a step toward discovery intelligence: scientific AI that advances through sustained collaboration with researchers and evolves alongside the research it supports. Website: http://science-buddy.io

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Multimodal Emergency Vehicle Classification via Audio-Visual Transformers and Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16535)
*Vijay John,Amar Dabaja*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: AVNet 是一个多模态音视频 Transformer，用于自动驾驶中紧急车辆检测（救护车、消防车、警车及道路背景）。它通过时间对齐的跨模态融合模块、学习到的空嵌入（处理模态缺失）以及知识蒸馏训练策略，在单模态或双模态模式下均能工作。在 AudioSet 281 个片段上，音视频联合模式达到 66.6% 的总体准确率，比音频分支高 +10.4%，比视频分支高 +15.0%，其中最难的救护车类别获得了 +29.5% 的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急车辆检测是自动驾驶中安全攸关的感知任务，需在多样且恶劣的真实条件下保持鲁棒。现有方法依赖单一模态（音频或视频），当该模态退化时会系统性失效：麦克风系统在嘈杂城市环境失效，摄像头系统在夜间或遮挡下失效。因此需要一个既能融合双模态又能在任一模态缺失时优雅降级的统一模型。

Method: 提出 AVNet 多模态音视频 Transformer，包含三个关键贡献：(1) 时间对齐的跨模态融合模块，利用音频频谱图和视频帧间精确的时间对应关系进行秒级交叉注意力，无需学习对齐机制；(2) 学习到的空嵌入替代缺失模态的 token，使单一模型无需重训练即可在音频-only、视频-only 或联合音视频模式下运行；(3) 知识蒸馏训练策略，通过软概率目标将单模态专家教师模型的类间暗知识迁移到多模态学生融合分支。

Result: 在 Google AudioSet 数据集的 281 个片段上评估，AVNet 在音视频联合模式下达到 66.6% 的总体准确率，比音频-only 分支高 +10.4%，比视频-only 分支高 +15.0%。最难类别 Ambulance 的融合增益最大，达到 +29.5%，说明两种模态提供了互补信息且被对齐交叉注意力机制有效利用。

Conclusion: 实验证明双模态提供互补信息，时间对齐的交叉注意力能有效融合它们，使紧急车辆检测在模态退化/缺失时仍保持鲁棒；AVNet 通过统一模型优雅处理模态缺失，无需重训练，且知识蒸馏策略进一步提升了融合分支性能。

Abstract: Emergency vehicle detection in autonomous driving is a safety-critical perception task that demands robustness under diverse and adverse real-world conditions. Existing approaches rely on a single modality, either audio or video, which leads to systematic failure when that modality is degraded: microphone-based systems fail in noisy urban environments, and camera-based systems fail at night or under occlusion. This report presents AVNet, a multimodal audio-visual transformer that classifies emergency vehicles (ambulance, fire engine, police car) and road background using both audio and video, while gracefully handling the absence of either modality at inference time. AVNet introduces three key contributions: (1) a temporally aligned cross-modal fusion module that performs second-level cross-attention between audio spectrogram tokens and video frame tokens, exploiting their exact temporal correspondence without any learned alignment mechanism; (2) learned null embeddings that substitute for missing modality tokens, enabling a single unified model to operate in audio-only, video-only, or joint audio-visual mode without retraining; and (3) a knowledge distillation training strategy in which specialist unimodal teacher models transfer inter-class dark knowledge into the multimodal student fusion branch via soft probability targets. Evaluated on 281 clips from the Google AudioSet dataset, AVNet achieves 66.6% overall accuracy in audio-visual mode, outperforming the audio-only branch by +10.4% and the video-only branch by +15.0%. The largest per-class gain is observed for the hardest class, Ambulance, where fusion achieves +29.5% over either unimodal branch alone, demonstrating that the two modalities provide complementary information that the aligned cross attention mechanism successfully exploits.

</details>


### [242] [Mechanism-Level Evaluation for Vision-Language Models: Controlled Activation-Replacement Diagnosis of Gender Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16651)
*Zhipeng Zhao,Wenxu Wang,Peishun Liu,Ruichun Tang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文主张在行为基准测试之外引入机制层面的评估，使用因果中介分析作为诊断性别偏见的工具，将性别线索效应分解为特定层激活的受控间接效应与其余通路的直接效应，生成逐层机制签名，并在六个跨三大家族的视觉语言模型上揭示了语言层激活敏感性最高、架构改变敏感性分布、反事实分数与表层分数脱节等发现，同时验证了机制诊断与干预效果仅弱相关（r=0.33），表明机制诊断能捕捉架构特定敏感性但无法独立确定最优干预靶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行为基准测试只能回答模型中存在哪些偏见（what），却无法揭示哪些内部组件对定向干预最敏感（which），从而阻碍了有原则的干预策略设计。作者主张以机制级评估作为必要补充，以理解偏见产生的内部机制。

Method: 采用因果中介分析（causal mediation analysis）作为诊断工具，将性别线索效应分解为：归因于特定层激活的受控间接效应（controlled indirect effect）和通过所有其他通路的直接效应，从而生成逐层的机制签名。在六个模型（LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、InstructBLIP的7B/13B版本以及两个8B规模架构）上进行系统性消融验证，并进行干预实验评估平均间接效应（AIE）与实际干预有效性之间的相关性。

Result: 三项主要发现：(1) 在受控干预下语言层激活表现出最大的输出敏感性，且直接成分常常携带相反的符号；(2) 架构选择会重新分布对激活替换的层敏感度；(3) 反事实分数与表层分数脱节，暴露了隐含关联。系统性消融验证了内部一致性。干预实验发现AIE与下游干预有效性仅弱相关（Pearson r=0.33），且第二大AIE的层产生的偏见变化接近零。

Conclusion: 机制级评估能够捕捉行为基准测试无法获得的架构特定敏感性模式，但机制诊断本身只能反映激活替换敏感性，无法独立识别最优干预靶点。作者建议将行为基准与机制评估配对使用，并将其确立为标准NLP实践。

Abstract: Behavioral benchmarking reveals \emph{what} biases exist in vision-language models but not \emph{which internal components} are most sensitive to targeted intervention, precluding principled intervention. We argue for mechanism-level evaluation as a necessary complement, demonstrating causal mediation analysis as a diagnostic instrument for gender bias. We decompose gender-cue effects into controlled indirect effects attributable to specific-layer activations and direct effects through all other pathways, producing layer-by-layer mechanistic signatures. Across six models spanning three architectural families (LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, InstructBLIP at 7B/13B) and two 8B-scale architectures, three findings emerge: language-layer activations exhibit the greatest output sensitivity under controlled intervention, with the direct component often carrying the opposite sign; architectural choices redistribute layer-wise sensitivity to activation replacement; and counterfactual scores diverge from surface-level scores, exposing implicit associations. Systematic ablation validates internal consistency. An intervention experiment finds that the average indirect effect (AIE) and downstream intervention effectiveness are only weakly correlated (Pearson $r = 0.33$), and the layer with the second-largest AIE produces near-zero bias change---indicating that mechanistic diagnosis captures activation-replacement sensitivity but does not, by itself, identify optimal intervention targets. These results show mechanism-level evaluation captures architecture-specific sensitivity patterns that behavioral benchmarks cannot; pairing both should become standard NLP practice. Code: https://github.com/zhaozhipeng1997/CARD-GenderBias.

</details>


### [243] [CTAN: Cycle-Temporal Attention Network for Embodied Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17420)
*Teng Liu,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Cycle-Temporal Attention Network (CTAN) 的框架，用于解决视听具身导航中跨模态语义交互不足的问题。该框架通过引入 Audio-Visual Reconstruction Cross-Attention (AVRCA) 模块，利用双向循环一致性约束强化视觉与声学表征间的空间语义属性，并设计了 Temporal Cross-Modal Memory (TCMM) 机制动态整合实时增强的多模态特征与历史上下文，以缓解听觉死区带来的性能下降。在 Replica 和 Matterport3D 基准上的实验表明，该方法在成功率(SR)、成功率加权路径长度(SPL)和场景导航准确率(SNA)上均优于以往方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视听具身导航中的特征融合策略多采用简单的多模态聚合方式，无法捕获异构模态（视觉与听觉，其特征分布差异显著）之间深层的几何与语义关系，导致在复杂环境中出现信息退化。核心痛点在于缺少主动的语义增强融合，且难以处理听觉死区场景。

Method: 提出 CTAN 框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) AVRCA 模块，通过视觉与声学表征间的双向循环一致性约束，强化两种模态的空间语义属性，实现更稳健的跨模态交互（强调主动语义增强融合而非简单组合）；(2) TCMM 机制，将实时增强后的多模态特征与历史上下文动态整合，从而减轻听觉死区导致的性能衰减。

Result: 在 Replica 和 Matterport3D 两个基准上，该方法在成功率(SR)、成功率加权路径长度(SPL)和场景导航准确率(SNA)三项指标上均取得了优于以往视听导航方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过循环一致性约束驱动的语义增强跨模态融合以及结合时序记忆的上下文整合，CTAN 有效提升了视听导航在复杂环境中的鲁棒性和导航性能，为具身智能中的异构模态融合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Audio-visual embodied navigation equips robots with the capability to infer the locations of sound sources by integrating visual inputs and acoustic information (e.g., depth observations and binaural audio cues). The core challenge lies in establishing effective semantic interactions across heterogeneous modalities (which exhibit distinct feature distributions). Existing feature fusion strategies, however, often rely on simple multimodal aggregation and therefore fail to capture the underlying geometric and semantic relationships, leading to information degradation in complex environments. To overcome these limitations, this work presents the Cycle-Temporal Attention Network (CTAN), a framework designed for active semantic-enhanced fusion (rather than straightforward multimodal combination). Specifically, the proposed Audio-Visual Reconstruction Cross-Attention (AVRCA) module employs a bidirectional cycle-consistency constraint (between visual and acoustic representations) to reinforce the spatial semantic attributes of both modalities, thereby facilitating more robust cross-modal interaction. Additionally, we design a Temporal Cross-Modal Memory (TCMM) mechanism to dynamically integrate real-time enhanced multimodal features with historical context, reducing performance drops caused by auditory dead zones. Experimental results obtained on the Replica and Matterport3D benchmarks indicate that the proposed approach achieves superior performance over previous audio-visual navigation methods in terms of success rate (SR), success weighted by path length (SPL), and scene navigation accuracy (SNA).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Causal neural set filtering for online multi-target tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16054)
*Zhongdi Liu,Huangyu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果神经集滤波（CNSF），一种新型多目标跟踪（MTT）框架。与MT3/Track-MT3等Transformer类跟踪器反复重新编码测量窗口不同，CNSF仅编码当前测量，同时通过结构化递归轨道状态传递过去证据。CNSF结合排他性Sinkhorn关联、关联条件化的Kalman式更新（含矩匹配）以及循环Bernoulli生命周期建模（含测量驱动的出生）。相比Track-MT3，在三个情景的保留测试集上，平均GOSPA和T-GOSPA分别降低19.3%和30.4%，参数减少55.9%，单线程CPU推理提速3.76倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的MTT方法（如MT3/Track-MT3）虽能联合学习数据关联与状态估计，但会反复重新编码测量窗口，导致大量冗余计算，计算效率低下。

Method: CNSF采用因果神经集滤波框架：仅编码当前测量，以结构化递归轨道状态携带历史证据。核心组件包括：(1)排他性Sinkhorn关联（施加软一对一约束）；(2)关联条件化的Kalman式更新结合矩匹配（传播关联引起的状态不确定性）；(3)循环Bernoulli生命周期建模与测量驱动出生（在漏检和出生-死亡转换下估计目标存在性）。

Result: 在三个情景的保留模拟测试集上：平均GOSPA降低19.3%、T-GOSPA降低30.4%；参数量减少55.9%；单线程CPU推理速度提升3.76倍，均优于Track-MT3基线。

Conclusion: CNSF通过因果化编码和结构化递归状态，在减少冗余计算的同时提升跟踪精度与效率，证明了在不损失甚至提升性能的前提下大幅降低Transformer类跟踪器计算开销的可行性。

Abstract: Transformer-based multi-target tracking (MTT) jointly learns data association and state estimation, but MT3/Track-MT3-style trackers repeatedly re-encode measurement windows, incurring redundant computation. We propose Causal Neural Set Filtering (CNSF)\footnote{\href{https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}{Code: https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}}, a neural set filter that encodes only current measurements while carrying past evidence in a structured recursive track state. CNSF combines exclusive Sinkhorn association, association-conditioned Kalman-shaped updates with moment matching, and recurrent Bernoulli lifecycle modeling with measurement-driven birth. These mechanisms impose soft one-to-one constraints, propagate association-induced state uncertainty, and support existence estimation under missed detections and birth--death transitions. On a held-out three-regime simulated test set, CNSF reduces mean GOSPA and T-GOSPA relative to Track-MT3 by 19.3\% and 30.4\%, with 55.9\% fewer parameters and a $3.76\times$ speedup in single-thread CPU inference.

</details>


### [245] [Managing Action Preconditions in Neuro-Symbolic RL: Three Placement Strategies for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16056)
*Norbert Oswald,Fabian Deuser,Thomas Bräunl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号强化学习方法，将行为先验知识形式化为结构动作的前置条件贝叶斯网络，并测试了三种注入位置（符号验证器、符号执行器、符号学习器）。在MiniGrid和Fetch两个基准上实验，结果显示符号执行器在MiniGrid上达到98.2%的解决方案质量，显著优于PPO+RND基线的88.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会将熟悉情境中的行为知识迁移到新任务中，无需从头学习。强化学习智能体也应如此，但现有关键问题在于知识注入点选择不当可能导致幻觉前置条件，引发安全性和可靠性问题。

Method: 将行为知识形式化为关于结构动作（如拾取钥匙、抓取方块、开关门、放下物体等）的前置条件贝叶斯网络，限制这些动作的触发时机。在RL循环中测试三种注入位置：(1)符号验证器（仅在推理时使用）；(2)符号执行器（训练和推理时都限制结构动作）；(3)符号学习器（将知识融入网络并自行学习限制）。在两个基准（长有序规划链的MiniGrid和连续操作的Fetch）上评估。

Result: 在MiniGrid上，三种注入方式都提升了相对PPO+RND基线的解决方案质量，其中符号执行器最优（98.2%对比88.8%）。Fetch实验结果因摘要截断未完整给出，但提到对比了解决方案质量、样本效率和可追溯性。

Conclusion: 将行为先验知识合理注入RL可以显著提升解决方案质量和效率，且符号执行器在训练和推理中都约束动作的使用，是最有效的方案。

Abstract: Humans carry behaviour knowledge of how to act in familiar situations into every new task rather than relearning it from scratch. There is no reason a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent shouldn't do the same: known behaviour patterns need not be learned, only applied. Neuro-symbolic RL bridges prior knowledge and RL by injecting symbolic knowledge alongside a learned policy. The point at which this knowledge is integrated is critical: a poor choice can produce, for instance, hallucinated preconditions, which surface as safety and reliability problems in agents acting in changing environments. We formalise this behavioural knowledge as a precondition Bayesian network (BN) over the agent's \emph{structural actions} - the actions whose legality depends on preconditions, such as picking up a key, grasping a block, toggling a door, or dropping an object. The BN restricts when these actions may fire, and we inject it into the RL loop at three placements: (1) a \emph{symbolic verifier}, consulted only at inference, that fires a structural action once its preconditions hold; (2) a \emph{symbolic enforcer}, active during both training and inference, that governs structural-action use throughout learning; and (3) a \emph{symbolic learner}, which folds the knowledge into the network and learns the restriction and use of structural actions itself. To test the three variants we run experiments on two benchmarks with opposite regimes: one built on long, ordered planning chains, the other on continuous manipulation. We compare against strong baselines on solution quality, sample efficiency, and traceability. The payoff is substantial. On MiniGrid, all three placements improve the \emph{solution quality} over the PPO+RND baseline, the symbolic enforcer leading at $98.2\%$ against the baseline's $88.8\%$. On Fetch, $\dots$

</details>


### [246] [OmniHarness: Harnessing Generalizable Visual Generation via Symbolic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16057)
*Xu Xu,Jinxiu Liu,Zhangbo Qiao,Jiaxing Lu,Xiangyu Zhang,Yubin Gu,Fangwei Ning,Yan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniHarness是一个通过符号化策略学习实现通用视觉生成的框架。它将已验证的执行过程抽象为符号化策略，涵盖共享流程与适用条件；引入中间验证以引导执行中的修正与故障恢复；通过自主驱动的探究，在下游目标明确前自动生成并执行接近能力极限的练习任务，从而在不修改模型参数的情况下持续扩展能力。在六个基准、三个MLLM骨干和三个视觉智能体框架上验证了其性能，并在ComfyBench的Creative任务上达到95.0%的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态大语言模型与多智能体系统在视觉生成方面虽有进展，但仍存在三大局限：任务特定经验的可泛化性有限；反思往往推迟到任务完成之后；知识仅在应对下游任务需求时才被动获取。作者旨在解决这三方面缺陷，实现可泛化且可持续扩展的视觉生成。

Method: OmniHarness将已验证的执行过程抽象为针对视觉生成任务族的符号化策略，抽取共享流程与适用条件并去除实例特定输入；通过实例化、适配与组合这些策略来应对新任务；在执行过程中引入中间验证以引导细化和故障恢复；通过自主驱动的探究，在明确下游目标前自动生成并执行接近能力边界的练习任务；执行反馈持续改进策略，而模型参数保持固定。

Result: 在六个基准、三个MLLM骨干和三个视觉智能体框架上进行实验，展示了强劲性能与持续能力扩展。在ComfyBench的Creative任务上，OmniHarness达到95.0%的解决率，超过最强基线27.5个百分点；冻结的策略快照可通过即插即用复用提升现有视觉智能体系统的表现。

Conclusion: OmniHarness通过符号化策略学习有效克服了可泛化性、延迟反思与被动知识获取三大局限，证明其既能显著提升视觉生成任务的性能，又能通过即插即用的策略快照增强现有系统，实现持续的能力扩展。

Abstract: Unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and multi-agent systems have advanced visual generation. However, three limitations remain. (1) Existing methods often distill task-specific experience with limited generalizability. (2) Reflection is often deferred until task completion. (3) Knowledge is often acquired only in response to downstream task demands. To address these limitations, we introduce OmniHarness, a framework for generalizable visual generation via symbolic policy learning. OmniHarness abstracts verified executions into symbolic policies for visual generation task families, capturing shared procedures and applicability conditions while removing instance-specific inputs. The harness instantiates, adapts, and composes these policies for new tasks. Intermediate verification guides refinement and failure recovery during execution. Through self-directed inquiry, OmniHarness autonomously generates and executes practice tasks near its capability limits before downstream objectives are specified. Execution feedback continually refines the policies while model parameters remain fixed. Experiments across six benchmarks, three MLLM backbones, and three visual agent frameworks demonstrate strong performance and continual capability expansion. On ComfyBench's Creative tasks, OmniHarness achieves a 95.0% resolve rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 27.5 percentage points. Frozen policy snapshots improve existing visual agent systems through plug-and-play reuse.

</details>


### [247] [Driver Behavior Estimation at Signalized Intersections Using a Physics-Constrained Decision-Conditioned Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16058)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,Pavel Laskov,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究信号交叉口处黄灯转换过程中人类驾驶员的停止-通过决策与纵向轨迹预测问题。作者采集了包含449次接近运行的实测数据集，结合RTK高精度GNSS定位、心率监测与舒适度评级，通过统计分析发现"所需减速度"是停止-通过决策的主导预测因子，并基于人类减速行为提炼出五个经验舒适区间。据此提出两阶段建模框架：第一阶段预测二元机动决策，第二阶段利用带物理约束（目标状态条件与急动度限制）的决策条件自回归Transformer生成纵向加速度轨迹。该架构在加速度MAE 0.49m/s²、距离MAE 0.62m上优于基线方法，并能从黄灯起始的单帧快照估计未来停车舒适度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号交叉口处的闯红灯与急刹是交通事故的主要诱因。理解并预测人类驾驶员在信号灯转换期间（尤其是黄灯阶段）的决策和纵向运动行为，对于提升交通安全、开发驾驶员辅助系统及评测自动驾驶人机共驾策略具有重要价值。

Method: 1) 数据采集：构建包含449次接近运行的多样化真实场景数据集，覆盖不同速度与距离条件，采用RTK校正GNSS厘米级定位记录车辆运动，并同步监测驾驶员心率及多级舒适度评分，通过时空标定保证车辆状态与信号灯时序精确对齐。2) 统计分析：识别"所需减速度"为停止-通过决策的单一主导预测因子；对峰值减速度进行异方差高斯建模，归纳出五个来自人类停车行为的经验舒适区间。3) 建模框架：两阶段模型，阶段一预测二元机动决策，阶段二采用决策条件化的自回归Transformer生成纵向加速度轨迹，并引入物理约束（目标状态条件化与急动度限制）。

Result: 所提两阶段框架优于基线方法，取得0.49m/s²的加速度MAE与0.62m的距离MAE；能够仅从黄灯起始的单帧快照估计人类驾驶员未来的停车舒适度级别；定性结果展现出逼真的人性化制动行为。数据集与源代码均已公开。

Conclusion: "所需减速度"是人类黄灯停止-通过决策的关键预测因素，基于人类停车行为提炼的五级舒适区间为轨迹生成提供了有效先验。两阶段决策+物理约束Transformer框架能够生成类人、安全且符合舒适度的制动轨迹，并具备前瞻性的舒适度估计能力，为信号交叉口驾驶员行为预测与安全辅助提供了可复现的研究基础。

Abstract: Red-light violations and harsh braking at signalized intersections are major contributors to traffic accidents. This paper analyzes and predicts human driver decision-making and longitudinal trajectory behavior during traffic light signal transitions. We collected a diverse real-world dataset comprising 449 approach runs under varying speed and distance conditions. Vehicle motion was recorded using RTK-corrected GNSS with centimeter-level accuracy, and driver heart rate and multi-level comfort ratings were monitored. Spatial and temporal calibration ensured precise alignment between vehicle state and signal timing. Statistical analysis identifies required deceleration as the dominant single predictor of the stop-go decision, and heteroscedastic Gaussian modeling of peak deceleration reveals five empirical comfort ranges derived from human stopping behavior. Based on this insight, we propose a two-stage modeling framework. Stage 1 predicts the binary maneuver decision, and Stage 2 generates the longitudinal acceleration trajectory using a decision-conditioned autoregressive Transformer with physics constraints, including target-state conditioning and jerk limits. The proposed architecture outperforms baseline methods and achieves 0.49m/s^2 acceleration MAE and 0.62m distance MAE. It also estimates the future stopping-comfort level of the human driver from a single yellow-onset snapshot. Qualitative results demonstrate realistic human-like braking behavior. The dataset and source code are publicly available.

</details>


### [248] [HintMiner: Automatic Question Hints Mining From Q&A Web Posts with Language Model via Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16060)
*Zhenyu Zhang,JiuDong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HintMiner，一个自动挖掘问答提示的工具，利用机器理解和序列生成技术，从网络问答帖子中提取提示帮助用户回答问题，在Stack Overflow数据集上评估有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在网上提问时，问答论坛（如Stack Overflow）往往不能及时、恰当地回答，因此需要自动生成提示来帮助用户找到答案。

Method: HintMiner首先检索大量网络问答帖子，然后使用基于语言模型构建的MiningNet从帖子中提取提示。MiningNet是一个基于Transformer和复制机制的神经编码器-解码器模型，利用海量在线问答帖子通过自监督目标进行训练。

Result: 在60,000个Stack Overflow问题上评估，平均BLEU分数为36.17%，平均ROUGE-2分数为36.29%，表明该方法有效。

Conclusion: HintMiner能有效自动生成问题提示，工具和实验数据已公开可用。

Abstract: Users often need ask questions and seek answers online. The Question - Answering (QA) forums such as Stack Overflow cannot always respond to the questions timely and properly. In this paper, we propose HintMiner, a novel automatic question hints mining tool for users to help them find answers. HintMiner leverages the machine comprehension and sequence generation techniques to automatically generate hints for users' questions. It firstly retrieve many web Q\&A posts and then extract some hints from the posts using MiningNet that is built via a language model. Using the huge amount of online Q\&A posts, we design a self-supervised objective to train the MiningNet that is a neural encoder-decoder model based on the transformer and copying mechanisms. We have evaluated HintMiner on 60,000 Stack Overflow questions. The experiment results show that the proposed approach is effective. For example, HintMiner achieves an average BLEU score of 36.17\% and an average ROUGE-2 score of 36.29\%. Our tool and experimental data are publicly available.

</details>


### [249] [POSPAN: Position-Constrained Span Masking for Language Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16061)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Lei Shen,Yuming Zhao,Meng Chen,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出POSPAN，一个通用的位置约束span掩码框架，通过结合span长度分布和位置约束分布，统一了所有现有的span级掩码方法。实验表明位置约束能广泛增强span级掩码效果，最佳POSPAN设置持续优于仅使用span长度的对应方法及原始MLM，并通过理论分析阐明了POSPAN有效的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: span级掩码语言建模（MLM）相比单token MLM对预训练语言模型更有利，因为实体/短语及其依赖关系对语言理解至关重要。然而此前工作仅考虑使用某些离散分布的span长度，而忽略了span之间的依赖关系，即假设掩码span的位置服从均匀分布，缺乏对位置约束的建模。

Method: 提出POSPAN通用框架，通过将span长度分布与位置约束分布相结合，实现多样化的位置约束span掩码策略，从而统一现有的所有span级掩码方法。框架通过调整位置约束分布来灵活控制掩码span的位置选择。

Result: 在多个NLU基准数据集上评估预训练效果，实验表明位置约束能够广泛地增强span级掩码性能，最佳POSPAN设置持续优于仅使用span长度的对应方法以及原始vanilla MLM。同时进行了关于位置约束在掩码语言模型中作用的理论分析，阐释了POSPAN有效的原理。

Conclusion: 位置约束对于span级掩码语言建模具有重要价值，POSPAN框架通过统一长度分布与位置约束证明了其合理性与必要性，为预训练语言模型提供了一种通用且有效的掩码策略。

Abstract: Span-level masked language modeling (MLM) has shown to be advantageous to pre-trained language models over the original single-token MLM, as entities/phrases and their dependencies are critical to language understanding. Previous works only consider span length with some discrete distributions, while the dependencies among spans are ignored, i.e., assuming that the positions of masked spans are uniformly distributed. In this paper, we present POSPAN, a general framework to allow diverse position-constrained span masking strategies via the combination of span length distribution and position constraint distribution, which unifies all existing span-level masking methods. To verify the effectiveness of POSPAN in pre-training, we evaluate it on the datasets from several NLU benchmarks. Experimental results indicate that the position constraint is capable of enhancing span-level masking broadly, and our best POSPAN setting consistently outperforms its span-length-only counterparts and vanilla MLM. We also conduct theoretical analysis for the position constraint in masked language models to shed light on the reason why POSPAN works well, demonstrating the rationality and necessity of POSPAN.

</details>


### [250] [Signed p-adic Residual Encodings of Finite-Domain All-Different Systems with a Sudoku Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16063)
*Greg Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带符号、加权的仿射 p-adic 剩余目标，将其作为有限域约束的“原生”编码。对于能分离有限字母表的素数，充分加权的正一元项可将每个系数限制在允许集合内，而负一元项则奖励不相等端点或子句满足。一个坐标支配定理保证所有全局极小值点都落在有限域内；在该域上，损失在相差一个加性常数后等于全不同冲突计数或负的满足 CNF 子句数。标准数独被用作 81 系数案例，无需 one-hot 提升；客户端实现展示了生成的数据帧、算术、诊断与搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是提出一种无需 one-hot 展开、直接用有限域约束的仿射 p-adic 目标编码方式，从而统一处理 all-different 和 CNF 类约束，并保证全局最优解落在有限字母表上。

Method: 采用带符号、加权的仿射 p-adic 剩余目标：正一元行把系数固定到允许集合，负一元行惩罚或奖励端点关系；通过坐标支配定理证明全局极小点位于有限域，并建立损失与冲突计数/满足子句数的等价关系。

Result: 证明了在素数分离字母表的条件下，带充分权重的仿射 p-adic 目标可以原生编码有限域约束；全局极小值域等价于有限域，损失与 all-different 冲突数或负 CNF 满足数仅差常数。数独案例验证了无需 one-hot 的 81 系数模型，客户端实现可输出数据帧、算术、诊断与搜索。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种替代 one-hot 的有限域约束编码方法，兼具理论保证与可解释的实现工具，适合数独等组合约束问题的原生建模与分析。

Abstract: We study signed, weighted affine $p$-adic residual objectives as native encodings of finite-domain constraints. For primes that separate the finite alphabet, sufficiently weighted positive unary rows pin each coefficient to its allowed set, while negative rows reward unequal endpoints or clause satisfaction. A coordinatewise domination theorem places every global minimiser in the finite domain; there the loss is, up to an additive constant, the all-different conflict count or the negative number of satisfied CNF clauses. Standard Sudoku provides an $81$-coefficient case study without a one-hot lift. A client-side implementation exposes the generated dataframes, arithmetic, diagnostics, and searches.

</details>


### [251] [You Don't Need To Train: Agentic Heuristic Learning Studio for Executable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16065)
*Siyu Yuan,He Zhang,Sizhen Bian,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"智能体启发式学习工作室"(AHL Studio)的工具，用于人类活动识别(HAR)。与传统基于梯度反向传播训练神经网络的方法不同，AHL从人类认知学习中获得启发：人类通过记忆示例、形成规则和修复错误来学习活动，而非通过反向传播。该工具在学习阶段使用智能体推理传感器协议、提出可执行的启发式策略、记录修复轨迹，并导出无需大语言模型(LLM)的策略用于边缘部署。在11个HAR数据集上，AHL策略在保持可检查、可编辑和可回放特性的同时，达到了强大的可执行策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HAR依赖神经网络的梯度训练，但这种方式缺乏可解释性且不适合资源受限的边缘部署场景。作者受到人类认知学习的启发——人类并非通过反向传播学习，而是通过记忆示例、形成规则和修复错误来学习——希望探索一种互补的、可检查、可编辑、可回放的学习范式，从而为HAR提供不依赖LLM的可执行策略。

Method: 该工具实现AHL用于HAR：在学习阶段，一个智能体对传感器协议进行推理，提出可执行的启发式策略；通过记录修复轨迹来迭代改进策略；最终导出一个不依赖LLM的轻量级策略以支持边缘部署。整个流程覆盖从数据集观察到边缘导出的端到端工作流。

Result: 在目前已评估的11个HAR数据集上，AHL策略达到了强大的可执行策略性能，同时保持了可检查(inspectable)、可编辑(editable)、可回放(replayable)的特性，并通过GitHub开源发布。

Conclusion: AHL Studio验证了一种互补于梯度训练的HAR学习范式：通过智能体引导的启发式学习和修复机制，可以构建出性能强劲、无需LLM、可直接用于边缘部署且具有良好透明性的HAR策略，为可解释、可维护的HAR系统提供了新的工具路径。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is usually framed as gradient-based training of neural networks. Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) Studio explores a complementary view inspired by human cognitive learning: people learn activities by remembering examples, forming rules, and repairing mistakes, not by backpropagating. This proposed tool implements AHL for HAR: a learning-time agent reasons over sensor protocols, proposes executable heuristic policies, records repair traces, and exports an LLM-free policy for edge deployment. We focus on the HAR benchmark family and provide an end-to-end workflow from dataset observation to edge-oriented export. On eleven HAR datasets evaluated so far, AHL policies reach strong executable-policy performance while remaining inspectable, editable, and replayable \footnote{https://github.com/zhaxidele/ahl-ts-studio}.

</details>


### [252] [A panoramic aerodynamic performance prediction method for turbomachinery cascades using transformer-enhanced neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16066)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全景性能预测框架，先预测Navier-Stokes方程的基本物理参数（温度、压力、密度），再据此预测涡轮级子午面的关键性能参数，使其类似CFD模拟器，可灵活评估多种设计目标。框架内置Transformer增强神经算子（TNO），在Rotor 37跨声速压气机叶片上训练，预测误差优于FNO和DeepONet，且可应用于敏感性分析和优化等下游任务，计算成本降低四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涡轮机械性能预测模型大多直接预测目标函数，缺乏灵活性，难以适应多种设计目标。为支持快速、灵活的气动性能评估，需要一种能像CFD模拟器一样预测任意感兴趣目标的框架。

Method: 先预测Navier-Stokes方程的基本物理量（温度、压力、密度），再基于这些物理量预测子午面的性能参数（如等熵效率、质量流量、总压比分布）。采用Transformer-enhanced神经算子（TNO）作为核心预测器，以Rotor 37叶片为参考进行训练。

Result: TNO能准确预测总压比分布等总量，预测误差小于FNO和DeepONet等最先进的深度学习算子。在下游任务（敏感性分析、多目标优化）中，TNO几乎能像CFD模拟器一样工作，同时将计算成本降低四个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的TNO框架有效且可靠，可支持多种下游任务，兼顾精度与计算效率，为涡轮机械快速气动性能评估提供了新途径。

Abstract: To enable flexible and rapid aerodynamic performance evaluation in turbomachinery design, this paper proposes a panoramic performance prediction framework. Unlike most previous prediction models that directly predict the objective functions of interest, our approach first predicts the basic parameters of the Navier-Stokes equations, such as temperature, pressure, and density. Utilizing these basic physical quantities, it subsequently predicts key performance parameters of the turbine stage meridian plane. By adopting this methodology, our proposed panoramic performance prediction framework functions similarly to a CFD simulator, capable of predicting various objective of interest to the designers. To enhance prediction accuracy, a transformer-enhanced neural operator (TNO) is introduced within this framework. Using the Rotor 37 blades as a reference, the proposed TNO is trained to predict the performance of a transonic compressor blade in the meridian plane. The TNO can accurately predict total quantities such as isentropic efficiency, mass flow, and distributions of total pressure ratio. Remarkably, the prediction error of TNO is observed to be smaller than that of state-of-the-art deep learning operators such as the FNO and DeepONet. Furthermore, the TNO is applied to downstream tasks, including sensitivity analysis and optimization of various objective functions. The results confirm that the TNO can operate almost like a CFD simulator, while reducing the computational cost of downstream tasks by four orders of magnitude. The effectiveness and reliability of the proposed TNO for solving different kinds of downstream tasks have been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [253] [A Dynamic Aggregation Strategy Enhanced Efficient Global Optimization Algorithm for Solving High-Dimensional Turbomachinery Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16067)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Yun Chen,Guangjian Ma,Liming Song,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种带动态聚合策略的高效全局优化算法DA-EGO，用于在有限预算内求解高维（d≥30）昂贵黑箱问题。该算法将高维设计空间分解为若干低维子空间进行代理模型优化搜索，并根据变量交互分析动态更新子空间划分，同时自适应调整各子空间的搜索范围。在21个基准实例（30/60/90维）及两类工程优化问题（跨声速转子叶片28变量、压气机级60变量）上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效全局优化（EGO）算法在处理高维（d≥30）且评估代价昂贵的黑箱问题时，受预算限制难以直接应用。高维空间中代理模型的精度与搜索效率迅速下降，亟需一种能够在有限评估次数内有效求解高维昂贵优化问题的策略。

Method: DA-EGO的核心思想是分治与动态聚合：（1）将原始高维设计空间分解为一组低维子空间，在各子空间内进行高效的代理模型优化搜索；（2）将各子空间的最优解组合成一个精英点用于全局搜索；（3）子空间划分并非固定，而是每一轮迭代根据子空间与全空间中的变量交互分析结果动态更新变量归属——采用扰动法和方差分析（ANOVA）检测变量交互；（4）为加速收敛，还会根据上一轮子空间优化结果的分析，自适应调整各子空间的搜索范围。

Result: 在21个基准实例（7个函数、30/60/90维）上，DA-EGO在1500次函数评估预算下对可分离及部分可分离问题有效。其优势因情形而异：对不可分离的平移Rosenbrock函数，GSGA在60、90维表现更好，而30维结果与IKAEA和GSGA在统计上相当。此外，在28变量的跨声速转子叶片气动优化及60变量的压气机级优化中，DA-EGO同样展现出优势。

Conclusion: 通过基准测试与两类工程优化案例的综合验证，DA-EGO的有效性得到了充分证明，能够高效求解高维昂贵黑箱优化问题。

Abstract: In order to solve the high-dimensional ($d \geq 30$) expensive black-box problems within budget, an efficient global optimization (EGO) algorithm with a dynamic aggregation strategy is proposed, labeled as DA-EGO. Specifically, the DA-EGO decomposes the original high-dimensional design space into a set of low-dimensional subspaces for efficient surrogate-based optimization search, and the optimal solutions of subspaces are combined as an elite point for the global search. Most importantly, the subspaces are not fixed. Instead, the subspace variables are updated in each iteration, according to the variable interaction analyses in the sub- and full-spaces. The perturbation method and the analysis of variance are used to detect variable interactions. To further accelerate the optimization progress, the searching ranges of subspaces are also adaptively adjusted according to the analyses of subspace optimization results of the previous iteration. Tests on 21 benchmark instances, comprising seven functions at 30, 60, and 90 dimensions, show that DA-EGO is effective on separable and partially separable problems under a budget of 1500 function evaluations. Its advantage is case-dependent: on the non-separable shifted Rosenbrock function, GSGA performs better at 60 and 90 dimensions, while the 30-dimensional results are statistically comparable to IKAEA and GSGA. Moreover, the advantage of DA-EGO is also seen in the aerodynamic optimization of a transonic rotor blade with 28 variables as well as the compressor stage optimization with 60 variables. With the above, the effectiveness of the proposed DA-EGO has been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [254] [Beyond Distribution Matching: Semantics-Consistent Tabular Diffusion with Weak Semantic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16069)
*Yili Wang,Ruxue Shi,Mengnan Du,Hangting Ye,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个语义一致的表格扩散框架（\ours），用于在弱化语义先验下生成高保真合成表格数据。该框架利用LLM从元数据中提取列内语义和列间符号规则两类先验，并在真实训练集上验证；随后将这些先验作为生成条件而非事后过滤器，将异构列值、列标识和语义先验映射到统一语义空间，执行按列的前向加噪和先验条件逆向去噪，以保持边际分布和符合规则的跨列依赖。在六个真实表格基准上的广泛实验表明，\ours在分布保真度、语义一致性和下游任务效用上均优于VAE、GAN、LLM和基于扩散的代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成器主要优化分布保真度，但未能显式建模表格模式（schema）和文本描述中编码的弱语义先验，导致合成数据即使匹配真实数据分布，仍可能违反控制合法表格行的语义约束。

Method: \ours提出一个两阶段方法：(1) 借助LLM从元数据中提取两类先验——列内语义和列间符号规则，并在真实训练划分上验证；(2) 将这些先验作为生成条件（而非事后过滤），将异构列值、列标识和语义先验映射到统一语义空间，采用按列前向加噪和先验条件的逆向去噪，同时保留边际分布和符合规则的跨列依赖。

Result: 在六个真实世界表格基准上的广泛实验表明，\ours在分布保真度、语义一致性和下游任务效用上，相对于VAE、GAN、LLM和基于扩散的代表性基线均取得一致改进。额外分析进一步展示了在语义先验部分缺失时\ours的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: \ours证明将弱语义先验作为显式生成条件可以有效弥合分布保真度与语义规范性之间的差距，为高保真、语义一致的合成表格数据生成提供了可行框架，且对先验不完整场景具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data can match real data distributions while still violating the semantic constraints that govern valid tabular rows. This reveals a key limitation of existing tabular generators: they mainly optimize distributional fidelity, but do not explicitly model weak semantic priors encoded in tabular schema and textual descriptions. In this paper, we propose \ours, a semantics-consistent tabular diffusion framework for high-fidelity synthetic data generation under weakly specified semantic priors. \ours\ first constructs two types of priors, namely intra-column semantics and inter-column symbolic rules, with LLM-assisted extraction from metadata and validation on the real training split. These priors are then used as generation conditions rather than post-hoc filters. Specifically, \ours\ maps heterogeneous column values, column identities, and semantic priors into a unified semantic space, and performs column-wise forward corruption and prior-conditioned reverse denoising to preserve both marginal distributions and rule-consistent cross-column dependencies. Extensive experiments on six real-world tabular benchmarks show that \ours\ consistently improves distributional fidelity, semantic consistency, and downstream task utility over representative VAE-, GAN-, LLM-, and diffusion-based baselines. Additional analyses further demonstrate the robustness of \ours\ when semantic priors are partially unavailable.

</details>


### [255] [Schema-Adaptive Action-Conditioned JEPA for Cross-Machine CNC Transfer under Partial Sensor Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16071)
*Ayoub Louaye Bouaziz,Matthieu Ostertag,Anton Demasles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究跨机床部署工业世界模型时的迁移问题，提出一种模式自适应、动作条件化的联合嵌入预测架构（SAAC-JEPA）用于CNC动力学建模。源机器有17个传感器通道，目标机器仅共享10个。严格评估（分组分离源数据、仅源归一化、留出式自监督验证、单元审计、锁定后的密封目标测试）表明：JEPA预训练对清洁源预测无增益（均方根误差0.811 vs 0.813）；源上搜索20个候选选出模式一致的JEPA，但零样本目标RMSE=0.546，仅优于持久性预测，不如带RevIN的PatchTST（0.503）和iTransformer（0.498）。预定配对消融显示RevIN可将同架构RMSE降至0.495±0.004，但损害目标校准（NLL=20.6）。结论：源域预测精度不足以评估工业预测表征，部分传感器重叠下的跨机器适应是独立评测维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机器部署工业世界模型需应对动力学变化、传感接口差异、采样体制和控制单元变化等转移挑战。本文关注CNC动力学场景中源机（17通道）与目标机（仅共享10通道）部分传感器重叠下的模型迁移，旨在探究源域预测精度是否足以评估工业预测表征，以及跨机器适应是否构成独立评测维度。

Method: 提出模式自适应、动作条件化的联合嵌入预测架构（SAAC-JEPA）。采用严格的评测协议：分组分离的源数据划分、仅使用源数据归一化、留出式自监督验证、单元审计（unit audits）、以及模型锁定后的密封目标测试。通过源上20个候选模型的搜索，并经7次种子稳定性检查选中模式一致的动作条件化JEPA。还执行了预定配对消融与锁定前适应扫描（limited target support）。

Result: 5个种子下，JEPA预训练对清洁源预测无增益：从零训练与预训练体模型RMSE分别为0.811±0.022和0.813±0.022。确认性目标阶段，锁定模型零样本RMSE=0.546，R²=0.012，NLL=0.52，优于持久性预测但不敌RevIN-PatchTST（0.503）和iTransformer（0.498）。配对消融表明同架构加RevIN将RMSE提升至0.495±0.004但使目标校准退化（NLL=20.6）；锁定前适应扫描在有限目标支持下将RMSE降至0.520。

Conclusion: 源域预测精度本身不足以评估工业预测表征的代表性；在部分传感器重叠条件下的跨机器适应应作为与源域精度相独立的评测轴线。该工作为工业世界模型的跨机器迁移评测提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: Cross-machine deployment of industrial world models requires transfer across changes in dynamics, sensing interfaces, sampling regimes, and control units. We study a schema-adaptive action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (SAAC-JEPA) for CNC dynamics, where the source machine has 17 canonical sensor channels and the target shares only 10. Evaluation uses group-disjoint source splits, source-only normalization, held-out self-supervised validation, unit audits, and a sealed target test after model locking. Across five seeds, JEPA pretraining gives no clean-source forecasting gain: scratch and pretrained-body models obtain \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.811\pm0.022\) and \(0.813\pm0.022\). A source-only search over 20 candidates selects a schema-consistent action-conditioned JEPA after seven-seed stability checks. On the confirmatory target pass, the locked model reaches zero-shot \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.546\), \(R^2=0.012\), and \(\mathrm{NLL}=0.52\), outperforming persistence but not RevIN-equipped PatchTST and iTransformer baselines (\(0.503\) and \(0.498\)). A pre-declared paired ablation shows that RevIN in the same architecture improves RMSE to \(0.495\pm0.004\) over three seeds, but degrades target calibration (\(\mathrm{NLL}=20.6\)) on stationary context windows. A pre-lock adaptation sweep further reduces RMSE to \(0.520\) with limited target support. These results show that source-domain forecasting accuracy alone is insufficient to assess industrial predictive representations, and that cross-machine adaptation under partial sensor overlap is a distinct evaluation axis.

</details>


### [256] [Pseudo-Label Augmentation for Affect Sensing in Small Collaborative Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16077)
*Meisam Jamshidi Seikavandi,Tanya Ignatenko,Fabricio Batista Narcizo,Paolo Burelli,Jesper Bünsow Boldt,Andrew Burke Dittberner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在自然群体互动中、稀疏标注场景下的伪标签增强情感感知方法。基于GroupAffect-4四人群组协作数据集，比较了无增强、高斯过程伪标签、人格感知信任加权、以及人格+置信度联合加权等目标构建方案。结果表明伪标签增强在已知团队设置下优于仅标注基线，但Big Five余弦相似度范围过窄(0.91-0.99)使细粒度人格加权无效，人格相似度主要作为同队筛选而非校准信任信号。平滑处理后SVM变体在Valence和Arousal上持平，联合人格+置信度变体在Dominance上得分最高。跨受试者LOSO迁移有希望(尤其Arousal)，而严格会话隔离LOGO消除了增强收益，因仅有10组，LOGO应视为未见组迁移的保守下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自然群体互动中，生理情感感知常受稀疏标签限制：可穿戴设备产生大量时间窗口，而自我报告每会话仅收集少数几次。作者希望利用伪标签增强来更好地利用稀疏标注的协作情感数据，并探究人格信息在伪标签信任加权中的作用。

Method: 基于GroupAffect-4四人群组协作数据集(含可穿戴生理、眼动、Big Five人格、任务后VAD标签)，在共享目标构建管道中比较四种方案：无增强、高斯过程伪标签、人格感知信任加权、人格加置信度联合加权；通过平滑机制，评估SVM变体在Valence、Arousal、Dominance上的表现，并进行LOSO(跨受试者)与LOGO(跨会话隔离)转移评估。

Result: 伪标签增强在已知团队设置下优于仅标注基线。但Big Five余弦相似度范围(0.91-0.99)过窄，使人格细粒度加权失效，人格相似度仅能作为同队筛选。平滑后增强SVM变体在Valence和Arousal上基本持平，联合人格+置信度变体在Dominance上最高。跨受试者LOSO转移表现有希望(尤其Arousal)，而严格会话隔离LOGO消除增强收益，因仅10组应视为保守下界。

Conclusion: 伪标签增强能在稀疏标注的协作情感数据中取得更好利用；人格信息最有用的角色是作为队内选择机制，而非校准的信任信号。未来在更多群体和更大人格差异条件下，人格加权可能发挥更大作用。

Abstract: Physiological affect sensing in naturalistic group interaction is often limited by sparse labels rather than sensor data: wearable devices produce many time windows, while self-reports are collected only a few times per session. Using GroupAffect-4, a four-person collaborative dataset with wearable physiology, eye tracking, Big Five personality, and post-task VAD labels, we study pseudo-label augmentation for affect sensing under sparse supervision. We compare no augmentation, Gaussian Process pseudo-labelling, personality-aware trust weighting, and joint personality-plus-confidence weighting within a shared target-construction pipeline. Results show that pseudo-label augmentation improves over the labelled-only baseline in the known-team setting. However, the narrow range of Big Five cosine similarities (0.91-0.99) makes fine-grained personality weighting ineffective; personality similarity functions mainly as a same-team filter rather than a calibrated trust signal. With smoothing, augmented SVM variants are effectively tied on Valence and Arousal, while the joint personality-plus-confidence variant gives the highest Dominance score. Cross-subject LOSO transfer remains encouraging, especially for Arousal, whereas strict session-isolated LOGO removes the augmentation benefit. Given only 10 groups, LOGO should be interpreted as a conservative lower bound on unseen-group transfer. Overall, the results suggest that pseudo-label augmentation can make better use of sparsely labelled collaborative affect data, while personality information is most useful as a within-team selection mechanism.

</details>


### [257] [Distilling Foundation Models for Agentic What-If Reasoning:Cost, Latency, and Governance in a Hybrid LLM+SLM Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16091)
*Sourish Dey,Aditya Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 TabPFN 教师模型蒸馏为紧凑的前馈学生模型，用于交互式 agent 循环中的热路径决策后端。在 UCI Adult 与五个 OpenML 基准上的业务决策模拟中，分类头从 53.2M 参数压缩至 8,546（6,220 倍），双头贷款流水线从 111.4M 压缩至 17,059（6,532 倍）。学生模型保持 95.4-100.5% 的准确率与 96.8-100.0% 的 AUC，硬标签对照组证明教师软目标带来 2.1-7.0 个 AUC 点的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型虽能通过上下文学习实现零训练预测并表现优异，但推理延迟过高，难以作为交互式 agentic 循环中的热路径决策后端。因此需要一种压缩方案，在保留预测能力的同时大幅降低延迟，使其适合实际部署。

Method: 将 TabPFN 教师蒸馏至紧凑的前馈学生模型，在 UCI Adult 和五个 OpenML 基准上开展业务决策模拟。学生采用前馈网络架构，并在贷款场景中设计双头（两任务的）流水线。以教师软标签（soft targets）作为训练信号进行蒸馏，并设置 alpha=0 的硬标签对照组以验证软目标的贡献。

Result: 分类头参数从 53.2M 压缩至 8,546（6,220 倍），双头贷款流水线从 111.4M 压缩至 17,059（6,532 倍）。学生保留 95.4-100.5% 的准确率与 96.8-100.0% 的 AUC，其中最低准确率保留出现在 credit-g（95.4%）。硬标签对照组表明教师软目标额外带来 2.1-7.0 个 AUC 点的增益。

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏，可将高延迟的 TabPFN 教师压缩为极轻量（数千参数规模）的前馈学生，在维持高保真预测性能（准确率与 AUC 几乎无损）的同时显著降低推理开销，从而满足交互式 agentic 循环对低延迟热路径后端的实际部署需求；软标签蒸馏是保留性能的关键。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models deliver strong zero-training predictive performance via in-context learning, but their high inference latency makes them impractical as hot-path decision backends in interactive agentic loops. We distill a TabPFN teacher into a compact feed-forward student across a business-decision simulation on UCI Adult and five OpenML benchmarks: the classification head compresses 53.2M parameters to 8,546 (6,220x); the deployed two-head loan pipeline compresses 111.4M parameters to 17,059 (6,532x). The student retains 95.4-100.5% accuracy and 96.8-100.0% AUC, with the lowest accuracy retention on credit-g at 95.4%; an alpha = 0 hard-label control shows that the teacher's soft targets provide a 2.1-7.0 AUC point gain.

</details>


### [258] [Evaluating Open-Weight E-Commerce Agents with Environment-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16093)
*Nimit Shah,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性、可复现的电商购物模拟环境，通过预提交顾客与轨迹参数、记录助手动作与状态，实现对话各环节的可验证评估，并支持双向交互以模拟更开放的场景。实验显示，相比单一任务成功率，该环境能区分出多种能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务成功率将购物对话中的多条路径压缩为一个分数，掩盖了失败的具体原因，难以细粒度评估助手行为。

Method: 构建确定性电商环境：预提交每次试验的顾客画像、难度、目标购物车及物品揭示计划；模拟顾客在环境引导下购物，记录所有助手动作与环境状态；评估器依据证据对各部分对话打分（如搜索未展示目标商品仅在用户提及后才惩罚），并根据工具调用偏差应用不同惩罚；环境与模拟器双向交互，可实时停止或注入指令。

Result: 对8个20B~35B开源模型各进行160次试验，使用44个指标，生成的能力画像能区分行动不足、过度购买、不支持的产品属性、搜索不佳等问题，而这些在终端成功率中被掩盖。

Conclusion: 该环境提供了更细粒度、可验证的购物对话评估方法，有助于诊断和改善模型能力。

Abstract: A shopping conversation has many routes to the same cart, and a task-success rate reduces all of them to one score. We build a deterministic and reproducible e-commerce environment that precommits each trial's customer and trajectory parameters, including the persona, difficulty, target cart, and an item reveal schedule. A simulated consumer attempts to buy a target cart from the environment with assistance from the evaluated model. The environment guides the simulator's actions and records every assistant action alongside the environment state at that point. After the trial, these records allow the evaluator to assess individual parts of the conversation against the retained evidence. For example, the evaluator penalizes a search for failing to surface a target product only when the customer has already mentioned that product. We further use this evidence to apply different penalties to tool calls depending on how the assistant's actions compare with an expected tool-call set. Our environment also interacts with the simulator bidirectionally, reading its output to stop the trial when the simulator determines that the customer has become too frustrated and injecting directives in real time that specify when to explore, defer buying an item, or recall a previous exchange. This interaction creates an open-ended and verifiable simulation. Across eight open-weight agents from 20B to 35B parameters, with 160 trials per agent and 44 metrics, the resulting capability profiles distinguish under-action, over-purchase, unsupported product attributes, and poor search, all of which terminal success obscures.

</details>


### [259] [SWB-DM: A Calibrated Sliced-Wasserstein-Barycenter Aggregator with Delayed-Momentum Caching for Byzantine-Robust Federated Learning under Partial Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16099)
*Saranraj S,Saranya M S,Alex David S,Ajay Kumar A*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出联邦学习鲁棒聚合方法依赖于"每轮参与者是整体人口的公平样本"这一脆弱假设，当每轮客户端数量少时，少量对抗者即可支配样本并破坏中位数、Krum、Bulyan、截断均值等方法的有限样本保证。为此提出SWB-DM方法：将客户端更新的每个切片视为一维分布，跨客户端计算截断Wasserstein重心，并借助medoid式规范固定步骤恢复坐标身份，同时采用DeMoA式延迟动量缓存全客户端群的更新。作者通过448种CIFAR-10配置及CIFAR-100、FEMNIST、500客户端可扩展性实验，发现了多种机制上截然不同的失败模式（如偶数样本中位数退化为确定性错误答案、Krum悄悄违反自身前提并发散、Bulyan阈值产生尖锐的通过/失败边界）。攻击方面，IPM比ALIE更可靠地击败包括SWB在内的序统计量防御。SWB-DM的缓存有真实预热成本，但在相同轮次预算下其CIFAR-10增益不成比例地大，而CIFAR-100上FLTrust受益更多且原因与缓存无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习鲁棒聚合方法隐蔽地依赖一个脆弱假设：每轮出现的客户端是整体人口的公平样本，但现实中很少如此。当每轮只有少量客户端参与时，即使对抗者占比不大也可能支配该样本，从而悄然破坏坐标中位数、Krum、Bulyan和截断均值所依赖的有限样本保证。作者旨在直接解决这一采样脆弱性问题。

Method: 提出SWB-DM：(1) 将客户端更新的每个切片视为一维分布，跨客户端计算截断Wasserstein重心；(2) 通过medoid式的规范固定（gauge-fixing）步骤恢复坐标身份——这是作者开发的经验启发式，不声称属于标准最优传输理论；(3) DeMoA式延迟动量每轮缓存全客户端群更新，将鲁棒性与谁恰好被采样解耦。截断比例校准至关重要：欠截断在攻击水平上会导致崩溃。

Result: 在448种CIFAR-10配置及CIFAR-100、FEMNIST、500客户端可扩展性实验中，发现多种机制迥异的失败模式：偶数样本坐标中位数退化为确定性错误答案；Krum悄悄违背自身n>2f+2前提并无警告地发散；Bulyan的n≥4f+3阈值产生清晰通过/失败边界。攻击上IPM比ALIE更可靠地击败含SWB在内的序统计量防御（经delta空间测量与收敛界确认）。SWB-DM缓存有真实预热成本，但扩展所有基线到相同轮次预算后，其CIFAR-10增益不成比例地大；CIFAR-100上FLTrust获益更多但原因与缓存无关。

Conclusion: 研究说明采样的非代表性是联邦学习鲁棒聚合的核心隐患，而SWB-DM通过分布级重心与全局缓存解耦策略，在多数场景下相比序统计量基线获得显著增益，但同时揭示防御存在多种机制不同的失败模式，且不同数据集上最有效防御各不相同，提示应谨慎评估鲁棒方法的适用边界。

Abstract: Robust aggregation methods for federated learning quietly rest on a fragile assumption: that whoever shows up in a given round is a fair sample of the full population. In practice, they rarely are. When only a handful of clients participate per round, even a modest fraction of adversaries can dominate that sample and silently invalidate the finite-sample guarantees that coordinate-wise median, Krum, Bulyan, and trimmed mean all depend on.
  We introduce SWB-DM to address this directly. SWB treats each slice of a client update as a one-dimensional distribution, computes a trimmed Wasserstein barycenter across clients, and recovers coordinate identity via a medoid-based gauge-fixing step -- a heuristic we developed and do not claim it belongs to standard optimal-transport theory. DeMoA-style delayed momentum then caches updates across the full client population each round, decoupling robustness from whoever happened to be sampled. Trim ratio calibration is not cosmetic: under-trimming causes collapse at corruption levels a properly calibrated model survives.
  Across 448 CIFAR-10 configurations, plus CIFAR-100, FEMNIST, and a 500-client scalability run, we find several mechanistically distinct failure modes. Even-sample coordinate-wise median degrades to a deterministic wrong answer. Krum silently violates its own n greater than 2f+2 precondition and diverges without warning. Bulyan's n greater than or equal to 4f+3 threshold produces a sharp pass/fail boundary. On attacks, IPM defeats order-statistic defenses -- including SWB -- more reliably than ALIE, confirmed through delta-space measurements against a convergence bound.
  SWB-DM's cache carries a real warm-up cost, but extending all baselines to the same round budget shows its CIFAR-10 gains are disproportionately large. On CIFAR-100, FLTrust benefits more -- for reasons entirely unrelated to caching.

</details>


### [260] [A Decision-Support Audit Protocol for Supervision Drift in Proxy-Labeled Credit-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16102)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Muhammad Shabanpour,Waldemar Karwowski,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个锁定式、多信号的审计协议（设计科学产物），用于代理标签信用风险预测中的监督漂移检测。该协议包含五层诊断（迁移性能、oracle-gap探针、校准诊断、特征-标签稳定性、合成阳性对照），并在解释前锁定阈值与决策规则。在LendingClub数据集（2013-2016时间迁移与跨段迁移）上，排序稳定、oracle-gap小；最清晰的时间信号是流行率与概率尺度错配，可由仅截距诊断性重校准大幅缓解，但其成因从现有发布数据中无法识别。阳性对照仅对较大注入偏移响应，细微漂移无法排除；诊断模式到治理行动的映射仅为概念性指导而非实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用风险模型基于代理标签训练并在时间变化与分段变化下部署，但现有单一迁移指标无法区分基准率漂移、概率尺度漂移与特征-标签关系变化这三种漂移类型，缺乏一个能系统分离与定位监督漂移的审计框架。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，锁定一个多信号审计协议：包含五层——迁移性能、oracle-gap探针、校准诊断、特征-标签稳定性、合成阳性对照；在解释结果之前预先锁定各层的阈值与决策规则，将有界解读作为设计目标。以公开LendingClub数据集（2013-2016时间迁移及跨段迁移）进行实证评估。

Result: 在LendingClub数据集上，排序稳定、oracle-gap较小；最清晰的时间信号是流行率与概率尺度错配，仅截距型诊断性重校准可大幅降低该错配，但其根本成因无法从可用发布数据中识别。合成阳性对照仅对更大的注入偏移产生响应，意味着更细微的漂移无法被排除。

Conclusion: 该审计协议能够部分区分并定位监督漂移，但存在明确边界：细微漂移不可检测、漂移成因不可识别，且将诊断模式映射到治理行动仅属概念性指导而非经过验证的结果。协议的锁定设计保障了解读的有界性与可复现性。

Abstract: Credit-risk models are trained on proxy labels and deployed under temporal and segment change, yet no single transfer metric separates base-rate shift, probability-scale shift, and feature-label relationship change. We contribute a design-science artifact: a locked, multi-signal audit protocol for supervision drift in proxy-labeled credit-risk prediction. Five layers (transfer performance, an oracle-gap probe, a calibration diagnostic, feature-label stability, and a synthetic positive control), thresholds, and decision rules were locked before interpretation; a bounded reading is a designed outcome. On a public LendingClub dataset (temporal 2013 to 2016 and cross-segment transfer), ranking is stable and oracle gaps are small; the clearest temporal signal is a prevalence and probability-scale mismatch that intercept-only diagnostic recalibration largely reduces, though its cause is not identifiable from the available release. The positive control responds only to larger injected shifts; subtler drift cannot be excluded. Mapping diagnostic patterns to governance actions is conceptual guidance, not validated here.

</details>


### [261] [LLMs as Master Forgers: Generating Synthetic Time Series Data for Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16155)
*Mantek Singh,Jeshwanth Challagundla,Prateek Karnal,Gagan Ganapathy,Vineet Shah,Ridam Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用大型语言模型（LLMs）为制造过程生成合成时间序列数据的新框架。通过微调预训练LLM并采用检索增强生成（RAG）技术来增强数据多样性和真实性。实验结果表明，该框架优于ARIMA和LSTM等传统时序建模方法，能够生成高质量合成数据，有效捕捉真实制造数据的时序依赖和统计特性，并提升下游任务（异常检测）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实制造场景中带标签的时间序列数据稀缺，这阻碍了鲁棒机器学习模型的开发。作者希望通过探索LLM学习复杂时序依赖和生成真实合成数据的潜力来解决这一数据稀缺问题。

Method: 方法包括：在制造过程指令上微调预训练LLM，并采用检索增强生成（RAG）技术以提升数据多样性和真实性。评估方面，与传统时序建模技术（ARIMA、LSTM）进行对比，使用定量指标、PCA分析和下游任务性能（异常检测）进行评价。

Result: 实验结果表明，该LLM驱动框架优于ARIMA和LSTM等基线方法，能够生成高质量合成时间序列数据，有效捕捉真实制造数据的时序依赖和统计特性，并带来下游任务性能的提升。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的框架在制造过程合成时序数据生成方面具有显著优势，能生成高质量且符合统计特性的数据，并改善下游异常检测等任务的性能，为数据稀缺场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate synthetic time series data for manufacturing processes. Motivated by the scarcity of labeled time-series data in real-world manufacturing settings, which hinders the development of robust machine learning models, we explore the potential of LLMs to learn complex temporal dependencies and generate realistic synthetic data. Our approach involves fine-tuning pre-trained LLMs on manufacturing process instructions and employing a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) technique to enhance data diversity and realism. We evaluate our method against traditional time series modeling techniques like ARIMA and LSTMs, using quantitative metrics, PCA analysis, and downstream task performance (anomaly detection). Results demonstrate that our LLM-driven framework outperforms these baselines, generating high-quality synthetic time series data that effectively captures temporal dependencies and statistical properties of real manufacturing data, leading to improvements in downstream task performance.

</details>


### [262] [LLM Inference in a Flash!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16161)
*Sebastian Zhao,Minseo Kim,Coleman Hooper,Luca Manolache,Michael W. Mahoney,Yakun Sophia Shao,Kurt Keutzer,Amir Gholami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM推理日益增长的挑战（长序列、重推理负载），以及硬件趋势中内存带宽与通信带宽增长缓慢的问题，提出利用Compute-in-Flash（闪存近存计算）来部署LLM推理的方案。核心贡献包括：1）端到端纯整数量化方法以消除高精度浮点运算；2）基于稀疏字典编码的KV缓存压缩策略以应对闪存写入耐久性限制。该方法同时将模型权重和KV缓存映射到闪存近存计算系统，减少数据传输。在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen-2.5-7B上，精度损失有限，且动态KV缓存流量降低了15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理已成为关键工作负载，但请求趋向更长序列和更重推理（由RAG、推理时计算扩展和长上下文应用驱动），而内存容量和通信带宽的扩展速度跟不上工作负载复杂度的增长，形成瓶颈。Compute-in-Flash通过近存计算和SSD大容量来解决内存带宽限制，但难点在于此类系统缺乏高精度浮点运算支持且写入耐久性有限，因此需要设计适配的推理算法。

Method: 1）提出端到端整数量化（integer-only quantization）推理算法，消除昂贵的浮点计算；2）设计基于稀疏字典编码（sparse dictionary coding）的KV缓存压缩策略，将每个KV向量表示为静态字典向量的线性组合，从而应对闪存写入耐久性限制。上述算法使模型权重和KV缓存都能充分利用Compute-in-Flash，最小化昂贵的数据迁移操作。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen-2.5-7B两个模型上，联合方法在保持有限精度损失的前提下，将动态KV缓存流量减少15倍。

Conclusion: 通过算法层面的创新（整数量化+字典式KV压缩），成功使LLM推理能够部署在具备大容量和近存计算优势的闪存计算系统上，有效缓解了内存带宽瓶颈与写入耐久性约束，为未来硬件趋势下的LLM推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across a range of natural language processing tasks, and LLM inference has emerged as a critical workload for enabling downstream applications. The demands of serving LLM inference are becoming increasingly challenging as requests shift toward longer sequences and heavier inference, driven by retrieval-augmented generation, inference-time compute scaling, and long-context applications. Additionally, these challenges are compounded by hardware trends, as memory capacity and communication bandwidth are not scaling as fast as increases in workload complexity. Compute-in-Flash is a promising solution to address memory bandwidth limitations by moving computation close to memory, and to exploit the large capacity of SSD technologies. However, it is challenging to deploy LLMs on these systems as they lack support for high-precision floating point operations and have limited write endurance. In our work, we aim to address these challenges by designing inference algorithms to enable LLM inference on Flash compute-in-memory devices. We present an end-to-end integer-only quantization approach to eliminate expensive floating-point computations. To address the limited write endurance, we design a dictionary-based KV cache compression strategy based on sparse dictionary coding that represents each KV vector as a linear combination of static dictionary vectors. These algorithmic improvements enable us to exploit the benefits of Compute-in-Flash for both model weights and KV cache, and to minimize expensive data transfer operations. Across Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen-2.5-7B, our combined method exhibits limited accuracy degradation while reducing dynamic KV cache traffic by 15$\times$.

</details>


### [263] [Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16170)
*Jordan Eckert,Henry Schenck*

Main category: cs.LG
