TL;DR: 本文提出 SPINE（Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions）方法，用以替代传统原型约减中有限点集表示，每个类别模型改为嵌入的1-复形（segments/边集）。初始边集合来自类别条件 Mapper 图，后续阶段在分类目标下拟合顶点，观测点归属于最近邻类别复形。在17个基准数据集上以分层10折交叉验证与7种原型约减方法（同等预算）对比，SPINE取得最高平均准确率与最佳平均排名，并对其中5个对手在Holm校正的Wilcoxon符号秩检验下显著更优。预算扫描显示整图分段决策规则在原型稀缺时贡献最大，整体在中等预算下表现最佳。构建成本属于判别式方法范畴，且在14/17数据集上比广义学习向量量化更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原型约减方法用有限点集作为训练集的小型表示，忽略了数据中的局部结构与拓扑关系；作者希望引入基于图的、由数据本身决定的复杂模型，以超越单纯点集的原型表示能力，从而提升分类决策的判别性。

Method: SPINE为每个类别构建一个嵌入的1-复形（即由顶点与边构成的图结构）。初始边集由类别条件Mapper图生成，让数据决定哪些局部聚类被连接；后续阶段在分类目标下拟合顶点位置，分类时观测点被分配到其复形距离最近的类别。最终决策规则同时利用整条图分段（segments）而非仅依赖拟合的顶点。

Result: 在17个基准数据集上，采用分层10折交叉验证，与7种原型约减方法（匹配预算）对比，SPINE取得最高平均准确率和最佳平均排名；在Holm校正的Wilcoxon符号秩检验下显著优于7个对手中的5个。预算扫描表明整图分段决策规则在原型稀缺时贡献最大，整体在中等预算下竞争力最强。构建成本使其归入判别式方法类别，并在14/17数据集上比广义学习向量量化更快。

Conclusion: SPINE通过用图复形替代点集原型，将数据驱动的拓扑结构和分类目标融合进决策规则，显著提升了原型约减的分类性能，并在精度与计算开销之间取得了良好平衡，是一种有竞争力的原型约减新范式。

Abstract: Prototype reduction replaces a training set with a smaller representation, and the established methods return a finite set of points. We propose Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions (SPINE). The model for each class is an embedded 1-complex rather than a point set. Its initial edge set is a class-conditional Mapper graph, so the data decide which localized clusters are joined. Later phases fit the vertices under a classification objective, and an observation is assigned to the class whose complex is nearest. The segments therefore enter the decision rule and not only the fitting. We evaluate SPINE on seventeen benchmark datasets under stratified 10-fold cross validation, against seven other prototype reduction methods at a matched budget. SPINE attains the highest mean accuracy and the best average rank. It is significantly better than five of the seven competitors under Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. A budget sweep shows that the decision rule using the entire graph segments contribute most when prototypes are scarce, while the method as a whole competes best at moderate budgets. Construction cost places SPINE with the discriminative methods, and it is faster than generalized learning vector quantization on fourteen of the seventeen datasets.

</details>


### [264] [Z-Loss Backward Geometry in Dense Output Heads and Sparse Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16179)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从反向传播视角重新分析Z-loss，提出"反向传输"（backward-transport）观点，将Z-loss梯度的标量幅度和softmax形状与传输因子分离，揭示前向Z-loss值近似相同但梯度与参数更新可能截然不同，并提出架构感知的Z-loss变体以改善反向几何拖尾并保持困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z-loss被广泛用于语言模型输出头与稀疏MoE路由器的logits上，通过约束softmax对数归一化因子来限制大logit偏移、减少有限精度舍入风险并避免训练发散。然而以往仅将其理解为对数归一化因子上的标量惩罚，忽略了其梯度在反向传播中受到架构与实现方式影响这一事实。本文旨在从反向传播视角深入理解Z-loss梯度的生成与传输。

Method: 提出一种Z-loss的反向传输观点：将Z-loss梯度（称为backward source）注入logit边界，分离其标量幅度与softmax形状，并识别多种传输因子——共同平移坐标、权重共享（tied-embedding）通路、输出到隐藏的增益、融合损失的源一致性、面向优化器的更新以及top-k路由器的缩减尺度。据此构建诊断指标并设计架构感知的Z-loss变体。

Result: 诊断表明：几乎相同的前向Z-loss值可以共存于截然不同的logit空间梯度，经架构与优化器传输后产生不同的参数更新。该方法解释了raw-logit Z-loss在为何不改变输出到隐藏增益的情况下仍能缩减标量尾部，以及active-route缩减如何改变有效路由器系数。在GPT-2与Pythia模型族、WikiText-103与FineWeb-Edu数据集上的评估显示，架构感知变体在低系数模式下可减少反向几何尾部并保持相当的验证困惑度。

Conclusion: Z-loss的实际效果不仅取决于其前向标量惩罚，更取决于其梯度在反向传播中的传输路径。理解并架构感知地设计Z-loss的后向几何，能够在保持模型性能的同时更有效地控制梯度与参数更新的分布形态。

Abstract: Z-loss has been widely applied to the logits of language-model output heads and sparse mixture-of-experts routers. Z-loss constrains the softmax log-normalizers of these output heads and routers, thereby limiting large-logit excursions, reducing finite-precision roundoff exposure, and avoiding training-loss divergence. These use cases arise in modern Transformer settings where large-vocabulary softmax heads, top-$k$ routing, fused losses, and mixed-precision optimizers interact. Z-loss has typically been understood only as a scalar penalty on the log-normalizer. This paper instead analyzes Z-loss from a backward-pass perspective, focusing on the gradients produced by the Z-loss penalty. The logit-space gradient, which we call the backward source, is injected at the logit boundary of the Z-loss branch of backpropagation; consequently, the backward source's effect depends on the architecture and implementation through which the gradient is transported. We develop a backward-transport view for Z-loss that separates the source's scalar amplitude and softmax shape from the transport factors. These factors include common-shift coordinates, tied-embedding pathways, output-to-hidden gain, fused-loss source consistency, optimizer-facing updates, and top-$k$ router reduction scale. These diagnostics show that nearly identical forward Z-loss values can coexist with distinct logit-space Z-loss gradients and, after architectural and optimizer transport, distinct parameter updates. The transport diagnostics also explain why raw-logit Z-loss can reduce scalar tails without changing output-to-hidden gain and why active-route reductions alter the effective router coefficient. Across evaluations of models in the GPT-2 and Pythia families on WikiText-103 and FineWeb-Edu, architecture-aware variants reduce backward-geometry tails while maintaining comparable validation perplexity in low-coefficient regimes.

</details>


### [265] [Anatomy of Associative Recall in Fixed-State Recurrences: A Matched-State Decomposition, an Interference Wall, and a Curriculum That Breaks It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16183)
*Julian Boesch,Andrew Wee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过精细的消融实验分解固定状态循环（线性注意力与状态空间模型）在联想回忆任务上的表现，发现短因果卷积是主导因素（约+0.5召回率），而循环的过渡结构（rank-1 delta规则 vs 对角）和衰减的贡献很小；并引入"距离课程学习"作为廉价干预，将架构未经修改地从不收敛（0.021）提升到完全收敛（1.000），揭示了训练是一种"锁定彩票"，从而用可控的分解和两个廉价干预替代了"循环模型不擅长回忆"的笼统结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献声称固定状态循环（线性注意力、状态空间模型）在联想回忆任务上落后于注意力，但整体架构对比无法定位究竟是哪个成分（卷积、过渡结构、衰减）导致了差距。作者希望将"混合多查询回忆"沿单旋钮轴分解，确定各成分的因果贡献，并寻找改变结论的干预手段。

Method: 在固定状态预算下，将掩码多查询回忆（masked multi-query recall）沿三个单旋钮轴分解：短因果卷积、过渡结构（rank-1 delta规则 vs 对角）、衰减。在匹配训练条件下进行成对消融（有无卷积、rank-1 vs 对角、状态匹配的Mamba/Mamba-2对照），并进行多种训练干预实验（稀疏监督、距离课程、形状化斜坡、按准确率门控、双向去噪器、S_5状态跟踪护栏），以多颗种子统计锁定率。

Result: 卷积主导（两个家族均约+0.5召回）；rank-1过渡在16/32对时优于对角消融（+0.19/+0.32），但双方均带卷积时差距缩至+0.03，且状态匹配的Mamba-2与无武装的rank-1单元打平，故无类别性绝对优势。能解32对回忆的单元在干扰物草堆中检索4对时退化到随机水平（跨长度与过渡结构均如此），表明问题在稀疏监督下的干扰而非容量；距离课程使不变架构从0.021升至1.000。训练是"锁定彩票"：种子要么锁定要么不锁定，课程是杠杆，锁定率从1/10升至7/10（p=0.02）；稠密监督无增益；L=256时形状化斜坡能重开均匀课程无法触及的边界（4/5 vs 0/9）；L=512时斜坡失效（0/6），按实测准确率门控则锁定6/6（p=0.001）。双向去噪器（先读查询再看草堆）相对因果训练无显著优势（十颗种子）；冲突键检索需两层。为回忆加装的单元在S_5状态跟踪护栏上所有深度都显著更好（p<=0.0044）且零成本。

Conclusion: "循环模型不擅长回忆"应被替换为"可测量的分解加两个廉价干预"：主导因素是卷积而非循环本身，且通过距离课程/准确率门控等训练干预能在不修改架构的情况下显著提升回忆能力。结论强调训练动态（锁定彩票）与数据形状的作用，而非容量或类别性架构差异。

Abstract: Fixed-state recurrences--linear attention and state-space models--are reported to lag behind attention on associative recall, but whole-architecture comparisons cannot say which ingredient is responsible. We decompose masked multi-query recall at a fixed state budget along three single-knob axes: a short causal convolution, the transition structure (rank-1 delta rule vs. diagonal), and decay. The convolution dominates (~+0.5 recall in both families under matched training): comparisons that pit convolution-free cells against a convolution-equipped Mamba measure the missing convolution, not the recurrence. The rank-1 transition beats its diagonal ablation by +0.19/+0.32 at 16/32 pairs, but the margin shrinks to +0.03 once both cells carry the convolution, and a state-matched Mamba-2 ties the unarmed rank-1 cell: no class claim survives. Cells that solve 32-pair recall degrade gracefully with load yet fall to chance retrieving 4 pairs from a distractor haystack--flat across lengths and transitions. Interference under sparse supervision, not capacity: a distance curriculum takes the unchanged architecture from 0.021 to 1.000. Training is a lock-in lottery--a seed either locks in or does not--and the curriculum is the lever. Lock-in rises from 1/10 to 7/10 (p=0.02); dense supervision adds nothing; at L=256 a shaped ramp reopens a boundary the uniform curriculum cannot (4/5 vs. 0/9); and at L=512, where the ramp collapses (0/6), gating it on measured accuracy locks in 6/6 (p=0.001). Bidirectional denoiser cells, reading the query before the haystack, show no measurable advantage over causal training (ten seeds), and collision-key retrieval needs two layers. Arming for recall is free on an S_5 state-tracking guardrail--the armed cell is significantly better at every depth (p<=0.0044). These replace "recurrent models are bad at recall" with a measured decomposition and two cheap interventions.

</details>


### [266] [Decoy Direction Optimization: A Post-Hoc Defense Against LLM Abliteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16204)
*Aashiq Muhamed,Mona T. Diab,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了 Decoy Direction Optimization (DDO)，一种无需基座模型微调的快速后置权重编辑防御方法。它通过在 MLP 神经元中注入高幅值非线性诱饵信号，破坏攻击者用于定位拒绝方向的对比估计器，诱骗其消融无害的正交特征，从而在保留安全机制的同时抵抗 Refusal Feature Ablation (RFA) 攻击，在六个模型族上实现 <10% 的攻击成功率，且优化成本比训练式防御低 30–450 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重语言模型的安全护栏可被 Refusal Feature Ablation (RFA) 轻易绕过——该方法从残差流中识别并投影出线性拒绝方向，往往在保持模型能力的同时取得很高的攻击成功率。现有防御手段对每个新检查点都需进行计算开销高昂的安全微调，缺乏一种既快速又低成本的通用防护方案。

Method: DDO 基于一个关键的机理洞察：消融攻击依赖对比估计器来寻找拒绝方向。与试图隐藏真实拒绝电路不同，DDO 主动向网络的 MLP 神经元注入高幅值、非线性的诱饵信号。当攻击者尝试定位拒绝方向时，诱饵会污染其估计器，诱使其消融一个无害的正交特征，而真实安全机制保持完好。作者还证明了刻画该效应的谱界（spectral bound）。

Result: 在六个模型族上，标准 RFA 攻击下的攻击成功率低于 10%。在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上，面对自适应多阶段攻击时 DDO（最坏情况 ASR 65%）与训练式防御（58%）表现相当；将 Heretic 权重级攻击的 ASR 从 88.7% 降至 18%；且每个配置的优化成本比训练式基线低 30 至 450 倍。

Conclusion: DDO 是一种快速、后置、免微调的有效防御方案，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持与训练式防御相当的鲁棒性，为防护可被 RFA 类消融攻击利用的开放权重模型提供了一种可扩展的替代路径。

Abstract: Safety guardrails in open-weight language models can be readily bypassed using Refusal Feature Ablation (RFA), a technique that identifies and projects out a linear refusal direction from the residual stream, often achieving a high attack success rate (ASR) while preserving model capability. Defending against these attacks typically requires computationally expensive safety finetuning for every new checkpoint. We introduce Decoy Direction Optimization (DDO), a fast, post-hoc weight-editing defense that requires no base-model finetuning. Our approach is based on a simple mechanistic insight: ablation attacks rely on contrastive estimators to find the refusal direction. Rather than trying to hide the true refusal circuitry, DDO actively injects a high-magnitude, nonlinear decoy signal into the network's MLP neurons. When an attacker attempts to locate the refusal direction, the decoy corrupts their estimator, tricking them into ablating a harmless orthogonal feature while the actual safety mechanism remains intact. We prove a spectral bound formalizing this effect and evaluate DDO across six model families, achieving <10% ASR under standard RFA. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, DDO remains comparable to trained defenses under adaptive multi-phase attacks (65% vs. 58% worst-case ASR) and reduces Heretic weight-level attack ASR from 88.7% to 18%, all at 30 to 450 times lower optimization cost per configuration than the trained baselines.

</details>


### [267] [How I learned to stop worrying and love StopGrads: Stationarity, Convergence, and a case study on Flow Map Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16222)
*Max W. Shen,Mark Goldstein,Zichu Wang,Aahlad Puli,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"停止梯度回归原理"（stopgrad regression principle），为广泛使用的停止梯度（stopgrad）训练提供理论依据。该原理以闭式解形式刻画了一类停止梯度目标函数的驻点及其唯一性，统一了流映射、强化学习和扩散采样器中的停止梯度目标。作者证明了对流映射目标进行停止梯度优化的理论正确性（唯一驻点即真实流映射），并给出Eulerian与Lagrangian目标（含MeanFlow及改进版）的正收敛性结果。一个显著发现是：在函数半梯度流下，学习的流映射具有闭式表达式（由初始流映射与真实流映射复合而成）。此外，该原理还被用于改进流映射目标中的停止梯度放置，使训练显存开销降低2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停止梯度（stopgrad）在机器学习训练中被广泛使用，但它会改变原始目标的梯度、驻点与收敛保证，导致停止梯度训练在理论上缺乏根基。作者旨在为这一常见但缺乏理论支撑的技术建立统一且严谨的数学基础。

Method: 引入"停止梯度回归原理"这一通用模板，以闭式形式刻画停止梯度目标函数的驻点及其唯一性；建立其与流映射、强化学习和扩散采样器等目标之间的联系；并通过对流映射目标进行理论分析（驻点唯一性、Eulerian/Lagrangian目标的正收敛性、函数半梯度流下的闭式解）来提供理论支撑，同时据此改进停止梯度的放置位置。

Result: 证明了停止梯度优化的流映射目标其唯一驻点即为真实流映射；对Eulerian和Lagrangian目标（包括MeanFlow和改进版MeanFlow）给出了正收敛性结果；并推导出函数半梯度流下所学流映射的闭式表达式（初始流映射与真实流映射的复合）。此外，提出的修改版停止梯度放置方案将训练显存占用减少了一半（2x）。

Conclusion: 该工作为停止梯度训练提供了统一且坚实的理论根基，证明了流映射目标中停止梯度优化的合理性与收敛性，同时带来了显存效率提升的实际收益，弥合了停止梯度实践与其理论理解之间的差距。

Abstract: Stopgrads are widely used in training machine learning models, but stopgrads can alter the gradient, stationary points and convergence guarantees of the original objective, which can make stopgrad training theoretically ungrounded. We introduce a stopgrad regression principle, which identifies a general template for stopgrad objectives with a closed-form characterization of stationary points and their uniqueness, unifying stopgrad objectives for flow maps, reinforcement learning, and diffusion samplers. We provide theoretical grounding for optimizing stopgrad flow map objectives by showing their unique stationary point is the true flow map, and showing positive convergence results for Eulerian and Lagrangian objectives, including MeanFlow and improved MeanFlow. Remarkably, we show that under functional semi-gradient flow, the learned flow map has a closed-form expression composing the initial flow map and the true flow map. We additionally use our stopgrad regression principle to propose modified stopgrad placements for flow map objectives which reduce training memory by 2x.

</details>


### [268] [Test-Time Unlearning via Sparse Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16229)
*Pingzhi Li,Jinhao Duan,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Nakul Agarwal,Kwonjoon Lee,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Sijia Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARIA 是一种基于自编码器门控的推理时（test-time）机器遗忘方法，不修改模型权重，而是在生成进入与遗忘知识相关状态时通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）潜变量训练的轻量线性检测器进行门控干预。在TOFU、R-TOFU和WMDP基准上，ARIA相较于基于权重的基线显著改善了遗忘-保留权衡，并对三种后遗忘对抗攻击保持稳健（遗忘变化<1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于权重的机器遗忘方法（梯度上升及其改进）存在尖锐的遗忘-效用权衡：更强的目标知识遗忘会损害模型整体效用，且被遗忘的知识在微调或提示攻击下可能复现。因此需要一种在测试时进行、不触碰权重且更稳健的遗忘方案。

Method: ARIA 保持模型权重不变，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）潜变量训练一个轻量线性检测器，用于识别生成过程中进入"遗忘相关状态"的时刻；当检测到该状态时，施加可解释的干预，从而门控访问不需要的知识。该方法仅在测试时生效，且测试时开销可忽略。

Result: 在TOFU、R-TOFU和WMDP上，ARIA在推理模型（DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-Qwen-1.5B）和指令模型（Gemma-3-1B-it）上均优于基于权重的基线，例如显著降低WMDP-cyber遗忘集准确率，同时将MMLU保持在未遗忘模型1%以内。针对权重空间与解码空间恢复的三种后遗忘对抗攻击下，ARIA遗忘改变小于1%。特征级案例研究表明，部分保留性能下降可能反映的是遗忘数据底层的响应风格，而非目标知识本身的泄漏。

Conclusion: ARIA 提供了一种不修改权重、可解释且稳健的推理时遗忘方案，在遗忘-保留权衡上优于现有权重类方法，并具有抗攻击鲁棒性；同时揭示了遗忘任务构建中可能存在由响应风格导致的偏置来源。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove specific knowledge from a trained large language model (LLM) without retraining from scratch. Existing methods modify model weights via gradient ascent and its advances. While effective on certain benchmarks, these weight-based approaches exhibit a sharp forget-utility trade-off, where stronger forgetting of target knowledge can degrade model utility, and unlearned knowledge may reappear under post-unlearning fine-tuning or prompt attacks. We propose ARIA (autoencoder-gated inference-time unlearning), a test-time unlearning method that leaves model weights intact and gates access to unwanted knowledge only when generation enters a forget-related state. ARIA uses sparse autoencoder (SAE) latents to train a lightweight linear detector, then applies an interpretable intervention on triggered states with negligible test-time overhead. Empirical evaluations on TOFU, R-TOFU, and WMDP show that ARIA improves the forget-retain trade-off over weight-based baselines across both a thinking model (DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-Qwen-1.5B) and an instruction model (Gemma-3-1B-it), e.g., reducing WMDP-cyber forget-set accuracy significantly while keeping MMLU within 1% of the pre-unlearning model. We further introduce three post-unlearning adversarial attacks targeting weight-space and decoding-space recovery, and find that ARIA remains robust under all three, with forgetting changing by less than 1% under attack. A feature-level case study leveraging the interpretability of ARIA suggests that some retain degradation may reflect response styles underlying the unlearning data rather than leakage of the targeted knowledge itself, highlighting a potential source of bias in unlearning task construction.

</details>


### [269] [Efficient Reasoning Distillation: Small Video-Language Models via Synthetic CoT and Difficulty-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16255)
*Mantek Singh,Jeshwanth Challagundla,Siddharth Raina,Jasmin Jarsania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效方法，利用约900个基于不确定度筛选的样本和4B教师模型生成的合成思维链（CoT）理由，对2B参数视频-语言模型（VLM）进行微调，使其在视频问答（VideoQA）任务上仅需单张A100不到2小时的训练即可超越4倍规模更大的模型，并在CinePile、ActivityNet-QA和MLVU上逼近4B教师表现。核心发现是：将CoT理由置于答案之后（颠覆常规提示方式）能显著提升紧凑模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型VLM（2B级）推理能力强但参数规模受限，如何在极低的计算成本下将大规模模型的链式推理能力高效迁移到这些小模型中，从而适配移动端和边缘设备部署，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 方法包括三个关键环节：(1) 采用不确定度选择策略，仅从大量数据中筛选约900个高价值训练样本；(2) 用4B教师模型为这些样本生成合成CoT理由；(3) 对2B模型进行微调，并采用"理由置于答案之后"的非常规提示布局来强化紧凑模型的推理。

Result: 2B模型在CinePile、ActivityNet-QA和MLVU三个基准上超越最高4倍规模的其他VLM，并逼近其4B教师模型的表现；同时发现将CoT放在答案之后这一反直觉的位置安排对紧凑模型推理提升显著。

Conclusion: 该研究为低资源条件下训练可部署、具备推理能力的VLM提供了实用蓝图，挑战了当前CoT提示的常规惯例，并揭示了在模型容量受限场景下的新对齐策略，适用于移动端和边缘应用。

Abstract: We present an efficient method to distill reasoning capabilities into compact video-language models (VLMs) for video question answering (VideoQA). Our approach fine-tunes a 2B-parameter model using only $\sim$900 uncertainty-selected examples, each augmented with synthetic chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales generated by a 4B teacher. Despite its minimal compute cost - under two hours on a single A100 GPU - our method enables the 2B model to outperform VLMs up to 4$\times$ larger, and generalize across CinePile, ActivityNet-QA, and MLVU, approaching the performance of its own 4B teacher. A key finding is that placing CoT rationales after the answer - contrary to standard prompting - substantially improves reasoning in compact models. This insight challenges prevailing CoT conventions and reveals new alignment strategies under limited model capacity. Our findings offer a practical blueprint for training deployable, reasoning-rich VLMs suited for mobile and edge applications.

</details>


### [270] [The record is part of the task: matched-record evaluation of text classifiers across maintenance, safety and recall reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16267)
*Hisham Ihshaish,Peter Mayhew,Tasnim M. A. Zayet,Ana Del Amo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多记录文档（同一事件在不同工作流程阶段、不同目的下被记录多次）对模型评估的影响。作者将"选择哪条记录"视为评估的一部分，并在三个系统（GE Aerospace 维修事件、NASA ASRS 安全报告、NHTSA 车辆召回）中，在固定标签和划分下比较同一案例的匹配记录。结果显示，标签来源与记录选择会显著影响模型性能（如 GE 中客户报告与技术报告的 macro-F1 相差 0.46，远超表示与架构差异）。作者建议评估应在预定决策点的可用信息上进行，并报告记录与标签的生成方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数操作案例会在不同工作流程阶段和不同目的下被多次记录，但模型评估通常只选择其中一条记录进行比对。这种"记录选择"本身可能引入偏见，却被当作既成事实忽略。作者旨在证明记录的选择应当被视为评估流程的一部分，而非先验给定。

Method: 作者提出将记录选择纳入评估框架：在三个系统（GE 维修事件、NASA ASRS 安全报告、NHTSA 车辆召回）中，在固定标签和固定数据划分下，对同一案例的匹配记录（不同来源/时间段）进行对比评估，并测试不同的表示方法（表示/架构差异）作为对照，以量化记录差异相对于模型差异的影响。

Result: GE 三个字段的 held-out macro-F1 范围为 0.33 到 0.91；客户报告（工单前）与技术报告（诊断后、标签产生前）之间相差 0.46，远超同一事件上的表示与架构差异。公共数据集呈现不同模式：NHTSA 缺陷摘要在所有模型家族下保持最强，而 ASRS 分析员摘要仅在学习的序列模型下优于记者叙事，但在词汇基线模型下不占优。部分模型比较结果也依赖记录选择。

Conclusion: 评估应该在预定决策点可用的信息上进行，并应报告记录和标签各自的产生过程。记录选择是评估的一部分，忽略它可能导致误导性的模型比较结论。

Abstract: Many operational cases are documented more than once, at different workflow stages and for different purposes, yet model evaluations normally select one of these records before model comparison begins. We treat that selection as part of the evaluation and compare matched records of the same cases under fixed labels and splits in three systems: GE Aerospace repair events, NASA ASRS safety reports and NHTSA vehicle recalls. Across the three GE fields, for events whose label comes from parts transactions independently of the narratives, held-out macro-F1 ranged from 0.33 to 0.91. A difference of 0.46 separated the customer report, written before shop work, from the technician report, written after diagnosis but before the transaction that generates the label. That difference is substantially larger than the representation and architecture differences tested on the same events. The public systems showed different patterns: the NHTSA defect summary remained strongest under every model family tested, whereas the ASRS analyst synopsis outperformed the reporter narrative under learned sequence models but not under lexical baselines. Secondary analyses showed that some model comparisons were also record-dependent. Evaluations should be run on the information available at the intended decision point and should report how both the record and the label were produced.

</details>


### [271] [Scaling Laws for Physics-Aware ACOPF Surrogate Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16282)
*Yijiang Li,Emon Dey,Stefano Fenu,Massimiliano Lupo Pasini,Teja Kuruganti,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了ACOPF学习代理模型在MSE与增广拉格朗日(AL)两种物理感知训练目标下的扩展性差异。发现两者约束违规率均随规模呈幂律改善，但MSE主要受模型容量支配，AL则在模型与数据间均衡分配；随网络规模增大，MSE下的违规增长速率约为AL的两倍。在匹配硬件上，AL以约10倍训练时间换取近30倍的违规降低，且内存开销可忽略。训练目标不仅决定代理模型的落点，更决定其质量随规模演化的斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的ACOPF代理模型相比传统求解器能大幅提速，但其工程应用价值不仅取决于预测精度，更取决于物理可行性（约束满足）。增广拉格朗日等物理感知目标能在额外单步开销下改善约束满足，然而这种精度—成本权衡如何随规模变化此前缺乏系统刻画，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者在多种电网拓扑上系统扫描模型规模（容量）与数据集规模两个维度，分别采用MSE与AL两种训练目标训练代理模型，量化约束违规如何随网络规模变化，并比较两者在不同资源配比下的表现。

Result: 两种目标下约束违规均按幂律改善但速率不同：MSE的改善主要受模型容量支配，而AL受模型与数据两者共同制约。约束违规在MSE下随网络规模的增速约为AL下的两倍。在匹配硬件条件下，AL以约一个数量级的额外训练时间换取近30倍的违规降低，且几乎不增加内存开销。

Conclusion: 训练目标的选择从根本上决定了ACOPF代理模型的扩展行为——不仅决定其最终精度水平，还决定其质量随数据、模型和网络规模增长的演化速率。这为在实际电力系统中依据可用计算预算选择合适训练目标提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Learning-based surrogates for AC optimal power flow (ACOPF) promise large speedups over classical solvers, but their operational value depends on physical feasibility as much as predictive accuracy. Physics-aware objectives such as the augmented Lagrangian (AL) improve constraint satisfaction at additional per-step cost, yet how this trade-off behaves with scale is uncharacterized. We sweep model and dataset sizes under both MSE and AL training, and characterize how constraint violation changes with network size across grids. Both objectives improve as power laws, but at different rates: MSE is governed primarily by model capacity, while AL is balanced across both. Violation grows roughly twice as fast with network size under MSE as under AL. On matched hardware, AL reduces violation by nearly $30\times$ for an order of magnitude more training time, with negligible added memory. The training objective determines not only where a surrogate lands but how its quality evolves with scale.

</details>


### [272] [Differentially Private Semantic Plans for Aggregate Insight Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16283)
*Behrooz Razeghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-SPIN，一个可信策展人（trusted-curator）框架，用于对独立于受保护目标记录的预定义语义概念进行差分隐私（DP）聚合度量与文本摘要。它将每条记录映射为概念上的有界稀疏非负向量并求和形成语义草图，经DP机制发布带噪声的概念-质量计划，再通过后处理与语言模型生成最终摘要，且由公共验证器校验陈述与计划的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有URANIA框架虽为数据依赖聚类的摘要提供端到端DP，但其聚类-关键词发布无法直接给出针对独立于受保护语料之外定义的语义概念的集合级聚合。原因在于：一条记录可能表达多个概念；表达相同概念的记录可能被分到不同聚类；不同分析中聚类身份不一定对应。因此聚类级统计无法在跨集合或重复分析中给出预定义概念的可比度量。

Method: DP-SPIN将每条记录映射为一个有界稀疏非负概念向量，其总和构成语义草图。一个DP机制发布包含已接受概念及加噪质量的“语义计划”；通过后处理获得归一化的语义支持值和支持分箱。为实现用户级隐私，每个用户的聚集贡献被裁剪到固定上限。语言模型仅接收该计划与固定解码指令，而公共验证器对计划中概念提及、报告值、比较及排名声明进行校验，从而借助后处理保证最终摘要满足DP。论文在增删（add/drop）与替换（replacement）邻接下证明了记录级与用户级隐私保证。

Result: 在记录级隐私下，DP-SPIN在CFPB投诉叙述、Amazon All Beauty评论和Yelp餐厅评论上进行评估；在用户级隐私下则在Amazon和Yelp上评估。对比对象包括非私有的计划与摘要参考、DP关键词与类别直方图基线，以及采用固定公开关键词词表的URANIA式基线。

Conclusion: DP-SPIN通过在概念空间上的语义草图、DP发布计划、后处理归一化以及带公共验证器的语言模型解码，实现了对独立预定义语义概念的记录级与用户级差分隐私聚合与摘要，克服了URANIA在跨分析聚类不对应、多概念归属等问题上的局限。

Abstract: \texttt{URANIA} provides end-to-end differential privacy (DP) for summaries of data-dependent clusters. However, its cluster--keyword release does not directly provide collection-wide aggregates for semantic concepts defined independently of the protected corpus. Records may express several concepts, records expressing the same concept may be assigned to different clusters, and cluster identities need not correspond across analyses. Consequently, cluster-level statistics do not directly provide comparable measurements of predefined concepts across collections or repeated analyses. We introduce \texttt{DP-SPIN}, a trusted-curator framework for aggregate measurement and summarization over semantic concepts fixed independently of the protected target records. Each record is mapped to a bounded sparse nonnegative vector over these concepts, whose sum forms a semantic sketch. A differentially private mechanism releases a semantic plan containing admitted concepts and noisy masses; normalized semantic-support values and support bins are obtained by post-processing. For user-level privacy, each user's aggregate contribution is clipped to a fixed bound. The language model receives only the plan and fixed decoding instructions, while a public verifier checks concept mentions, reported values, comparisons, and rank claims against the released plan. The final summary is differentially private by post-processing. We establish record- and user-level DP guarantees under add/drop and replacement adjacency. We evaluate \texttt{DP-SPIN} under record-level privacy on CFPB complaint narratives, Amazon All Beauty reviews, and Yelp restaurant reviews, and under user-level privacy on Amazon and Yelp. We compare \texttt{DP-SPIN} with non-private plan and summary references, DP keyword and category histogram baselines, and a \texttt{URANIA}-style baseline with a fixed public keyword vocabulary.

</details>


### [273] [Drift Field Net: Learning Ocean Lagrangian advection fields from in-situ and satellite observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16288)
*Théo Archambault,Pierre Garcia,Mattia Romero,Anastase Charantonis,Dominique Béréziat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了漂流场网络(DFN)，一种基于深度神经网络的方法，用于从业务化卫星观测中预测海洋表层流场，以优化北太平洋副热带环流(NPSG)塑料碎片聚集区的清洁策略。DFN采用两阶段训练策略：先在模拟数据上预训练，再基于平流一致性损失函数进行拉格朗日微调。在7天预报中，DFN相比业务化物理模型将平均定位误差降低20km，而拉格朗日微调进一步减少10km误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北太平洋副热带环流(NPSG)是漂浮塑料碎片的全球主要聚集区，其形成源于盆地尺度的汇聚性海洋环流。制定有效的清洁策略依赖于对拉格朗日粒子漂移的准确预报。现有的业务化物理预测系统在轨迹精度上存在提升空间，因此需要引入新的方法（如深度学习）来提高海洋表层流场预测和粒子轨迹预报的准确性。

Method: 提出了深度神经网络Drift Field Net(DFN)，用于从业务化卫星观测中预测海洋表面流场。训练采用新颖的两阶段策略：(1) 在模拟数据上进行预训练；(2) 基于平流一致损失函数进行拉格朗日微调。这种物理信息约束的优化方法直接提升了粒子轨迹预测的精度。

Result: DFN在7天预报中相比业务化物理模型，将原位漂流浮标轨迹的平均定位误差减少了20km。此外，采用所提出的平流损失的拉格朗日微调进一步将定位误差减少了10km，验证了在训练过程中引入拉格朗日约束的增益。

Conclusion: 研究表明，深度学习在海洋表层流场预测方面具有潜力，能够超越业务化物理预报系统。将拉格朗日约束（平流一致损失）纳入训练过程可显著提升粒子轨迹预报精度，为NPSG区域塑料碎片的清洁与治理提供了更有效的预报工具。

Abstract: The North Pacific Subtropical Gyre (NPSG) is a major accumulation zone for floating plastic debris, resulting from basin-scale convergent ocean circulation. Effective cleanup strategies in this region rely on accurate forecasts of Lagrangian particle drift. Here, we introduce Drift Field Net (DFN), a deep neural network that predicts ocean surface flow fields from operational satellite observations. DFN is trained using a novel two-stage strategy that combines pretraining on simulated data with Lagrangian fine-tuning based on an advection-consistent loss function. This physics-informed optimization directly improves the accuracy of particle trajectory predictions. We evaluate DFN against an operational physics-based forecasting system and demonstrate the potential of deep learning for ocean surface flow prediction. On in situ drifter trajectories, DFN reduces the mean positioning error by 20 km after a 7-day forecast compared with the operational model. Furthermore, Lagrangian fine-tuning with the proposed advection loss further reduces the positioning error by 10 km, highlighting the benefits of incorporating Lagrangian constraints into the training process.

</details>


### [274] [Agentic Search Spaces for Tabular Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16309)
*Renat Sergazinov,Artem Chistyakov,Sergey Pankevich,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体能否为既定表格模型设计扩展的超参数优化(HPO)搜索空间，以超越模型作者提供的标准搜索空间。通过将每个表格模型表示为模块化流水线（预处理、嵌入、架构、训练、推理），让智能体为每个模块提出候选代码实现，再用经典HPO算法联合优化这些候选与默认超参数。在45个数据集上，扩展空间几乎对每个模型族都提升了性能，平均相对增益0.6%，在中小型回归数据集上达2.0%，且不增加调参成本，并能在TabArena基准上提升Elo分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的智能体在规划、代码生成和调试方面进展迅速，但其在表格机器学习中的实用价值仍未被充分探索。作者希望验证前沿智能体能否为已有表格模型设计出超越作者默认搜索空间的扩展HPO搜索空间，从而拓展该领域的实用性边界。

Method: 将每个表格模型形式化为涵盖预处理、嵌入、架构、训练和推理的模块化流水线；委派智能体为每个模块生成候选代码实现，从而扩展搜索空间；随后使用经典HPO算法将这些候选与模型的默认超参数进行联合优化。该方法在不增加调参预算的前提下扩大了搜索空间。

Result: 在45个数据集上，扩展搜索空间相比基础HPO空间几乎对每个模型族都提升了性能，平均相对增益0.6%，在中小型回归数据集上提升至2.0%。这些增益在相同的调参和集成预算下获得。在TabArena基准上，智能体设计的空间提升了五个模型族中四个的官方Elo分数，两个最强的智能体集成超过了最优的AutoGluon常规模型集成。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM智能体可通过扩展设计空间为表格机器学习提供实用价值，在零额外调参成本的条件下带来一致的性能提升，并证明了该方法的有效性在近期基准上也能迁移。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of LLM-based agents for planning, code generation, and debugging, their practical value for tabular machine learning remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate a concrete use case: whether state-of-the-art agentic AI systems can design extended HPO search spaces for established tabular models that outperform the standard search spaces provided by the model authors. Specifically, we represent each tabular model as a modular pipeline covering preprocessing, embeddings, architecture, training, and inference. We then task the agent to propose candidate code implementations for each module and use a classical HPO algorithm to jointly optimize over these candidates and the model's default hyperparameters. Compared with the base HPO spaces, the expanded search spaces improve the performance of nearly every model family across a suite of 45 datasets, with average relative gains of 0.6%, rising to 2.0% on small-to-medium regression datasets. Notably, these gains come at no extra tuning cost: the enlarged spaces outperform the base under the same tuning and ensembling budgets. The gains transfer to the recent TabArena benchmark, where the agentic spaces improve the official Elo scores of four of the five model families and the two strongest agentic ensembles surpass the best AutoGluon ensemble of conventional models. Overall, our study suggests that LLM agents can provide practical value for tabular ML by expanding the design space.

</details>


### [275] [Robust Fault Detection in Mechanical Multimodal Time Series via Self-Supervised Cross-Modal Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16314)
*Magnus Munk Jensen,Dorte Hammershøi,Rafał Wiśniewski,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于跨模态重构的多模态异常检测框架，利用异构时间序列传感器数据，通过在模态之间相互重构来学习系统动态，并结合自适应测试时阈值机制应对分布偏移，在三个工业案例中显著提升了故障检测性能与分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动异常检测方法通常独立处理各传感模态或仅采用简单的特征级融合，难以利用刻画正常系统行为的跨模态关系；同时这些方法假设训练与部署分布相似，而真实工业环境中的工况变化、环境影响和系统退化会引起分布偏移，导致在未见工况下检测性能下降。

Method: 提出基于跨模态重构的多模态异常检测框架：不单独建模每个模态，而是通过从其他模态重构每个模态来学习系统动态，从而利用跨模态互补信息。该方法无需显式时间对齐或相同采样率，并增强对传感器噪声、缺失测量和单模态扰动的鲁棒性。针对部署中的分布偏移，采用跨模态重构误差与自适应测试时阈值机制来适应变化的工况。

Result: 在三个工业案例研究上的实验表明，该方法表现出较强的故障检测性能，并在分布外条件下显著提升了鲁棒性，其中在最具挑战性的工况下获得了最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 跨模态重构结合自适应阈值的框架能够有效利用异构模态间的互补关系刻画正常系统行为，在应对真实工业环境中的分布偏移和复杂工况时表现出优越的故障检测鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Fault detection is essential in industrial systems, enabling early identification of abnormal behaviour and improving safety, reliability, and operational efficiency. Modern systems increasingly rely on heterogeneous sensing modalities that capture complementary aspects of the underlying physical process. However, existing data-driven anomaly detection methods often process each modality independently or use simple feature-level fusion, limiting their ability to exploit cross-modal relationships that characterize normal system behaviour. Their performance also commonly assumes similar training and deployment distributions, whereas real-world operation is affected by changing operating conditions, environmental influences, and system degradation that induce distribution shifts and reduce detection performance, especially in unseen regimes.
  In this work, we propose a multimodal anomaly detection framework based on cross-modal reconstruction of heterogeneous time-series sensor data. Rather than modeling each modality independently, the framework learns system dynamics by reconstructing each modality from the others, thereby exploiting complementary information across modalities. This integrates information across sensing channels without requiring explicit temporal alignment or identical sampling rates, while improving robustness to sensor noise, missing measurements, and modality-specific disturbances. To address distribution shifts during real-world deployment, anomalies are identified using cross-modal reconstruction error and an adaptive test-time thresholding mechanism that adjusts to changing operating conditions. Experiments on three industrial case studies show strong fault detection performance and substantially improved robustness under out-of-distribution conditions, with the largest gains observed in the most challenging operating regimes.

</details>


### [276] [Generative models for simulation based filtering: Formulations and Empirical Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16317)
*Mohammad Al-Jarrah,Wei Deng,Bamdad Hosseini,Amirhossein Taghvaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的公式化框架，将非线性滤波问题中的分析步骤视为预报分布到后验分布的传输（transport），不同生成模型方法仅在选择和学习该传输方式上有所区别。作者基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）、其确定性流匹配极限（flow-matching limit）以及通过前向-后向SDE实现的Schrödinger桥推导出三种新滤波器，并开发了将生成模型训练与在线精修分离的两阶段调参流程。通过与最优传输滤波器（OTF）、Knothe–Rosenblatt滤波器（KRF）、序贯重要性重采样（SIR）粒子滤波器和集合卡尔曼滤波器（EnKF）在精度、计算时间和集合规模/状态维数敏感性等方面的受控数值比较，结果显示所有生成滤波器都能解析EnKF和SIR无法处理的多模态后验，但没有任何单一生成框架全面占优，最佳方法取决于在线预算和集合规模，且各滤波器产生的粒子轨迹正则性存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性滤波问题（如数据同化）中，分析步骤通常需要在无解析形式的情况下将预报分布更新为后验分布。传统方法如EnKF和SIR粒子滤波器在处理多模态后验时存在局限性。作者希望提供一个统一的视角来理解和比较各类生成式方法，并据此设计新的、更灵活的滤波器，以应对多模态分布和在线计算约束。

Method: 作者首先建立一个统一公式：分析步骤被实现为从预报分布到后验分布的传输，不同方法仅在选择和学习该传输的方式上有别。据此推导三种新滤波器——基于随机插值、其确定性流匹配极限、以及由前向-后向SDE实现的Schrödinger桥。同时提出两阶段调参流程，将生成模型的训练与在线精修分离。数值比较在精度、计算时间以及集合规模与状态维数敏感性等维度上，与OTF、KRF、SIR和EnKF进行受控对比。

Result: 数值实验表明：(1) 所有生成式滤波器均能解析EnKF和SIR无法正确处理的多模态后验；(2) 没有单一的生成框架在所有情形下占优，最佳方法由可用的在线预算和集合规模决定；(3) 各滤波器在它们生成的粒子轨迹的正则性方面存在差异。

Conclusion: 生成模型方法为非线性滤波提供了有效且通用的途径，尤其擅长处理多模态后验。但方法选择需要权衡在线计算预算与集合规模等实际约束，不存在普适最优的生成滤波器；不同方法在粒子轨迹正则性上的差异也提示在具体应用中需根据目标特性选择合适方案。

Abstract: This letter presents a unified formulation and a controlled numerical comparison of generative-model approaches to the nonlinear filtering problem. Under this formulation the analysis step is realized by a transport of the forecast distribution to the posterior, the approaches differing only in how that transport is selected and learned. We derive three new filters, based on stochastic interpolants, their deterministic flow-matching limit, and Schrödinger bridges realized through forward--backward SDEs. We develop a two-stage tuning procedure that separates the training of the generative model from its online refinement. The resulting methods are compared against the optimal transport filter (OTF), the Knothe--Rosenblatt filter (KRF), the sequential importance resampling (SIR) particle filter and the ensemble Kalman filter (EnKF), in terms of accuracy, computational time, and sensitivity to ensemble size and state dimension. The results indicate that every generative filter resolves multimodal posteriors that the EnKF and SIR do not, that no single generative framework dominates, the preferred method being set by the available online budget and ensemble size, and that the filters differ in the regularity of the particle trajectories they produce.

</details>


### [277] [Channel-Informed Neural Network for Physical Layer Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16341)
*Jose Angel Sanchez Viloria,George Sklivanitis,Dimitris Pados,Elizabeth Serena Bentley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信道信息的神经网络用于物理层密钥生成（PKG），该网络直接从接收的IQ测量中提取二进制密钥特征，利用多任务循环神经网络联合学习保持互易性的二进制特征与辅助信道估计。通过结构化信道探测获得信道估计，并利用Sionna-RT射线追踪扩充训练数据。在POWDER无线测试台的内外场景实测验证中，该方法使合法收发双方的比特不一致率低于窃听者，且射线追踪数据增强将唯一密钥率提升至0.94/0.99/0.99，密钥通过NIST随机性测试。研究同时揭示了密钥多样性与协调可靠性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘网络中分布式且资源受限的设备需要轻量级密钥建立机制，且对集中式基础设施的依赖有限。物理层密钥生成（PKG）能从互逆的信道观测中建立共享密钥而不直接交换密钥，契合这一需求。然而现有PKG方法在特征提取与信道模型结合上存在不足，需要一种能把学习到的表征显式锚定在底层多径信道中的方法，以提升密钥生成的可靠性、多样性与安全性。

Method: 提出基于信道信息的神经网络框架：采用多任务循环神经网络，联合学习保持互易性的二进制密钥特征与辅助信道估计；训练目标结合深度度量学习与信道信息监督。结构化信道探测用于从空中测量中估计信道，Sionna-RT射线追踪用于扩充训练数据以覆盖更多传播条件。在POWDER测试台上的室内外软件无线电实测数据上评估。

Result: 在所有评估场景中，所提模型对互易的Alice-Bob观测产生的比特不一致率均低于与Eve相关的观测。射线追踪数据增强显著提升密钥多样性，将唯一密钥率在室内及两个室外场景分别提升至0.94、0.99和0.99。成功协调的信道信息密钥在SHA-3隐私放大前通过了所选NIST随机性测试。

Conclusion: 研究证明了信道信息表征学习在去中心化无线密钥建立中的潜力，同时揭示了密钥多样性与协调可靠性之间重要的权衡关系。该框架为资源受限的边缘设备提供了轻量、无需集中基础设施的密钥生成途径。

Abstract: Physical-layer key generation (PKG) enables wireless devices to establish shared keys from reciprocal channel observations without directly exchanging the key. This capability is attractive for edge networks, where distributed and resource-constrained devices may require lightweight key establishment with limited access to centralized infrastructure. We introduce a channel-informed neural network for PKG that derives binary key features directly from received IQ measurements while explicitly grounding the learned representation in the underlying multipath channel. The proposed multi-task recurrent neural network jointly learns reciprocity-preserving binary features and an auxiliary channel estimate using a training objective that combines deep metric learning with channel-informed supervision. Structured channel sounding enables channel estimation from over-the-air measurements, while Sionna-RT ray tracing is used to augment training with additional propagation conditions. We evaluate the framework using indoor and outdoor software-defined-radio measurements collected on the POWDER radio testbed. Across all evaluated scenarios, the proposed model produces lower bit disagreement for reciprocal Alice-Bob observations than for Eve-related observations. Ray-traced data augmentation substantially improves key diversity, increasing the unique-key rate to 0.94, 0.99, and 0.99 across the indoor and two outdoor scenarios, respectively. Successfully reconciled channel-informed keys pass the selected NIST randomness tests prior to SHA-3 privacy amplification. The results demonstrate the potential of channel-informed representation learning for decentralized wireless key establishment while highlighting an important tradeoff between key diversity and reconciliation reliability.

</details>


### [278] [Multi-Label Proportion Learning for Sea-Ice Type Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16347)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了海冰类型预测问题，将其视为一个弱监督的多标签比例学习问题，从而直接利用多边形级别的冰图标注，避免了传统方法中将多边形标注近似为补丁级标签所带来的病态学习问题。作者提出了一个两模块框架：先用多实例学习（MIL）进行水-冰分类，再用多标签比例学习（MLPL）进行冰型成分预测，并进一步通过整合SAR图像、AMSR2亮温及ERA5再分析数据的多模态模型与模态引导的辅助正则化进行扩展。在AI4Arctic数据集上，SAR-only模型较最优监督基线MAE降低14.5%、平均冰类F1提升一倍以上；多模态模型较SAR-only模型MAE再降21.5%、平均F1提升41.2%，较监督多模态基线提升52.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰类型预测对极地气候监测、海上航行和决策至关重要，其标签数据主要来源于由冰分析师手工绘制的冰图（ice chart），但这过程劳动密集且昂贵，促使人们尝试用深度学习实现自动化。然而，深度模型需要补丁级或像素级标签，而冰图只提供多边形级标注，现有监督方法常通过将样本赋予所属多边形的优势冰型来构造近似标签，这会造成本质上近似的病态学习问题。因此本文旨在避免不必要的标签近似，以提升预测精度。

Method: 论文将海冰类型预测重新定义为弱监督的多标签比例学习问题，从而直接使用多边形级冰图标签。提出一个两模块框架：第一模块采用多实例学习（MIL）进行水-冰分类；第二模块引入多标签比例学习（MLPL）用于冰型成分预测。此外，还扩展出一种多模态模型，集成了SAR图像、AMSR2亮温数据和ERA5再分析数据，并通过模态引导的辅助正则化来整合多源信息。

Result: 在AI4Arctic数据集上的评估显示，SAR-only模型相比最优监督基线将MAE降低了14.5%，且平均冰类F1分数提升超过一倍。多模态模型进一步将MAE比SAR-only模型降低21.5%，平均F1提升41.2%，并比监督多模态基线提升52.7%。

Conclusion: 通过将海冰类型预测重构为弱监督多标签比例学习问题，该方法能够直接利用多边形级冰图标注，避免标签近似，从而显著提升预测精度；多模态融合进一步增强了性能，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Abstract: Sea-ice type prediction is important for climate monitoring, maritime navigation, and decision-making in polar regions. The main source of label data for this task is the ice chart, produced manually by ice analysts who interpret satellite imagery to delineate ice zones into polygons. Although ice charts are valuable, their production is labor-intensive and expensive, motivating recent efforts to automate the process using deep learning. However, deep learning models require patch-level (or pixel-level) label data for training, while ice charts provide only polygon-level annotations. As a workaround, supervised approaches often create approximate patch-level labels from polygon-level ice chart labels by assigning each sample the dominant ice type of its parent polygon. This approach enables supervised training but creates an ill-posed learning problem with intrinsically approximate solution. In this paper, we redefine sea-ice type prediction as a weakly supervised multi-label proportion learning problem to be able to directly use the polygon-level ice chart labels and avoid unnecessary label approximation for improved prediction accuracy. To address this problem, we propose a two-module framework where first Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) is used for water--ice classification, and then a multi-label proportion learning (MLPL) is introduced for ice-type composition prediction. We further extend this framework with a multimodal model that integrates SAR imagery with AMSR2 brightness temperatures and ERA5 reanalysis data through modality-guided auxiliary regularization. Evaluated on the AI4Arctic dataset, the SAR-only model reduces MAE by 14.5\% and more than doubles mean ice-class F1 over the best supervised baseline. The multimodal model further reduces MAE by 21.5\% and raises mean F1 by 41.2\% over the SAR-only model, and by 52.7\% over the supervised multimodal baseline.

</details>


### [279] [Federated stochastic bilevel optimization with fully first-order gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16350)
*Yihan Zhang,Rohit Dhaipule,Chiu C Tan,Haibin Ling,Hongchang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖一阶oracle的联邦随机方差缩减双层梯度下降算法，避免计算二阶Hessian和Jacobian矩阵，采用常量单时间尺度学习率协调变量更新，并给出新的收敛率证明策略，实验验证算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦随机双层优化在机器学习中应用广泛，但现有算法大多需要计算二阶Hessian和Jacobian矩阵，导致实际运行时间过长，亟需更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出联邦随机方差缩减双层梯度下降算法，仅使用一阶oracle，无需计算二阶矩阵；引入常量单时间尺度学习率机制协调不同变量的更新；提出新的收敛率推导策略。

Result: 实验结果表明所提算法在运行时间和收敛性能上均优于现有方法，验证了算法的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过消除二阶计算需求和创新的学习率机制，显著提升了联邦随机双层优化的效率，为相关应用提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Federated stochastic bilevel optimization has been actively studied in recent years due to its widespread applications in machine learning. However, most existing federated stochastic bilevel optimization algorithms require the computation of second-order Hessian and Jacobian matrices, which leads to longer running times in practice. To address these challenges, we propose a novel federated stochastic variance-reduced bilevel gradient descent algorithm that relies solely on first-order oracles. Specifically, our approach does not require the computation of second-order Hessian and Jacobian matrices, significantly reducing running time. Furthermore, we introduce a novel learning rate mechanism, i.e., a constant single-timescale learning rate, to coordinate the update of different variables. We also present a new strategy to establish the convergence rate of our algorithm. Finally, the extensive experimental results confirm the efficacy of our proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [280] [Autonomous Droplet Navigation via Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16369)
*Rajneesh Anand,Mayuresh V. Kothare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型强化学习的自主液滴导航方法，在重力驱动的Labyrinth平台上，通过薄硅油膜减少钉扎、双轴倾斜提供驱动力，利用离线训练的策略在部分可观测条件下实现复杂几何路径（直线、直角、曲线及外角）的可靠导航，并展示策略可迁移至更复杂几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴在受限几何中的自主运输仍具挑战，因其接触角滞后、可变形性和毛细钉扎导致响应非线性且历史依赖，经典控制器和预编程轨迹无法应对多转弯环境。

Method: 采用基于模型的强化学习：薄硅油膜降低接触线钉扎，双轴倾斜提供重力驱动，顶视相机实时追踪液滴。离线训练策略从有限物理交互数据中学习，无需仿真或解析液滴模型，且系统在部分可观测条件下工作（隐藏油膜厚度、瞬时接触角、变形状态）。

Result: 学习策略在直线、直角、弧形路径（包括外角几何）上实现可靠导航；在一较简单几何上训练的策略可零样本迁移至直角和阶梯路径，且仅用五分之一的训练数据即可在弧形路径上完全成功。

Conclusion: 结果表明模型强化学习能为液滴微流控系统提供智能导航能力，有望使液滴平台成为智能化学实验室。

Abstract: Precise manipulation of liquid droplets underpins lab-on-a-chip platforms for diagnostics, chemical synthesis, and biological assays. Yet autonomous droplet transport through confined geometries of varying complexity remains an open challenge. Droplets exhibit contact-angle hysteresis, deformability, and capillary pinning, which make their response to actuation nonlinear and history dependent, that classical controllers and pre-programmed trajectories cannot cope in multi-turn environments. Here we demonstrate autonomous navigation of a liquid droplet through geometries of increasing complexity on a gravity driven (Labyrinth) platform using model-based reinforcement learning. A thin silicone oil film reduces contact-line pinning while two-axis tilt supplies the gravitational driving force, and an overhead camera tracks the droplet in real time. An offline-trained policy discovers effective tilt strategies from limited physical interaction data, without simulation or analytical droplet models. The system operates under partial observability, as oil-film thickness, instantaneous contact angle, and droplet deformation state remain hidden from the controller. Despite these challenges, the learned policy achieves reliable navigation across straight, right-angle, and curved-arc paths, including outside-corner geometries. We further demonstrate that a policy trained on a simpler geometry transfers to complex ones, succeeding zero-shot on right-angle and staircase paths and reaching full success on a curved arc with a fifth of the training data. The findings suggest promising avenues for enabling droplet based microfluidic systems to serve as intelligent chemical laboratories.

</details>


### [281] [Certified Uncertainty Propagation in One-Shot Federated Bayesian Models via Posterior Event Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16373)
*Mahyar Mohammadi,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Abolfazl Yaghmaei,Hamed Kebriaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向联邦贝叶斯神经网络的可部署一致性认证框架。针对单轮联邦贝叶斯学习中通过聚合客户端后验参数获得部署模型、而本地证书无法直接保证聚合模型安全性的问题，作者通过将本地后验事件传播到部署聚合规则中，实现了精确的安全概率下界估计。对于联邦平均（FedAvg），给出了精确的几何刻画：客户端构建参数空间中的不相交超矩形区域并计算概率质量，服务端对这些区域做笛卡尔积并映射到部署规则，只有通过验证的乘积事件才被保留。在客户端后验独立假设下，乘积事件概率可分解为局部质量的乘积，对已验证不相交事件求和即为部署模型安全概率的下界。对于非负聚合系数的FedAvg，轴对齐超矩形笛卡尔积的像恰好是加权超矩形，无集合过近似。实验（MNIST/Fashion-MNIST，标签Dirichlet异质性）表明，所提出的传输事件证书为22.51%-46.89%，而直接全局证书为72.05%-91.39%，显示预测准确率与可认证安全性不一定同步变化，且全局后验构造在不同架构下表现不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦贝叶斯学习中，部署模型通过对客户端后验分布采样的参数进行聚合获得。但本地（客户端）的安全证书仅针对本地模型，无法直接保证聚合后的全局模型满足安全属性。现有方法忽略了局部证书到部署模型的传播机制，缺少与部署规则一致的安全认证框架，因此需要开发一种能将本地后验事件传播到部署聚合规则的认证方法。

Method: 提出部署一致性认证框架：核心思想是将本地后验事件（不相交超矩形区域及其概率质量）通过部署聚合规则传播。具体地，每个客户端在参数空间构造互不相交的轴对齐超矩形区域并计算概率质量；服务端对客户端区域构造笛卡尔积，通过部署规则（FedAvg）映射这些乘积区域，仅保留聚合像被验证满足安全属性的事件。在客户端后验独立假设下，乘积事件概率因式分解为局部质量之积，对所有已验证不相交事件求和得到部署模型安全概率的下界。关键的几何性质是：对非负聚合系数的FedAvg而言，轴对齐超矩形笛卡尔积的聚合像是精确的加权超矩形，因此不引入任何集合过近似。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上，采用标签Dirichlet异质性设置进行实验。结果表明：所提出的传输事件（FedAvg）证书范围在22.51%到46.89%之间，而直接全局证书（基于FedAvg诱导的后验和Product-of-Gaussians聚合）范围在72.05%到91.39%之间。发现预测准确率与可认证安全性的变化趋势并不一致，且不同的全局后验构造在不同网络架构下呈现不同的认证行为。

Conclusion: 本文首次为联邦贝叶斯神经网络提供了与部署规则一致的（deployment-consistent）概率认证方法，通过事件传输实现了FedAvg下无过近似的精确几何刻画。实验揭示了本地证书（直接全局证书）与部署证书之间存在显著差距，说明仅靠本地/全局后验认证不足以保证实际部署模型的安全。同时表明预测性能与安全可认证性之间的解耦关系，以及架构对后验构造认证行为的影响，为联邦安全部署提供了新的视角和方法基础。

Abstract: Probabilistic certification of Bayesian neural networks lower-bounds the posterior probability that a model satisfies a verifier-defined safety property. In one-shot federated Bayesian learning, however, the deployed model is obtained by aggregating parameters drawn from client-specific posterior distributions, so local certificates do not directly guarantee safety of the aggregated model. This paper develops a deployment-consistent certification framework by propagating local posterior events through the deployment aggregation rule, with an exact geometric characterization for Federated Averaging (FedAvg). Each client constructs disjoint hyper-rectangular regions in parameter space and computes their probability masses. The server forms Cartesian products of these regions, maps them through the deployment rule, and retains a product event only when its aggregation image is verified to satisfy the safety property. Under independent client posteriors, each product-event probability factorizes into local masses, and summing verified disjoint events yields a lower bound on safety probability of the deployed model. For FedAvg with nonnegative aggregation coefficients, the image of a Cartesian product of axis-aligned hyper-rectangles is exactly a weighted hyper-rectangle, introducing no set over-approximation. We distinguish the proposed transported-event certificate from direct certification under posterior distributions induced by FedAvg and Product-of-Gaussians aggregation. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST under label-Dirichlet heterogeneity show that the transported FedAvg certificate ranges from 22.51% to 46.89%, while direct global certificates range from 72.05% to 91.39%. Results show that predictive accuracy and certifiable safety do not necessarily follow the same trend, and that global posterior constructions can exhibit distinct certification behavior across architectures.

</details>


### [282] [Bounded Adjustment with Reliability-Guided Embedding for Imbalanced Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16380)
*Mushir Akhtar,Akarsh J.,M. Tanveer,Mohd. Arshad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BARGE（带可靠性引导几何的有界调整），一种单阶段目标函数，结合有界的先验调整密度功率评分与可靠性引导的角几何，同时解决类别不平衡学习与标签噪声的耦合失败模式。它无需噪声率或转移矩阵，使用单一网络，推理不变。在 CIFAR-10/100 和 Tiny ImageNet 的长尾与阶梯不平衡、干净标签及 20%/40% 随机错误标签替换下，12 个干净设置中总体排名第二并在四个中取得最低误差；在所有六个标签损坏设置中取得最低平均平衡误差（从最强竞争者的 72.32% 降至 70.00%），并在每个损坏标签设置中获得最高 macro-F1 和 macro-AUPRC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别平衡学习与标签噪声会形成耦合的失败模式：频率校正能防止多数类主导决策规则，但会放大错误标注的少数类样本。现有方法通常需要估计噪声率或转移矩阵，且无法同时兼顾类平衡与噪声鲁棒性。

Method: BARGE 是单阶段目标，由两部分组成：(1) 有界、先验调整的密度功率评分——该分类评分在调整后概率空间中是严格适当的，在干净监督和真实类先验下恢复平衡贝叶斯排序；在标签污染下，其有限范围将固定预测器的分类风险扰动限制在界内，其 logit 梯度在模型自信地反驳所给标签时呈红下降（redescend）。(2) 可靠性引导的几何——调整后的目标概率对类别等权特征紧致性加权，单侧分离项阻止对齐的类别方向。

Result: 在 12 个干净设置中总体排名第二，四个设置取得最低误差；在所有六个数据集-损坏设置中取得最低平均平衡误差，将六设置平均值从最强竞争者的 72.32% 降至 70.00%；在每个损坏标签设置中获得最高 macro-F1 和 macro-AUPRC。消融实验显示类别等权角紧致性优于仅用有界评分。

Conclusion: 有界的预测影响力与可靠性引导的几何是处理不确定标签下不平衡学习的互补机制，两者结合可有效缓解类频率校正与标签噪声的耦合失败模式。

Abstract: Class-balanced learning and label noise create a coupled failure mode: frequency correction prevents majority classes from dominating the decision rule, but can amplify incorrectly labeled minority examples. We introduce BARGE (Bounded Adjustment with Reliability-Guided Embeddings), a single-stage objective combining a bounded, prior-adjusted density-power score with reliability-guided angular geometry. Its classification score is strictly proper in the adjusted probability space and recovers balanced Bayes ordering under clean supervision and the true class prior. Under label contamination, its finite range bounds classification-risk perturbation at a fixed predictor, while its logit gradient redescends when the model confidently contradicts the supplied label. The adjusted target probability also weights class-equal feature compactness, and a one-sided separation term discourages aligned class directions. BARGE requires neither a noise rate nor a transition matrix, uses one network, and leaves inference unchanged. We evaluate it on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet under long-tail and step imbalance, clean labels, and 20% and 40% random incorrect-label replacement. Across 12 clean settings, BARGE ranks second overall and attains the lowest error in four. Under corruption, it achieves the lowest mean balanced error in all six dataset-corruption settings, reducing the six-setting average from 72.32% for the strongest competitor to 70.00%. It also obtains the highest macro-F1 and macro-AUPRC in every corrupted-label setting. Ablations show that class-equal angular compactness improves on the bounded score alone. These results support bounded predictive influence and reliability-guided geometry as complementary mechanisms for imbalanced learning with uncertain labels.

</details>


### [283] [Attention Mean Fields Predict Average Representation Dynamics and Reveal Context-Specific Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16382)
*Micah Adler,John W. Byers,Mark Crovella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力机制的均场（mean-field）分析框架，将token间的平均注意力定义为核（kernel），该核在层间传递表征并可迭代建模表征几何的动态演化。通过两种条件化解读——语料条件和上下文条件——该核分别预测表征几何的平均演化与特定上下文的预期几何，而注意力头相对预测的偏离（均场偏差）则隔离了均场遗漏的上下文特定计算。研究还发现早期训练中模型与语料均场不可区分，直至归纳（induction）出现才发生分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的表征几何并非预先决定，而是在模型运行过程中动态演化的。忠实描述该几何必须捕捉这一动态过程，因此不能仅基于与模型无关的统计量（如共现频率）。现有方法忽视了表征演化的过程性，作者希望建立一种能刻画表征几何动态变换的、与模型相关的分析框架。

Method: 引入注意力的均场分析：将从一个token到另一个token的平均注意力定义为核函数，该核将表征在层间传递并可在网络中迭代，从而建模几何的变换方式。核的两种条件化方式：(1)以整个语料为条件，得到表征几何的平均演化预测（开环模型）；(2)以单一上下文为条件，预测该上下文的预期几何。注意力头与预测的偏离（均场偏差）被定义为隔离上下文特定计算的手段。开环模型仅凭输入嵌入和冻结权重迭代核与MLP，不参考任何实测偏差。

Result: 语料条件解读下，开环模型的预测高度准确。在训练早期，模型与其语料均场不可区分——用均场替换每个注意力头不改变真实文本上的损失；在归纳（induction）出现时两者分化，且随表征被上下文化（contextualized）差距扩大。上下文条件解读下，偏差是任务无关的上下文计算度量，残差可加性分解为异常注意力路由与所传输值的上下文化两部分；在受控归纳和少样本场景中，更大的偏差对应更强的上下文信息依赖。

Conclusion: 均场分析为理解语言模型表征几何的动态演化提供了有效框架，不仅能够高精度预测平均表征演化（开环模型），还能将注意力头的计算拆解为可预测的均场部分与上下文特定偏差部分，并揭示模型训练的早期均场行为与后期上下文化分化的阶段转变规律。

Abstract: A language model's representation geometry is not predetermined; it evolves as the model runs. A faithful account of that geometry must capture that dynamic process, and so cannot be based solely on model-independent statistics such as co-occurrence. Here we introduce a mean-field analysis of attention. The average attention from one token to another defines a kernel that carries representations layer to layer and can be iterated through the network to model how the geometry is transformed. We condition this average two ways. Conditioned on a whole corpus, the kernel predicts the average-case evolution of representation geometry. Conditioned instead on a single context, it predicts the expected geometry for that context. A head's departure from that prediction, its \emph{mean-field deviation}, isolates the context-specific computation that the mean field misses.
  Under the corpus-conditional reading, the kernel yields an open-loop model: from the input embeddings and the frozen weights alone, we can iterate the kernel and the model's own MLPs over token representations, never consulting a measured deviation at any layer. The resulting prediction is highly accurate.
  In early training the model and its corpus mean field are indistinguishable. Replace every attention head with its mean field, and the substitution leaves the loss on real text unchanged. Around the onset of induction, the two diverge, and the gap widens as representations become contextualized.
  Under the context-conditional reading, deviation from the mean field is a task-agnostic measure of context-specific computation. The residual decomposes additively into unusual attention routing and contextualization of the transported values. Across controlled induction and few-shot settings, greater deviation tracks greater reliance on in-context information.

</details>


### [284] [How Good Are Time-Series Foundation Models for Pedestrian Crowd Count Forecasting? A Cross-Dataset Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16415)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Ziteng Li,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Hoogendoorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统benchmark了七种单变量行人计数预测方法（季节性朴素、梯度提升树、深度学习与预训练时间序列基础模型），在特殊事件数据集SAIL2025与墨尔本长期行人传感器数据集上比较MAE/RMSE。结果揭示：有限历史数据下季节性朴素在长时域预测中仍是强基线；提升树在低流量传感器上有竞争力但对高流量传感器的事件性偏移敏感；基础模型在数据丰富且强季节性的长上下文场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期时间序列基础模型在异构预测基准上报告了较强的零样本精度，但这些增益能否可靠迁移到行人传感部署场景仍不明确。为行人导向的智能交通系统（如人群监测、人员调度、风险缓解）选择合适预测模型需要一个系统性的比较研究。

Method: 构建七种单变量预测方法、四种范式的基准测试：季节性朴素、梯度提升树（LightGBM、CatBoost）、深度学习模型（N-HiTS、PatchTST）、预训练基础模型（TimesFM、Chronos-2）。实验涵盖两个互补场景：(i) 3分钟分辨率、域内历史有限的特大型活动数据集SAIL2025；(ii) 2010–2017年多年度小时级、强季节性的墨尔本行人传感器数据。按传感器及多预测时域比较MAE与RMSE。

Result: 得到三个一致发现：(1) 历史数据有限时，季节性朴素对高流量传感器的长时域预测仍是强基线，而训练模型在次日与历史差异较大时可能退化；(2) 提升树在低流量传感器上具备竞争力，但在事件驱动偏移下对高流量传感器较敏感；(3) 基础模型在数据丰富且季节性强的长上下文配置下表现最佳。

Conclusion: 行人计数预测模型的选择应同时依据底层数据条件与时域长度，不存在普适最优模型。研究为部署行人导向ITS提供了模型选择依据。

Abstract: Pedestrian-count forecasting supports pedestrian-oriented Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), including crowd monitoring, pedestrian-traffic staffing and routing, and proactive risk mitigation during surges. Recent time-series foundation models (FMs) report strong zero-shot accuracy on heterogeneous forecasting benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains transfer reliably to pedestrian sensing deployments. We benchmark seven univariate forecasting approaches spanning four paradigms: Seasonal Naive, gradient-boosted trees (LightGBM, CatBoost), deep learning models (N-HiTS, PatchTST), and two pretrained FMs (TimesFM, Chronos-2). Experiments cover two complementary regimes: (i) a five-day special event dataset SAIL2025 at 3-minute resolution with limited in-domain history; and (ii) Melbourne pedestrian sensors as a multi-year hourly dataset (2010--2017) with strong seasonality. We compare the MAE and RMSE results per sensor across datasets and multiple forecast horizons. Results show three consistent findings. First, with limited historical data, Seasonal Naive remains a strong baseline for long-horizon forecasting on high-volume sensors, while trained models can degrade when the next day differs substantially from prior days. Second, boosted trees can be competitive on lower-volume sensors but exhibit higher sensitivity on high-volume sensors under event-driven shift. Third, FMs excel in the seasonal and data-rich regime under long-context configuration. The findings highlight the importance of choosing pedestrian forecasting models based on both the underlying data conditions and the forecasting horizon.

</details>


### [285] [Interpreting and Steering LLM Agents for Social Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16436)
*Jiayue Gaveal Fan,Arul Murugan,Shreyas Krishnan,Abhishek Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何“打开”大语言模型（LLM）这一黑盒，以增强其在社会科学仿真中的应用价值。作者比较了三种方法——基于提示词的操作、SAE（稀疏自编码器）特征引导、以及基于探针的方向引导——并在风险态度、利他主义（偏好）与创造性发散、产品创新（能力）四类经典经济与创造性任务中评估它们的效用。结果表明，SAE和探针技术通常优于简单的提示词引导方法，二者结合构成了社会科学研究中解释与引导智能体的有效管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM仿真在理解人类行为方面潜力巨大，但LLM本质上是基于深度神经网络的“黑盒”，限制了其在社会科学中的价值。这种限制源于两个缺失：(i) 可解释性，即无法清晰指派驱动观察行为的机制；(ii) 可引导性，即无法静音或放大特定理论上有意义的行动机制来驱动特定模型行为。因此作者希望打开黑盒以进一步丰富基于LLM的仿真。

Method: 作者比较三类方法：(1) 基于提示词的操作（prompt-based manipulation）；(2) SAE导出的特征引导（SAE-derived feature steering）；(3) 基于探针的方向引导（probe-based direction steering）。研究通过解释和引导人类行为的两个基础组成——偏好（风险态度、利他主义）与能力（发散性创造力、产品创新）——来检验这些方法的效用，并将四类经典的经济与创造性任务实现为自然语言交互。

Result: 总体结果显示，SAE和基于探针的技术在引导LLM智能体方面通常优于基础的提示词方法，尽管这一优势取决于具体的提示策略。SAEs将智能体的内部表征分解为人类可读的特征，随后探针能够可靠地将智能体的行为转向指定方向，二者共同构成有效的管线。

Conclusion: SAE与探针的组合代表了社会科学研究者解释并引导仿真智能体的高效管线，为未来使用LLM智能体进行社会科学仿真提供了方法论启示。作者还讨论了这些方法对未来工作的意义。

Abstract: Simulations based on large language models (LLMs) have proven to be powerful for understanding human behavior, making them valuable additions to the social scientific toolkit. However, LLMs are ultimately black boxes based on deep neural networks which limits their value for social science. This is because of a lack of (i) interpretability: i.e. the ability to assign clear mechanisms driving observed behavior; and a lack of (ii) steerability: i.e. the ability to mute or amplify specific theoretically meaningful mechanisms of action to drive specific model behavior. Here, we demonstrate how the black box could be opened up to further enrich LLM-based simulations. Specifically, we compare three types of methods: (1) prompt-based manipulation, (2) SAE-derived feature steering, and (3) probe-based direction steering and examine their utility for LLM-based social scientific simulations. We do so by interpreting and steering two foundational components of human behaviors, namely preferences (risk attitudes, altruism) and capabilities (divergent creativity, product innovation), operationalized using four classic economic and creative tasks implemented as natural-language interactions. Overall, our results show that SAE- and probe-based techniques often outperform basic prompt-based methods for steering LLM agents, although this advantage depends on the specific prompting strategy involved. Together, SAEs and probes constitute an effective pipeline for social scientists seeking to interpret and steer agents in social simulations: SAEs decompose agents' internal representations into human-readable features, after which probes can reliably shift agents' behaviors in specified directions. We discuss implications of these methods for future work using LLM agents for social scientific simulations.

</details>


### [286] [Adaptive Bayesian Partner Selection for Federated Clinical Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16446)
*Navid Seidi,Satyaki Roy,Sajal K. Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ABPS（自适应贝叶斯伙伴选择），一种面向医疗联邦学习的点对点协作框架，通过Beta-Bernoulli后验估计伙伴的Shapley边际效用并用UCB排序，经轻量级提议-拒绝机制选择协作对象，并允许在无互惠伙伴时主动放弃通信。在MIMIC-IV的230个非独立同分布临床中心上的院内死亡预测任务中，ABPS-X以FedAvg 0.09倍的通信成本达到最强基线FedDyn的精度（AUROC 0.758）且方差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗联邦学习面临显著的跨中心异质性和时间概念漂移，现有方法依赖持续的全局通信，带来大量带宽开销，且与对齐不良的伙伴协作会产生负迁移风险。因此需要一种能动态、自适应地决定"与谁协作、何时协作、以何种成本协作"的机制，以降低通信开销并避免负迁移。

Method: ABPS采用点对点框架：每个中心维护关于候选伙伴Shapley边际效用的Beta-Bernoulli后验，以UCB准则对伙伴排序，通过轻量级的提议-拒绝机制形成协作，并在无互惠伙伴时选择放弃通信（主动隔离）。该框架具备随机决策解释，给出有限样本集中性和O(kappa log T)伙伴选择遗憾的保证，并推导出负迁移下主动隔离最优的条件。扩展方面包括头部个性化、bfloat16量化通信、可调激活集大小以及面向目标的元数据过滤器，支持机构特定协作策略。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV中抽取的230个非独立同分布临床中心上，针对ICU住院前24小时的二分类院内死亡预测任务，完整ABPS-X变体以FedAvg 0.09倍的通信成本达到最强联邦基线FedDyn的AUROC 0.758，且变异性降低；多样性驱动配置下相当比例的中心触发了主动隔离。

Conclusion: 自适应、效用感知的协作机制能在中心数量多且规模小的场景下在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅削减通信开销，为医疗联邦学习提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) in healthcare faces pronounced heterogeneity and temporal concept drift across clinical centers, where evolving patient populations and care practices shift data distributions. Existing approaches rely on persistent global communication, incurring substantial bandwidth overhead while risking negative transfer from poorly aligned peers. We propose Adaptive Bayesian Partner Selection (ABPS), a peer-to-peer framework that governs who collaborates, when, and at what cost. Each center maintains a Beta-Bernoulli posterior over prospective peers' Shapley marginal utility, ranks candidates with an Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) criterion, and forms collaborations through a lightweight propose-reject mechanism, with the option to abstain from communication when no mutually beneficial partner exists. The framework admits a stochastic decision interpretation, yielding finite-sample concentration guarantees and O(kappa log T) regret in partner selection, along with conditions under which intentional isolation is optimal under negative transfer. Lightweight extensions (head personalization, bfloat16 quantized communication, and a tunable active-set size) further improve efficiency, and a goal-aware metadata filter enables institution-specific collaboration strategies. On binary in-hospital mortality prediction over the first 24 hours of an ICU stay, with 230 non-IID clinical centers drawn from MIMIC-IV, the full ABPS-X variant matches the strongest federated baseline (FedDyn, AUROC 0.758) at 0.09x the communication cost of FedAvg, with reduced variability. A diversity-driven configuration activates intentional isolation for a substantial fraction of centers. These results show that adaptive, utility-aware collaboration reduces communication without sacrificing accuracy when centers are numerous and small, offering a scalable paradigm for healthcare FL.

</details>


### [287] [OPD-Aha: From Linguistic Momentum to Visual Reflection in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16459)
*Chenhao Qiu,Dawei Li,Yechao Zhang,Lei Gong,Zhen Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OPD-Aha，一种基于特权图文蒸馏（privileged on-policy distillation）的多模态推理矫正方法。其核心洞察是：当学生因早期图像误读而产生错误推理时，标准跨模型监督会因教师与学生"合谋"同一幻觉而失效；但教师基于特权视觉证据的矫正偏好并未丢失。OPD-Aha 通过对比教师面对真实图像与视觉空输入（visual null）的输出，将这一孤立的视觉偏好重构为蒸馏目标，从而强力抑制与图像矛盾的续写，促使学生在生成中自发插入反思 token（如 wait、actually）并转向依赖视觉证据，最终在细粒度感知与复杂多模态推理基准上取得广泛一致的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特权图文蒸馏中，教师与学生基于相同的学生生成前缀对轨迹打分。当学生早期误读图像时，累积的错误推理会逐渐把教师"拉离"其视觉证据，导致教师与学生收敛到同一幻觉上，此时标准跨模型监督恰好在最需要纠正的地方失效。因此作者旨在解决这一监督崩塌问题，找到在误导性一致下仍可用的矫正信号。

Method: 作者引入 OPD-Aha：关键洞察是教师在误导性一致下并未丢失视觉矫正偏好——通过比较同一教师面对真实图像与视觉空输入（visual null）的预测，可分离出特权证据对正确解释的推送作用。OPD-Aha 直接基于这一被孤立的视觉偏好重构蒸馏目标，而非依赖脆弱的教师-学生差异。该重建目标会强力抑制与图像相矛盾的续写，从而驱动学生学会在自身错误推理中自发插入反思 token（wait、actually），并在反思后更多依赖视觉证据而非已累积的错误文本。

Result: 经由此目标训练，学生能在生成中途打断自身有缺陷的推理并插入反思 token（如 wait、actually）；反思后的生成减少了对累积错误文本的依赖、增加了对视觉证据的依赖。这种生成中段的矫正从根本上改变了推理过程，在多样化的细粒度感知与复杂多模态推理基准上带来广泛且一致的性能提升。代码与模型已开源。

Conclusion: OPD-Aha 证明即使在教师-学生发生幻觉"合谋"的崩溃场景下，特权视觉证据仍保有可提取的矫正信号。通过将这种孤立的视觉偏好重构为蒸馏目标，能够突破标准监督失效的瓶颈，赋予学生自主反思与纠错能力，从而在多模态推理任务上获得稳健提升。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation improves multimodal reasoning by allowing a teacher to evaluate student trajectories using rich, training-only visual evidence. Both models score these trajectories while conditioning on the same student-generated prefix. When a student misinterprets an image early in a response, this accumulating erroneous rationale eventually pulls the teacher away from its visual evidence. The teacher and student converge on the same hallucination, causing standard cross-model supervision to collapse precisely where correction is most needed. We find that the teacher's visual corrective preference is not lost under this misleading agreement. Comparing the predictions of the identical teacher given the real image and a visual null reveals that the privileged evidence still pushes the model toward the correct interpretation. We introduce OPD-Aha, which reconstructs the distillation target directly from this isolated visual preference rather than relying on the fragile teacher-student discrepancy. This reconstructed target aggressively suppresses continuations that contradict the image. Trained with this objective, students learn to naturally interrupt their own flawed reasoning with reflection tokens such as wait and actually. After reflection, subsequent generation relies less on the accumulated erroneous text and more on the visual evidence. Correcting these trajectories mid-generation fundamentally alters the reasoning process, yielding broad and consistent improvements across diverse fine-grained perception and complex multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/Echochef/OPD-Aha.

</details>


### [288] [Online Gradient Computation for Warping Gaussian Process Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16472)
*Emilio Ruiz-Moreno,Konstantinos Slavakis,Baltasar Beferull-Lozano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的在线扭曲高斯过程方法，该方法的创新点在于证明了扭曲GP的瞬时负对数似然梯度可以进行精确的递归计算，从而能够同时更新潜在GP矩并优化扭曲参数，弥补了现有流式方法在参数优化与分析可溯性之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲高斯过程通过参数化变换（扭曲）将非高斯观测映射到潜在标准GP，以处理非高斯数据。但现有的流式变体要么周期性地优化扭曲参数，要么为了获得更高的模型容量而牺牲解析可溯性。作者希望同时兼顾参数在线优化与解析可溯性，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者证明了扭曲GP的瞬时负对数似然的梯度允许精确的递归计算。基于这一理论结果，提出了一种新颖的在线方法，能够同时更新潜在GP的矩（均值与协方差）并优化扭曲参数。

Result: 本文的主要成果是推导出扭曲GP瞬时负对数似然梯度的精确递推计算形式，并据此构建了一个可联合更新潜在GP矩与优化扭曲参数的在线学习算法。

Conclusion: 该在线方法打破了现有流式扭曲GP方法在参数优化与分析可溯性之间的权衡，实现了对扭曲参数和潜在GP状态的联合在线优化，提升了模型的实用性与适应能力。

Abstract: Warped Gaussian processes (GPs) handle non-Gaussian observations by mapping them into a latent standard GP via a parametric transformation called warping. Existing streaming variants, however, either optimize the warping parameters periodically or sacrifice analytical tractability for a higher model capacity. To bridge this gap, we show that the gradient of the instantaneous negative log-likelihood of a warped GP admits an exact recursive computation. Based on this result, we propose a novel online method for warped GPs that jointly updates the latent GP moments and optimizes the warping parameters.

</details>


### [289] [Decoder Design Matters for ECG Delineation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16489)
*Joseph Scharpf,William Han,Chaojing Duan,Michael A. Rosenberg,Emerson Liu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R-U-Net，一种将ResNet-18编码器与U-Net解码器配对的ECG波形边界检测模型。在SemiSegECG基准上，R-U-Net在16种域内设置中均优于最强的ResNet-18+全卷积网络（FCN）头基线，mIoU提升3.3-13.0；在跨域设置中达到82.6 mIoU，较基线提升8.1。消融实验表明解码器设计对性能的贡献大于所评估的半监督学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电信号波形分割（ECG delineation）通过识别P波、QRS波群和T波的边界，为AI模型解读心电图提供结构化标注，但训练精准的分割模型需要大量稀缺且耗时的人工标注。现有工作虽通过半监督学习（SSL）缓解标注不足问题，但对架构尤其是解码器的设计关注不足，本文旨在通过改进解码器架构来提升分割性能。

Method: 提出R-U-Net模型，采用ResNet-18作为编码器，搭配U-Net解码器进行ECG波形边界分割。在SemiSegECG基准上与ResNet-18+全卷积网络（FCN）头基线进行对比，并设计受控消融实验，分别评估解码器设计与半监督学习方法对性能的贡献。

Result: R-U-Net在16种域内设置中均超越最强基线，mIoU提升3.3-13.0；在跨域设置中达到82.6 mIoU，较基线提升8.1 mIoU。消融实验显示解码器设计带来的性能提升大于所评估的SSL方法。

Conclusion: 解码器架构设计对ECG分割性能的贡献被低估，其影响甚至超过半监督学习方法本身。该发现为ECG delineation任务中架构的进一步探索提供了动机，同时作者开源了全部代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) delineation identifies the boundaries of P waves, QRS complexes, and T waves, providing structural annotations that can guide AI models in learning to interpret ECGs. However, training accurate delineation models requires manual annotations that are scarce and time-consuming to obtain. Recent work addresses this limitation through semi-supervised learning (SSL), but the design of the architecture, particularly the decoder, has received less attention. To this end, we propose R-U-Net, an ECG delineation model that pairs a ResNet-18 encoder with a U-Net decoder. On SemiSegECG, R-U-Net outperforms the strongest evaluated ResNet-18 + fully convolutional network (FCN) head baseline in each of the 16 in-domain settings by 3.3-13.0 mIoU and achieves 82.6 mIoU in the cross-domain setting, an improvement of 8.1 mIoU. Controlled ablations show that decoder design contributes more to performance gains than the evaluated SSL methods, motivating further exploration of architectures for ECG delineation. All code is open-source at github.com/ELM-Research/ECG-Delineation.

</details>


### [290] [High-Performance Tensor Formulation of the Viterbi Algorithm for Hidden Semi-Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16500)
*Lorenzo Piarulli,Elia Belli,Daniele De Sensi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量运算的HSMM（隐半马尔可夫模型）Viterbi算法新表述，将内层循环重构为张量操作以适配SIMD单元和并行架构。基于该表述，作者提供了单核、多核CPU及首次实现的GPU优化版本。实验表明，相比先进顺序基线，单核加速最高14倍、多核超过200倍、GPU超过570倍，为大规模HSMM解码建立了新的性能基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSMM是计算生物学、金融和信号处理等领域广泛使用的基础概率模型，Viterbi算法用于解码最可能的状态序列，并可通过迭代应用于从头学习。然而现有Viterbi实现均为顺序执行，且完全缺乏GPU加速方案，导致大规模HSMM解码难以实际应用。

Method: 提出基于张量的Viterbi算法表述，将传统算法中的内层循环重构为张量操作，从而自然映射到SIMD单元和超大规模并行架构。基于该核心表述，实现了覆盖单核、多核CPU以及GPU（首次提出）的多种优化版本。

Result: 实验评估显示：单核上相对先进顺序基线最高加速14倍，多核版本超过200倍，GPU版本超过570倍，确立了大规模HSMM解码的全新性能基准。

Conclusion: 张量化表述有效突破了HSMM解码的算力瓶颈，GPU加速的首次引入使HSMM解码得以支持大规模应用场景，显著提升了该领域性能上限。

Abstract: Hidden Semi-Markov Models (HSMMs) are fundamental probabilistic models widely adopted across diverse domains, from computational biology to finance and signal processing. The Viterbi algorithm decodes the most likely state sequence given an HSMM and can be applied iteratively for ab initio model learning. However, existing Viterbi implementations remain sequential, and GPU-accelerated solutions are entirely absent, making HSMM decoding impractical for large-scale workloads. We present a tensor-based formulation of the Viterbi algorithm for HSMMs, restructuring the inner loops into tensor operations that naturally map onto SIMD units and massively parallel architectures. Building on this formulation, we provide optimized implementations spanning single- and multi-core CPUs, and, for the first time, GPU. Experimental evaluation demonstrates speedups of up to 14x on a single core, over 200x with multi-core, and over 570x on GPU over the state-of-the-art sequential baseline, establishing a new performance baseline for large-scale HSMM decoding.

</details>


### [291] [FlowATC: Aircraft Trajectory Prediction via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16528)
*Mathurin Petit,Emir Torun,Louis Brusset,Jordan Kam,Alexandre M. Bayen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于流匹配（Flow Matching）架构的航空器轨迹预测模型，仅用历史ADS-B轨迹数据训练（无需航路标签或航图监督），在旧金山湾区115万条轨迹窗口上训练。模型直接在原生非规则ADS-B采样间隔上训练，将轨迹预测建模为序列填补（inpainting）任务，采用块因果Transformer以历史状态为条件去噪未来状态token。在参数匹配时，条件流匹配（CFM）在minADE@20上比扩散模型（DDPM）优11-26%，两类生成式目标均比CVAE基线优31-41%。误差随预测时域gracefully退化，且在时间降采样数据上重训仍有效。多次独立采样可生成空间概率占用估计，供下游冲突风险评估使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建下一代空中交通管制决策支持工具需要稳健的轨迹预测模型，现有方法依赖航路标签或航图监督，制约其泛化能力。作者希望设计一种仅依赖历史轨迹数据的生成式轨迹预测架构，以准确复现空域结构（如SFO的NIITE FOUR离场程序形状），并服务下游冲突风险评估。

Method: 采用流匹配架构：将轨迹预测视为序列填补任务，用块因果Transformer基于观测历史去噪未来状态token，分别采用条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching, CFM）和去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）两种生成目标；直接在原生不规则ADS-B采样间隔上训练，不使用航路标签或航图信息；与常速度、确定性LSTM、CVAE基线对比，并测试时间降采样重训与预测时域退化。

Result: 模型生成的轨迹分布与历史交通高度吻合，复现了SFO发布的NIITE FOUR离场程序形状。参数匹配下，CFM在minADE@20上比DDPM优11-26%，两类生成目标比CVAE基线优31-41%；误差随预测时域gracefully退化，时间降采样重训仍保持有效性；K次独立采样可得到空间概率占用估计。

Conclusion: 流匹配结合块因果Transformer的生成式轨迹预测在精度上优于DDPM、CVAE和确定性LSTM基线，且无需航路标签/航图监督即可复现空域结构，具备graceful退化特性与概率化输出能力，可有效服务下游冲突风险评估与下一代空管决策支持。

Abstract: Building accurate decision-support tools for next-generation air traffic control requires robust trajectory prediction models. We present a flow-matching architecture trained exclusively on historical aircraft trajectories, with no route labels or chart supervision. Trained on 1.15 million Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast trajectory windows collected over the San Francisco Bay Area, the model generates aircraft trajectory distributions that closely match historical traffic, reproducing known airspace structure around San Francisco Airport such as the shape of SFO's published NIITE FOUR departure procedure. Our model is trained directly on the native, irregular ADS-B sampling interval. Trajectory prediction is cast as sequence inpainting using a block-causal Transformer that denoises future state tokens conditioned on the observed history using Conditional Flow Matching or Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models. We compare our architecture against constant-velocity, deterministic-Long Short Term Memory, and Conditional Variational Autoencoders baselines. At matched parameter count, CFM outperforms DDPM by 11-26% in minADE@20, and both generative objectives surpass the CVAE baseline by 31-41%. We further show that the error degrades gracefully with prediction horizon, and the architecture remains effective when retrained on temporally decimated feeds. Lastly, we sample $K$ independent completions, yielding spatial probabilistic occupancy estimates that can serve as input to downstream conflict-risk estimation.

</details>


### [292] [What Does Layer-Importance Reveal About Transformers and State-Space Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16537)
*Istabrak Abbes,Nizar Islah,Irina Rish,Sarath Chandar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"必要性"(Necessity)与"可塑性"(Plasticity)两个维度对层重要性进行解耦分析，发现Transformer与状态空间模型(SSM)在层重要性分布上存在根本性差异：在所有评估的残差Transformer（最高14B参数）中，必要性与可塑性在深度方向上反对齐；而在Mamba风格SSM中二者指向重叠区域。对齐的符号还能预测下游适配行为——在Transformer中将更新集中在可塑性最高的层会加剧灾难性遗忘，而该层间效应在Mamba风格SSM中消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer与状态空间模型(SSM)是序列建模的两大主导家族，但针对Transformer积累的分析知识能否迁移到SSM仍是一个核心开放问题。本文以层重要性为切入点，旨在弥合两大模型家族之间的理论桥梁。

Method: 作者将层重要性解耦为两个独立概念：必要性(Necessity)衡量预训练模型对某层既有贡献的依赖程度，通过绕过该层导致的损失增量来度量；可塑性(Plasticity)衡量微调过程中模型在何处吸收新信息，通过任务特定权重更新的幅度来度量。基于这两个指标，作者对残差Transformer（最高14B参数）和Mamba风格SSM进行了跨深度对齐分析。

Result: 实验结果揭示了两个模型家族的定性差异：所有被评估的残差Transformer中，必要性与可塑性在深度维度上呈反对齐（anti-align）；而在Mamba风格SSM中二者指向重叠区域。此外，对齐的符号可预测下游适配行为——在Transformer中，将更新集中于可塑性最高的层会显著加剧灾难性遗忘；而该按层区分的效应在Mamba风格SSM中消失。

Conclusion: 两大序列建模家族在层重要性结构上存在根本性差异，这决定了针对Transformer建立的分析知识与压缩、选择性微调、可解释性等实践方法不能不加验证地直接迁移到SSM。该发现对理解SSM的内部工作机制以及设计跨家族的通用方法与理论具有重要意义。

Abstract: Transformers and state-space models (SSMs) are the two dominant families of sequence models, and a central open question is how far the analytical knowledge built for transformers transfers to SSMs. We address this through the lens of layer importance which underpins compression, selective fine-tuning, and interpretability across both families. We decompose layer importance into two distinct notions. \emph{Necessity} captures how much the pretrained model depends on a layer's existing contribution, measured by the loss increase from bypassing it. \emph{Plasticity} captures where the model absorbs new information during fine-tuning, measured by the magnitude of task-specific weight updates. Our analysis reveals that the two families behave fundamentally differently: in every evaluated residual transformer up to $14$B parameters, Necessity and Plasticity anti-align across depth, whereas in the evaluated Mamba-style SSMs they point to overlapping regions. The sign of this alignment also predicts downstream adaptation behavior. In the evaluated transformers, concentrating updates in the most plastic layers increases catastrophic forgetting, while this tier-dependent effect disappears in the evaluated Mamba-style SSMs.

</details>


### [293] [On the Importance of Gating: Memorization vs. In-Context Learning in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16540)
*William L. Tong,Aryo Lotfi,Emmanuel Abbe,Kostas Vaggelakos,Vishnu Banna,Etai Littwin,Josh Susskind,Cengiz Pehlevan,Eran Malach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验证明了门控机制是决定状态空间模型(SSM)在上下文学习与精确检索任务上表现优劣的关键因素：门控会促使模型优先学习"权重内记忆(memorization)"方案，从而延迟甚至阻碍其收敛到正确的上下文学习方案，即便架构本身并不存在根本性的容量限制；同时，门控又有利于改善模型在长序列上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM作为Transformer的替代方案，具有恒定内存和线性计算的优势，虽然性能尚可，但在需要上下文学习和精确检索的任务上仍落后于Transformer，这阻碍了其在大规模语言建模中的广泛应用。作者希望揭示SSM在这些领域成败背后的根本机制，以便理解和改进这类线性时间模型。

Method: 作者通过理论分析与实验相结合的方法，聚焦于现代循环网络中的常见组件——门控机制，系统研究其在SSM训练过程中的作用。具体地，对比了SSM在有门控与无门控条件下的上下文学习与记忆行为的训练动态，并考察其对长序列泛化的影响。

Result: 结果表明：门控机制导致SSM首先学会"权重内记忆"的解决方案，同时推迟甚至阻止其收敛到正确的上下文学习方案，这种情况即使在没有架构或容量根本限制时也会出现；相反，门控在提升长序列长度泛化方面通常是受益的。

Conclusion: 门控机制在塑造SSM的训练动态和泛化能力中扮演着至关重要的角色，这一发现为理解和改进线性时间模型提供了理论基础，并为后续设计更优的SSM架构指明了方向。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) have emerged as a compelling alternative to Transformers, enabling sequence modeling with constant memory and linear compute. Although SSMs exhibit reasonable performance and favorable computational characteristics, they continue to lag behind Transformers on tasks that require in-context learning and precise retrieval, slowing their adoption for large-scale language modeling. In this work, we demonstrate that both the success and failure of SSMs in these domains can be explained by studying the role of the gating mechanism, a prevalent component in modern recurrent networks. Specifically, we show through theory and experiments that this gating mechanism causes SSMs to first learn an in-weights "memorization" solution, while delaying, or even preventing, convergence to a correct in-context learning solution. Importantly, this happens even in cases where there are no fundamental limitations due to the architecture or its memory capacity. On the other hand, we find that gating is often beneficial for improving generalization to long sequence lengths. Our results illuminate the crucial role of the gating mechanism in shaping both the training dynamics and generalization of SSMs, and provide a basis for understanding and improving linear-time models.

</details>


### [294] [AsyncCouple-Flow: Asynchronous Cross-Modal Coupling and Flow Matching for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16573)
*Zhixiang Wu,Yining Liu,Bo Zhao,Szu-Yu Chen,Huiran Duan,Chu Lin,Chuanguang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG
